9 月 27 日你自己说的重点 —— 直接从这三个开始：
脂肪酸合成 · 甘油三酯与磷脂 · 胆固醇（你说「脂质合成有点模糊」）
维生素与辅酶 · 谁搬什么（你说「完全没碰」）
磷酸戊糖 · 糖异生 · 糖原 · 酮体 · 尿素循环（除三大以外的代谢）

同物异名 · 考官换个名字问，你要认得出是同一个

  • Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions = reductive pentose phosphate cycle（卡尔文循环 = 暗反应）· "dark" 是误导：白天照跑，只是不直接用光。carbon fixation 是它干的事（第一步 rubisco），不算同义词

Structure & properties

Protein structure

卡 01 · 四层结构，每层靠什么键

Must say

Four levels. Primary is the covalent level, the order of residues. Secondary is held by main-chain hydrogen bonds only, independent of the side chains. Tertiary is held mainly by the hydrophobic effect. Quaternary is several chains, all non-covalent.

二级只靠主链自己的氢键，跟侧链无关——这一句是分水岭。

Numbers

α-helix: 3.6 residues per turn, rise 0.54 nm, hydrogen bond from C=O to the N–H of i+4. Trp and Tyr absorb at 280 nm.

反平行 β 折叠（= β-sheet = β-pleated sheet）比平行的稳（氢键正对着）。

Hook

Denaturation destroys the higher structure but not the primary structure. Sickle-cell is one residue: Glu → Val at position 6 of the β chain.

Say this

A protein has four levels of structure, and the useful way to remember them is by what holds each one together. The primary structure is the covalent level: the order of residues joined by peptide bonds (also called amide bond), including the disulfide positions. The secondary structure is local, and it is held by hydrogen bonds of the main chain only — the side chains take no part. The tertiary structure is the fold of one whole chain, held mainly by the hydrophobic effect, with ionic bonds, hydrogen bonds and disulfides as well. The quaternary structure is several chains together, and it is entirely non-covalent. ⚠ Denaturation destroys everything above the primary level, so the protein loses its activity, but the peptide bonds are not broken.

约 40 秒 · 「按什么键把它们绑住」是这一段的骨架

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hudeček、Martínek

① 肽键是残基之间唯一的共价键；它不能旋转，所以链只能在它两侧折 partial double bond · planar · trans

The peptide bond is the only covalent bond linking one residue to the next. Its partial double-bond character makes it planar and unable to rotate, so the chain can only turn at the bonds on either side of it. It is almost always trans; cis occurs practically only before proline.

一级结构里的键就是它。

② 二硫键之所以要单说：多数蛋白里它是唯一的共价交联，所以三级结构不是纯非共价 disulfide bond

The disulfide bond is, in most proteins, the only covalent cross-link, which is why tertiary structure is not purely non-covalent. It is an S–S bond between two cysteines, made by oxidation in the endoplasmic reticulum, not in the reducing cytosol. It joins the A and B chains of insulin.

三级结构里唯一的共价键。

③ 氢键和其余非共价力不同，它有方向性，所以能把二级结构固定住 hydrogen bond

The hydrogen bond, unlike the other non-covalent forces, has a fixed direction, and that is why it can hold the α-helix and β-sheet (also called β-pleated sheet) in shape. It is an attraction between a hydrogen on nitrogen or oxygen and a lone pair on another nitrogen or oxygen.

C–H 不能形成氢键。

④ 盐桥是 pH 一变就消失的那一种力，所以强酸强碱能让蛋白变性 salt bridge · ionic bond

The salt bridge, the second non-covalent force, is the one that pH switches off, which is why strong acid or base denatures a protein. It is the attraction between a negative side chain, aspartate or glutamate, and a positive one, lysine or arginine.

所以强酸强碱能使蛋白变性。

⑤ 范德华力靠数量不靠强度：单个极弱，但蛋白内部堆得紧，数量极多 van der Waals

The van der Waals attraction, the third, counts by number, not by strength. Each one is very weak, but the protein interior is tightly packed and contains a very large number of them. It acts between any atoms in close contact.

没有方向性，决定内部堆积得多紧。

⑥ 疏水效应是折叠的主要动力，而且根本不是键：是水的熵增加 hydrophobic effect

The hydrophobic effect is the main force that folds the chain, and it is not a bond at all. Water ordered around nonpolar side chains is released when those side chains come together, so the entropy of the water increases.

说出「不是键」是加分。

⑦ 变性破坏二、三、四级结构，一级结构不变，因为没有断肽键 denaturation

Denaturation destroys the secondary, tertiary and quaternary structure but leaves the primary structure, because no peptide bond is broken. Heat, urea, strong acid or base, and detergents break the non-covalent forces, and β-mercaptoethanol reduces the disulfides.

煮鸡蛋不是水解。

⑧ 书外：Anfinsen 的核糖核酸酶实验是「序列决定结构」的经典证明 Anfinsen · ribonuclease

Christian Anfinsen's ribonuclease experiment is the classic proof that the sequence determines the structure. He denatured ribonuclease with urea and β-mercaptoethanol, removed them slowly, and the enzyme refolded and became active again. The name Anfinsen and the ribonuclease experiment are general knowledge rather than from my textbook.

「序列决定结构」的实验证据。

⑨ 但在细胞里折叠不都是自发的：很多蛋白要分子伴侣挡住聚集 chaperones

In the cell, however, folding is not spontaneous for every protein: many need chaperones (also called heat shock protein (many of them)) such as Hsp70 and the GroEL–GroES complex, which bind partly folded chains and stop them from aggregating.

试管里能自己折，细胞里常需要分子伴侣（= chaperone = heat shock protein (many of them)）。

⑩ 书外：淀粉样纤维是折叠失败的产物；朊病毒更极端，不带核酸也能传病 amyloid · prion

When folding fails, proteins can aggregate into amyloid fibres, as amyloid-β does in Alzheimer's disease. A prion is the extreme case: a misfolded protein that converts normal copies of itself, so the disease spreads without any nucleic acid, as in Creutzfeldt–Jakob disease. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑪ 圆二色谱和色氨酸荧光问的是两个不同的问题 ⭐ Hudeček CD · tryptophan fluorescence

The two answer different questions. Circular dichroism measures the difference in absorption of left and right circularly polarised light, which gives the α-helix and β-sheet content. Tryptophan fluorescence shifts to a longer wavelength when a buried tryptophan is exposed to water, which shows the protein has unfolded.

先说测的物理量，再说推出了什么。

⑫ 分子动力学模拟用的正是前面这些键和力 ⭐ Martínek molecular dynamics

A molecular dynamics simulation is where all these bonds and forces are used directly: it calculates the movement of a protein in water from them. It can propose a hypothesis, but an experiment has to test it.

「模拟提出假设，实验证明它」。

⑬ 盐析把蛋白挤出溶液但不解折叠，所以它是纯化不是变性 salting out

Salting out takes the protein out of solution without unfolding it, which is why it is purification and not denaturation. The solubility of a globular protein depends on ionic strength, named salting in and salting out; high salt takes away the water the protein needs.

一条调 pH，一条调盐，两条别混。

⑭ 书外 · general：硫酸铵盐析是可逆的，这正是它和变性的区别 ammonium sulfate precipitation

Ammonium sulfate precipitation is reversible, and that is the difference from denaturation: the precipitate dissolves again with its activity intact. Ammonium sulfate is the usual reagent because it is very soluble and does not denature the protein. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

盐析只是把它挤出来，没有解折叠。

⑮ 书外 · general：chaperonin 是分子伴侣里带封闭腔的那一类，GroEL–GroES 是标准例子 chaperonin · GroEL–GroES

A chaperonin is the kind of chaperone with a closed chamber. GroEL–GroES is the standard example: a barrel with a cap that encloses one chain so that it folds without meeting another. The textbook names GroEL and GroES; the word chaperonin itself is general knowledge.

chaperone 是上位词，chaperonin 是其中一类。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the hydrophobic effect a bond?" — No, it is an increase in the entropy of water. · 不是键。
  • "Does denaturation break peptide bonds?" — No; only hydrolysis by acid or a protease does. · 不断肽键（= peptide bond = amide bond）。
  • "Do salt bridges hold the α-helix?" — No, main-chain hydrogen bonds do. · 螺旋靠主链氢键。
  • "Is tertiary structure entirely non-covalent?" — No, disulfide bonds are covalent. · 别忘了二硫键。
  • "Can a C–H group form a hydrogen bond?" — No, carbon is not electronegative enough. · C–H 不行。

定义

  • partial double bond — a bond between a single and a double bond, which cannot rotate · 部分双键
  • trans / cis — the two α-carbons on opposite / the same side of the peptide bond · 反式 / 顺式
  • reducing — keeping –SH groups as –SH, so disulfides do not form · 还原性
  • entropy — a measure of disorder · 熵
  • urea — a small molecule that unfolds proteins at high concentration · 尿素
  • β-mercaptoethanol — a reducing agent that breaks disulfide bonds · β-巯基乙醇
  • denaturation — loss of secondary, tertiary and quaternary structure · 变性
  • chaperone — a protein that helps other proteins fold · 分子伴侣
  • amyloid — insoluble fibres of misfolded protein · 淀粉样纤维
  • prion — a misfolded protein that converts normal copies of itself · 朊病毒
  • circular dichroism — difference in absorption of left and right circularly polarised light · 圆二色谱
  • molecular dynamics — computer simulation of atomic movement over time · 分子动力学
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acid — a substance that gives up protons, lowering the pH of a solution · 酸
  • activity — the ability of a protein to do its job, measured as a rate · 活性
  • aggregation — misfolded chains sticking to one another into insoluble clumps · 聚集
  • α-helix — a right-handed coil of the backbone, the commonest secondary structure · α 螺旋
  • Alzheimer's disease — a degenerative brain disease in which amyloid-β is deposited · 阿尔茨海默病
  • amino acid — a molecule carrying an amino group, a carboxyl group and a side chain on one carbon · 氨基酸
  • ammonium sulfate — a very soluble salt used to salt proteins out of solution · 硫酸铵
  • amyloid-β — the peptide that forms amyloid deposits in Alzheimer's disease · β 淀粉样蛋白
  • arginine — the amino acid with the most basic, positively charged side chain · 精氨酸
  • aspartate — an amino acid with a second carboxyl group, so negatively charged · 天冬氨酸
  • base — a substance that accepts protons, raising the pH of a solution · 碱
  • β-sheet — secondary structure of extended strands lying side by side, joined by main-chain hydrogen bonds · β 折叠
  • chaperonin — a chaperone with a closed chamber that encloses one chain while it folds · 伴侣蛋白
  • charge — an excess or shortage of electrons, making a group positive or negative · 电荷
  • Christian Anfinsen — showed with ribonuclease that the amino acid sequence determines the folded structure · 安芬森
  • conformation — one of the three-dimensional arrangements a molecule can take without breaking a bond · 构象
  • covalent bond — a bond in which two atoms share a pair of electrons · 共价键
  • Creutzfeldt–Jakob disease — a fatal human brain disease caused by a prion · 克雅氏病
  • cysteine — the sulfur-containing amino acid whose thiol forms disulfide bonds · 半胱氨酸
  • cytosol — the fluid part of a cell, outside the organelles · 胞质溶胶
  • detergent — a molecule with a polar head and a nonpolar tail, which dissolves membranes and unfolds proteins · 去污剂
  • disulfide bond — a covalent S–S bond between the thiols of two cysteines · 二硫键
  • endoplasmic reticulum — the membrane compartment where secreted proteins fold and are modified · 内质网
  • folding — the process by which a chain takes up its three-dimensional shape · 折叠
  • globular protein — a soluble protein folded into a compact, roughly spherical shape · 球状蛋白
  • glutamate — an amino acid with a second carboxyl group, so negatively charged · 谷氨酸
  • GroEL–GroES — the bacterial chaperonin: a barrel, GroEL, closed by a cap, GroES · GroEL–GroES
  • Hsp70 — a chaperone that binds exposed hydrophobic stretches of a partly folded chain · Hsp70
  • hydrogen bond — attraction between a hydrogen on nitrogen or oxygen and a lone pair on another nitrogen or oxygen · 氢键
  • hydrolysis — breaking a bond by adding water across it · 水解
  • hydrophobic effect — nonpolar groups coming together because the ordered water around them is released · 疏水效应
  • i+4 — the residue four places further along the chain, whose N–H accepts the α-helix hydrogen bond · 第 i+4 位残基
  • insulin — the hormone that lowers blood glucose, two chains held together by disulfide bonds · 胰岛素
  • ionic strength — a measure of the total concentration of ions in a solution · 离子强度
  • lysine — an amino acid with a positively charged amino group at the end of its side chain · 赖氨酸
  • main chain — the repeating N–Cα–C=O atoms of every residue, without the side chains · 主链
  • non-covalent interaction — an attraction between groups that does not involve shared electrons · 非共价作用
  • nonpolar — carrying no charge and no polar group, so not attracted to water · 非极性
  • oxidation — loss of electrons; here the loss of two hydrogens that joins two thiols · 氧化
  • peptide bond — the amide bond joining the carboxyl of one residue to the amino group of the next · 肽键
  • pH — the negative logarithm of the hydrogen ion concentration of a solution · pH
  • polypeptide — one chain of amino acids joined by peptide bonds · 多肽
  • primary structure — the order of residues in the chain, including where the disulfides are · 一级结构
  • proline — the amino acid whose side chain closes back onto its own nitrogen, leaving no N–H · 脯氨酸
  • protease — an enzyme that hydrolyses peptide bonds · 蛋白酶
  • protein — a chain of amino acids joined by peptide bonds, folded into a defined shape that determines its function · 蛋白质
  • quaternary structure — the arrangement of several folded chains within one protein · 四级结构
  • residue — one amino acid as it stands inside a chain, after water was lost in forming the bond · 残基
  • ribonuclease — an enzyme that hydrolyses RNA; the protein of Anfinsen's refolding experiment · 核糖核酸酶
  • salt bridge — the attraction between a negatively charged and a positively charged side chain · 盐桥
  • salting in — the rise in a protein's solubility when a small amount of salt is added · 盐溶
  • salting out — precipitating a protein with high salt, without unfolding it · 盐析
  • secondary structure — local regular shape held by main-chain hydrogen bonds alone · 二级结构
  • sequence — the order in which the residues follow one another along the chain · 序列
  • sickle-cell anaemia — the disease in which glutamate 6 of the β chain is replaced by valine · 镰状细胞贫血
  • side chain — the R group that differs between one amino acid and another · 侧链
  • solubility — how much of a substance dissolves in a given volume of solvent · 溶解度
  • tertiary structure — the three-dimensional fold of one whole chain · 三级结构
  • tryptophan — the aromatic amino acid with a two-ring side chain, absorbing most strongly at 280 nm · 色氨酸
  • tryptophan fluorescence — light re-emitted by tryptophan, shifting to a longer wavelength when it becomes exposed to water · 色氨酸荧光
  • tyrosine — an aromatic amino acid carrying a hydroxyl on its ring, absorbing at 280 nm · 酪氨酸
  • van der Waals force — a weak attraction between any two atoms in close contact · 范德华力

Proline & glycine — the two odd ones

卡 01 · 几乎必被追问的一对

Must say

Proline is the helix breaker, for two reasons: its nitrogen has no N–H left to donate the hydrogen bond, and its five-membered ring locks one backbone angle. The same two reasons put it in β-turns (also called reverse turn, β-bend). Glycine has only a hydrogen as its side chain, so it is the most flexible and the only one with no chiral carbon.

Numbers

On a Ramachandran plot glycine has the largest allowed region and proline the smallest.

拉氏图画的是 φ 和 ψ 两个二面角的允许区。

Hook

Collagen needs a glycine every third residue — only glycine fits the crowded centre of the triple helix.

Say this

Two residues behave differently from the other eighteen. Proline is the helix breaker, for two reasons: its side chain closes back onto its own nitrogen, so there is no N–H left to donate a hydrogen bond, and the five-membered ring locks one of the backbone dihedral angles. Those same two reasons make it common in beta turns, where the chain has to change direction. Glycine is the opposite: its side chain is a single hydrogen, so it is the most flexible residue and the only one without a chiral carbon. On a Ramachandran plot glycine has the largest allowed region and proline the smallest. ⚠ And that flexibility is why collagen needs a glycine at every third position — nothing else fits the centre of the triple helix.

约 40 秒 · 脯氨酸那两个理由要一口气说完

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Lipovová

① 二十种氨基酸只在侧链上不同，所以分组就按侧链；甘氨酸是唯一没有手性碳的 grouped by side chain

The twenty differ only in the side chain, which is what groups them into nonpolar, polar uncharged, negatively charged and positively charged. Glycine, whose side chain is one hydrogen, is the only one with no chiral carbon.

非极性 8 · 极性不带电 6 · 负 2 · 正 3 · 甘氨酸单列。

② Ala、Val、Leu、Ile 的侧链是纯碳氢，所以它们待在蛋白质内部 Ala · Val · Leu · Ile

Alanine, valine, leucine and isoleucine have hydrocarbon side chains, which is why they sit in the protein interior. Alanine has a methyl side chain; valine replaces glutamate in sickle-cell haemoglobin; leucine is purely ketogenic; isoleucine has two chiral carbons.

Ala 甲基 · Val 镰状细胞 · Leu 纯生酮 · Ile 两个手性碳。

③ 芳香环之所以要说，是因为蛋白在 280 nm 的吸收就来自它们，色氨酸最强 Phe · Tyr · Trp

The aromatic rings are why a protein absorbs at 280 nm, most strongly through tryptophan. Phenylalanine, an essential amino acid, is converted into tyrosine; tyrosine is both aromatic and polar and the precursor of adrenaline and thyroxine; and tryptophan is the precursor of serotonin.

Phe → Tyr · Tyr 做肾上腺素、甲状腺素 · Trp 做血清素。

④ 半胱氨酸和甲硫氨酸都带硫，起作用的正是那个硫 Cys · Met

Cysteine and methionine both carry sulfur, and in both it is the sulfur that reacts. Cysteine, through its thiol, is in most proteins the only residue that cross-links covalently, in a disulfide bond; methionine starts every new chain, and as S-adenosylmethionine it is the cell's main methyl donor.

Cys 二硫键 · Met 起始 ＋ SAM 供甲基。

⑤ 丝氨酸、苏氨酸重要就重要在一个羟基：加上酪氨酸，就是激酶磷酸化的位点 Ser · Thr

Serine and threonine matter because of one group, the hydroxyl: with tyrosine they are the residues that kinases phosphorylate. Serine and threonine also carry O-linked sugars, and serine is the catalytic residue of chymotrypsin and trypsin.

Ser、Thr、Tyr = 磷酸化位点。

⑥ 谷氨酸是转氨的中心；它和天冬氨酸带第二个羧基，所以带负电 Asp · Glu · Asn · Gln

Glutamate is the centre of amino-group transfer, and with aspartate it carries a second carboxyl group, so both are negatively charged. Asparagine and glutamine are their amides: asparagine carries the N-linked sugars, glutamine carries ammonia in the blood.

Glu 转氨中心 · Asn 挂 N-糖 · Gln 运氨。

⑦ 赖氨酸挂生物素；精氨酸最碱、出尿素；组氨酸是唯一在 pH 7 附近改变电荷的，所以能搬质子 Lys · Arg · His

Lysine has a terminal amino group, where biotin is attached to its enzymes. Arginine, the most basic side chain, has a guanidino group and releases urea in the urea cycle (also called ornithine cycle, Krebs–Henseleit cycle). Histidine is the only side chain that changes charge near pH 7, which is why it moves protons in catalysis; its imidazole ring binds nickel in a His-tag column.

Lys 挂生物素 · Arg 最碱、出尿素 · His 搬质子、His-tag。

⑧ 氨基酸还是原料，这一条把血红素、核苷酸、信号三个主题接起来 beyond proteins

Amino acids are also raw material, which is how this topic connects to haem, nucleotides and signalling: glycine with succinyl-CoA makes haem and enters the purine ring, arginine gives nitric oxide, and glutamate gives GABA.

接血红蛋白、核苷酸两个主题。

⑨ 羟脯氨酸稳定胶原的三螺旋，而做这一步要维生素 C，缺了就是坏血病 hydroxyproline · vitamin C · scurvy

In collagen many prolines are converted into hydroxyproline, which stabilises the triple helix, and that step needs vitamin C. Without vitamin C the collagen is weak, which is scurvy, with bleeding gums.

接维生素主题。

⑩ 「必需」的意思是人体根本做不出来，九种全靠吃 essential amino acids

Essential means the body cannot make them at all, so all nine must come from food: threonine, tryptophan, phenylalanine, leucine, isoleucine, lysine, methionine, valine and histidine.

苏 色 苯 · 亮 异 赖 · 甲 缬 组。

⑪ 定点突变换成丙氨酸，是因为它只去掉侧链、不动主链 ⭐ Lipovová site-directed mutagenesis

Alanine is the standard replacement in site-directed mutagenesis, because it removes the side chain without changing the backbone, and swapping lysine for glutamate tests a salt bridge.

从氨基酸表回到实验。

⑫ 书外：硒代半胱氨酸和吡咯赖氨酸插在终止密码子上，那个密码子被读过去了 selenocysteine · pyrrolysine

Selenocysteine and pyrrolysine are amino acids 21 and 22, inserted at a UGA or UAG stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon) that is read through instead. Selenocysteine occurs in glutathione peroxidase, pyrrolysine in archaea. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is glycine L or D?" — Neither; it has no chiral carbon. · 都不是。
  • "Is tyrosine aromatic or polar?" — Both; different books put it in different groups. · 两者都是。
  • "Cysteine or cystine?" — Cystine is two cysteines joined by a disulfide bond. · 胱氨酸是两个半胱氨酸。
  • "Which side chain is the most basic?" — Arginine. · 精氨酸。
  • "Does a His-tag bind through lysine?" — No, through the imidazole of histidine. · 是组氨酸的咪唑。

定义

  • side chain — the R group that differs between amino acids · 侧链
  • chiral carbon — a carbon carrying four different groups · 手性碳
  • ketogenic — degraded to acetyl-CoA or acetoacetate, not to glucose · 生酮
  • S-adenosylmethionine — activated methionine that donates methyl groups · S-腺苷甲硫氨酸
  • phosphorylation — adding a phosphate group, done by a kinase · 磷酸化
  • imidazole — the five-membered ring with two nitrogens in histidine · 咪唑
  • guanidino group — the strongly basic group of arginine · 胍基
  • GABA — gamma-aminobutyric acid, an inhibitory neurotransmitter · γ-氨基丁酸
  • scurvy — disease caused by lack of vitamin C · 坏血病
  • essential amino acid — one the body cannot synthesise · 必需氨基酸
  • site-directed mutagenesis — changing one chosen codon in a gene · 定点突变
  • stop codon — UAA, UAG or UGA, which normally ends translation · 终止密码子（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon）
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • adrenaline — the fight-or-flight hormone of the adrenal gland, made from tyrosine · 肾上腺素
  • alanine — the amino acid whose side chain is a single methyl group · 丙氨酸
  • α-helix — a right-handed coil of the backbone held by main-chain hydrogen bonds · α 螺旋
  • amino acid — a molecule carrying an amino group, a carboxyl group and a side chain on one carbon · 氨基酸
  • amino group — an –NH₂ group, which takes up a proton and becomes positive · 氨基
  • ammonia — NH₃, the toxic nitrogen compound released when amino acids are broken down · 氨
  • archaea — one of the three domains of life, single-celled organisms distinct from bacteria · 古菌
  • arginine — the amino acid with the most basic side chain, a guanidino group · 精氨酸
  • aromatic — containing a flat ring of delocalised electrons, which absorbs ultraviolet light · 芳香族
  • asparagine — the amide of aspartate; it carries N-linked sugar chains · 天冬酰胺
  • aspartate — an amino acid with a second carboxyl group, so negatively charged · 天冬氨酸
  • backbone — the repeating N–Cα–C=O atoms running along the chain, without the side chains · 主链
  • β-turn — a short region where the chain reverses direction, often containing proline · β 转角（= β-turn = reverse turn = β-bend）
  • biotin — the vitamin that carries carbon dioxide, attached to a lysine of its enzyme · 生物素
  • carboxyl group — a –COOH group, which gives up a proton and becomes negative · 羧基
  • chymotrypsin — a digestive serine protease that cuts after large hydrophobic residues · 胰凝乳蛋白酶
  • codon — three consecutive bases of mRNA, read as one amino acid or as a stop · 密码子
  • collagen — the fibrous protein of connective tissue, a triple helix with glycine at every third position · 胶原蛋白
  • column — a tube packed with beads, through which a mixture is passed to separate its components · 层析柱
  • cysteine — the amino acid whose thiol forms disulfide bonds · 半胱氨酸
  • dihedral angle — φ and ψ, the two rotations of the backbone on either side of each α-carbon · 二面角
  • disulfide bond — a covalent S–S bond between the thiols of two cysteines · 二硫键
  • glutamate — an amino acid with a second carboxyl group, so negatively charged · 谷氨酸
  • glutamine — the amide of glutamate; it carries ammonia in the blood · 谷氨酰胺
  • glutathione peroxidase — a selenocysteine enzyme that destroys peroxides · 谷胱甘肽过氧化物酶
  • glycine — the smallest amino acid, its side chain a single hydrogen · 甘氨酸
  • haem — an iron atom held in a flat porphyrin ring · 血红素
  • His-tag — a short run of histidines added to a protein so that it binds a nickel column · 组氨酸标签
  • histidine — the only amino acid whose side chain changes charge near pH 7 · 组氨酸
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • hydroxyl group — an –OH group · 羟基
  • hydroxyproline — proline with an added hydroxyl, which stabilises the collagen triple helix · 羟脯氨酸
  • isoleucine — a branched hydrocarbon amino acid, carrying two chiral carbons · 异亮氨酸
  • kinase — an enzyme that transfers a phosphate group from ATP onto another molecule · 激酶
  • leucine — a branched hydrocarbon amino acid, degraded only to acetyl-CoA, never to glucose · 亮氨酸
  • lysine — an amino acid with a positively charged amino group at the end of its side chain · 赖氨酸
  • methionine — the sulfur amino acid that starts every new chain · 甲硫氨酸
  • methyl group — a –CH₃ group, transferred from one molecule to another in methylation · 甲基
  • N-linked — a sugar chain attached to the nitrogen of an asparagine side chain · N-连接
  • nitric oxide — NO, a gas that acts as a signalling molecule, made from arginine · 一氧化氮
  • nonpolar — carrying no charge and no polar group, so not attracted to water · 非极性
  • O-linked — a sugar chain attached to the oxygen of a serine or threonine · O-连接
  • phenylalanine — an essential aromatic amino acid, converted in the body into tyrosine · 苯丙氨酸
  • polar — having an uneven distribution of charge, so able to interact with water · 极性
  • precursor — the molecule from which another is made · 前体
  • proline — the amino acid whose side chain closes back onto its own nitrogen · 脯氨酸
  • purine — the double-ring bases, adenine and guanine · 嘌呤
  • pyrrolysine — the 22nd amino acid, inserted at a UAG codon in some archaea · 吡咯赖氨酸
  • Ramachandran plot — a plot of the two backbone dihedral angles showing which combinations are allowed · 拉氏图
  • residue — one amino acid as it stands inside a chain, after water was lost in forming the bond · 残基
  • salt bridge — the attraction between a negatively charged and a positively charged side chain · 盐桥
  • selenocysteine — the 21st amino acid, cysteine with selenium in place of sulfur, inserted at a UGA codon · 硒代半胱氨酸
  • serine — an amino acid with a hydroxyl side chain, and a site of phosphorylation · 丝氨酸
  • serotonin — a neurotransmitter made from tryptophan · 血清素
  • sickle-cell haemoglobin — haemoglobin with valine in place of glutamate at position 6 of the β chain · 镰状细胞血红蛋白
  • succinyl-CoA — an intermediate of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）; with glycine it begins haem synthesis · 琥珀酰辅酶 A
  • sugar — a carbohydrate unit: an aldehyde or ketone carrying several hydroxyl groups · 糖
  • thiol — an –SH group · 巯基
  • threonine — an amino acid with a hydroxyl side chain, and a site of phosphorylation · 苏氨酸
  • thyroxine — the iodine-containing thyroid hormone, made from tyrosine · 甲状腺素
  • triple helix — three separate chains wound around one another, as in collagen · 三股螺旋
  • trypsin — a digestive serine protease that cuts after lysine or arginine · 胰蛋白酶
  • tryptophan — the aromatic amino acid with a two-ring side chain, absorbing most strongly at 280 nm · 色氨酸
  • tyrosine — an aromatic amino acid carrying a hydroxyl on its ring · 酪氨酸
  • urea — the nitrogen waste product excreted in urine · 尿素
  • urea cycle — the liver pathway that converts ammonia into urea · 尿素循环（= urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle）
  • valine — a branched hydrocarbon amino acid · 缬氨酸
  • vitamin C — ascorbic acid, needed to hydroxylate the prolines of collagen · 维生素 C

Myoglobin & haemoglobin

卡 01 · 协同效应的教科书例子

Must say

Myoglobin is one subunit and gives a hyperbolic curve — it stores oxygen. Haemoglobin is four subunits and gives a sigmoid curve — it transports it. The sigmoid shape is positive cooperativity: binding the first oxygen pulls the subunits from the T state (also called tense state, deoxy form) to the R state (also called relaxed state, oxy form).

Numbers

Four subunits, α₂β₂, four oxygens.

Hook

Three things stabilise the T state and make it release oxygen in the tissues: H⁺ and CO₂ (the Bohr effect) and 2,3-BPG (also called 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate, 2,3-DPG), which sits in the positively charged central cavity. Without 2,3-BPG haemoglobin barely releases any oxygen at all.

Say this

Myoglobin has one subunit and a hyperbolic binding curve, and it stores oxygen in muscle. Haemoglobin has four subunits, α₂β₂, and a sigmoid curve, and it transports oxygen. The sigmoid shape means positive cooperativity: binding the first oxygen shifts the subunits from the T state, which binds weakly, to the R state, which binds strongly. Three things stabilise the T state and therefore make haemoglobin release oxygen where it is needed: protons and carbon dioxide, which together are the Bohr effect, and 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate, which sits in the positively charged central cavity. ⚠ Without 2,3-BPG, haemoglobin would hold onto its oxygen and hardly release any in the tissues.

约 40 秒 · 「双曲线储氧 / S 形运输」是开口第一句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Martínková、Hodek、Hudeček

① 氧结合在二价铁上但不把它氧化；铁一旦成了三价就是高铁血红蛋白，带不了氧 haem · Fe²⁺ · methaemoglobin

Oxygen binds the ferrous iron, Fe²⁺, of haem without oxidising it; if the iron does become Fe³⁺, the protein is methaemoglobin and cannot bind oxygen.

铁必须是二价。

② 肌红蛋白是储氧不是运氧：单链一个血红素，鲸和海豹特别多 myoglobin

Myoglobin, a single chain with one haem, stores oxygen rather than carrying it; it gives muscle its red colour, and whales and seals have high amounts for long dives.

储氧的蛋白，潜水动物最多。

③ 二氧化碳不是照着氧的方式运的：主要以碳酸氢根走，只有一小部分结合在血红蛋白上 CO₂ as bicarbonate

Carbon dioxide is not carried the way oxygen is. Most of it travels as bicarbonate, made in red cells by carbonic anhydrase; only a smaller part binds to haemoglobin itself.

接 Bohr 效应和血液 pH。

④ 胎儿必须把氧从母血里接过来，所以亲和力必须更高：靠的是和 2,3-BPG 结合得弱 foetal haemoglobin

Foetal haemoglobin has to take oxygen from the mother's blood across the placenta, so it must bind oxygen more tightly. Its two α and two γ chains bind 2,3-BPG weakly, which is what raises the affinity.

同一个调节分子，结合弱就抓氧更牢。

⑤ 调节放氧的是 2,3-BPG，不是血红蛋白本身：高原和贫血时它增加 high altitude

One way the body tunes oxygen delivery is by changing 2,3-BPG rather than haemoglobin itself. At high altitude and in anaemia red cells make more, so more oxygen is released in the tissues.

2,3-BPG（= 2,3-BPG = 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate = 2,3-DPG） 来自糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的一条支路。

⑥ 一氧化碳毒两次：结合得比氧牢得多，还把其余亚基锁在 R 态不放氧 carbon monoxide

Carbon monoxide poisons twice over. It binds the haem iron far more tightly than oxygen, and it holds the other subunits in the R state, so the oxygen still bound is not released. It also inhibits Complex IV (also called cytochrome c oxidase) of the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain).

带得少，带着的也不放。

⑦ 血红素把氨基酸和柠檬酸循环接到这个主题上：甘氨酸 ＋ 琥珀酰辅酶 A 合成，降解成胆红素 haem synthesis · bilirubin

Haem connects this topic to amino acids and the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle), because it is made from glycine and succinyl-CoA. When old red cells are broken down it becomes biliverdin and then bilirubin, the yellow bile pigment that colours stool and urine.

接氨基酸和柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）两个主题。

⑧ 肌红蛋白是第一个被解出来的蛋白质结构：Kendrew 1957；Perutz 解出血红蛋白，1962 年共获诺贝尔奖 Kendrew · Perutz

Myoglobin was the first protein structure solved, by John Kendrew in 1957 using X-ray crystallography. Max Perutz solved haemoglobin, and they shared the 1962 Nobel Prize in Chemistry. The Perutz part and the Nobel Prize are general knowledge, not from my textbook.

最早解出的两个蛋白结构。

⑨ 血红素传感蛋白里，构象变化本身就是信号——和 T→R 同一个原理 ⭐ Martínková haem-based gas sensors

This is the same T-to-R principle: in haem-based gas sensors the conformational change is itself the signal. O₂, NO or CO binds the haem iron, the protein changes shape, and a histidine kinase or another domain at the far end is switched on; soluble guanylate cyclase, the NO receptor, works this way.

「一个位点的结合改变构象，构象本身就是信号」。

⑩ 同一个血红素，P450 拿氧去氧化药物而不是运氧；名字就来自 450 nm ⭐ Hodek cytochrome P450

Cytochrome P450, in the liver, contains the same haem, but uses oxygen to oxidise drugs and foreign compounds rather than carrying it. The name is from the 450 nm absorption of its carbon monoxide complex.

同一个血红素，两种功能。

⑪ 氧合和脱氧血红蛋白的吸收光谱不同，所以不抽血也能测血氧饱和度 ⭐ Hudeček UV-Vis absorption spectrum

Because oxyhaemoglobin and deoxyhaemoglobin have different absorption spectra, oxygen saturation can be measured by light alone, with a spectrophotometer or a pulse oximeter.

动脉血鲜红、静脉血暗红就是这个差别。

⑫ 书外：运氧蛋白不一定用铁——章鱼和螃蟹的血蓝蛋白用铜 haemocyanin · leghaemoglobin · thalassaemia

Haemocyanin uses copper, not iron, to carry oxygen in octopus and crabs; leghaemoglobin in legume root nodules keeps oxygen away from nitrogenase; and in thalassaemia too little α or β chain is made. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。镰状细胞是链错了，地贫是链少了。

⑬ 血红素、叶绿素、维生素 B12 是同一个环换三种金属 tetrapyrrole · haem vs chlorophyll

Haem, chlorophyll and vitamin B12 are all tetrapyrroles — four pyrrole rings in a circle around one metal. Haem holds iron, chlorophyll holds magnesium, vitamin B12 holds cobalt. Haem and chlorophyll are even built along the same pathway and separate only at the end, where one enzyme puts iron into the ring and another puts magnesium in. ⚠ The difference is what the metal does: the iron in haem changes between Fe2+ and Fe3+, which is how it carries electrons and binds oxygen, while the magnesium in chlorophyll never changes charge — the electron that light knocks out comes from the ring, not from the metal.

同一个骨架换金属：Fe 搬氧、Mg 采光、Co 做甲基转移。叶绿素还多一条疏水尾巴，把它钉在膜上。

陷阱问法

  • "Why not use myoglobin for transport?" — Its hyperbolic curve would release little oxygen in the tissues. · 双曲线在组织里放不出氧。
  • "Does 2,3-BPG bind oxyhaemoglobin?" — No, it binds the deoxy T state. · 只结合脱氧态。
  • "Is the Hill coefficient the number of sites?" — No; for haemoglobin it is about 2.8 with four sites. · 不是位点数。
  • "Can Fe³⁺ haem carry oxygen?" — No, that is methaemoglobin. · 三价铁不行。
  • "Is haemoglobin an example of tertiary structure?" — No, of quaternary; myoglobin is the tertiary example. · 它是四级结构的例子。

定义

  • haem — an iron atom held in a porphyrin ring · 血红素
  • porphyrin — a flat ring of four pyrroles that binds a metal · 卟啉
  • methaemoglobin — haemoglobin with Fe³⁺, unable to bind oxygen · 高铁血红蛋白
  • bicarbonate — HCO₃⁻, the main form of CO₂ in blood · 碳酸氢根
  • carbonic anhydrase — the enzyme that converts CO₂ and water to bicarbonate · 碳酸酐酶
  • R state — the high-affinity conformation of haemoglobin · R 态（= R state = relaxed state = oxy form）
  • bilirubin — yellow pigment from haem breakdown · 胆红素
  • histidine kinase — an enzyme that phosphorylates its own histidine in bacterial signalling · 组氨酸激酶
  • soluble guanylate cyclase — the haem protein that senses NO and makes cGMP · 可溶性鸟苷酸环化酶
  • cytochrome P450 — haem enzymes that oxidise drugs and foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450
  • pulse oximeter — a clip that measures oxygen saturation by light absorption · 脉搏血氧仪
  • haemocyanin — copper-containing oxygen carrier of molluscs and arthropods · 血蓝蛋白
  • leghaemoglobin — haemoglobin of legume root nodules · 豆血红蛋白
  • thalassaemia — inherited reduced production of α or β globin chains · 地中海贫血
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • tetrapyrrole — a ring built from four pyrrole units around a central metal ion · 四吡咯
  • porphyrin — the tetrapyrrole ring of haem, which holds the iron · 卟啉
  • 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate — a small phosphate compound of red cells that binds deoxyhaemoglobin and makes it release oxygen · 2,3-二磷酸甘油酸
  • absorption spectrum — how strongly a substance absorbs light at each wavelength · 吸收光谱
  • affinity — how tightly a protein holds the molecule it binds · 亲和力
  • anaemia — too little haemoglobin, or too few red cells, to carry enough oxygen · 贫血
  • bile — the fluid the liver secretes into the intestine, carrying bile pigments and bile salts · 胆汁
  • biliverdin — the green pigment formed first when haem is broken down · 胆绿素
  • binding curve — a graph of how much ligand is bound against its concentration · 结合曲线
  • Bohr effect — the release of oxygen from haemoglobin when protons or carbon dioxide rise · 波尔效应
  • carbon monoxide — CO, a colourless gas that binds haem iron far more tightly than oxygen does · 一氧化碳
  • cGMP — cyclic guanosine monophosphate, a small molecule that carries signals inside a cell · 环鸟苷酸
  • citric acid cycle — the mitochondrial cycle that oxidises acetyl-CoA to carbon dioxide · 柠檬酸循环
  • Complex IV（= Complex IV = cytochrome c oxidase） — cytochrome c oxidase, the respiratory chain enzyme that hands electrons to oxygen · 复合物 IV
  • conformational change — a change in a protein's shape with no covalent bond broken · 构象变化
  • deoxyhaemoglobin — haemoglobin with no oxygen bound · 脱氧血红蛋白
  • domain — a part of a protein chain that folds and works as a unit on its own · 结构域
  • Fe²⁺ — ferrous iron, the state of haem iron that binds oxygen · 二价铁
  • Fe³⁺ — ferric iron, the oxidised state, which cannot bind oxygen · 三价铁
  • foetal haemoglobin — the form with two α and two γ chains, which binds oxygen more tightly · 胎儿血红蛋白
  • globin — the protein part of haemoglobin or myoglobin, without the haem · 珠蛋白
  • glycolysis — the pathway that splits one glucose into two pyruvate · 糖酵解
  • haem-based gas sensor — a protein that changes shape, and so signals, when a gas binds its haem · 血红素气体传感蛋白
  • haemoglobin — the oxygen-transporting blood protein of four subunits, each holding one haem · 血红蛋白
  • Hill coefficient — a number describing how cooperative binding is; about 2.8 for haemoglobin · 希尔系数
  • hyperbolic curve — a binding curve that rises and levels off, showing no cooperativity · 双曲线
  • John Kendrew — solved the structure of myoglobin, the first protein structure, in 1957 · 肯德鲁
  • kinase — an enzyme that transfers a phosphate group from ATP onto another molecule · 激酶
  • Max Perutz — solved the structure of haemoglobin · 佩鲁茨
  • myoglobin — the single-chain, single-haem protein that stores oxygen in muscle · 肌红蛋白
  • nitric oxide — NO, a gas that acts as a signalling molecule and binds haem iron · 一氧化氮
  • nitrogenase — the bacterial enzyme that reduces nitrogen gas to ammonia, and is destroyed by oxygen · 固氮酶
  • oxidation — loss of electrons, as when Fe²⁺ becomes Fe³⁺ · 氧化
  • oxygen saturation — the percentage of haemoglobin oxygen-binding sites that are occupied · 血氧饱和度
  • oxyhaemoglobin — haemoglobin with oxygen bound · 氧合血红蛋白
  • pigment — a substance that is coloured because it absorbs some wavelengths of visible light · 色素
  • polypeptide chain — one chain of amino acids joined by peptide bonds（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 多肽链
  • positive cooperativity — binding at one site raising the affinity of the sites still empty · 正协同效应
  • quaternary structure — the arrangement of several folded chains within one protein · 四级结构
  • red blood cell — the cell that carries haemoglobin, and in mammals has no nucleus · 红细胞
  • respiratory chain — the chain of carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that passes electrons to oxygen · 呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）
  • sigmoid curve — an S-shaped binding curve, the sign of positive cooperativity · S 形曲线
  • spectrophotometer — an instrument that measures how much light a sample absorbs · 分光光度计
  • subunit — one polypeptide chain within a protein built of several chains · 亚基
  • succinyl-CoA — an intermediate of the citric acid cycle; with glycine it begins haem synthesis · 琥珀酰辅酶 A
  • T state — the low-affinity conformation of haemoglobin · T 态（= T state = tense state = deoxy form）
  • tertiary structure — the three-dimensional fold of one whole chain · 三级结构
  • X-ray crystallography — working out a molecule's structure from how a crystal of it diffracts X-rays · X 射线晶体学

Enzymes & kinetics

卡 02 · 17 · 19

Must say

An enzyme only lowers the activation energy. It does not change the equilibrium constant and it does not change ΔG. The active site has the highest affinity for the transition state, not for the substrate.

「对过渡态亲和力最高」这一句是理解的分水岭，不是背的。

Numbers

Km (also called Michaelis constant) is the substrate concentration at half Vmax. It is a property of the enzyme, independent of enzyme concentration, and a larger Km means weaker binding. kcat/Km is the specificity constant (also called kcat/Km, catalytic efficiency), limited by diffusion at 10⁸–10⁹ M⁻¹s⁻¹.

Hook

Competitive: Km up, Vmax unchanged, lines meet on the y-axis. Non-competitive: Km unchanged, Vmax down, lines meet on the x-axis. Uncompetitive: both down, lines are parallel. Classic examples: sulfonamides, and malonate on succinate dehydrogenase.

Say this

An enzyme lowers the activation energy and nothing else — it does not change the equilibrium constant and it does not change ΔG, so it only changes how fast equilibrium is reached, not where it lies. ⚠ The key idea is that the active site has its highest affinity for the transition state, not for the substrate, and the binding energy released is what pays for lowering the barrier. Km is the substrate concentration at half Vmax; it is characteristic of the enzyme, independent of enzyme concentration, and a larger Km means weaker binding. For inhibition: competitive raises Km and leaves Vmax, non-competitive lowers Vmax and leaves Km, and uncompetitive lowers both.

约 45 秒 · 「对过渡态亲和力最高」是区分「懂」和「背」的那句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① 之所以要用 kcat/Km，是因为细胞里底物浓度本来就远低于饱和 specificity constant

The specificity constant, kcat/Km, matters because cells normally work far below saturation. It is the rate constant for free enzyme and free substrate going to product, so it gives the efficiency at low substrate concentration.

把结合和催化两步合成一个数。

② 之所以有上限，是因为酶再快也快不过它遇到底物的速度：约 10⁸–10⁹ M⁻¹s⁻¹ diffusion limit

No enzyme can work faster than it meets its substrate, so kcat/Km has a ceiling: the diffusion limit, about 10⁸ to 10⁹ per molar per second. Triose phosphate isomerase and acetylcholinesterase come close and are called catalytically perfect.

到了上限，再改只能改稳定性和专一性。

③ 之所以不能只看 Km，是因为两个底物竞争时速率之比就是 kcat/Km 之比 comparing substrates

When two substrates compete for one enzyme, their rates are in the ratio of their kcat/Km values. That is why kcat/Km, and not Km, is the right number for comparing substrates.

只看 Km 等于只看结合，不看反应。

④ 之所以能到这种速度，是因为活性中心同时用四种手段，每种都有一个标准例子 catalytic strategies

Active sites reach such rates by four means, each with a standard example: acid–base catalysis by histidine; covalent catalysis by the serine of chymotrypsin; metal ions, the zinc of carbonic anhydrase; and holding the substrates close together and correctly oriented.

接氨基酸主题：His 传质子，Ser 进攻底物。

⑤ 之所以缺了维生素代谢就停，是因为大多数辅酶就是维生素做的 coenzymes

Most coenzymes are made from vitamins, which is why those vitamins are needed: NAD⁺ from niacin, FAD from riboflavin, coenzyme A from pantothenic acid, pyridoxal phosphate from vitamin B6.

酶蛋白 ＋ 辅因子 = 全酶。接维生素主题。

⑥ 四种调节手段里，只有酶原激活撤不回来 regulation

Of the four ways of regulating enzymes, only zymogen (also called proenzyme) activation cannot be undone: trypsinogen is cut into trypsin. The reversible three are allosteric effectors, ATP on phosphofructokinase; phosphorylation of glycogen phosphorylase; and feedback inhibition by the end product.

别构 · 磷酸化 · 反馈 · 酶原（= zymogen = proenzyme）。

⑦ 同工酶之所以有用，是因为血里的酶谱能指出坏的是哪个器官；乳酸脱氢酶是标准例子 isoenzymes

Isoenzymes (also called isozyme) are worth naming because their pattern in blood shows which organ is damaged. They catalyse the same reaction but differ in structure; lactate dehydrogenase is the standard example, with different forms in heart and skeletal muscle.

LDH 是标准例子。

⑧ 酶的量之所以用活性报，是因为它称不出来：单位是 U 或 katal enzyme activity

An enzyme is quantified by what it does, not by mass: activity is the rate of product formation, often followed by absorbance, for example NADH at 340 nm, and reported in units or katals.

U = 每分钟 1 μmol；katal = 每秒 1 mol。

⑨ 抑制酶之所以重要，是因为常见的药就是抑制剂：阿司匹林、青霉素、他汀、沙林 drugs as inhibitors

Ordinary drugs are enzyme inhibitors: aspirin irreversibly inhibits cyclooxygenase, penicillin the bacterial enzyme that cross-links the cell wall, statins HMG-CoA reductase in cholesterol synthesis, and sarin, irreversibly, acetylcholinesterase.

不可逆 vs 可逆，接抑制那一段。

⑩ 药物相互作用之所以发生，是因为一种药改变了 P450 清除另一种药的速度 ⭐ Hodek P450 · drug interactions

Drug interactions happen because drugs act on the enzymes that remove them: cytochrome P450 clears many drugs, and grapefruit juice inhibits it while rifampicin induces it, so another drug's level rises or falls.

浓度升到中毒，或降到无效。

⑪ 改造成功与否，报的还是第 ① 条那个 kcat/Km ⭐ Lipovová enzyme engineering

Enzyme engineering reports its success as kcat/Km, the same efficiency number as before: site-directed mutagenesis changes an enzyme's specificity or thermostability, and engineered proteases and amylases are used in washing powders.

流水线：构建 → 表达 → 纯化 → 测活。

⑫ 书外：催化抗体直接证明「结合过渡态就是催化」；核酶是有催化活性的 RNA ribozymes · catalytic antibodies

Catalytic antibodies, raised against a transition-state analogue, show that binding the transition state is itself catalysis. Ribozymes are catalytic RNAs, including the ribosome's peptidyl transferase. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

后者直接证明「结合过渡态 = 催化」。

⑬ 双倒数图的用处全在两个截距上：竖轴 1/Vmax，横轴 −1/Km ⭐ Hudeček Lineweaver–Burk plot

The point of the Lineweaver–Burk plot is its two intercepts: 1/Vmax on the vertical axis, −1/Km on the horizontal. Taking reciprocals of the Michaelis–Menten equation turns the hyperbola into a straight line, one over v against one over substrate, of slope Km/Vmax.

两个截距，两个常数。

⑭ 之所以要拉成直线，是因为直接读 Vmax 得测极高的底物浓度，又不准又费料 ⭐ Hudeček why linearise

The reason is practical, not theoretical. Reading Vmax off the saturation curve needs measurements at very high substrate concentration, which the textbook calls inaccurate and expensive in material. The line gives both constants from the range you can actually measure.

他要的是「这个数到底怎么量出来的」。

⑮ 抑制类型是按动力学定义的，所以唯一要问的是「哪个常数变了」 telling the types apart

The types of inhibition were defined by kinetics, not by chemistry, so the only question to ask about an inhibitor is which of the two constants it changes. On this plot each answer is a pattern of intercepts.

先说哪个数变，再画线，比先画线稳。

⑯ 之所以先分可逆/不可逆，是因为这两类根本不用同一套方程 irreversible inhibition

The first division among inhibitors is irreversible against reversible, because the two are not even described by the same equations. An irreversible inhibitor binds firmly, usually covalently, modifying a functional group, so the enzyme is permanently inactivated.

第一刀切的是「还下不下得来」。

⑰ 不可逆抑制之所以让米氏方程失效，是因为有活性的酶越来越少，而方程假设总量不变 why Michaelis–Menten fails

Irreversible inhibition breaks the Michaelis–Menten equation, because the equation assumes the total enzyme concentration is constant and that only the amount in complex changes. Here enzyme is removed permanently, so the concentration of the active form becomes a function of time.

这一条把名字上的区别变成模型上的区别。

⑱ 书外 · general：自杀性抑制剂之所以叫这个名字，是因为酶自己把它活化成了封死自己的东西 suicide inhibitor · mechanism-based

A suicide inhibitor, also called mechanism-based, is so named because the enzyme itself converts this substrate analogue into a reactive species that then bonds covalently in its own active site. Penicillin acts this way on the bacterial transpeptidase that cross-links the cell wall. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

酶自己把凶器造出来。

⑲ 锁钥模型要求酶和底物本来就互补，而书说不必：酶接触到底物才改变构象 lock and key vs induced fit

The older lock and key picture required enzyme and substrate to be complementary already, as a key fits a lock. The textbook says they need not be: the enzyme changes its conformation to adapt on contact, which is induced fit.

书明说旧图景已经被取代了。

⑳ EC 编号编的是反应，不是蛋白，所以同工酶共用一个号；六大类，四个数字 EC classification

An EC number identifies the reaction, not the protein, which is why isoenzymes share one. The six classes are oxidoreductases, transferases, hydrolases, lyases, isomerases and ligases — “Our Tiny Horse Lives In Lemons”; lactate dehydrogenase is EC 1.1.1.27, systematically L-lactate:NAD⁺ oxidoreductase.

氧 转 水 · 裂 异 连。四个数字：大类 · 亚类 · 受体类型 · 顺序号。

陷阱问法

  • "Does a high kcat/Km mean tight binding?" — No, it combines binding and turnover. · 不等于结合牢。
  • "Is Km the affinity?" — Only when the chemical step is slow. · 只在催化步很慢时。
  • "Can an enzyme exceed the diffusion limit?" — Only when substrate is passed directly between enzymes in a complex, called substrate channelling. · 单个酶不能。
  • "Does an enzyme shift the equilibrium?" — No, it only changes how fast equilibrium is reached. · 不改平衡。

定义

  • kcat — turnover number: substrate molecules converted per enzyme molecule per second · 转换数（= kcat = turnover number）
  • specificity constant — kcat/Km, the efficiency at low substrate · 专一性常数（= specificity constant = kcat/Km = catalytic efficiency）
  • diffusion limit — the rate at which two molecules meet in water · 扩散极限
  • acid–base catalysis — speeding a reaction by giving or taking a proton · 酸碱催化
  • covalent catalysis — the enzyme forms a temporary covalent bond with the substrate · 共价催化
  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule an enzyme needs · 辅酶
  • allosteric effector — a molecule that binds outside the active site and changes activity · 别构效应物
  • feedback inhibition — the end product inhibits the first enzyme of its pathway · 反馈抑制
  • zymogen — an inactive enzyme precursor activated by cleavage · 酶原
  • isoenzymes — different proteins catalysing the same reaction · 同工酶（= isoenzyme = isozyme）
  • katal — the amount of enzyme converting one mole per second · 开特
  • induction — the cell making more of an enzyme · 诱导
  • thermostability — resistance of a protein to heat · 热稳定性
  • transition-state analogue — a stable molecule shaped like the transition state · 过渡态类似物
  • substrate channelling — passing an intermediate directly from one active site to the next · 底物通道
  • Lineweaver–Burk plot — one over rate against one over substrate, a straight line · 双倒数图
  • irreversible inhibition — the inhibitor does not come off, so the enzyme is permanently inactivated · 不可逆抑制
  • suicide inhibitor — a substrate analogue the enzyme activates, which then destroys that enzyme · 自杀性抑制剂
  • lock and key — the older picture, in which enzyme and substrate are already complementary · 锁钥模型
  • EC number — the four-part code identifying the reaction an enzyme catalyses · 酶学委员会编号
  • lyase — the fourth EC class: breaks a bond without water and without oxidation · 裂合酶
  • ligase — the sixth EC class: joins two molecules at the cost of ATP · 连接酶
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • absorbance — how much light a sample takes out of a beam at one wavelength · 吸光度
  • acetylcholinesterase — the enzyme that hydrolyses the neurotransmitter acetylcholine · 乙酰胆碱酯酶
  • activation energy — the energy a reaction needs to reach its transition state · 活化能
  • active site — the pocket of an enzyme in which the substrate binds and reacts · 活性中心
  • amylase — an enzyme that hydrolyses the α-1,4 bonds of starch · 淀粉酶
  • antibiotic — a compound that kills bacteria or stops them growing · 抗生素
  • antibody — a protein made by the immune system that binds one particular molecule · 抗体
  • aspirin — a drug that irreversibly inhibits cyclooxygenase by acetylating it · 阿司匹林
  • binding energy — the energy released when the substrate binds, which pays for lowering the barrier · 结合能
  • carbonic anhydrase — the zinc enzyme that converts carbon dioxide and water into bicarbonate · 碳酸酐酶
  • catalysis — speeding up a reaction by a substance that is not itself used up · 催化
  • catalyst — a substance that speeds a reaction by lowering its activation energy and is recovered unchanged · 催化剂
  • catalytic antibody — an antibody raised against a transition-state analogue, which then catalyses that reaction · 催化抗体
  • catalytically perfect — describing an enzyme whose kcat/Km has reached the diffusion limit · 催化完美
  • cell wall — the rigid cross-linked layer outside the membrane of a bacterial or plant cell · 细胞壁
  • chymotrypsin — a digestive serine protease that cuts after large hydrophobic residues · 胰凝乳蛋白酶
  • coenzyme A — the coenzyme made from pantothenic acid that carries acyl groups on its thiol · 辅酶 A
  • cofactor — any non-protein part an enzyme needs to work, a metal ion or an organic coenzyme · 辅因子
  • competitive inhibition — the inhibitor binds the active site, so Km rises and Vmax is unchanged · 竞争性抑制
  • concentration — the amount of a substance per unit volume of solution · 浓度
  • conformation — one of the three-dimensional shapes a protein can take without breaking a bond · 构象
  • cyclooxygenase — the enzyme that makes prostaglandins from arachidonic acid · 环氧合酶
  • cytochrome P450 — haem enzymes, mainly of the liver, that oxidise drugs and foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450
  • diffusion — the spreading of molecules through a solution by their own random movement · 扩散
  • enzyme — a biological catalyst, almost always a protein, that speeds a reaction by lowering its activation energy without being consumed · 酶
  • enzyme engineering — deliberately changing an enzyme's sequence to alter its specificity or stability · 酶工程
  • enzyme–substrate complex — the enzyme with its substrate bound in the active site · 酶-底物复合物
  • equilibrium — the state at which forward and reverse reactions run at the same rate · 平衡
  • equilibrium constant — the ratio of product to substrate concentrations once equilibrium is reached · 平衡常数
  • FAD — flavin adenine dinucleotide, the riboflavin coenzyme that accepts two electrons and two protons · 黄素腺嘌呤二核苷酸
  • functional group — a group of atoms that gives a molecule a particular chemical reactivity · 官能团
  • Gibbs free energy change — ΔG, the energy change that decides whether a reaction can proceed · 自由能变化
  • glycogen phosphorylase — the enzyme that releases glucose 1-phosphate from glycogen, controlled by phosphorylation · 糖原磷酸化酶
  • histidine — the amino acid whose side chain changes charge near pH 7, so it can move protons · 组氨酸
  • HMG-CoA reductase — the rate-limiting enzyme of cholesterol synthesis · HMG-CoA 还原酶
  • hydrolase — the third EC class: breaks a bond by adding water · 水解酶
  • hydrolysis — breaking a bond by adding water across it · 水解
  • hyperbola — the curve that rises and levels off towards a maximum, the shape of the Michaelis–Menten plot · 双曲线
  • induced fit — the enzyme changing its shape on contact so that it fits the substrate · 诱导契合
  • inhibitor — a molecule that binds an enzyme and lowers its rate · 抑制剂
  • isomerase — the fifth EC class: rearranges the atoms within one molecule · 异构酶
  • kinetics — the study of reaction rates and of what changes them · 动力学
  • Km — the Michaelis constant, the substrate concentration that gives half Vmax · 米氏常数（= Km = Michaelis constant）
  • lactate dehydrogenase — the enzyme that interconverts pyruvate and lactate using NADH · 乳酸脱氢酶
  • malonate — a three-carbon diacid resembling succinate, a competitive inhibitor of succinate dehydrogenase · 丙二酸
  • metal-ion catalysis — using a metal ion bound in the active site to help the reaction along · 金属离子催化
  • Michaelis–Menten equation — the equation giving rate as Vmax times substrate over Km plus substrate · 米氏方程
  • NAD⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, the niacin coenzyme that accepts two electrons as a hydride · 烟酰胺腺嘌呤二核苷酸
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, which absorbs light at 340 nm · 还原型 NAD
  • niacin — vitamin B3, the vitamin from which NAD⁺ is made · 烟酸
  • non-competitive inhibition — the inhibitor binds away from the active site, so Vmax falls and Km is unchanged · 非竞争性抑制
  • oxidation — loss of electrons from a substance · 氧化
  • oxidoreductase — the first EC class: moves electrons from one substrate to another · 氧化还原酶
  • pantothenic acid — vitamin B5, the vitamin from which coenzyme A is made · 泛酸
  • penicillin — an antibiotic that irreversibly inhibits the transpeptidase cross-linking the bacterial cell wall · 青霉素
  • peptidyl transferase — the ribosome's catalytic activity, carried by its RNA, which forms the peptide bond（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 肽基转移酶
  • phosphofructokinase — the main regulated enzyme of glycolysis（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）, inhibited by ATP · 磷酸果糖激酶
  • phosphorylation — adding a phosphate group to a molecule, done by a kinase · 磷酸化
  • product — the molecule an enzyme releases after the reaction · 产物
  • protease — an enzyme that hydrolyses peptide bonds · 蛋白酶
  • proton — a hydrogen ion, H⁺ · 质子
  • pyridoxal phosphate — the vitamin B6 coenzyme, used above all in transferring amino groups · 磷酸吡哆醛
  • rate — how much product is formed in unit time · 反应速率
  • reduction — gain of electrons by a substance · 还原
  • reversible inhibition — inhibition in which the inhibitor comes off again, so activity returns · 可逆抑制
  • riboflavin — vitamin B2, the vitamin from which FAD is made · 核黄素
  • ribozyme — an RNA molecule with catalytic activity · 核酶
  • rifampicin — an antibiotic that inhibits bacterial RNA polymerase, and induces liver P450 · 利福平
  • sarin — a nerve agent that irreversibly inhibits acetylcholinesterase · 沙林
  • saturation — the condition in which substrate occupies essentially every active site · 饱和
  • serine — the amino acid whose hydroxyl attacks the substrate in a serine protease · 丝氨酸
  • site-directed mutagenesis — changing one chosen codon in a gene so that one residue is replaced · 定点突变
  • specificity — how narrowly an enzyme selects which substrate it will act on · 专一性
  • statin — a drug that inhibits HMG-CoA reductase and so lowers cholesterol · 他汀
  • substrate — the molecule an enzyme acts on, which binds in the active site · 底物
  • succinate dehydrogenase — the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） enzyme that oxidises succinate to fumarate · 琥珀酸脱氢酶
  • sulfonamide — an antibacterial drug that competitively blocks a bacterial enzyme of folate synthesis · 磺胺类药
  • transferase — the second EC class: moves a chemical group from one molecule to another · 转移酶
  • transition state — the highest-energy arrangement of atoms on the way from substrate to product · 过渡态
  • transpeptidase — the bacterial enzyme that cross-links the cell wall, the target of penicillin · 转肽酶
  • triose phosphate isomerase — the glycolytic enzyme that interconverts the two three-carbon phosphates · 丙糖磷酸异构酶
  • trypsin — a digestive serine protease that cuts after lysine or arginine · 胰蛋白酶
  • trypsinogen — the inactive precursor from which trypsin is cut · 胰蛋白酶原
  • uncompetitive inhibition — the inhibitor binds only the enzyme–substrate complex, so Km and Vmax both fall · 反竞争性抑制
  • unit — U, the amount of enzyme converting one micromole of substrate per minute · 酶活力单位
  • vitamin — an organic compound needed in small amounts that the body cannot make itself · 维生素
  • vitamin B6 — the vitamin from which pyridoxal phosphate is made · 维生素 B6
  • Vmax — the maximum rate, reached when the enzyme is saturated with substrate · 最大反应速度

Carbohydrates

卡 06

Must say

A monosaccharide is an aldehyde or a ketone with two or more hydroxyls, general formula (CH₂O)ₙ, that cannot be hydrolysed to a smaller sugar.

Numbers

Maltose α-1,4 · lactose β-1,4 · sucrose α-1,β-2 · cellulose β-1,4. Glycogen and starch: main chain α-1,4, branches α-1,6.

Hook

Sucrose is the only non-reducing one of those, because both anomeric carbons are used in the bond. Humans cannot digest cellulose because we have no enzyme for β-1,4.

Say this

A monosaccharide is an aldehyde or a ketone carrying two or more hydroxyl groups, with the general formula (CH₂O)ₙ, and it cannot be hydrolysed to anything smaller. In water it interconverts between the open chain and the ring, which is mutarotation, giving the alpha and beta anomers. The linkages are worth naming precisely: maltose is α-1,4, lactose is β-1,4, sucrose is α-1,β-2, and cellulose is β-1,4. ⚠ Sucrose is the only non-reducing one of those, because both anomeric carbons are used in the bond. Glycogen and starch are both α-1,4 chains with α-1,6 branches, and humans cannot digest cellulose because we have no enzyme for a β-1,4 bond.

约 40 秒 · 蔗糖为什么非还原，理由要说出口

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hodek、Hudeček、Lipovová

① 糖有四个功能：储能、遗传信息、结构支撑、细胞识别 four functions

Carbohydrates have four functions: energy storage as starch and glycogen, information as the ribose of RNA and the deoxyribose of DNA, support as cellulose and chitin, and recognition as the sugar chains on cell surfaces.

开口先给这四个词。

② 葡萄糖存成糖原，是因为渗透压数的是分子个数，不是重量 glycogen · osmotic pressure

Glucose is stored as glycogen because osmotic pressure counts molecules, not mass. Glycogen, the storage form in liver and muscle, joins many thousands of glucose units into one molecule, so a lot is stored without drawing in water.

十万个葡萄糖在渗透压上只算一个粒子。

③ 分支之所以多，是因为糖原磷酸化酶只从非还原端切，分支越多，能同时开工的末端越多 branching

Branching is what makes glycogen fast. Glycogen phosphorylase releases glucose only from the non-reducing ends, and every branch adds another end for the enzyme to work on.

分支越多，同时开工的末端越多。

④ 同样是葡萄糖，只差一个键的构型：α 卷成螺旋好消化，β 是直的成纤维 α storage · β structure

The same glucose, and only the bond differs. α-1,4 chains coil into a helix that amylase digests easily. β-1,4 chains are straight and hydrogen-bond into fibres, as in cellulose, the most abundant organic polymer on Earth. So is chitin, the polymer of N-acetylglucosamine in insect cuticle and fungal walls.

同是葡萄糖，键的构型决定用途。

⑤ β 键使纤维素需要另一种酶：牛和白蚁靠肠道微生物的纤维素酶，人没有 cellulase · gut microbes

The β bond is why cellulose needs a different enzyme: cows and termites digest it with cellulase from the microbes in their gut, and humans, having none, pass it through as dietary fibre.

动物自己都没有纤维素酶。

⑥ 细胞里的糖几乎都带磷酸，因为那个电荷让它出不去；另有糖醛酸、氨基糖、脱氧糖、糖醇 derivatives

Sugars are phosphorylated inside the cell, and the charge keeps them from leaving, so metabolism runs on phosphate esters such as glucose 6-phosphate. Others are glucuronic acid, glucosamine, 2-deoxyribose, sorbitol and ascorbic acid, which is vitamin C.

每个衍生物都是改了一个基团。

⑦ 末端一个糖就定下 ABO 血型；糖链通过天冬酰胺（N-连接）或丝氨酸、苏氨酸（O-连接）接到蛋白上 glycoproteins · ABO blood groups

One terminal sugar is enough to decide a blood group: N-acetylgalactosamine for A, galactose for B, and none for O. Sugar chains are attached to proteins through asparagine, N-linked, or through serine and threonine, O-linked.

接「蛋白质翻译后修饰」主题。

⑧ 糖胺聚糖的负电就是它的功能：把水留在结缔组织里 glycosaminoglycans · heparin

Glycosaminoglycans are long chains of repeating disaccharides whose negative charge holds water in connective tissue: hyaluronic acid in joint fluid, chondroitin sulfate in cartilage, and heparin, which prevents blood clotting.

关节液 · 软骨 · 抗凝。

⑨ 书外：糖化不需要酶，所以它的量就记下了血糖有多高——HbA1c 看两三个月的平均 glycation · HbA1c

Because glycation needs no enzyme, the amount of it simply records how much glucose was there. HbA1c, glycated haemoglobin, shows a diabetic patient's average blood glucose over the past two to three months. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

接血红蛋白主题。主动标「书外」。

⑩ 第二相反应的目的就是让药物溶于水，肝脏挂上葡萄糖醛酸就是这么做的 ⭐ Hodek glucuronidation

Phase II of drug metabolism exists to make a compound water-soluble, and glucuronic acid is how the liver does it. It is attached to drugs, bilirubin and toxins, which are then excreted in bile or urine.

第一相 P450 氧化，第二相挂上葡萄糖醛酸。

⑪ 手性让糖可以在旋光仪上量出来：变旋现象就是从旋光角的慢慢变化发现的 ⭐ Hudeček optical rotation · polarimetry

Chirality is what makes a sugar measurable in a polarimeter: a solution rotates plane-polarised light, and mutarotation was discovered as a slow change in that angle after glucose is dissolved. Iodine stains amylose blue.

先说测的物理量：旋光角。

⑫ 糖苷酶专一到能分辨 α 和 β，这正是淀粉能消化、纤维素不能的原因 ⭐ Lipovová glycosidases · transglycosylation

Glycosidases hydrolyse glycosidic bonds and are specific enough to tell α from β and one sugar from another, which is why we digest starch and not cellulose. α-L-fucosidase removes only terminal fucose. Under suitable conditions they also transfer sugars to build new chains, which is transglycosylation.

回到她的岩藻糖苷酶。

陷阱问法

  • "Starch and cellulose are both glucose; why can we digest only starch?" — Starch has α bonds; we have no enzyme for β-1,4. · α 和 β 的区别。
  • "How many reducing ends does glycogen have?" — One, but very many non-reducing ends. · 还原端只有一个。
  • "Is fructose always a five-membered ring?" — When bound, as in sucrose, yes; free fructose prefers the six-membered ring. · 游离时偏好六元环。
  • "Why does muscle store glycogen rather than fat for sprinting?" — Glycogen can be used quickly and without oxygen. · 快，而且不需要氧。

定义

  • osmotic pressure — pressure that draws water towards more dissolved particles · 渗透压
  • non-reducing end — a chain end whose anomeric carbon is in a bond · 非还原端
  • chitin — β-1,4 polymer of N-acetylglucosamine · 几丁质
  • cellulase — enzyme that hydrolyses β-1,4 bonds of cellulose · 纤维素酶
  • dietary fibre — plant polysaccharides that humans cannot digest · 膳食纤维
  • phosphate ester — a sugar hydroxyl joined to phosphate · 磷酸酯
  • glucuronic acid — glucose with carbon 6 oxidised to a carboxyl · 葡萄糖醛酸
  • glycosaminoglycan — repeating disaccharide chain of uronic acid and amino sugar · 糖胺聚糖
  • glycation — non-enzymatic attachment of a sugar to a protein · 糖化
  • phase II — the conjugation step of drug metabolism · 第二相反应
  • polarimeter — instrument measuring the rotation of polarised light · 旋光仪
  • glycosidase — enzyme that hydrolyses a glycosidic bond · 糖苷酶
  • transglycosylation — transfer of a sugar to an acceptor other than water · 转糖基
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • ABO blood group — the blood group system set by which terminal sugar the red-cell surface chain carries · ABO 血型
  • aldehyde — a carbon double-bonded to oxygen at the end of a chain, –CHO · 醛
  • α-1,4 bond — a glycosidic bond from carbon 1, in the α configuration, to carbon 4 of the next sugar · α-1,4 键
  • α-1,6 bond — the glycosidic bond that starts a branch in glycogen and starch · α-1,6 键
  • α-L-fucosidase — an enzyme that removes only a terminal fucose from a sugar chain · α-L-岩藻糖苷酶
  • amylase — an enzyme that hydrolyses the α-1,4 bonds of starch · 淀粉酶
  • amylose — the unbranched α-1,4 component of starch · 直链淀粉
  • anomer — the two ring forms, α and β, differing only in the direction of the anomeric hydroxyl · 异头物
  • anomeric carbon — the new chiral carbon made from the carbonyl when the sugar closes into a ring · 异头碳
  • ascorbic acid — vitamin C · 抗坏血酸
  • asparagine — the amino acid whose side-chain nitrogen carries N-linked sugar chains · 天冬酰胺
  • β-1,4 bond — a glycosidic bond from carbon 1, in the β configuration, to carbon 4 of the next sugar · β-1,4 键
  • bilirubin — the yellow pigment from haem breakdown, excreted after conjugation · 胆红素
  • branch — a side chain of sugar units joined onto the main chain, here through an α-1,6 bond · 分支
  • carbohydrate — a compound of carbon, hydrogen and oxygen built from sugar units · 碳水化合物
  • cell wall — the rigid layer outside the membrane of a plant, fungal or bacterial cell · 细胞壁
  • cellulose — the unbranched β-1,4 polymer of glucose that forms plant cell walls · 纤维素
  • chirality — the property of a molecule that cannot be superimposed on its mirror image · 手性
  • chondroitin sulfate — the glycosaminoglycan of cartilage · 硫酸软骨素
  • configuration — the fixed spatial arrangement at a chiral centre, which only bond breaking can change · 构型
  • connective tissue — the supporting tissue between organs, such as cartilage, tendon and bone · 结缔组织
  • deoxyribose — ribose without the hydroxyl on carbon 2, the sugar of DNA · 脱氧核糖
  • diabetes — the disease in which blood glucose stays too high · 糖尿病
  • digestion — hydrolysing food polymers into units small enough to be absorbed · 消化
  • disaccharide — two monosaccharides joined by one glycosidic bond · 二糖
  • excretion — removing a waste or foreign substance from the body · 排泄
  • fructose — the six-carbon ketone sugar of fruit, and half of sucrose · 果糖
  • fucose — a deoxy sugar found at the ends of sugar chains · 岩藻糖
  • galactose — a six-carbon aldehyde sugar differing from glucose only at carbon 4 · 半乳糖
  • glucosamine — glucose with an amino group in place of the hydroxyl on carbon 2 · 氨基葡萄糖
  • glucose — the six-carbon aldehyde sugar that is the cell's main fuel · 葡萄糖
  • glucose 6-phosphate — glucose with a phosphate on carbon 6, charged and so trapped inside the cell · 6-磷酸葡萄糖
  • glycogen — the branched α-1,4 polymer of glucose stored in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • glycogen phosphorylase — the enzyme that releases glucose 1-phosphate from a non-reducing end · 糖原磷酸化酶
  • glycoprotein — a protein carrying covalently attached sugar chains · 糖蛋白
  • glycosidic bond — the bond joining the anomeric carbon of a sugar to another group · 糖苷键
  • HbA1c — glycated haemoglobin, which reports the average blood glucose of the past two to three months · 糖化血红蛋白
  • heparin — a highly sulfated glycosaminoglycan that prevents blood clotting · 肝素
  • hyaluronic acid — the glycosaminoglycan of joint fluid · 透明质酸
  • hydrolysis — breaking a bond by adding water across it · 水解
  • hydroxyl group — an –OH group · 羟基
  • iodine — the element whose solution stains amylose blue · 碘
  • ketone — a carbon double-bonded to oxygen within the chain rather than at its end · 酮
  • lactose — milk sugar: galactose joined β-1,4 to glucose · 乳糖
  • maltose — two glucose units joined α-1,4 · 麦芽糖
  • microbe — a single-celled organism too small to see, such as a bacterium · 微生物
  • monosaccharide — a single sugar unit, which cannot be hydrolysed to anything smaller · 单糖
  • mutarotation — the slow change in optical rotation as one pure anomer equilibrates with the other · 变旋
  • N-acetylgalactosamine — galactosamine carrying an acetyl group; the terminal sugar of blood group A · N-乙酰半乳糖胺
  • N-acetylglucosamine — glucosamine carrying an acetyl group; the unit of chitin · N-乙酰葡糖胺
  • N-linked — a sugar chain attached to the nitrogen of an asparagine side chain · N-连接
  • O-linked — a sugar chain attached to the oxygen of a serine or threonine · O-连接
  • optical rotation — the angle through which a solution turns the plane of polarised light · 旋光角
  • plane-polarised light — light whose waves vibrate in one plane only · 平面偏振光
  • polymer — a long molecule built from many repeating units · 聚合物
  • polysaccharide — many monosaccharides joined into one long chain · 多糖
  • reducing sugar — a sugar with a free anomeric carbon, which can therefore reduce another compound · 还原糖
  • ribose — the five-carbon sugar of RNA · 核糖
  • serine — the amino acid whose hydroxyl carries O-linked sugar chains · 丝氨酸
  • sorbitol — the sugar alcohol obtained by reducing glucose · 山梨醇
  • specificity — how narrowly an enzyme selects which substrate or bond it will act on · 专一性
  • starch — the plant storage polymer of glucose: α-1,4 chains with α-1,6 branches · 淀粉
  • sucrose — table sugar: glucose joined α-1,β-2 to fructose, using both anomeric carbons · 蔗糖
  • sugar — a carbohydrate unit: an aldehyde or ketone carrying several hydroxyl groups · 糖
  • threonine — the amino acid whose hydroxyl carries O-linked sugar chains · 苏氨酸
  • water-soluble — able to dissolve in water, and so able to be excreted in urine or bile · 水溶性

Nucleic acid structure

卡 03

  1. ⚠ 这道题问的是「区别」，所以第一句先说有几条轴 四条轴：糖、碱基、链数、功能。前三条是化学，第四条不一样——第四条是「各自干什么」， 而前三条的化学就是在为它付账。⚠ 这一句是这道题的全部结构，先给轴，再往轴上填。 （卷子上这道题出现过两次，是重复最多的几道之一。）
  2. 轴一 · 糖 —— 差别只有一个 2′-OH DNA 是 2-脱氧-D-核糖，RNA 是 D-核糖，差别就是 2′ 碳上那个羟基。 ⚠ 「2′」的撇是为了跟碱基上的编号分开。这一个羟基决定了后面所有事： 它让 RNA 在碱里容易水解，所以 RNA 不稳定、DNA 稳定——存长期信息的那个必须稳。
  3. 轴二 · 碱基 —— 三个共用，一个各有 A、G、C 两边都有；尿嘧啶只在 RNA，胸腺嘧啶主要在 DNA。 ⚠ 为什么 DNA 要多花一个甲基做胸腺嘧啶：胞嘧啶会自发脱氨变成尿嘧啶， DNA 里如果本来就有尿嘧啶，修复系统就分不清哪个是损伤。所以 DNA 里出现 U 一定是错误。
  4. 轴三 · 链数 —— 双链对单链，但别说死 DNA 通常是右手双螺旋，mRNA 和 rRNA 是单链。 ⚠ 别说「RNA 是单链」就完：rRNA 和 tRNA 会自己折回来配对，形成局部的螺旋， tRNA 的三叶草就是这么来的。准确的说法是「RNA 是单链，但可以自己配对」。
  5. 轴四 · 功能 —— 这条跟前三条不是一类 DNA 存全部遗传信息；RNA 是其中一条指令的临时拷贝。 ⚠ 而「拷贝」这个角色只属于 mRNA——tRNA 和 rRNA 不是拷贝，它们本身就是干活的分子。 落点：脱氧核糖是从核糖还原来的，所以 RNA 的化学出现在 DNA 之前（接 RNA world）。
Must say

B-DNA is a right-handed double helix with antiparallel strands. A=T is two hydrogen bonds, G≡C is three, so more GC means a more stable helix. Denaturation gives the hyperchromic effect: absorbance at 260 nm rises as the strands separate.

Numbers

10.5 base pairs per turn, 0.34 nm per base. Tm (also called melting temperature) is the temperature at which half is denatured, and it rises with GC content and ionic strength. Pure DNA A260/A280 = 1.8, pure RNA = 2.0.

Hook

Most transcription factors read the sequence through the major groove. The peptidyl transferase activity is in the rRNA — the ribosome is a ribozyme.

Say this

There are four differences between DNA and RNA. Three are chemical — which sugar, which bases, and how many chains — and the fourth is what each one is for, which is what the chemistry pays for. The sugar in DNA is 2-deoxy-D-ribose and in RNA D-ribose, so the difference is a single 2′ hydroxyl, and that hydroxyl is why RNA is hydrolysed in alkali and DNA is stable. Both use A, G and C, but uracil is in RNA and thymine in DNA; cytosine deaminates to uracil, so a uracil found in DNA is always damage. DNA is usually double-stranded and RNA single-stranded, although transfer RNA and ribosomal RNA fold back and pair with themselves. And the fourth difference: DNA stores all the information, while RNA is a working copy of one instruction — though only messenger RNA is a copy, because transfer RNA and ribosomal RNA are working molecules in their own right. B-DNA is a right-handed double helix with two antiparallel strands, about 10.5 base pairs per turn and 0.34 nanometres per base. A pairs with T through two hydrogen bonds and G with C through three, so a higher GC content gives a more stable helix and a higher melting temperature. When it denatures the strands separate and the absorbance at 260 nanometres rises — that is the hyperchromic effect — and Tm is the temperature at which half of it has melted. ⚠ For purity, pure DNA has an A260 over A280 of 1.8 and pure RNA 2.0. And most transcription factors read the sequence through the major groove, because that is where the bases are accessible.

约 75 秒 · 增色效应和 Tm 的定义常被连着问

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hudeček、Lipovová、Hodek

① 每个核苷酸带一个负电，所以电泳只按长度分开 DNA；主链上唯一的键是磷酸二酯键 phosphodiester bond

Every nucleotide carries one negative charge, so DNA is negative along its whole length and agarose gel electrophoresis separates it by size alone. That charge sits on the phosphodiester bond, the only bond of the backbone, joining the 3′ carbon of one sugar to the 5′ carbon of the next.

一个核苷酸一个负电 → 电泳按长度分。

② DNA 聚合酶起不了头，只能往游离的 3′-OH 上接，所以必须先有引物 free 3′-OH · primer

DNA polymerase can never start a chain, because it can only add a nucleotide to a free 3′-OH. That is also why the chain grows 5′ to 3′, and why primase first makes a short RNA primer.

RNA 聚合酶不需要引物。接复制主题。

③ 书外：少一个 3′-OH 就停链——同一条规则撑起了 Sanger 测序和抗 HIV 药 AZT chain termination

One missing hydroxyl underlies two unrelated things: a dideoxynucleotide has no 3′-OH, so the chain stops — the basis of both Sanger sequencing and the HIV drug AZT. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

同一条规则，一个用于测序，一个用于抗病毒。

④ 碱基和糖之间的键会自发断掉，断的多半是嘌呤，所以这件事叫脱嘌呤 N-glycosidic bond · depurination

The bond between base and sugar breaks by itself, most often at a purine, which is why depurination is common spontaneous damage. It is the N-glycosidic bond, β configuration, to the 1′ carbon of the sugar, and the empty site is repaired by base excision repair.

嘧啶 CUT 小单环，嘌呤 AG 大双环。不是磷酸二酯键，别混。接 DNA 修复。

⑤ DNA 用胸腺嘧啶，是为了让出现的尿嘧啶一定算损伤；RNA 的 2′-OH 让它在碱中易水解 2′-OH · thymine vs uracil

DNA uses thymine so that any uracil found in it must be damage, from cytosine deamination, and can be removed. The other difference is the 2′-OH of ribose, which makes RNA easy to hydrolyse in alkali.

DNA 用来长期保存，RNA 用来临时工作。

⑥ 只有 mRNA 是临时拷贝，tRNA、rRNA、snRNA 都是常驻的干活部件 mRNA · tRNA · rRNA · snRNA

Only mRNA is a temporary copy, carrying the coding sequence to the ribosome; the others are working parts. tRNA carries an amino acid and reads the codon with its anticodon, rRNA forms the ribosome and catalyses the peptide bond (also called amide bond), and snRNA forms the spliceosome, which removes introns (also called intervening sequence).

只有 mRNA 是临时拷贝，其余都是结构和功能成分。

⑦ 长度就把非编码 RNA 分开了：约 21 个核苷酸的沉默 mRNA，超过 200 的调控基因 miRNA · siRNA · lncRNA · ribozyme

Length sorts the non-coding RNAs: at about 21 nucleotides, miRNA and siRNA silence a complementary mRNA; above 200, lncRNAs regulate genes, as Xist inactivates one X chromosome. A ribozyme is RNA with catalytic activity, which supports the RNA world hypothesis.

小 RNA 沉默 · 长 RNA 调控 · 核酶催化。

⑧ Z-DNA 是唯一的左手螺旋，出现在富 GC 序列上；脱水给 A 型，细菌 DNA 超螺旋，真核缠组蛋白 A-DNA · Z-DNA · supercoiling · histones

Z-DNA, formed by some GC-rich sequences, is the only left-handed form; dehydrated DNA takes the A form. Circular bacterial DNA is supercoiled by DNA gyrase (also called DNA gyrase, topoisomerase II (bacteria)), and eukaryotic DNA is wound around positively charged histones in nucleosomes.

只有 Z 是左手。接染色质主题。

⑨ 书外：Griffith 1928——死细胞里有东西把无害的 R 型变成致死的 S 型，「转化」这个词由此而来 Griffith · transformation

Frederick Griffith in 1928 opened the question of what the genetic material is, because something from dead cells turned a harmless strain into a lethal one. Live S strain pneumococcus, smooth with a capsule, killed mice; live R strain, rough without a capsule, and heat-killed S were each harmless. But live R plus heat-killed S killed the mouse, and live S was recovered. That is where the word transformation comes from; it is general knowledge, not from my textbook.

S 有荚膜致死，R 无荚膜不致死；死 S ＋ 活 R → 小鼠死，还分离出活 S。

⑩ 1944 Avery：三种酶里只有 DNase 能拦住转化，所以转化因子是 DNA Avery · MacLeod · McCarty

Of three enzymes, only DNase stopped the transformation: protease and RNase did not. That is how Oswald Avery, Colin MacLeod and Maclyn McCarty showed in 1944 that the transforming principle is DNA.

书 p009 年表只有这一行：1944，Avery，遗传信息由 DNA 传递。

⑪ 书外：蛋白含硫不含磷、DNA 含磷不含硫，所以两种同位素能分开标记——1952 只有 ³²P 进了细菌 Hershey–Chase

Protein has sulfur but no phosphorus, DNA the reverse, so they label separately. Alfred Hershey and Martha Chase in 1952 labelled a bacteriophage with ³⁵S or ³²P, and only the ³²P entered the bacteria. That is general knowledge, not from my textbook.

蛋白含硫不含磷，DNA 含磷不含硫，所以两种同位素能分开标记。

⑫ A=T、G=C 这个比例本身就是碱基配对的线索；加上 Franklin 的衍射图，1953 年出双螺旋 discovery

Erwin Chargaff's finding that A equals T and G equals C was the clue to base pairing. With Rosalind Franklin's X-ray diffraction pictures of a helix, James Watson and Francis Crick proposed the double helix in 1953.

A=T、G=C 的比例 → 碱基配对的线索。

⑬ 解链时 260 nm 吸光度升高，是因为堆积着的碱基本来就吸得少 ⭐ Hudeček base stacking · CD

Melting raises the 260 nm absorbance because stacked bases absorb less than free ones, and the absorbance comes from the bases. Circular dichroism distinguishes right-handed B-DNA from left-handed Z-DNA.

增色效应的物理原因。

⑭ S1 核酸酶之所以好用，正是因为它只切单链，所以能找出没有配对的区域 ⭐ Lipovová nucleases · S1 nuclease

S1 nuclease is useful precisely because it cuts only single-stranded DNA and RNA, so it removes single-stranded ends and maps unpaired regions. Like all nucleases it hydrolyses phosphodiester bonds; it comes from Aspergillus oryzae.

回到她的 S1 核酸酶。

⑮ P450 本来是解毒的，苯并芘却是被它活化才致癌：环氧化物共价结合到鸟嘌呤上 ⭐ Hodek DNA adducts

Cytochrome P450 normally detoxifies, but benzo[a]pyrene, a carcinogen in tobacco smoke, is activated by it into an epoxide. That epoxide binds covalently to guanine, forming a DNA adduct that can cause mutations.

解毒反而活化 → Hodek 的研究方向。

⑯ 每个核苷酸都是三件东西：五碳糖 ＋ 含氮碱基 ＋ 磷酸 nucleotide = sugar + base + phosphate

Every nucleotide is made of three parts: a five-carbon sugar, a nitrogen-containing base and a phosphate group. Sugar plus base alone is a nucleoside; adding the phosphate makes it a nucleotide.
