糖 ＋ 碱基 ＝ 核苷；再加磷酸 ＝ 核苷酸。

⑰ DNA 缠在组蛋白上形成核小体，再盘成染色质，最后压成染色体 chromatin · chromosome

In eukaryotes the DNA is wound around histones, positively charged proteins that bind the negative backbone, giving nucleosomes; nucleosomes coil into chromatin, and chromatin condenses into a chromosome.

组蛋白带正电，DNA 带负电。

⑱ 碱基分两族，记法是「CUT the PY」：C、U、T 是嘧啶，剩下 A、G 是嘌呤 purine vs pyrimidine

The bases fall into two families. Cytosine, uracil and thymine are pyrimidines — remember “CUT the PY” — and the two left over, adenine and guanine, are purines: “PURe As Gold”. ⚠ A purine is the bigger molecule, with two fused rings, even though its name is shorter.

嘧啶名字长，环反而小（单环）；嘌呤双环。每个碱基对总是一嘌呤配一嘧啶，所以双螺旋才等宽。

⑲ 两条链反平行，不是规定，是配对逼出来的；复制那条「一连续一断开」也是它逼出来的 antiparallel

The two strands are antiparallel: one runs 5′ to 3′ and the other 3′ to 5′. They have to, because the bases can only pair when the two backbones point in opposite directions. ⚠ It is also why replication is semi-discontinuous: polymerase only works 5′ to 3′, so one new strand is made continuously and the other in pieces.

反平行 → 只能一条连续、一条断成片段。被问「为什么要有冈崎片段」，答案就在这。

⑳ 互补不只是一个事实，是一条原理：一条链写定了另一条链 complementary base pairing

Each base has one partner and only one: adenine with thymine, guanine with cytosine. That is what complementary means, and it is why one strand fully specifies the other. ⚠ Everything that reads DNA lives off this one rule — replication copies a strand from its partner, transcription copies RNA from a template, a PCR primer finds its site, and a probe finds its target on a blot. It is also why a denatured double helix can find its own partner again and re-form: hybridisation.

被问「为什么 DNA 适合当遗传物质」，答这一条：互补 → 一条链就够了，另一条可以照着重建。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the base joined to the sugar by a phosphodiester bond?" — No, by an N-glycosidic bond. · 是 N-糖苷键。
  • "Why can't DNA polymerase start a chain?" — It needs a free 3′-OH to add to. · 需要 3′-OH。
  • "Is RNA always single-stranded?" — No, it folds back and base-pairs with itself, as in tRNA. · 会自身配对。
  • "Are A-DNA and Z-DNA both left-handed?" — No, only Z-DNA. · 只有 Z。
  • "Is GC more stable only because of three hydrogen bonds?" — No, base stacking also contributes. · 还有碱基堆积。

定义

  • phosphodiester bond — one phosphate esterified to two sugar hydroxyls · 磷酸二酯键
  • agarose gel electrophoresis — separating DNA by size in an electric field · 琼脂糖凝胶电泳
  • primase — the RNA polymerase that makes primers · 引物酶
  • dideoxynucleotide — a nucleotide lacking the 3′-OH · 双脱氧核苷酸
  • AZT — azidothymidine, an HIV drug that terminates reverse transcription · 齐多夫定
  • depurination — loss of a purine base by breaking the N-glycosidic bond · 脱嘌呤
  • base excision repair — repair that removes a damaged or missing base and refills it · 碱基切除修复
  • deamination — loss of an amino group · 脱氨
  • spliceosome — the RNA–protein complex that removes introns · 剪接体
  • Xist — the lncRNA that inactivates one X chromosome in female cells · Xist
  • RNA world — the hypothesis that early life used RNA for both information and catalysis · RNA 世界
  • DNA gyrase — bacterial topoisomerase that introduces supercoils · DNA 旋转酶（= DNA gyrase = topoisomerase II (bacteria)）
  • pneumococcus — Streptococcus pneumoniae, the bacterium that causes pneumonia · 肺炎链球菌
  • capsule — a polysaccharide coat around a bacterium that protects it from the immune system · 荚膜
  • transformation — uptake of DNA from outside that changes a cell's heritable traits · 转化
  • transforming principle — the substance from dead S cells that caused transformation · 转化因子
  • bacteriophage — a virus that infects bacteria · 噬菌体
  • histones — basic proteins around which DNA is wound · 组蛋白
  • base stacking — flat bases lying on top of each other in the helix · 碱基堆积
  • S1 nuclease — a fungal nuclease specific for single-stranded nucleic acid · S1 核酸酶
  • DNA adduct — a foreign molecule covalently bound to DNA · DNA 加合物
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • hybridisation — the pairing of two separate single strands into a double helix because their sequences are complementary · 杂交
  • chromatin — the complex of DNA and histone proteins that makes up a eukaryotic chromosome · 染色质
  • nucleoside — a base joined to a sugar, with no phosphate · 核苷
  • 2′-hydroxyl — the –OH on carbon 2 of ribose, which DNA does not have · 2′-羟基
  • 3′-OH — the free hydroxyl on carbon 3 of the last sugar, where the next nucleotide is added · 3′-羟基
  • ³²P — a radioactive isotope of phosphorus, used to label nucleic acid · 磷-32
  • ³⁵S — a radioactive isotope of sulfur, used to label protein · 硫-35
  • A-DNA — the wider, flatter right-handed form taken when DNA is dehydrated · A 型 DNA
  • A260/A280 ratio — the ratio of two absorbances used to judge purity: 1.8 for DNA, 2.0 for RNA · 纯度比值
  • absorbance — how much light a sample takes out of a beam at one wavelength · 吸光度
  • adenine — a purine base, pairing with thymine in DNA and uracil in RNA · 腺嘌呤
  • Alfred Hershey and Martha Chase — showed in 1952, with labelled phage, that DNA and not protein enters the cell · 赫尔希和蔡斯
  • alkali — a base dissolved in water, giving a pH above 7 · 碱
  • anticodon — the three bases of a tRNA that pair with a codon on mRNA · 反密码子
  • antiparallel — the two strands running in opposite directions, one 5′ to 3′ against the other 3′ to 5′ · 反平行
  • Aspergillus oryzae — a mould, the organism from which S1 nuclease is obtained · 米曲霉
  • B-DNA — the ordinary hydrated right-handed double helix · B 型 DNA
  • backbone — the alternating sugar and phosphate running along each strand, carrying no bases · 骨架
  • base — the flat nitrogen-containing ring of a nucleotide: adenine, guanine, cytosine, thymine or uracil · 碱基
  • base pair — two bases held across the helix by hydrogen bonds, A with T and G with C · 碱基对
  • benzo[a]pyrene — a carcinogen of tobacco smoke, activated by P450 into a DNA-binding epoxide · 苯并芘
  • carcinogen — a substance that causes cancer · 致癌物
  • charge — an excess or shortage of electrons, making a group positive or negative · 电荷
  • chromosome — one long DNA molecule with its bound proteins, carrying many genes · 染色体
  • circular dichroism — the difference in absorption of left and right circularly polarised light · 圆二色谱
  • coding sequence — the part of an mRNA that is read as codons and turned into protein · 编码序列
  • codon — three consecutive bases of mRNA, read as one amino acid or as a stop · 密码子
  • complementary — having a sequence that base-pairs with another along its length · 互补
  • covalent bond — a bond in which two atoms share a pair of electrons · 共价键
  • cytosine — a pyrimidine base, pairing with guanine · 胞嘧啶
  • D-ribose — the five-carbon sugar of RNA · D-核糖
  • denaturation — separation of the two strands, with no covalent bond broken · 变性
  • deoxyribose — ribose without the hydroxyl on carbon 2, the sugar of DNA · 脱氧核糖
  • detoxification — chemically changing a foreign compound so that the body can excrete it · 解毒
  • DNA — deoxyribonucleic acid, the double-stranded molecule that stores a cell's genetic information · 脱氧核糖核酸
  • DNA polymerase — the enzyme that adds nucleotides one at a time onto a free 3′-OH · DNA 聚合酶
  • DNase — an enzyme that hydrolyses DNA · DNA 酶
  • double-stranded — made of two strands paired with each other along their length · 双链
  • epoxide — a reactive three-membered ring of two carbons and one oxygen · 环氧化物
  • Erwin Chargaff — found that in any DNA the amount of A equals T and G equals C · 查伽夫
  • Frederick Griffith — showed in 1928 that something from dead bacteria transforms live ones · 格里菲斯
  • GC content — the proportion of the base pairs in a sequence that are G–C · GC 含量
  • gene — a stretch of DNA whose sequence specifies one RNA or protein product · 基因
  • gene silencing — switching off a gene's product, here by destroying or blocking its mRNA · 基因沉默
  • groove — the gap running along the outside of the helix between the two backbones · 沟
  • guanine — a purine base, pairing with cytosine · 鸟嘌呤
  • helix — a spiral; in DNA two strands wound about a common axis · 螺旋
  • hydrolysis — breaking a bond by adding water across it · 水解
  • hyperchromic effect — the rise in absorbance at 260 nm as the strands separate · 增色效应
  • intron — a stretch inside a gene that is removed from the RNA before it is used · 内含子（= intron = intervening sequence）
  • ionic strength — a measure of the total concentration of ions in a solution · 离子强度
  • isotope — a form of an element with the usual protons but a different number of neutrons · 同位素
  • James Watson and Francis Crick — proposed the double-helix model of DNA in 1953 · 沃森和克里克
  • labelling — marking a molecule, for example with a radioactive atom, so that it can be followed · 标记
  • lncRNA — long non-coding RNA, above 200 nucleotides, which regulates genes · 长链非编码 RNA
  • major groove — the wider of the two grooves, where a protein can read the bases · 大沟
  • melting temperature — Tm, the temperature at which half the DNA has denatured · 解链温度（= Tm = melting temperature）
  • miRNA — microRNA, a short RNA that silences a complementary mRNA · 微小 RNA
  • mRNA — messenger RNA, the working copy of one gene, carried to the ribosome · 信使 RNA
  • mutation — a change in the base sequence of DNA · 突变
  • N-glycosidic bond — the bond joining a base to carbon 1 of its sugar · N-糖苷键
  • nuclease — an enzyme that hydrolyses the phosphodiester bonds of nucleic acids · 核酸酶
  • nucleic acid — a chain of nucleotides joined by phosphodiester bonds, DNA or RNA · 核酸
  • nucleosome — DNA wound around a core of histone proteins · 核小体
  • nucleotide — one base, one five-carbon sugar and one or more phosphate groups · 核苷酸
  • Oswald Avery, Colin MacLeod and Maclyn McCarty — showed in 1944 that the transforming principle is DNA · 埃弗里三人
  • peptidyl transferase — the ribosome's catalytic activity, carried by its RNA, which forms the peptide bond（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 肽基转移酶
  • phosphate group — a phosphorus atom with four oxygens, carrying negative charge · 磷酸基
  • primer — a short piece of nucleic acid giving a polymerase a free 3′-OH to extend · 引物
  • protease — an enzyme that hydrolyses peptide bonds · 蛋白酶
  • purine — the double-ring bases, adenine and guanine · 嘌呤
  • purity — how free a preparation is of substances other than the one wanted · 纯度
  • pyrimidine — the single-ring bases, cytosine, thymine and uracil · 嘧啶
  • R strain — the rough pneumococcus, without a capsule, which does not kill mice · R 型菌
  • ribosome — the RNA and protein particle on which proteins are made · 核糖体
  • ribozyme — an RNA molecule with catalytic activity · 核酶
  • RNA — ribonucleic acid, the nucleic acid built with ribose and uracil, usually one strand · 核糖核酸
  • RNase — an enzyme that hydrolyses RNA · RNA 酶
  • Rosalind Franklin — took the X-ray diffraction pictures that showed DNA is a helix · 富兰克林
  • rRNA — ribosomal RNA, the RNA that builds the ribosome and forms the peptide bond · 核糖体 RNA
  • S strain — the smooth pneumococcus, with a capsule, which kills mice · S 型菌
  • Sanger sequencing — reading a sequence from chains stopped at each position by dideoxynucleotides · 桑格测序
  • single-stranded — consisting of one strand, not paired with a partner · 单链
  • siRNA — small interfering RNA, about 21 nucleotides, which silences a matching mRNA · 小干扰 RNA
  • snRNA — small nuclear RNA, the RNA that forms the spliceosome · 核内小 RNA
  • strain — a population within one bacterial species with a particular inherited character · 菌株

  • sugar — here the five-carbon ring of each nucleotide, ribose or deoxyribose · 糖
  • supercoiling — extra twisting of a closed circular DNA upon itself · 超螺旋
  • thymine — the pyrimidine used in DNA in place of uracil, pairing with adenine · 胸腺嘧啶
  • transcription factor — a protein that binds DNA and controls the transcription of a gene · 转录因子
  • tRNA — transfer RNA, which carries an amino acid and reads the codon with its anticodon · 转运 RNA
  • uracil — the pyrimidine used in RNA in place of thymine, pairing with adenine · 尿嘧啶
  • X-ray diffraction — the pattern X-rays make after passing through a crystal or fibre, from which structure is worked out · X 射线衍射
  • Z-DNA — the left-handed form taken by some GC-rich sequences · Z 型 DNA

Metabolism — the ledger

Where it all joins up

⚠ 脑子糊的时候先看这一节 —— 一条缝能同时固定两块。 每条都压成一个抓手：一个缩写、一句对仗，或者一串多米诺

  1. ABC — 生物素羧化酶要的三样 ATP · Biotin · CO₂。三个酶正好各属一条路： 丙酮酸羧化酶（糖异生）· 乙酰-CoA 羧化酶（脂肪合成）· 丙酰-CoA 羧化酶（奇数碳脂肪酸）。 ⚠ 说「biotin-dependent carboxylases」才准：rubisco 和 PEP 羧化酶都不用生物素、也不花 ATP， γ-谷氨酰羧化酶用的是维生素 K。
  2. 磷酸化 ＝ 饥饿模式 — 拆的开，造的关 这一条能一口气解释五个酶：糖原磷酸化酶开 · 糖原合酶关 · HSL 开（拆脂肪）· ACC 关（别造脂肪）· PDH 关（省着葡萄糖）。 ⚠ 方向不用一个个背 —— 问「饿的时候身体想干嘛」就行。
  3. 柠檬酸 ＝ 货 ＋ 令 它是 TCA 的中间物，是把 acetyl-CoA 运出线粒体的车， 同时还是信号：关 PFK-1、开 ACC。 ⚠ 一个分子既是货又是命令：「碳多了，别烧糖，去存脂肪」。
  4. 丙二酰-CoA ＝ 造出来的东西，反手当门闩 它是脂肪合成的第一个产物，而它关掉 CPT-1，也就是氧化的门。 ⚠ 产物本身就是对向通路的开关 —— 跟上一条合起来，脂代谢互控就全了。
  5. No NAD⁺, no glycolysis（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway） — 一条线串四块 GAPDH 那一步必须有 NAD⁺ → 缺氧时靠乳酸或乙醇再生 → 有氧时靠呼吸链再生 → 所以柠檬酸循环自己不用氧，却离了氧就停。 ⚠ 糖酵解 · 发酵 · 呼吸链 · TCA 四块，是这一条线缝起来的。
  6. 红细胞的多米诺 — 一个事实倒出四个答案 没有线粒体 → 只能糖酵解 → 所以靠 PPP 拿 NADPH 抗氧化 → 所以 G6PD 缺乏会溶血 → 而 2,3-BPG 正好是糖酵解的旁路产物。 ⚠ 推第一句，后面四句自己出来。
  7. 维生素 ＝ 全书的索引（一个动词一个 B） B₁ 去羧（TPP：PDH · α-KG · 转酮酶）· B₇ 加羧（所有生物素羧化酶）· B₆ 转氨（PLP：所有转氨酶）· B₁₂ 重排（甲基丙二酰-CoA 变位酶）· B₉ 搬一碳（THF）· B₅ 搬酰基（CoA）· B₂ / B₃ 搬氢。 ⚠ B₁ 把碳拿下来，B₇ 把碳装上去 —— 这一对最好记。 背维生素的时候你顺带复习了六条路。
  8. 花一个 CO₂ 买「不可逆」— 同一招用了两次 ACC 加上 CO₂ → FAS 缩合时又放掉；丙酮酸羧化酶加上 → PEPCK 又放掉。 ⚠ 两处都不是为了把碳留下，是为了让下一步不可逆。
  9. 不可逆 → 控制点 → 有调节物 → 对向通路换酶 ⚠ 这不是四个知识点，是一条推理链。 碰到任何一条陌生的路，找那个不可逆的酶，其余三句能推出来。 调节物按四类猜：自己的产物 · 能量信号 · 激素磷酸化 · 别条路的中间物。
  10. β-氧化（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）前三步 ＝ 柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）最后三步 脱氢 → 加水 → 脱氢，一模一样的化学（succinate → fumarate → malate → OAA）。 ⚠ 同一套化学在两条路上复用 —— 白拿的加分。
  11. PDH 和 α-KG 脱氢酶是同一台机器 三个酶、五个辅因子、同样的氧化脱羧（支链氨基酸那个是第三台）。 ⚠ 背一次会三个。
  12. 光合和呼吸互为逆过程 — 同一个数，两个方向 光合把水拆开放氧，ΔG°′ ＝ +2867 kJ/mol（吸能，所以要光）； 葡萄糖完全氧化把氧变回水，−2870。 ⚠ 两个数几乎相等，正负号相反 —— 说出这一点比背两个数强。
  13. 从原理走到药 — 过渡态那一条 酶对过渡态的亲和力最高 → 所以长得像过渡态的分子是最强的抑制剂 → 他汀就是 HMG-CoA 还原酶的竞争性抑制剂。 ⚠ 一句原理接一个药名，是很漂亮的收尾。

Say this

A few rules tie most of metabolism together. The biotin-dependent carboxylases all need the same three things — ATP, biotin and CO₂ — and there are three of them: pyruvate carboxylase, acetyl-CoA carboxylase and propionyl-CoA carboxylase. Phosphorylation generally means the fasting state: it switches breakdown on and synthesis off, which is why it activates glycogen phosphorylase but inactivates glycogen synthase, acetyl-CoA carboxylase and pyruvate dehydrogenase. Citrate is both cargo and signal: it carries acetyl units out of the mitochondrion, and at the same time it inhibits phosphofructokinase and activates acetyl-CoA carboxylase. Malonyl-CoA does the mirror image: it is the first product of synthesis and it blocks carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1), so synthesis and oxidation can never run together. And underneath all of it: control always sits at the irreversible step, because that is the only place where the enzyme, rather than the concentrations, decides the flux.

约 55 秒 · 这一段可以当「代谢调控」那道题的开场，也可以当任何代谢题的收尾

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Ryšlavá

① 四个分子就把各条途径接在一起，因为每一个都能进不止一条途径 four junction molecules

The pathways are connected at four molecules — glucose-6-phosphate, pyruvate, acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate — because each of these can enter more than one pathway.

四个连接分子。

② 三类燃料最后都变成乙酰 CoA，所以一套循环加一条呼吸链就够烧掉全部 convergence on acetyl-CoA

Sugar, fat and most amino acids all end up as acetyl-CoA, and that is why a single citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) and respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) can oxidise all three.

三类燃料共用一套氧化酶。

③ 丙酮酸脱氢酶不可逆，这就是脂肪酸的碳不能变成糖的原因 pyruvate dehydrogenase is irreversible

Pyruvate dehydrogenase cannot run backwards. That is the reason acetyl-CoA never becomes pyruvate again, and why sugar can become fat, but fatty-acid carbons cannot become glucose.

原因在 PDH 不可逆。

④ 胰岛素做的每一件事都指向同一个方向——储存：分解葡萄糖、合成糖原，多余的转成脂肪 fed state · insulin

Everything insulin does after a meal points the same way, towards storage: cells take up and break down glucose, build glycogen, and turn the surplus into fatty acids.

胰岛素促进储存。

⑤ 胰高血糖素把这几件事全部反过来，而且顺序是重点：糖原 → 糖异生 → 脂肪 → 酮体 fasting · glucagon

Glucagon reverses all of that, and the order is the point: the liver empties glycogen first, then makes glucose by gluconeogenesis. Fat tissue releases fatty acids, and in a long fast the liver makes ketone bodies for the brain.

顺序：糖原 → 糖异生 → 脂肪酸 → 酮体。

⑥ Cori 循环要紧的地方是乳酸不是废物：肌肉缺氧产的乳酸，肝会把它变回葡萄糖 Cori cycle

The point of the Cori cycle (also called lactic acid cycle) is that the lactate is not waste: muscle short of oxygen makes it in hard exercise, and the liver turns that carbon back into glucose.

名字「Cori cycle」书上没有。

⑦ 器官分工来自酶的差别：只有肝有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶，所以只有肝能向血里放葡萄糖 organ specialisation

Organs differ because their enzymes differ: only the liver has glucose-6-phosphatase, so only the liver releases glucose into the blood. Muscle keeps its own, the brain uses mainly glucose, and adipose tissue stores and releases fat.

器官的差别来自酶的差别。

⑧ 合成和分解同时跑只会白烧 ATP，所以同一个信号开一个关另一个，而且常在不同区室 reciprocal regulation

If synthesis and breakdown of one molecule ran at the same time, they would only consume ATP. So one signal switches one on and the other off, and often they sit in different compartments: fatty-acid oxidation in the mitochondria, fatty-acid synthesis in the cytosol.

反向调控 ＋ 不同区室。

⑨ 回补反应补上被拿走的草酰乙酸；植物里干这件事的，正是 C4 光合作用那两个酶 ⭐ Ryšlavá anaplerosis

Anaplerotic reactions replace the oxaloacetate withdrawn for gluconeogenesis, and in plants the enzymes that do it are ones already known from C4 photosynthesis: PEP carboxylase and malic enzyme, working here in non-photosynthetic cells.

Ryšlavá 研究的就是这几个酶。

⑩ 书外：肌肉做不了尿素，所以把氨基氮做成丙氨酸送到肝，肝再把葡萄糖送回来 glucose–alanine cycle

The glucose–alanine cycle (also called Cahill cycle) exists because muscle cannot make urea: it sends amino groups to the liver as alanine, and the liver sends glucose back. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "So no part of a fat can become glucose?" — The glycerol can; only the fatty-acid carbons cannot. · 甘油能，脂肪酸不能。
  • "Why can muscle glycogen not raise blood glucose?" — Muscle lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, so it cannot release free glucose. · 肌肉没有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。
  • "Why do you get fat from eating too much bread?" — Extra glucose becomes acetyl-CoA, and acetyl-CoA becomes fatty acids. · 多余的糖变成脂肪酸。

定义

  • insulin — hormone released after a meal that promotes uptake and storage · 胰岛素
  • glucagon — hormone released in fasting that makes the liver release glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glycogen — branched glucose polymer stored in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • compartment — a separate space in the cell, such as the cytosol or the mitochondrial matrix · 区室
  • anaplerotic reaction — a reaction that replaces citric acid cycle intermediates · 回补反应
  • glucose-6-phosphatase — enzyme that removes the phosphate so glucose can leave the cell · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group joined by a thioester bond to coenzyme A · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • acetyl-CoA carboxylase — the biotin enzyme that adds CO₂ to acetyl-CoA to give malonyl-CoA · 乙酰 CoA 羧化酶
  • adipose tissue — the tissue that stores fat as triacylglycerol（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat） and releases fatty acids · 脂肪组织
  • alanine — an amino acid whose carbon skeleton is pyruvate · 丙氨酸
  • biotin — vitamin B₇, the coenzyme that carries CO₂ for carboxylases · 生物素
  • C4 photosynthesis — photosynthesis in which CO₂ is first fixed into a four-carbon acid · C4 光合作用
  • carboxylase — an enzyme that attaches CO₂ to a substrate, usually using ATP and biotin · 羧化酶
  • carnitine acyltransferase I — the enzyme that moves a fatty acyl group onto carnitine at the mitochondrion · 肉碱酰基转移酶（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） I
  • citrate — the six-carbon tricarboxylic acid formed from acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · 柠檬酸
  • citric acid cycle — the mitochondrial cycle that oxidises acetyl-CoA to two CO₂ · 柠檬酸循环
  • concentration — the amount of a substance per unit volume of solution · 浓度
  • Cori cycle — muscle sends lactate to the liver, which returns it as glucose · Cori 循环（= Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle）
  • cytosol — the fluid of the cell outside the organelles · 胞质
  • fasting — the metabolic state hours after a meal, when glucagon dominates and stores are released · 空腹状态
  • fatty acid — a long hydrocarbon chain with a carboxyl group at one end · 脂肪酸
  • fed state — the metabolic state after a meal, when insulin dominates and fuel is stored · 进食状态
  • flux — the rate at which material passes through a pathway · 通量
  • glucose-6-phosphate — glucose carrying a phosphate on carbon six · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸
  • glucose–alanine cycle — muscle sends amino nitrogen to the liver as alanine and receives glucose back · 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环（= glucose–alanine cycle = Cahill cycle）
  • glycerol — the three-carbon alcohol that forms the backbone of fats · 甘油
  • glycogen phosphorylase — the enzyme that cuts glucose units off glycogen as glucose-1-phosphate · 糖原磷酸化酶
  • glycogen synthase — the enzyme that adds glucose units onto glycogen · 糖原合酶
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on cells elsewhere · 激素
  • irreversible reaction — a reaction held far from equilibrium, so it runs in one direction only · 不可逆反应
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid formed by reducing pyruvate · 乳酸
  • malic enzyme — converts malate to pyruvate and produces NADPH · 苹果酸酶
  • malonyl-CoA — the three-carbon product of acetyl-CoA carboxylase, the first intermediate of fatty acid synthesis · 丙二酰 CoA
  • metabolism — the whole set of chemical reactions a cell uses to obtain energy and build molecules · 代谢
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid that accepts the acetyl group at the start of the cycle · 草酰乙酸
  • oxidation — loss of electrons by a molecule, often as loss of hydrogen or gain of oxygen · 氧化
  • pathway — a fixed sequence of enzyme-catalysed reactions converting one metabolite into another · 代谢途径
  • PEP carboxylase — plant and bacterial enzyme adding CO₂ to phosphoenolpyruvate to give oxaloacetate · PEP 羧化酶
  • phosphofructokinase — the enzyme that phosphorylates fructose-6-phosphate, the main control point of glycolysis · 磷酸果糖激酶
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group to a molecule · 磷酸化
  • propionyl-CoA carboxylase — the biotin enzyme that adds CO₂ to propionyl-CoA in odd-chain fatty acid breakdown · 丙酰 CoA 羧化酶
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon keto acid that ends glycolysis · 丙酮酸
  • pyruvate carboxylase — the biotin enzyme that adds CO₂ to pyruvate to give oxaloacetate · 丙酮酸羧化酶
  • pyruvate dehydrogenase — the complex that oxidatively decarboxylates pyruvate to acetyl-CoA · 丙酮酸脱氢酶
  • reciprocal regulation — one signal switching a synthetic pathway on and the opposing breakdown pathway off · 反向调控
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）
  • synthesis — building a larger molecule from smaller ones, which consumes energy · 合成
  • tissue — a group of similar cells carrying out the same function · 组织
  • urea — the nitrogen waste made in the liver from ammonia and excreted by the kidney · 尿素

The ledger: what each pathway makes, and where it goes

卡 gly · 24 · 25 · 23 · 32 · 一张表管到底：在哪儿 · 要不要氧 · 产什么 · 产物去哪儿

  1. ⚠ 先记住一句话：整条主干上，真正用到氧的只有一步 氧只在呼吸链的最后一步被用掉，它在那里接走电子、变成水。 除此之外，糖酵解、柠檬酸循环、β-氧化本身都不用氧。 那为什么没氧它们全停？因为它们都要 NAD⁺，而把 NADH 变回 NAD⁺ 的只有呼吸链，呼吸链要氧。 所以「需不需要氧」要分两种答法：直接用氧的（只有呼吸链），和离了氧就停的（几乎所有其他的）。 ⚠ 这个区分本身就是考点，被问「柠檬酸循环需要氧吗」时，答「不直接用，但没氧就停」。
  2. CO₂ 和 H₂O 各自从哪儿来 你呼出的 CO₂ 全部来自脱羧反应：丙酮酸脱氢酶一个、柠檬酸循环两个、磷酸戊糖途径一个。 代谢水来自呼吸链——氧接走电子和质子变成水。 ⚠ 常见误答：以为 CO₂ 是在呼吸链产生的。呼吸链不产 CO₂，只产水。
  3. 产物去哪儿，看它是「能量」「碳架」还是「氮」 能量类（NADH、FADH₂ → 呼吸链 → ATP；NADPH 不去呼吸链，留给合成和抗氧化）。 碳架类（乙酰 CoA → 柠檬酸循环烧掉，或出线粒体去做脂肪酸和胆固醇，或在肝里做酮体； 循环的中间物 → 氨基酸、血红素、葡萄糖）。 氮类（氨基酸的氮 → 谷氨酸/谷氨酰胺 → 尿素 → 肾 → 尿）。
  4. 同一个产物，在不同器官去向不同 葡萄糖-6-磷酸：肝里能脱磷酸变成葡萄糖放进血液，肌肉没有那个酶，所以肌肉的糖原只供自己用。 乳酸：肌肉产生，进血液到肝，再变回葡萄糖（Cori 循环）。 酮体：肝里造，但肝自己用不了，送去脑、心和肌肉。 ⚠ 「谁造、谁用」经常是同一道题的第二问。
通路在哪儿要氧吗产什么产物去哪儿
Glycolysis 糖酵解cytosol 胞质不用pyruvate · ATP · NADH丙酮酸 → 线粒体；NADH → 穿梭 → 呼吸链
Fermentation 发酵cytosol无氧时才走lactate 或 ethanol ＋ CO₂目的是把 NAD⁺ 还回去；乳酸 → 肝（Cori）

Citric acid cycle 柠檬酸循环matrix间接CO₂ · NADH · FADH₂ · GTP还原型 → 呼吸链；中间物 → 氨基酸、血红素、糖
β-oxidation β-氧化matrix间接acetyl-CoA · NADH · FADH₂同上；肝里过剩时 → 酮体
Ketogenesis 生酮liver matrix 肝间接acetoacetate · β-hydroxybutyrate进血液 → 脑、心、肌肉（肝自己用不了）

Gluconeogenesis 糖异生liver 胞质＋线粒体耗 ATPglucose进血液 → 脑、红细胞
Glycogen 糖原cytosol（肝、肌）不用glucose-1-P → glucose-6-P肝 → 血糖；肌肉 → 只供自己

Amino acid breakdown 氨基酸分解liver 肝间接NH₃ → urea · 碳架尿素 → 肾 → 尿；碳架 → 循环 / 糖 / 酮体
Light reactions 光反应thylakoid 类囊体膜⚠ 产氧O₂ · ATP · NADPH氧放掉；ATP 和 NADPH → 卡尔文循环
Calvin cycle 卡尔文循环stroma 基质不用triose phosphate 三碳糖→ 蔗糖、淀粉

Say this

Across the whole of metabolism, oxygen is used directly at only one point: at the end of the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), where it accepts the electrons and becomes water. Glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway), the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) and fatty acid oxidation (also called β-oxidation) do not use oxygen themselves, but they all stop without it, because they all need NAD⁺, and only the respiratory chain turns NADH back into NAD⁺. The carbon dioxide we breathe out comes from decarboxylations — one in pyruvate dehydrogenase, two in each turn of the citric acid cycle — not from the respiratory chain. As for where the products go: NADH and FADH₂ go to the chain to make ATP, while NADPH does not — it is kept for biosynthesis and antioxidant defence. Acetyl-CoA is either oxidised in the cycle, or carried out of the mitochondrion as citrate to build fatty acids and cholesterol, or turned into ketone bodies in the liver. And the nitrogen of amino acids ends up as urea, which the kidney excretes.

约 60 秒 · 「只有一步直接用氧」和「CO₂ 不是呼吸链产的」是两个区分度最高的点

术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetoacetate — one of the two main ketone bodies, a four-carbon keto acid made in the liver · 乙酰乙酸
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group joined by a thioester bond to coenzyme A · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • aerobic — taking place in the presence of oxygen · 有氧的
  • anaerobic — taking place without oxygen · 无氧的
  • antioxidant defence — the enzymes and small molecules that remove reactive oxygen species · 抗氧化防御
  • β-hydroxybutyrate — the reduced ketone body, formed from acetoacetate · β-羟丁酸
  • β-oxidation — the cycle that shortens a fatty acid by two carbons, giving acetyl-CoA, NADH and FADH₂ · β-氧化（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）
  • biosynthesis — the enzymatic building of larger molecules from smaller ones · 生物合成
  • Calvin cycle — the chloroplast pathway that fixes CO₂ into sugar · 卡尔文循环（= Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions）
  • citrate — the six-carbon tricarboxylic acid formed from acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · 柠檬酸
  • citric acid cycle — the mitochondrial cycle that oxidises acetyl-CoA to two CO₂, giving NADH, FADH₂ and GTP · 柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）
  • compartment — a separate space in the cell bounded by membrane, such as the cytosol or the matrix · 区室
  • cytosol — the fluid of the cell outside the organelles · 胞质
  • decarboxylation — removal of a carboxyl group from a molecule, released as CO₂ · 脱羧
  • electron acceptor — a molecule that takes up electrons and is thereby reduced · 电子受体
  • FADH₂ — the reduced form of flavin adenine dinucleotide, an electron-carrying coenzyme · 还原型 FAD
  • fermentation — reducing pyruvate to lactate or ethanol so that NAD⁺ is regenerated without oxygen · 发酵
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • glucose-1-phosphate — glucose carrying a phosphate on carbon one, the product of glycogen breakdown · 葡萄糖-1-磷酸
  • glucose-6-phosphate — glucose carrying a phosphate on carbon six · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸
  • glycogen — a branched polymer of glucose, the storage form of sugar in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • glycolysis — the ten-step cytosolic pathway from glucose to pyruvate · 糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）
  • GTP — guanosine triphosphate, a nucleotide energetically equivalent to ATP · 鸟苷三磷酸
  • inner mitochondrial membrane — the folded inner membrane that holds the respiratory chain and ATP synthase（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase） · 线粒体内膜
  • ketogenesis — making ketone bodies from acetyl-CoA, which happens in the liver · 生酮作用
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid formed by reducing pyruvate · 乳酸
  • light reactions — the photosynthetic reactions of the thylakoid membrane that make ATP, NADPH and O₂ · 光反应
  • metabolism — the whole set of chemical reactions a cell uses to obtain energy and build molecules · 代谢
  • mitochondrial matrix — the space enclosed by the inner mitochondrial membrane · 线粒体基质
  • mitochondrion — the double-membraned organelle that houses the citric acid cycle and the respiratory chain · 线粒体
  • NAD⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, the oxidised electron carrier of breakdown reactions · 氧化型 NAD
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, carrying two electrons · 还原型 NAD
  • NADPH — the reduced form of NADP⁺, the electron donor used for building molecules · 还原型 NADP
  • palmitate — the sixteen-carbon saturated fatty acid, the product of fatty acid synthase · 棕榈酸
  • pathway — a fixed sequence of enzyme-catalysed reactions converting one metabolite into another · 代谢途径
  • pentose phosphate pathway — the cytosolic pathway making NADPH and ribose-5-phosphate from glucose-6-phosphate · 磷酸戊糖途径（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway）
  • product — the molecule an enzyme releases after the reaction · 产物
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon keto acid that ends glycolysis · 丙酮酸
  • pyruvate dehydrogenase — the complex that oxidatively decarboxylates pyruvate to acetyl-CoA · 丙酮酸脱氢酶
  • reduction — gain of electrons by a molecule, the opposite of oxidation · 还原
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）
  • ribose-5-phosphate — the five-carbon sugar phosphate from which nucleotides are built · 核糖-5-磷酸
  • stroma — the soluble interior of the chloroplast, outside the thylakoids · 叶绿体基质
  • thylakoid — the internal membrane sacs of the chloroplast that carry the light reactions · 类囊体
  • triose phosphate — a three-carbon sugar bearing a phosphate group · 磷酸丙糖
  • urea — the nitrogen waste made in the liver from ammonia and excreted by the kidney · 尿素

Glycolysis

卡 gly · 十步分两相：前五步花钱，后五步赚钱

  1. 什么时候跑 — 一直在跑 饭后血糖高的时候最快；缺氧的时候它是唯一的能量来源； 红细胞一辈子只能靠它（没有线粒体）。在胞质，不需要氧。
  2. 投入相（第 1–5 步）— 先花 2 个 ATP 磷酸化 → 异构 → 再磷酸化 → 劈开 → 互换 glucose →[hexokinase|ATP] glucose-6-P →[phosphoglucose isomerase] fructose-6-P →[PFK-1|ATP · 限速] fructose-1,6-bisP →[aldolase] DHAP ＋ GAP DHAP 再由 triose phosphate isomerase 变成 GAP，所以一个葡萄糖给出两个 GAP。 ⚠ 为什么一上来要花钱：磷酸基带负电，把葡萄糖锁在细胞里出不去； 而且拉低胞内的游离葡萄糖浓度，进来的梯度才维持得住。
  3. 回收相（第 6–10 步）— 每个三碳赚 2 个 ATP 氧化装磷 → 给 ATP → 挪磷 → 脱水 → 再给 ATP GAP →[GAPDH|NAD⁺ ＋ Pᵢ] 1,3-bisphosphoglycerate →[phosphoglycerate kinase|→ATP] 3-phosphoglycerate →[phosphoglycerate mutase] 2-phosphoglycerate →[enolase|−H₂O] PEP →[pyruvate kinase|→ATP] pyruvate ⚠ GAPDH 是全程唯一的氧化步，它把无机磷装上去做成高能的酰基磷酸， 能量是从这里进来的，后面两次给 ATP 都是靠它。
  4. 三个关卡 — 全是 kinase，全不可逆 hexokinase（肝里是 glucokinase：Km 高、不被 G6P 抑制，饭后才大量吃糖）· PFK-1（最核心：ATP 和柠檬酸关它，AMP、ADP 和果糖-2,6-二磷酸开它）· pyruvate kinase（被果糖-1,6-二磷酸前馈激活；肝型被磷酸化关掉）。
  5. 分叉 — 丙酮酸三条去路，取决于有没有氧 有氧 → 进线粒体，PDH 做成 acetyl-CoA。 缺氧（动物） → 乳酸。缺氧（酵母） → 乙醇 ＋ CO₂。 ⚠ 后两条的目的不是产能，是再生 NAD⁺ —— 没有 NAD⁺，GAPDH 那一步就停， 整条路卡死。这一句被问到的频率很高。
  6. 别的糖从哪儿插进来 果糖（肝里经果糖激酶和醛缩酶 B 进到三碳那一层）· 半乳糖（经三个酶变成 G1P 再进）· 糖原（磷酸解直接给 G1P → G6P，省掉第一个激酶）。
  7. 总账 无氧：净 2 ATP ＋ 2 乳酸（毛 4 净 2）。 有氧：你的书说 38，现代教材 30–32。 ⚠ 差在两处：每个 NADH 折算 3 还是 2.5；以及胞质的两个 NADH 靠哪条穿梭进线粒体 （见呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）那一段）—— 这就是 30 和 32 的区别。

Say this

Glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) runs in the cytosol and needs no oxygen. It has ten steps in two phases. The first five spend two ATP — phosphorylation, isomerisation, a second phosphorylation, cleavage, and interconversion of the two trioses. The phosphate keeps the sugar inside the cell and keeps the gradient for uptake. The second five recover four, so the net is two ATP and two NADH per glucose. The only oxidation is at glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate (also called GAP, G3P) dehydrogenase, and that is where the energy enters. Three steps are irreversible and all three are kinases: hexokinase, phosphofructokinase-1, which is the main control point, and pyruvate kinase. Pyruvate then has three fates: with oxygen it goes to acetyl-CoA; without it, to lactate in animals or ethanol in yeast — and the point of that is to regenerate NAD-plus, without which the pathway would stop.

约 55 秒 · 「再生 NAD⁺」那一句别漏

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Martínek

① 糖酵解不需要氧，也不需要线粒体，所以从细菌到人几乎每个细胞都有它 universal pathway

Because glycolysis needs neither oxygen nor mitochondria, almost every cell from bacteria to humans can run it, and it is thought to be one of the oldest pathways.

不用氧、不用线粒体。

② 叫「底物水平磷酸化」，是因为磷酸直接从底物转到 ADP 上，不靠质子梯度 substrate-level phosphorylation

It is called substrate-level phosphorylation because the phosphate comes straight off a high-energy intermediate onto ADP, and not from a proton gradient. Phosphoglycerate kinase and pyruvate kinase are the two enzymes that do it.

与氧化磷酸化对比。

③ 生物化学是从这条途径上开始的：无细胞提取液照样发酵，第一个辅酶也是在这里找到的 discovery

This pathway is where biochemistry began: Buchner showed that a cell-free (also called cell-free protein synthesis, CFPS, in vitro transcription–translation) yeast extract still ferments sugar, so fermentation does not need a living cell. Harden and Young then found the first coenzyme, NAD, in that extract.

两件都在书 p009 的表里。

④ 发酵的目的是把 NADH 重新氧化成 NAD⁺，糖酵解才能继续：酵母做乙醇，肌肉做乳酸 fermentation

Fermentation exists to regenerate NAD⁺ so that glycolysis can continue. Yeast reduces pyruvate to ethanol and CO₂, which is why bread rises and wine contains alcohol; working muscle reduces it to lactate.

三个生活问题同一个答案。

⑤ 乳酸脱氢酶是同工酶的标准例子：两种亚基组成五个四聚体，反应相同、动力学不同 LDH isoenzymes

Lactate dehydrogenase, the enzyme that makes lactate, is the standard example of isoenzymes (also called isozyme). Two subunits, H for heart and M for muscle, combine into five tetramers with the same reaction but different kinetics.

书上同工酶（= isoenzyme = isozyme）的标准例子（p064）。

⑥ 有氧时一个葡萄糖给出的 ATP 多得多，所以细胞消耗的葡萄糖反而少得多 Pasteur effect

Because each glucose yields far more ATP when oxygen is present, yeast or muscle uses much less glucose then, and that is the Pasteur effect.

名字是通用知识。

⑦ 糖酵解的中间物个个是岔路口，所以它不只产 ATP，还供应糖原、核糖、甘油和氨基酸 links to other pathways

Its intermediates are branch points, so glycolysis also supplies building blocks: glycogen, the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway), glycerol for fats and alanine and serine. In the liver it runs in reverse as gluconeogenesis.

说到哪就接到哪个主题。

⑧ 容易搞反：氰化物堵的是呼吸链，不是糖酵解；氟化物抑制烯醇化酶，所以血糖管里加它 cyanide · fluoride

Cyanide blocks the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) and not glycolysis, but glycolysis alone cannot supply the brain and heart, so it still kills. Fluoride inhibits enolase, which is why blood-glucose tubes contain it: red cells then stop consuming the glucose before it is measured.

两个都是通用知识。

⑨ 己糖激酶结合葡萄糖后才闭合，把水挡在外面，所以不会白白水解 ATP ⭐ Martínek induced fit

Hexokinase, the first enzyme of the pathway, closes around glucose only after glucose binds, which shuts water out and stops it from simply hydrolysing ATP. That is the classic example of induced fit.

Martínek 做结合口袋和构象变化。

⑩ 书外：Warburg 效应是巴斯德效应的例外——癌细胞有氧也发酵，葡萄糖摄取高，所以 PET 查得出来 Warburg effect

The Warburg effect is the exception to the Pasteur effect: many cancer cells ferment glucose to lactate even with oxygen, and because their glucose uptake is so high, PET scans find the tumours. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Glycolysis is anaerobic — so it runs only without oxygen?" — No, it runs all the time; it simply does not need oxygen. · 一直在跑，只是不需要氧。
  • "Is glucose oxidised to CO₂ in glycolysis?" — No, only to pyruvate; the CO₂ is released later. · 只到丙酮酸。
  • "Is lactate a waste product?" — No, the liver converts it back to glucose. · 肝会把它变回葡萄糖。

定义

  • substrate-level phosphorylation — making ATP by transferring a phosphate from a metabolite directly to ADP · 底物水平磷酸化
  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule an enzyme needs to work · 辅酶
  • isoenzymes — different forms of an enzyme that catalyse the same reaction · 同工酶
  • Pasteur effect — lower glucose consumption in the presence of oxygen · 巴斯德效应
  • enolase — glycolytic enzyme that removes water from 2-phosphoglycerate · 烯醇化酶
  • induced fit — the enzyme changes shape when the substrate binds · 诱导契合
  • PET scan — imaging that detects a radioactively labelled glucose analogue · 正电子发射断层扫描
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group joined by a thioester bond to coenzyme A · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • alanine — an amino acid whose carbon skeleton is pyruvate · 丙氨酸
  • anaerobic — taking place without oxygen · 无氧的
  • Buchner — Eduard Buchner showed that a cell-free yeast extract still ferments sugar · 布赫纳
  • cell-free extract — the soluble contents of broken cells, which can still carry out reactions · 无细胞（= cell-free protein synthesis = CFPS = in vitro transcription–translation）提取液
  • cleavage — splitting one molecule into two by breaking a bond · 裂解
  • control point — the step of a pathway whose enzyme is regulated and therefore sets the rate · 控制点
  • cyanide — a poison that binds the iron of Complex IV（= Complex IV = cytochrome c oxidase） and blocks the respiratory chain · 氰化物
  • cytosol — the fluid of the cell outside the organelles · 胞质
  • Embden–Meyerhof pathway — the name of glycolysis after the two workers who established its steps · 糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的别名
  • fermentation — reducing pyruvate to lactate or ethanol so that NAD⁺ is regenerated without oxygen · 发酵
  • fluoride — an inhibitor of enolase, added to blood tubes so the sample stops using glucose · 氟化物
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate — the three-carbon sugar phosphate at which the two halves of glycolysis meet · 甘油醛-3-磷酸（= glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate = GAP = G3P）
  • glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate dehydrogenase — the one oxidising enzyme of glycolysis, using NAD⁺ and inorganic phosphate · GAPDH
  • glycerol — the three-carbon alcohol that forms the backbone of fats · 甘油
  • glycogen — a branched polymer of glucose, the storage form of sugar in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • gradient — a difference in concentration across a membrane, which drives movement · 梯度
  • Harden and Young — Arthur Harden and William Young found a heat-stable coenzyme, NAD, in yeast extract · 哈登和杨
  • hexokinase — the enzyme that phosphorylates glucose to glucose-6-phosphate · 己糖激酶
  • high-energy intermediate — a metabolite whose group-transfer potential is high enough to phosphorylate ADP · 高能中间物
  • intermediate — a compound formed in one step of a pathway and consumed in the next · 中间物
  • isomerisation — rearranging a molecule into an isomer, with the same atoms differently arranged · 异构化
  • kinase — an enzyme that transfers a phosphate group, usually from ATP · 激酶
  • kinetics — how the rate of a reaction depends on substrate concentration and other conditions · 动力学
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid formed by reducing pyruvate · 乳酸
  • lactate dehydrogenase — the enzyme that interconverts pyruvate and lactate using NAD⁺ · 乳酸脱氢酶
  • NAD⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, the oxidised electron carrier of breakdown reactions · 氧化型 NAD
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, carrying two electrons · 还原型 NAD
  • oxidation — loss of electrons by a molecule, often as loss of hydrogen · 氧化
  • pathway — a fixed sequence of enzyme-catalysed reactions converting one metabolite into another · 代谢途径
  • pentose phosphate pathway — the cytosolic pathway making NADPH and ribose-5-phosphate from glucose-6-phosphate · 磷酸戊糖途径（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway）
  • phosphofructokinase-1 — the enzyme that phosphorylates fructose-6-phosphate; the main control point · 磷酸果糖激酶-1
  • phosphoglycerate kinase — the enzyme that transfers a phosphate from 1,3-bisphosphoglycerate to ADP · 磷酸甘油酸激酶
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group to a molecule · 磷酸化
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon keto acid that ends glycolysis · 丙酮酸
  • pyruvate kinase — the enzyme that transfers the phosphate of phosphoenolpyruvate to ADP · 丙酮酸激酶
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链
  • serine — an amino acid with a hydroxymethyl side chain, built from an intermediate of glycolysis · 丝氨酸
  • subunit — one folded polypeptide chain within a protein made of several chains · 亚基
  • tetramer — a protein built from four subunits · 四聚体
  • triose — a sugar with three carbon atoms · 丙糖
  • Warburg effect — tumour cells ferment glucose to lactate even when oxygen is available · Warburg 效应

Pentose phosphate pathway

⚠ 她说没碰过的那一批 · 两段，第一段不可逆、第二段可逆

  1. 氧化段 — 两次脱氢，掉一个碳（不可逆）glucose-6-P →[G6PD|NADPH ①] 6-phosphogluconolactone →[lactonase|＋H₂O] 6-phosphogluconate →[6PGDH|NADPH ② · −CO₂] ribulose-5-PG6PD 是限速酶。⚠ 它的信号不是 ATP，是 NADP⁺ 够不够——这条最容易答错。
  2. 非氧化段 — 把糖架重排（可逆）核酮糖-5-磷酸可以异构成核糖-5-磷酸（给核酸），也可以经转酮酶（搬 2 碳）和转醛酶（搬 3 碳）重排回果糖-6-磷酸和甘油醛-3-磷酸，接回糖酵解。⚠ 这一段是可逆的，所以细胞可以按需要决定要核糖还是要 NADPH。

Say this

The pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway) runs in the cytosol and has two parts. The oxidative part is irreversible: two dehydrogenations and one decarboxylation give two NADPH, one CO₂ and ribulose-5-phosphate. The control enzyme is glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase, and what controls it is the supply of NADP-plus, not ATP. The non-oxidative part is reversible: transketolase moves two carbons and transaldolase moves three, so the sugars can be rearranged back into fructose-6-phosphate and glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate (also called GAP, G3P). The whole pathway neither makes nor spends ATP. Its two products are NADPH, for reductive synthesis and antioxidant defence, and ribose-5-phosphate, for nucleotides.

约 40 秒 · 「0 ATP」和「看 NADP⁺ 不看 ATP」是两个必说

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Ryšlavá

① 它的脱氢用的是 NADP⁺ 而不是 NAD⁺，所以电子去合成，不去呼吸链 a pathway for biosynthesis

Its dehydrogenases reduce NADP⁺, not NAD⁺, so the electrons go to biosynthesis and not the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain): the products are NADPH and ribose-5-phosphate.

两个产物，都用于合成。

② 第二段是可逆的，所以细胞能自己定 NADPH 和核糖的比例 adjustable output

Because the second part is reversible, the cell sets the ratio of the two products: it can make ribose without the oxidative part, or take only NADPH and send the surplus sugars back into glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway).
🪄 “Girls Love Gold · Rich Rats Take Two Free Gifts” ＝ glucose-6-P · lactone · 6-phosphogluconate ｜ ribulose-5-P · ribose-5-P ｜ transketolase · transaldolase · F6P · GAP.

两个产物的比例可以调。

③ 每一个核苷酸的糖都是这条途径给的，所以分裂中的细胞离不开它 nucleotide synthesis

Every nucleotide is built on the ribose-5-phosphate this pathway supplies, so rapidly dividing cells depend on it.

接核酸主题。

④ 合成脂肪酸和胆固醇要消耗 NADPH，所以肝、脂肪组织和肾上腺皮质里这条途径最活跃 active tissues

Making fatty acids and cholesterol consumes NADPH, so the pathway runs hardest in the liver, adipose tissue and adrenal cortex, the tissues that do most of that synthesis.

接脂肪合成、胆固醇两个主题。

⑤ NADPH 把谷胱甘肽保持在还原态，还原型谷胱甘肽清除过氧化氢，所以红细胞靠它活 glutathione

NADPH keeps glutathione in its reduced form, and reduced glutathione removes hydrogen peroxide, which is why red blood cells, which have no mitochondria, depend on this pathway.

接呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）里的活性氧。

⑥ 书外：G6PD 缺乏时谷胱甘肽还原不过来，所以吃蚕豆或服伯氨喹会溶血；疟疾流行区常见 G6PD deficiency

Glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase deficiency leaves too little NADPH to keep glutathione reduced, so fava beans or primaquine, an antimalarial drug, cause haemolysis. It is common where malaria was, because it gives some protection. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑦ 转酮酶用的辅因子，就是丙酮酸脱氢酶那个硫胺素焦磷酸，两个碳的单位挂在它身上 thiamine

The cofactor of transketolase is thiamine diphosphate, made from vitamin B₁ — the same one as in pyruvate dehydrogenase — and it carries the two-carbon unit.

接维生素、PDH 两个主题。

⑧ 两边要做的是同一件事——重排三到七碳的糖，所以 Calvin 循环用同样的转酮酶和醛缩酶 Calvin cycle

Both pathways face the same problem, rearranging three- to seven-carbon sugars, so the Calvin cycle (also called Calvin–Benson cycle, dark reactions, light-independent reactions) uses the same transketolase and aldolase reactions to regenerate ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate, the CO₂ acceptor.

接光合作用主题。

⑨ 细胞色素 P450 氧化药物所要的电子来自 NADPH，所以肝里的药物代谢也靠这条途径 ⭐ Hodek cytochrome P450

Cytochrome P450 takes its electrons from NADPH, so the liver's oxidation of drugs and other foreign compounds also draws on this pathway.

Hodek 做的就是 P450。

⑩ 植物受胁迫时活性氧增多，谷胱甘肽和抗坏血酸要不断再生，还原力同样来自 NADPH ⭐ Ryšlavá plant oxidative stress

Stress raises reactive oxygen species in plants, and regenerating the reduced glutathione and ascorbate that remove them costs NADPH from this pathway.

Ryšlavá 做植物抗氧化酶。

陷阱问法

  • "Does its NADPH go into the respiratory chain?" — No, it is used for synthesis and antioxidant defence. · 不进呼吸链。
  • "Is it only for making pentoses?" — No, it can run just to make NADPH and return the sugars to glycolysis. · 也可以只为 NADPH。
  • "Is it the same as the Calvin cycle?" — They share transketolase and aldolase reactions, but the Calvin cycle fixes CO₂. · 共用反应，目的不同。

定义

  • nucleotide — the building block of DNA and RNA · 核苷酸
  • glutathione — a small thiol-containing peptide that removes peroxides · 谷胱甘肽
  • reactive oxygen species — superoxide, hydrogen peroxide and related reactive forms of oxygen · 活性氧
  • haemolysis — breakdown of red blood cells · 溶血
  • transketolase — enzyme that transfers a two-carbon unit between sugars · 转酮酶
  • Calvin cycle — the pathway in which plants fix CO₂ into sugar · 卡尔文循环（= Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions）
  • cytochrome P450 — haem enzymes that oxidise drugs and foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • adrenal cortex — the outer layer of the adrenal gland, which makes steroid hormones · 肾上腺皮质
  • aldolase — an enzyme that joins or splits sugars by an aldol reaction · 醛缩酶
  • antioxidant defence — the enzymes and small molecules that remove reactive oxygen species · 抗氧化防御
  • ascorbate — vitamin C, a water-soluble reducing agent that removes reactive oxygen species · 抗坏血酸
  • biosynthesis — the enzymatic building of larger molecules from smaller ones · 生物合成
  • cholesterol — the four-ring sterol of animal membranes and precursor of steroid hormones · 胆固醇
  • cofactor — a non-protein component an enzyme needs to be active, such as a metal ion or coenzyme · 辅因子
  • cytosol — the fluid of the cell outside the organelles · 胞质
  • decarboxylation — removal of a carboxyl group from a molecule, released as CO₂ · 脱羧
  • dehydrogenase — an enzyme that oxidises a substrate by removing hydrogen onto a coenzyme · 脱氢酶
  • fructose-6-phosphate — fructose carrying a phosphate on carbon six, an intermediate of glycolysis · 果糖-6-磷酸
  • glucose-6-phosphate — glucose carrying a phosphate on carbon six · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸
  • glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase — the first and rate-limiting enzyme of this pathway, controlled by the NADP⁺ supply · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸脱氢酶
  • glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase deficiency — an inherited lack of that enzyme, so red cells cannot keep glutathione reduced · G6PD 缺乏
