  • hydrogen peroxide — H₂O₂, a reactive oxygen species removed by catalase and glutathione peroxidase · 过氧化氢
  • irreversible reaction — a reaction held far from equilibrium, so it runs in one direction only · 不可逆反应
  • malaria — a disease caused by Plasmodium parasites carried by mosquitoes · 疟疾
  • NADP⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide phosphate, the oxidised electron carrier used for building molecules · 氧化型 NADP
  • NADPH — the reduced form of NADP⁺, the cell's electron donor for reductive synthesis · 还原型 NADP
  • oxidative stress — a state in which reactive oxygen species are produced faster than they are removed · 氧化胁迫
  • pathway — a fixed sequence of enzyme-catalysed reactions converting one metabolite into another · 代谢途径
  • phosphogluconate pathway — another name for the pentose phosphate pathway · 磷酸戊糖途径的别名
  • primaquine — an antimalarial drug that causes haemolysis in people lacking G6PD · 伯氨喹
  • pyruvate dehydrogenase — the complex that oxidatively decarboxylates pyruvate to acetyl-CoA · 丙酮酸脱氢酶
  • reduction — gain of electrons by a molecule, the opposite of oxidation · 还原
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链
  • reversible reaction — a reaction near equilibrium, which can run in either direction · 可逆反应
  • ribose — the five-carbon sugar of RNA and of the nucleotide coenzymes · 核糖
  • ribose-5-phosphate — the five-carbon sugar phosphate from which nucleotides are built · 核糖-5-磷酸
  • ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate — the five-carbon sugar that accepts CO₂ in the Calvin cycle · 核酮糖-1,5-二磷酸
  • ribulose-5-phosphate — the five-carbon ketose phosphate made by the oxidative part of this pathway · 核酮糖-5-磷酸
  • thiamine diphosphate — the coenzyme made from vitamin B₁ that carries two-carbon aldehyde units · 硫胺素焦磷酸
  • transaldolase — the enzyme that transfers a three-carbon unit between sugars · 转醛醇酶
  • vitamin B₁ — thiamine, the water-soluble vitamin from which thiamine diphosphate is made · 维生素 B₁

Pyruvate dehydrogenase

糖和脂之间的单行道 · 三个酶、五个辅因子的接力

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有氧、而且细胞要烧糖的时候 它是糖酵解和柠檬酸循环之间的闸门。能量够了就关，不够就开。 在线粒体基质，丙酮酸得先被转运进去。
  2. 一句话总反应 pyruvate ＋ CoA-SH ＋ NAD⁺ →[pyruvate dehydrogenase complex] acetyl-CoA ＋ CO₂ ＋ NADH 氧化脱羧：掉一个碳变 CO₂，剩两个碳挂到 CoA 的硫上，脱下的氢给 NAD⁺。
  3. 三个酶的接力 E1 脱羧（TPP 上去接住，CO₂ 跑掉）→ E2 把那两个碳经硫辛酰胺的摆臂交给 CoA → E3 收拾残局（把硫辛酰胺重新氧化，氢经 FAD 交给 NAD⁺）。
  4. 五个辅因子 — 四个是 B 族维生素 TPP（B₁）· 硫辛酰胺 · CoA（B₅）· FAD（B₂）· NAD⁺（B₃） 口诀 Tender Loving Care For Nancy。 ⚠ α-酮戊二酸脱氢酶用的是同一套，背一次会两个。
  5. 调控 — 两层，方向一致 产物抑制：acetyl-CoA、NADH、ATP 多了就关。 共价修饰：PDH kinase 磷酸化 → 关；磷酸酶去磷酸化 ＋ Ca²⁺ 和 ADP → 开。 ⚠ 磷酸化＝关，跟糖原磷酸化酶正好相反，这个反向爱考。
  6. 为什么它这么要紧 ⚠ 这一步不可逆，所以 acetyl-CoA 回不到丙酮酸。 糖能变脂肪，脂肪变不回糖，断点就在这儿。 ⚠ 缺硫胺素这一步就瘫，丙酮酸和乳酸堆起来，脑子最先垮（脑只烧糖）。

Say this

Pyruvate dehydrogenase is the gate between glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) and the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle), in the mitochondrial matrix, and the reaction is an oxidative decarboxylation: pyruvate gives acetyl-CoA, CO₂ and NADH. It is a complex of three enzymes using five cofactors — TPP, lipoamide, coenzyme A, FAD and NAD-plus — and four of those are B vitamins. It is switched off by its own products, acetyl-CoA and NADH, and also by phosphorylation, which is the opposite direction from glycogen phosphorylase. ⚠ And because this step is irreversible, acetyl-CoA can never go back to pyruvate — that is why fat cannot be converted to glucose.

约 40 秒 · 结尾那句「所以脂肪变不成糖」是整段的落点

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hudeček

① 三个酶装在同一个颗粒上，所以中间物根本不释放，直接传到下一个活性中心 substrate channelling

Pyruvate dehydrogenase holds its three enzymes in one particle, so the intermediate is passed directly from one active site to the next and never released into solution. It is the classic example of substrate channelling.

中间物不释放。

② 三个复合体做的是同一种反应——α-酮酸的氧化脱羧，所以结构也是同一种 a family of three complexes

All three complexes do the same reaction, the oxidative decarboxylation of an α-keto acid: α-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase in the citric acid cycle and branched-chain α-keto acid dehydrogenase in valine, leucine and isoleucine breakdown.

三个复合体，同一种结构。

③ 它产生的乙酰 CoA 有三个去处，第三个只在循环接不下的时候才走 fates of acetyl-CoA

Its acetyl-CoA is oxidised in the citric acid cycle, used for fatty acids and cholesterol, or turned into ketone bodies if the cycle cannot take it.

接四个主题。

④ 乙酰 CoA 多了就活化丙酮酸羧化酶、抑制丙酮酸脱氢酶，所以丙酮酸去做糖而不是被烧掉 pyruvate carboxylase

When acetyl-CoA accumulates it activates pyruvate carboxylase and inhibits pyruvate dehydrogenase, so pyruvate is carboxylated to oxaloacetate for gluconeogenesis instead of being oxidised.

乙酰 CoA 让丙酮酸去做糖，而不是去烧。

⑤ 这个复合体是决定「葡萄糖烧不烧」的开关：饭后胰岛素开它，空腹磷酸化关它 fed vs fasted

This complex is the switch that decides whether glucose is burned. After a meal insulin promotes the active form; in fasting it is phosphorylated and switched off, so glucose is kept for the brain.

接代谢整合。

⑥ 酵母的丙酮酸脱羧酶用同一个硫胺素焦磷酸，但只脱羧不氧化，所以拿到的是乙醛 pyruvate decarboxylase

In fermenting yeast, pyruvate decarboxylase uses the same thiamine diphosphate to remove CO₂ but does not oxidise, so the product is acetaldehyde, which is then reduced to ethanol.

接糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）和发酵。

⑦ 缺硫胺素这一步就停，丙酮酸只能变乳酸，所以乳酸堆积，神经和心脏先坏 beriberi

Without thiamine this step stops, so pyruvate can only become lactate and lactate accumulates. That is beriberi, in which the nervous system and heart are damaged first. The name beriberi is general knowledge.

接维生素主题。

⑧ NADH 在 340 nm 有吸收、NAD⁺ 没有，所以这个反应可以在分光光度计里看着它走 ⭐ Hudeček absorbance of NADH

NADH absorbs light at 340 nm and NAD⁺ does not, so the reaction can be followed directly in a spectrophotometer as the absorbance rises.

Hudeček 做光谱学。

⑨ 书外：磷酸化是关，所以抑制 PDH 激酶的二氯乙酸能让复合体一直开着 dichloroacetate

Since phosphorylation switches the complex off, dichloroacetate inhibits pyruvate dehydrogenase kinase so that the complex stays active, and it has been tested for lactic acidosis. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is it part of the citric acid cycle?" — No, it links glycolysis to the cycle. · 不是循环的一步。
  • "Does phosphorylation switch it on?" — No, phosphorylation switches it off. · 磷酸化是关。
  • "Can acetyl-CoA be made into glucose?" — Not in animals, because this step is irreversible. · 不能，这一步不可逆。

定义

  • substrate channelling — passing an intermediate between active sites without releasing it · 底物通道
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • pyruvate carboxylase — enzyme that adds CO₂ to pyruvate to give oxaloacetate · 丙酮酸羧化酶
  • acetaldehyde — two-carbon aldehyde that yeast reduces to ethanol · 乙醛
  • beriberi — disease caused by thiamine deficiency · 脚气病
  • spectrophotometer — instrument that measures how much light a solution absorbs · 分光光度计
  • lactic acidosis — too much lactate and acid in the blood · 乳酸酸中毒
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • absorbance — the fraction of light a solution takes up at a given wavelength · 吸光度
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group joined by a thioester bond to coenzyme A · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • active site — the pocket of an enzyme where the substrate binds and the reaction happens · 活性中心
  • α-keto acid — an acid carrying a keto group on the carbon next to the carboxyl group · α-酮酸
  • α-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase — the citric acid cycle complex that turns α-ketoglutarate into succinyl-CoA · α-酮戊二酸脱氢酶
  • branched-chain amino acids — valine, leucine and isoleucine, whose side chains are branched · 支链氨基酸
  • branched-chain α-keto acid dehydrogenase — the third complex of this family, acting in branched-chain amino acid breakdown · 支链 α-酮酸脱氢酶
  • cholesterol — the four-ring sterol of animal membranes and precursor of steroid hormones · 胆固醇
  • citric acid cycle — the mitochondrial cycle that oxidises acetyl-CoA to two CO₂ · 柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）
  • coenzyme A — the thiol coenzyme, made from vitamin B₅, that carries acyl groups as thioesters · 辅酶 A
  • cofactor — a non-protein component an enzyme needs to be active, such as a metal ion or coenzyme · 辅因子
  • dichloroacetate — a compound that inhibits pyruvate dehydrogenase kinase, keeping the complex active · 二氯乙酸
  • enzyme complex — several enzymes held together in one particle so they act in sequence · 多酶复合体
  • FAD — flavin adenine dinucleotide, a tightly bound coenzyme from vitamin B₂ that carries two electrons · 黄素腺嘌呤二核苷酸
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • glycogen phosphorylase — the enzyme that cuts glucose units off glycogen; phosphorylation switches it on · 糖原磷酸化酶
  • glycolysis — the ten-step cytosolic pathway from glucose to pyruvate · 糖酵解
  • insulin — the hormone released after a meal that promotes uptake and storage of fuel · 胰岛素
  • intermediate — a compound formed in one step of a pathway and consumed in the next · 中间物
  • irreversible reaction — a reaction held far from equilibrium, so it runs in one direction only · 不可逆反应
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid formed by reducing pyruvate · 乳酸
  • lipoamide — lipoic acid linked to a lysine of the second enzyme; its arm carries the acetyl group and electrons · 硫辛酰胺
  • mitochondrial matrix — the space enclosed by the inner mitochondrial membrane · 线粒体基质
  • NAD⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, the oxidised electron carrier of breakdown reactions · 氧化型 NAD
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺; it absorbs light at 340 nm and NAD⁺ does not · 还原型 NAD
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid that accepts the acetyl group at the start of the citric acid cycle · 草酰乙酸
  • oxidation — loss of electrons by a molecule, often as loss of hydrogen · 氧化
  • oxidative decarboxylation — removing a carboxyl group as CO₂ while oxidising the rest of the molecule · 氧化脱羧
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group to a molecule · 磷酸化
  • product — the molecule an enzyme releases after the reaction · 产物
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon keto acid that ends glycolysis · 丙酮酸
  • pyruvate decarboxylase — the yeast enzyme that removes CO₂ from pyruvate to give acetaldehyde, without oxidising it · 丙酮酸脱羧酶
  • pyruvate dehydrogenase kinase — the kinase that phosphorylates the complex and switches it off · PDH 激酶
  • thiamine — vitamin B₁, the water-soluble vitamin from which thiamine diphosphate is made · 硫胺素
  • thiamine diphosphate — the coenzyme made from thiamine that carries two-carbon aldehyde units · 硫胺素焦磷酸
  • vitamin — an organic compound the body cannot make in sufficient amount and must obtain from food · 维生素

Citric acid cycle

卡 24 · 汇合点，也是原料库 —— 两个身份都要说

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有氧，而且随时 糖、脂、氨基酸都变成 acetyl-CoA 到这里汇合。在线粒体基质。 ⚠ 循环本身不用氧，但离了氧就停 —— 因为 NADH 要靠呼吸链换回 NAD⁺。
  2. 八步 — 中间物是难点，酶名可以现推 acetyl-CoA ＋ oxaloacetate →[citrate synthase] citrate →[aconitase] isocitrate →[isocitrate dehydrogenase|NAD⁺ · −CO₂] α-ketoglutarate α-ketoglutarate →[α-KG dehydrogenase|NAD⁺ · −CO₂] succinyl-CoA →[succinyl-CoA synthetase|GDP＋Pᵢ] succinate →[succinate dehydrogenase|FAD · 内膜上] fumarate →[fumarase|＋H₂O] malate →[malate dehydrogenase|NAD⁺] oxaloacetate 口诀 Can I Keep Selling Sex For Money, Officer?
  3. 碳怎么走 — 进两个，出两个 4 ＋ 2 ＝ 6 → 掉一个碳 5 → 再掉一个 4。 ⚠ acetyl-CoA 和草酰乙酸是「合体」不是「转化」：OAA 是托盘，转一圈还回来，净增 0。 ⚠ 而且第一圈掉的那两个 CO₂ 不是刚进来的那两个碳。
  4. 账 — 一圈四样 2 CO₂ · 3 NADH · 1 FADH₂ · 1 GTP。 换成 ATP：你的书说 12，现代算法 10。 ⚠ 陷阱问法：「循环产多少 ATP」→ 它直接产的只有 1 个 GTP，其余都是呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）造的。
  5. 三个关卡 ＋ 刹车 citrate synthase · isocitrate dehydrogenase（最关键）· α-KG dehydrogenase。 三个的刹车都是 NADH —— 所以真正决定转速的是呼吸链跑不跑得动。 Ca²⁺ 开其中两个：肌肉一收缩，钙进来，循环立刻提速。
  6. 特殊的一步 — 琥珀酸脱氢酶 ⚠ 循环里唯一长在内膜上的酶，而且它就是呼吸链复合体 Ⅱ。 它不用 NAD⁺ 用 FAD，被结构类似的丙二酸竞争性抑制。
  7. 两用性 — 它同时是原料库 中间物随时被抽走去做别的：α-酮戊二酸 → 谷氨酸· 草酰乙酸 → 天冬氨酸、糖异生· 琥珀酰-CoA → 血红素· 柠檬酸 → 出线粒体做脂肪酸。 ⚠ 抽走了就得补回来，这叫回补反应（anaplerotic）， 最主要的一个是 pyruvate carboxylase（丙酮酸 ＋ CO₂ → 草酰乙酸）。 这个词书上有（p140）。

Say this

The citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) runs in the mitochondrial matrix and it is the convergence point — carbohydrate, fat and amino acids all arrive as acetyl-CoA. Each turn gives two CO₂, three NADH, one FADH₂ and one GTP. ⚠ Note that the cycle itself makes only that one GTP directly; everything else is made later by the chain. Three steps are irreversible: citrate synthase, isocitrate dehydrogenase, which is the main control point, and α-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase, and all three are braked by NADH. Succinate dehydrogenase is the only membrane-bound enzyme of the cycle and it is respiratory Complex II (also called succinate dehydrogenase). And the cycle is amphibolic: its intermediates are constantly withdrawn for biosynthesis, so they have to be replaced by anaplerotic reactions, mainly pyruvate carboxylase.

约 50 秒 · 「只直接产 1 个 GTP」和「两用性」是两个区分度高的点

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hodek

① 它叫 amphibolic，是因为两件事都做：燃料在这里烧掉，中间物又被拿去合成 amphibolic

It is called amphibolic because it does both jobs: sugar, fat and protein are burned here as acetyl-CoA, while its intermediates are withdrawn to build amino acids, fatty acids, glucose and haem.

amphibolic ＝ 既分解又合成。

② 草酰乙酸每一圈都重新生成，所以极少的量就能烧掉大量乙酰 CoA oxaloacetate is regenerated

Oxaloacetate, which accepts the acetyl group at the start, is made again at the end of every turn. So a catalytic amount can burn an unlimited amount of acetyl-CoA, and that is why it is a cycle.

这就是它叫「循环」的原因。

③ 饥饿时草酰乙酸被肝拿去做葡萄糖，乙酰 CoA 就进不了循环，只能变酮体 ketone bodies

In fasting the liver pulls oxaloacetate into gluconeogenesis. Acetyl-CoA can then no longer enter the cycle and is turned into ketone bodies, which the brain can use as fuel.

一句话接上糖异生和酮体两个主题。

④ 容易搞反：循环本身不用氧，但 NADH 要靠呼吸链氧化回来，交不出去就停 respiratory chain

The cycle itself uses no oxygen. But its NADH and FADH₂ must be reoxidised by the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), which makes most of the ATP, so without oxygen the cycle stops.

循环本身不用氧，但 NADH 交不出去就停。

⑤ 循环的速度跟着需求走：ATP 和 NADH 多就慢，ADP 和钙升高就快 regulation

Its speed follows demand: high ATP and NADH slow it down, while rising ADP and calcium speed it up, calcium because it signals that the muscle is contracting.

一句话讲完调控。

⑥ 真核细胞把它放在线粒体基质，紧挨着呼吸链；细菌没有线粒体，所以在细胞质里跑 location

In eukaryotes it runs in the mitochondrial matrix, next to the respiratory chain that takes its NADH; bacteria have no mitochondria, so they run it in the cytoplasm.

几乎所有用氧的生物都有它。

⑦ 乙醛酸循环跳过两步脱羧，碳不丢，所以植物和细菌能把脂肪净变成糖，动物不能 glyoxylate cycle

The glyoxylate cycle of plants and bacteria skips the two steps that release CO₂, so no carbon is lost and fat can become sugar. Animals lack it, which is why we cannot make glucose from fatty acids.

常考：动物不能把脂肪净转成葡萄糖。

⑧ 一点点柠檬酸就让耗氧量大涨，远超它自己能解释的量，所以它一定是被反复使用的 discovery

In pigeon breast muscle in 1937, Hans Krebs found that a small amount of citrate raised oxygen consumption far more than its own oxidation could explain. So it had to be regenerated and reused. He received the Nobel Prize in 1953.

加一点点柠檬酸，耗氧量涨得远超它本身 → 它在被反复使用。

⑨ 氟乙酸本身无毒，是细胞自己的酶把它变成氟柠檬酸堵住循环——致死合成的标准例子 ⭐ Hodek fluoroacetate · lethal synthesis

Fluoroacetate, a rat poison, is harmless itself, but the cell's own enzymes convert it into fluorocitrate, which blocks the cycle. Becoming more toxic after metabolism is what lethal synthesis means, and it is the central idea of xenobiotic metabolism.

Hodek 做的就是外源物代谢。

⑩ 书外：突变的异柠檬酸脱氢酶产生 2-羟基戊二酸，它长得像 α-酮戊二酸，所以挡住去甲基化酶 oncometabolite

In some brain tumours a mutated isocitrate dehydrogenase makes 2-hydroxyglutarate, which resembles α-ketoglutarate and so blocks demethylases, changing DNA and histone methylation and gene expression. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does the cycle need oxygen?" — Not directly, but it stops without it, because NADH cannot be reoxidised. · 不直接用，没氧就停。
  • "How much ATP does it make?" — Directly only one GTP per turn; the rest is made by the respiratory chain. · 直接只有 1 个 GTP。
  • "Can fat become glucose?" — Not in animals; plants can, with the glyoxylate cycle. · 动物不能，植物能。

定义

  • amphibolic — both breaks down and builds up · 两用性
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • glyoxylate cycle — a variant of the cycle in plants and bacteria that skips the two CO₂-releasing steps · 乙醛酸循环
  • lethal synthesis — the body's own enzymes turning a harmless compound into a poison · 致死合成
  • xenobiotic — a foreign compound, such as a drug or poison · 外源物
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 2-hydroxyglutarate — the metabolite made by a mutated isocitrate dehydrogenase; it resembles α-ketoglutarate · 2-羟基戊二酸
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group joined by a thioester bond to coenzyme A · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • aconitase — the enzyme that converts citrate into isocitrate by removing and re-adding water · 顺乌头酸酶
  • α-ketoglutarate — the five-carbon acid of the cycle, also the carbon skeleton of glutamate · α-酮戊二酸
  • α-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase — the complex that oxidatively decarboxylates α-ketoglutarate to succinyl-CoA · α-酮戊二酸脱氢酶
  • anaplerotic reaction — a reaction that refills a citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） intermediate that has been withdrawn · 回补反应
  • catalytic amount — an amount far smaller than the substrate, because the molecule is regenerated and reused · 催化量
  • citrate — the six-carbon tricarboxylic acid formed from acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · 柠檬酸
  • citrate synthase — the enzyme that joins acetyl-CoA to oxaloacetate to give citrate · 柠檬酸合酶
  • Complex II（= Complex II = succinate dehydrogenase） — the respiratory complex that oxidises succinate; it is succinate dehydrogenase and pumps no protons · 复合体 II
  • control point — the step of a pathway whose enzyme is regulated and therefore sets the rate · 控制点
  • cytoplasm — the contents of a cell inside the plasma membrane and outside the nucleus · 细胞质
  • demethylase — an enzyme that removes a methyl group from DNA or from a protein · 去甲基化酶
  • eukaryote — an organism whose cells have a nucleus and other membrane-bounded organelles · 真核生物
  • FADH₂ — the reduced form of flavin adenine dinucleotide, an electron-carrying coenzyme · 还原型 FAD
  • fluoroacetate — a rat poison that is itself harmless until the cell's own enzymes convert it · 氟乙酸
  • fluorocitrate — the toxic product made from fluoroacetate, which blocks the cycle · 氟柠檬酸
  • fumarase — the enzyme that adds water across the double bond of fumarate to give malate · 延胡索酸酶
  • fumarate — the four-carbon unsaturated acid of the cycle · 延胡索酸
  • gene expression — the process by which a gene is transcribed and translated into its product · 基因表达
  • GTP — guanosine triphosphate, a nucleotide energetically equivalent to ATP · 鸟苷三磷酸
  • haem — the iron-containing porphyrin group of haemoglobin and the cytochromes · 血红素
  • histone — a basic protein that DNA is wound around to form the nucleosome · 组蛋白
  • intermediate — a compound formed in one step of a pathway and consumed in the next · 中间物
  • irreversible reaction — a reaction held far from equilibrium, so it runs in one direction only · 不可逆反应
  • isocitrate — the isomer of citrate made by aconitase, and the substrate of isocitrate dehydrogenase · 异柠檬酸
  • isocitrate dehydrogenase — the enzyme that oxidatively decarboxylates isocitrate; the main control point · 异柠檬酸脱氢酶
  • Krebs — Hans Krebs worked the cycle out in pigeon breast muscle and received the Nobel Prize in 1953 · 克雷布斯
  • malate — the four-carbon hydroxy acid oxidised in the last step of the cycle · 苹果酸
  • malate dehydrogenase — the enzyme that oxidises malate to oxaloacetate, reducing NAD⁺ · 苹果酸脱氢酶
  • metabolism — the whole set of chemical reactions a cell uses to obtain energy and build molecules · 代谢
  • methylation — attaching a methyl group, for example to DNA or to a histone · 甲基化
  • mitochondrial matrix — the space enclosed by the inner mitochondrial membrane · 线粒体基质
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, carrying two electrons · 还原型 NAD
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid that accepts the acetyl group at the start of the cycle · 草酰乙酸
  • oxidation — loss of electrons by a molecule, often as loss of hydrogen · 氧化
  • pyruvate carboxylase — the biotin enzyme that adds CO₂ to pyruvate to give oxaloacetate · 丙酮酸羧化酶
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链
  • succinate — the four-carbon acid oxidised by succinate dehydrogenase · 琥珀酸
  • succinate dehydrogenase — the only membrane-bound enzyme of the cycle; it uses FAD and is Complex II · 琥珀酸脱氢酶
  • succinyl-CoA — the thioester intermediate of the cycle, also the starting material for haem · 琥珀酰 CoA
  • succinyl-CoA synthetase — the enzyme that converts succinyl-CoA to succinate, making one GTP · 琥珀酰 CoA 合成酶
  • turn — one complete round of the cycle, in which one acetyl group is oxidised to two CO₂ · 一圈

Respiratory chain & oxidative phosphorylation

卡 25 · 两件事，只靠质子梯度连着

  1. 什么时候跑 — 只要有氧和还原型载体 前面所有路交上来的 NADH 和 FADH₂ 都在这里兑现。在线粒体内膜上。 ⚠ 真正限制速度的不是氧，是 ADP —— 没有 ADP 可磷酸化，链就慢下来（呼吸控制）。
  2. 电子从哪进 — 两个入口 NADH 从复合体 Ⅰ 进；FADH₂ 从复合体 Ⅱ 进（琥珀酸脱氢酶就是它）。 ⚠ Ⅱ 不泵质子，所以从这儿进的电子少推一段，FADH₂ 才比 NADH 值钱少。
  3. 四个复合体 ＋ 两个搬运工 Ⅰ → Q → Ⅲ → cyt c → Ⅳ → O₂ 辅基：Ⅰ FMN · Ⅱ FAD · Ⅲ b/c₁ · Ⅳ a/a₃＋Cu（口诀 Fat Frogs Bite Ankles）。 ⚠ 泛醌 Q 是唯一的非蛋白组分，脂溶性，带 2 电子 ＋ 2 质子； cyt c 是内膜外侧的外周蛋白，一次只带 1 个电子。
  4. 泵出来多少质子 现代：Ⅰ 泵 4 ＋ Ⅲ 泵 4 ＋ Ⅳ 泵 2 ＝ 每个 NADH 10 个；FADH₂ 只走后两段，6 个。 你的书：Ⅳ 不算泵，每个 NADH 6 个 ≈ 3 ATP，FADH₂ 4 个 ≈ 2 ATP。 ⚠ 两套都要会，而且要说得出差在哪。
  5. ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase） — 一台被质子推着转的机器 F₀ 是嵌在膜里的质子通道，质子流过去让它转；F₁ 是伸进基质的头， 转动改变三个催化位点的构象，把 ADP ＋ Pᵢ 挤成 ATP。 ⚠ 每个 ATP 要 4 个质子：3 个用来转，第 4 个用来把磷酸运进基质。 这就是 10 ÷ 4 ＝ 2.5 的来历。
  6. 胞质的 NADH 怎么进来 — 两条穿梭，而且值钱不一样 NADH 本身过不了内膜，糖酵解造的那两个得换个方式进去： 苹果酸-天冬氨酸穿梭（肝、心）→ 在基质里还原出 NADH，值 2.5； 甘油磷酸穿梭（肌肉、脑）→ 交给 FAD，变成 FADH₂，只值 1.5。 ⚠ 这就是「一个葡萄糖 30 还是 32」的答案 —— 看用哪条穿梭。
  7. 毒物三类 — 分清楚才不会答反 ① 堵复合体：鱼藤酮 Ⅰ · 抗霉素 Ⅲ · 氰化物 / CO / 叠氮 Ⅳ → 链停，ATP 也停。 ② 解偶联（DNP、褐色脂肪的 UCP）：质子漏回去 → ATP 停，但链跑得更快、耗氧更多，能量变热。 ③ 堵合酶（寡霉素）：质子出不去、淤积 → ATP 和链一起停。 ⚠ ②和③的对比是最常考的一对。
  8. ⭐ P/O ratio — 「一个 NADH 等于三个 ATP」说的就是这个数 P/O ＝ phosphorus to oxygen ratio（磷氧比）：每还原一个氧原子，能做出几个 ATP。 你的书（p146）给的是：每个 NADH 泵 6 个质子 ≈ 3 ATP，每个 FADH₂ 泵 4 个 ≈ 2 ATP， 总式写成 NADH ＋ H⁺ ＋ ½ O₂ ＋ 3 ADP ＋ 3 Pᵢ → NAD⁺ ＋ 3 ATP ＋ 4 H₂O ⚠ 现代的数是 2.5 和 1.5（每个 NADH 泵 10 个质子，合酶转一圈要 4 个——三个推转子、一个把磷酸运进来）。 所以这个比值不是整数，而「3 和 2」是老教材的取整。 ⚠ 两个数都要会说，而且要说清哪个是你书上的：先给书的 3 和 2，被追问再给 2.5 和 1.5 并说明为什么不是整数。
  9. ⭐ 干扰这条链只有两种方式，答反了就全错 一、抑制剂（inhibitor）——把链堵住。堵住之后耗氧停、ATP 也停， 上游全部停在还原态、下游全部停在氧化态。记法 R-A-C 堵 1-3-4： 鱼藤酮 rotenone 堵复合体 Ⅰ · 抗霉素 A antimycin A 堵 Ⅲ · 氰化物和一氧化碳堵 Ⅳ； 丙二酸 malonate 竞争性抑制琥珀酸脱氢酶（Ⅱ）；寡霉素 oligomycin 堵的不是链，是 ATP 合酶。 二、解偶联剂（uncoupler）——把梯度漏掉。2,4-二硝基苯酚 2,4-dinitrophenol 带着质子直接穿回基质， 不走合酶。结果是链跑得更快、耗氧更多，但一个 ATP 都不产，能量全变成热。 ⚠ 「解偶联」不等于「抑制」：抑制是耗氧停，解偶联是耗氧反而增加。这一句最容易答反。 棕色脂肪里的解偶联蛋白 thermogenin（UCP1）做的就是同一件事，那是生理性的产热。
  10. ⚠ 寡霉素那个实验 — 一句话证明「耦合的是梯度」 寡霉素只堵合酶，可是电子传递也跟着停了——因为质子出不去、梯度顶住了泵。 这时候加一个解偶联剂，电子传递立刻重新跑起来，但不产 ATP。 ⚠ 所以把两者连在一起的既不是酶、也不是某个化学中间物，而是质子梯度本身—— 这正是 Mitchell 化学渗透学说的证据。这一条书里一个字都没有，说的时候标 general。 「链只做一件事：把电子的落差换成膜外的质子。合酶只做一件事：把质子放回来换 ATP。」

Say this

The respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) and ATP synthesis are two separate things, coupled only through the proton gradient — that is chemiosmosis, and no chemical intermediate passes between them. Electrons from NADH enter at Complex I and from FADH₂ at Complex II (also called succinate dehydrogenase), which pumps no protons, and that is why FADH₂ is worth less. They pass along ubiquinone (also called coenzyme Q, CoQ), Complex III, cytochrome c and Complex IV (also called cytochrome c oxidase) to oxygen. On the modern count, ten protons are pumped per NADH and six per FADH₂, and the synthase needs four protons per ATP — three to turn it and one to bring the phosphate in — which gives 2.5 and 1.5. My own textbook counts six protons and about three ATP instead. ⚠ Cytosolic NADH cannot cross the membrane, so it enters by a shuttle: the malate–aspartate shuttle delivers NADH and the glycerol phosphate shuttle delivers FADH₂, and that is why a glucose is worth thirty or thirty-two depending on the tissue.

约 60 秒 · 穿梭那一句是这一段新加的重点

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hudeček、Martínková

① 一步放完的能量没法收，所以要经过一串载体，每一小步都能泵质子 stepwise transfer

Energy released in a single step could not be captured. That is why electrons pass from NADH to oxygen through several carriers, each smaller step coupled to proton pumping.

不是一步放完。

② 我们之所以必须呼吸氧，是因为全部代谢里只有这一处直接用氧 oxygen as final acceptor

The reason we must breathe is here: oxygen is used directly at only one point in all of metabolism, the end of this chain. It has the most positive redox potential, and is reduced to water.

「为什么要呼吸氧」。

③ 这串顺序不用死记，是电位逼出来的：电子总是从更负流向更正 redox potential

The order is not arbitrary but set by the redox potentials: electrons always move from the more negative carrier to the more positive one. That gives NADH → Complex I → Q → Complex III → cytochrome c → Complex IV → oxygen.

电位决定顺序。🪄 念「一 Q 三 c 四」。

④ 顺序之所以能被证实，是因为细胞色素有颜色 ⭐ Hudeček spectroscopy of cytochromes

We know the order because cytochromes absorb visible light: block one step, and a spectrophotometer shows every carrier before it reduced and every carrier after it oxidised.

Hudeček 做光谱学。

⑤ 氰化物和一氧化碳之所以致命，是因为它们占住了氧自己要结合的那个铁 ⭐ Martínková haem and gases

Cyanide and carbon monoxide are lethal because in Complex IV, cytochrome c oxidase, they bind the same haem iron that oxygen binds, and the whole chain stops.

Martínková 研究气体结合血红素。

⑥ 用氧是有代价的：电子会提前漏出变成超氧，所以细胞必须常备清除酶 reactive oxygen species

Using oxygen has a cost. An electron sometimes leaks out early, mainly at Complexes I and III, and reduces oxygen to superoxide; superoxide dismutase, catalase and glutathione peroxidase remove it.

清除最终靠 NADPH，接 PPP。

⑦ 能做多少 ATP 受膜面积限制，所以内膜折成嵴，把面积做大 cristae

How much ATP a mitochondrion can make is limited by membrane area, so the inner membrane is folded into cristae, holding far more of the chain and of ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase).

「内膜为什么折叠」。

⑧ 呼吸链是两套基因组一起造出来的，因为线粒体来自细菌 mitochondrial DNA

The chain is built from two genomes: mitochondria descend from bacteria by endosymbiosis and kept their own DNA, which still codes for some subunits of the chain.

接细胞器、遗传主题。

⑨ 同一套原理，电子来源相反：叶绿体的电子来自水，由光推动 photosynthesis

Chloroplasts use the same principle, but with the opposite electron source. An electron transport chain in the thylakoid membrane builds a proton gradient that drives ATP synthase, and the electrons come from water, energised by light.

接光合作用主题。

⑩ 书外：链和 ATP 之间没有高能中间物，连接它们的就是梯度本身 Peter Mitchell

Peter Mitchell proposed that no high-energy chemical intermediate links the chain to ATP synthesis: the link is the proton gradient itself, chemiosmosis. He received the Nobel Prize for it. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书写了原理，没写名字。

⑪ ⭐ 每还原一个氧原子做几个 ATP：书给 3 和 2，现代给 2.5 和 1.5 P/O ratio

The P/O ratio, the phosphorus to oxygen ratio, counts how many ATP are made per oxygen atom reduced. My textbook gives three for NADH and two for FADH₂, but the real values are fractional, 2.5 and 1.5, because the proton counts do not divide evenly.

先给书的 3 和 2，被追问再给 2.5 和 1.5。

⑫ ⭐ 四个抑制剂里只有寡霉素不堵链，堵的是合酶，可结果一样 rotenone · antimycin A · cyanide · oligomycin

Rotenone blocks Complex I, antimycin A Complex III, and cyanide and carbon monoxide Complex IV; oligomycin alone blocks not the chain but ATP synthase. With any of them, oxygen consumption and ATP synthesis both stop.

R-A-C 堵 1-3-4；寡霉素堵的是合酶。

⑬ ⭐ 最容易背反的一条：解偶联剂让耗氧增加，不是减少 2,4-dinitrophenol · thermogenin

An uncoupler makes oxygen consumption rise, not fall. 2,4-dinitrophenol carries protons back across the membrane, bypassing ATP synthase, so the chain runs faster and makes no ATP, releasing the energy as heat; thermogenin in brown fat does this physiologically.

⚠ 解偶联 ≠ 抑制：一个耗氧增加，一个耗氧停。

⑭ ⭐ 书外：化学渗透的直接证据就在这里——寡霉素停住电子传递，加解偶联剂又跑起来 respiratory control

This is direct evidence for chemiosmosis. Oligomycin blocks only the synthase, yet electron transport stops as well, because the gradient builds up against the pumps; add an uncoupler and transport restarts with no ATP made. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

化学渗透学说的证据。

⑮ 复合体 I、II 里的铁不是血红素，是铁硫簇；不管几个铁，一次只交一个电子 iron-sulfur cluster · non-haem iron

An iron-sulfur cluster is a small inorganic centre made of iron and sulfur atoms, held on the protein by cysteine side chains. This is non-haem iron: there is no porphyrin ring, unlike the cytochromes. Complex I holds eight clusters, Complex II three, and Complex III one, called the Rieske centre. ⚠ However many irons a cluster holds, it hands on only one electron at a time, while NADH and FADH2 each bring two — so the chain has to split every pair, and that is what the clusters are for.

铁硫簇 ＝ 铁 ＋ 硫，挂在半胱氨酸上，非血红素铁。两电子进来，一个一个出去。柠檬酸循环的 aconitase 也有一个，但它是用来抓住底物的，不传电子。

陷阱问法

  • "So the respiratory chain makes the ATP?" — No, it makes the proton gradient; ATP synthase makes the ATP. · 链建梯度，合酶做 ATP。
  • "Is oxygen burned?" — No, it is reduced to water. · 氧被还原成水。
  • "Does NADPH enter the chain?" — No, NADPH is used for biosynthesis. · 不进。

定义

  • redox potential — how strongly a molecule attracts electrons · 氧化还原电位
  • cytochrome — haem protein that carries electrons by changing the charge of its iron · 细胞色素
  • cytochrome c oxidase — Complex IV（= Complex IV = cytochrome c oxidase）, which passes electrons to oxygen · 细胞色素 c 氧化酶
  • superoxide — oxygen that has taken one extra electron · 超氧阴离子
  • cristae — folds of the inner mitochondrial membrane · 嵴
  • endosymbiosis — one cell living inside another; the origin of mitochondria · 内共生
  • thylakoid membrane — the internal membrane of the chloroplast · 类囊体膜
  • chemiosmosis — using a proton gradient across a membrane to make ATP · 化学渗透
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • iron-sulfur cluster — a small centre of iron and sulfur atoms held on a protein by cysteines, which passes one electron at a time · 铁硫簇
  • non-haem iron — protein-bound iron that sits in an iron-sulfur cluster rather than in a porphyrin ring · 非血红素铁
  • Rieske centre — the single iron-sulfur cluster of Complex III, unusual because two histidines hold it instead of two cysteines · 里斯克中心
  • 2,4-dinitrophenol — an uncoupler that carries protons back across the membrane, bypassing ATP synthase · 2,4-二硝基苯酚
  • antimycin A — an inhibitor that blocks electron flow through Complex III · 抗霉素 A
  • ATP synthase — the enzyme that makes ATP from ADP and phosphate as protons flow back through it · ATP 合酶
  • carbon monoxide — CO, a gas that binds the haem iron of Complex IV and stops the chain · 一氧化碳
  • catalase — the haem enzyme that breaks hydrogen peroxide down to water and oxygen · 过氧化氢酶
  • chloroplast — the double-membraned plant organelle in which photosynthesis takes place · 叶绿体
  • coenzyme Q — another name for ubiquinone · 辅酶 Q（= ubiquinone = coenzyme Q = CoQ）
  • Complex I — NADH dehydrogenase, which takes electrons from NADH to ubiquinone and pumps protons · 复合体 I
  • Complex II（= Complex II = succinate dehydrogenase） — succinate dehydrogenase, which feeds electrons from FADH₂ to ubiquinone and pumps no protons · 复合体 II
  • Complex III — the cytochrome bc₁ complex, which passes electrons from ubiquinol to cytochrome c and pumps protons · 复合体 III
  • coupling — linking two processes so that one can only proceed when the other does · 偶联
  • cyanide — a poison that binds the haem iron of Complex IV and blocks the chain · 氰化物
  • cytochrome c — a small haem protein on the outer face of the inner membrane that carries one electron at a time · 细胞色素 c
  • electron carrier — a molecule that is alternately reduced and reoxidised as it passes electrons along · 电子载体
  • electron transport chain — another name for the respiratory chain · 电子传递链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）
  • FADH₂ — the reduced form of flavin adenine dinucleotide, an electron-carrying coenzyme · 还原型 FAD
  • FMN — flavin mononucleotide, the flavin group that accepts the electrons of NADH in Complex I · 黄素单核苷酸
  • glutathione peroxidase — a selenium enzyme that removes peroxides using reduced glutathione · 谷胱甘肽过氧化物酶
  • glycerol phosphate shuttle — carries the electrons of cytosolic NADH into the mitochondrion as FADH₂ · 甘油磷酸穿梭
  • haem — the iron-containing porphyrin group that binds oxygen, cyanide and carbon monoxide · 血红素
  • high-energy intermediate — a chemical compound that would store the energy between two processes; here there is none · 高能中间物
  • inhibitor — a molecule that binds an enzyme or carrier and stops it working · 抑制剂
  • malate–aspartate shuttle — carries the electrons of cytosolic NADH into the matrix as NADH · 苹果酸-天冬氨酸穿梭
  • malonate — a competitive inhibitor of succinate dehydrogenase, which resembles succinate · 丙二酸
  • mitochondrial DNA — the small circular genome inside the mitochondrion, coding for some subunits of the chain · 线粒体 DNA
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, carrying two electrons · 还原型 NAD
  • oligomycin — an inhibitor that blocks the proton channel of ATP synthase, not the chain itself · 寡霉素
  • oxidation — loss of electrons by a molecule, often as loss of hydrogen · 氧化
  • oxidative phosphorylation — making ATP from ADP using the energy of the proton gradient built by the chain · 氧化磷酸化
  • P/O ratio — phosphorus to oxygen ratio: how many ATP are made per oxygen atom reduced · 磷氧比
  • Peter Mitchell — proposed the chemiosmotic theory, that the proton gradient links the chain to ATP synthesis · 米切尔
  • proton gradient — the difference in H⁺ concentration and charge across the inner membrane · 质子梯度
  • proton pumping — moving H⁺ across the membrane against its gradient, using the energy of electron transfer · 泵质子
  • reduction — gain of electrons by a molecule, the opposite of oxidation · 还原
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链
  • respiratory control — the chain slows when there is no ADP left to phosphorylate · 呼吸控制
  • rotenone — a plant insecticide that blocks Complex I · 鱼藤酮
  • spectrophotometer — an instrument that measures how much light a solution absorbs · 分光光度计
  • subunit — one folded polypeptide chain within a protein made of several chains · 亚基
  • succinate dehydrogenase — the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） enzyme that is also respiratory Complex II · 琥珀酸脱氢酶
  • superoxide dismutase — the enzyme that converts superoxide into hydrogen peroxide and oxygen · 超氧化物歧化酶
  • terminal electron acceptor — the last molecule of the chain to take the electrons; here, oxygen · 末端电子受体
  • thermogenin — the uncoupling protein of brown fat that lets protons return without making ATP · 解偶联蛋白
  • ubiquinone — the small lipid-soluble carrier in the membrane, the only non-protein component of the chain · 泛醌
  • uncoupler — a compound that lets protons cross the membrane without passing through ATP synthase · 解偶联剂

Fatty acid oxidation

⚠ 她说太短的那一段，补全了 · 三步进门，一个循环，三个分叉

  1. 什么时候跑 — 空腹、长时间运动、饥饿 脂肪酸是身体最主要的能量来源，尤其是空腹的时候。 血糖一低，胰岛素降、胰高血糖素和肾上腺素升， 激素敏感性脂肪酶（HSL）把脂肪组织里的甘油三酯拆开， 放出游离脂肪酸，靠白蛋白在血里运到肌肉和肝。 ⚠ 拆下来的甘油另走一路：去肝里做糖异生。 ⚠ 而脑用不了脂肪酸 —— 这正是酮体存在的理由： 肝把脂肪酸烧出的 acetyl-CoA 改装成酮体送给脑。
  2. 活化 — 在胞质，先做成硫酯 fatty acid ＋ CoA ＋ ATP →[acyl-CoA synthetase] acyl-CoA ＋ AMP ＋ PPᵢ ⚠ ATP 断成 AMP，不是 ADP，焦磷酸随后又被水解掉 —— 所以这一步的代价是 两个高能磷酸键，不是一个。书说得很明白：游离脂肪酸进不了任何代谢通路，合成和分解都得先活化。
  3. 肉碱穿梭 — 真正的限速门 长链酰基-CoA 过不了线粒体内膜。外膜上的 CPT-1 把酰基换到肉碱上， translocase 把它换进去，内侧的 CPT-2 再换回 CoA。 ⚠ CPT-1 是整条路的限速步，而它被丙二酰-CoA 关掉 —— 那是脂肪酸合成的第一个产物。 合成一开，氧化的门就锁上。⚠ 中短链脂肪酸不用这道门，直接就进去了。
  4. 四步循环 — oxidation · hydration · oxidation · thiolysis（O-H-O-T） acyl-CoA →[acyl-CoA dehydrogenase|FAD] trans-Δ²-enoyl-CoA →[enoyl-CoA hydratase|＋H₂O] 3-hydroxyacyl-CoA →[3-hydroxyacyl-CoA dehydrogenase|NAD⁺] 3-ketoacyl-CoA →[thiolase|＋CoA] acetyl-CoA ＋ acyl-CoA（短两个碳） 每轮出 1 FADH₂ ＋ 1 NADH ＋ 1 acetyl-CoA，轮数 ＝ 碳数 ÷ 2 − 1。 ⚠ 白拿的加分：前三步跟柠檬酸循环最后三步是同一套化学（脱氢 → 加水 → 再脱氢）。
  5. 分叉一 · 奇数碳 — 最后剩三个碳 偶数碳链切到最后正好是两个 acetyl-CoA，干净利落。 奇数碳链最后一轮剩下的是丙酰-CoA（3 碳），它得另走一条路： propionyl-CoA →[propionyl-CoA carboxylase|ABC] methylmalonyl-CoA →[methylmalonyl-CoA mutase|B₁₂] succinyl-CoA ⚠ 琥珀酰-CoA 是柠檬酸循环的中间物，能变成草酰乙酸，所以能做糖。 这是脂肪酸里唯一生糖的那一小块 —— 而「脂肪不能变成糖」的大原则还是对的， 因为那说的是偶数碳链给出的 acetyl-CoA。 ⚠ 顺带：这里是 B₁₂ 在代谢里露面的两处之一。
  6. 分叉二 · 超长链（C > 22）— 先去过氧化物酶体 线粒体处理不了太长的链。C22 以上的先在过氧化物酶体里切几轮，短了再交给线粒体。 ⚠ 那里的第一步不一样：用的是氧化酶，电子直接交给氧， 产物是 H₂O₂ 而不是 FADH₂（过氧化氢酶随后把它拆掉）。 所以那几轮不产 ATP，能量变成热。 ⚠ 病名知道就行：X 连锁肾上腺脑白质营养不良（超长链进不了过氧化物酶体）。
  7. 分叉三 · 不饱和 — 要多两个酶 天然双键是顺式，而循环只认反式 Δ²。 所以双键在奇数位时要一个异构酶把它搬正；在偶数位时还要一个 还原酶（用 NADPH）先把两个双键并成一个再搬。 ⚠ 每绕过一个双键，就少一次 FAD 脱氢，所以不饱和脂肪酸产的 ATP 略少。
  8. 总账 — 一整条棕榈酸（C16） 7 轮 → 8 acetyl-CoA ＋ 7 FADH₂ ＋ 7 NADH，再减去活化花掉的 2 个高能键。 现代算法 ≈ 106 ATP；按你书的折算率（acetyl-CoA 12、NADH 3、FADH₂ 2）≈ 129。 ⚠ 报数之前先说是按哪套算的。

Say this

Fatty acids are the body’s main fuel, especially when fasting. When blood glucose falls, insulin drops and glucagon rises, hormone-sensitive lipase breaks down the stored triacylglycerol (also called triglyceride, neutral fat), and the free fatty acids travel to the tissues bound to albumin. ⚠ The brain cannot use them, which is exactly why the liver turns them into ketone bodies. Beta-oxidation (also called β-oxidation, fatty acid oxidation) itself happens in the mitochondrial matrix, and there are two things to get through first. The fatty acid must be activated to a thioester with coenzyme A, which costs two high-energy bonds because the ATP goes all the way to AMP. Then the acyl group has to cross the inner membrane on carnitine, and carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1) is the rate-limiting step of the whole pathway — it is inhibited by malonyl-CoA, so switching synthesis on switches oxidation off. The cycle itself is four steps: oxidation, hydration, oxidation and thiolysis, giving one FADH₂, one NADH and one acetyl-CoA per round, and the chain is two carbons shorter each time. An even-numbered chain ends as acetyl-CoA. An odd-numbered one leaves propionyl-CoA, which is carboxylated and then rearranged by a B12-dependent mutase into succinyl-CoA — and that is the only part of a fatty acid that can become glucose. Very long chains, above about twenty-two carbons, are shortened in the peroxisome first, where the first oxidation gives hydrogen peroxide instead of FADH₂, so that part yields heat rather than ATP.

约 70 秒 · ⚠ 开口先说「什么时候跑」，再说怎么跑 —— 考官想听的是你知道这条路为什么存在

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hodek

① 脂代谢比 β-氧化宽得多：分解、合成、胆固醇、运输四块，β-氧化只在第一块里 lipid metabolism vs fatty acid oxidation

Lipid metabolism is much wider than β-oxidation, and has four parts: breakdown by lipases, β-oxidation and ketone bodies; synthesis by acetyl-CoA carboxylase and fatty acid synthase into triacylglycerol and phospholipids; cholesterol synthesis via HMG-CoA reductase; and transport in lipoproteins. β-oxidation is one route inside the first part, and its four steps are oxidation, hydration, oxidation and thiolysis: O-H-O-T.

分解 · 合成 · 胆固醇 · 运输。四步记 O-H-O-T。

② 脂肪能存几周而糖原只能存一天，因为脂肪酸更还原，而且储存时不带水 fat as energy store

Fat holds weeks of energy while glycogen holds about a day, for two reasons: a fatty acid is more reduced than glucose, and fat is stored without water. That is why β-oxidation matters at all.

糖原约一天，脂肪几周。

③ 心脏主要烧脂肪酸，脑几乎不用，红细胞完全不能——它们没有线粒体 tissues

Tissues differ sharply here: the heart and resting skeletal muscle use mainly fatty acids, the brain uses almost none, and red blood cells cannot use them at all, because they have no mitochondria.

脑不用脂肪酸，所以需要酮体。

④ 空腹时肝烧脂肪酸不是为自己，而是给糖异生付 ATP；用不完的乙酰 CoA 做成酮体 gluconeogenesis · ketone bodies

In fasting the liver oxidises fatty acids largely to pay for something else: the ATP of gluconeogenesis. The acetyl-CoA it cannot use is turned into ketone bodies for the brain, which cannot use fatty acids.

接「糖异生」和「酮体」。

⑤ 奇数碳末端的丙酰 CoA 是脂肪酸里唯一能到达葡萄糖的部分，但这一步要 B₁₂ vitamin B12

An odd-chain fatty acid ends as propionyl-CoA, the only part of a fatty acid that can reach glucose. It needs vitamin B12 to become succinyl-CoA, so in B12 deficiency methylmalonic acid accumulates in blood and urine.

接「维生素」。

⑥ 超长链只能在过氧化物酶体里缩短；X-ALD 坏的是转运蛋白 ABCD1，不是酶，底物根本进不去 peroxisome · X-ALD

Very long chains can only be shortened in the peroxisome, so what fails in X-linked adrenoleukodystrophy, an inherited disease, is not an enzyme but the transporter ABCD1: the chains never get in, and they damage brain white matter and the adrenal glands.

病名和转运蛋白名知道就行。

⑦ 天然双键都是顺式，而 β-氧化只接受反式，所以不饱和脂肪酸多需要一个异构酶 unsaturated fatty acids

Natural double bonds are cis, but β-oxidation can only handle trans, so unsaturated fatty acids need one extra enzyme, enoyl-CoA isomerase, before the cycle continues.

不饱和 → 多一个异构酶。

⑧ 乙醛酸循环做动物做不到的事：把脂肪变成糖；植物和酵母的 β-氧化全在过氧化物酶体里 plants and yeast

The glyoxylate cycle does what no animal can: it turns the acetyl-CoA of a germinating oil seed into sugar. It sits in the peroxisome, and in plants and yeast all β-oxidation takes place there too.

种子能把油变成糖。

⑨ 书外：ω 是希腊字母的最后一个，所以 ω-氧化是从链的另一头、甲基端下手 ⭐ Hodek ω-oxidation

It is called ω-oxidation because omega is the last Greek letter: cytochrome P450 enzymes in the endoplasmic reticulum oxidise the methyl end, not the carboxyl end. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · P450 既代谢药物也代谢脂肪酸。

⑩ 书外：低血糖通常伴着酮体升高，低血糖而没有酮体就是脂肪酸氧化缺陷 carnitine · MCAD deficiency

Fasting hypoglycaemia normally comes with ketone bodies, so hypoglycaemia without ketone bodies points to a fatty-acid oxidation defect: carnitine deficiency or MCAD deficiency. The treatment is to avoid fasting. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

低血糖 ＋ 无酮体 ＝ 脂肪酸氧化缺陷。

⑪ 书外：每次掉两个碳，是 1904 年 Knoop 喂狗证明的，比相关的酶被发现早几十年 Knoop 1904

The two-carbon step was known long before any enzyme was: in 1904 Franz Knoop, a German biochemist, fed dogs fatty acids with a benzene ring on the end, and even chains gave phenylacetic acid, odd chains benzoic acid. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

偶数 → 苯乙酸，奇数 → 苯甲酸。

陷阱问法

  • "Is lipid metabolism the same as β-oxidation?" — No, β-oxidation is one breakdown route within it. · 不是，只是分解的一条路。
  • "Can the brain use fatty acids?" — Almost not; it uses glucose, and ketone bodies in prolonged fasting. · 几乎不能。
  • "Can fat become glucose?" — Only the glycerol and the propionyl-CoA from odd chains can. · 只有甘油和奇数碳末端。
  • "Does β-oxidation need oxygen?" — Not directly, but its NADH and FADH₂ are reoxidised only by the respiratory chain. · 不直接用，没氧就停。

定义

  • lipase — an enzyme that hydrolyses fatty acids off glycerol · 脂肪酶
  • lipoprotein — a particle of lipid and protein that carries lipids in blood · 脂蛋白
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • methylmalonic acid — the compound that accumulates when the B12-dependent step fails · 甲基丙二酸
  • peroxisome — an organelle that shortens very-long-chain fatty acids · 过氧化物酶体
  • ABCD1 — the peroxisomal membrane transporter for very-long-chain fatty acids · ABCD1 转运蛋白
  • enoyl-CoA isomerase — the enzyme that moves a cis double bond to the trans position · 烯酰 CoA 异构酶
  • glyoxylate cycle — a variant of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） in plants and bacteria that allows fat to become sugar · 乙醛酸循环
  • cytochrome P450 — a family of haem enzymes that insert an oxygen atom into molecules · 细胞色素 P450
  • carnitine — the molecule that carries long-chain acyl groups into the mitochondrion · 肉碱
  • MCAD — medium-chain acyl-CoA dehydrogenase · 中链酰基 CoA 脱氢酶
  • hypoglycaemia — abnormally low blood glucose · 低血糖
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetyl-CoA — an acetyl group carried on coenzyme A, the common two-carbon product of fuel breakdown · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • activation — converting a molecule into a reactive form, here a fatty acid into its coenzyme A thioester · 活化
  • acyl-CoA — a fatty acid joined to coenzyme A through a thioester bond · 脂酰辅酶 A
  • acyl-CoA dehydrogenase — the FAD enzyme of the first oxidation step of the cycle · 脂酰 CoA 脱氢酶
  • acyl-CoA synthetase — the enzyme that activates a free fatty acid to its coenzyme A thioester · 脂酰 CoA 合成酶
  • adrenal gland — the hormone-producing gland sitting on top of the kidney · 肾上腺
  • albumin — the main blood protein; it carries free fatty acids through the plasma · 白蛋白
  • β-oxidation — the four-step cycle that shortens a fatty acyl chain by two carbons each round · β-氧化（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）
  • benzene ring — a flat six-carbon aromatic ring · 苯环
  • benzoic acid — benzene carrying one carboxyl group · 苯甲酸
  • carboxyl group — the -COOH group that makes a fatty acid an acid · 羧基
  • carnitine deficiency — an inherited lack of carnitine, so long-chain fatty acids cannot enter mitochondria · 肉碱缺乏
  • catalase — the enzyme that breaks hydrogen peroxide down to water and oxygen · 过氧化氢酶
  • chain length — the number of carbon atoms in a fatty acid chain · 链长
  • cholesterol — a four-ring sterol of animal membranes and the precursor of steroid hormones · 胆固醇
  • cis — a double bond with the two chain segments on the same side · 顺式
  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule an enzyme needs, which carries a group or electrons · 辅酶
  • coenzyme A — the thiol carrier that binds acyl groups as thioesters · 辅酶 A
  • CPT-I — carnitine palmitoyltransferase（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） I, the outer-membrane enzyme that puts the acyl group onto carnitine · 肉碱棕榈酰转移酶 I
  • CPT-II — carnitine palmitoyltransferase II, which returns the acyl group to coenzyme A inside · 肉碱棕榈酰转移酶 II
  • double bond — two shared electron pairs between two carbon atoms, which stiffens the chain · 双键
  • endoplasmic reticulum — the membrane network where lipids are made and modified · 内质网
  • FADH₂ — the reduced form of FAD, which hands its electrons to the respiratory chain · 还原型 FAD
  • fat — triacylglycerol, the neutral lipid in which animals store energy · 脂肪
  • fatty acid — a long hydrocarbon chain ending in a carboxyl group · 脂肪酸
  • Franz Knoop — the German biochemist who showed in 1904 that fatty acids are shortened two carbons at a time · 克努普
  • glucagon — the pancreatic hormone of fasting, which raises blood glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the main fuel circulating in blood · 葡萄糖
  • glycerol — the three-carbon alcohol backbone of triacylglycerol · 甘油
  • glycogen — the branched glucose polymer stored in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • high-energy bond — a bond whose hydrolysis releases a large amount of free energy · 高能键
  • HMG-CoA reductase — the rate-limiting enzyme of cholesterol synthesis · HMG-CoA 还原酶
  • hormone-sensitive lipase — the adipose enzyme that releases fatty acids from stored fat in fasting · 激素敏感性脂肪酶
  • hydration — addition of a water molecule across a double bond · 加水
  • hydrogen peroxide — H₂O₂, made by the peroxisomal oxidase and destroyed by catalase · 过氧化氢
  • inherited disease — a disease caused by a mutation passed on from a parent · 遗传病
  • insulin — the pancreatic hormone of the fed state, which lowers blood glucose · 胰岛素
  • intermediate — a compound made in one step of a pathway and used up by the next · 中间物
  • isomerase — an enzyme that rearranges the atoms within one molecule · 异构酶
  • lipid — a water-insoluble biological molecule such as a fat, a phospholipid or a sterol · 脂质
  • lipid metabolism — breakdown, synthesis, cholesterol and lipoprotein transport taken together · 脂代谢
  • lipolysis — hydrolysis of stored triacylglycerol into fatty acids and glycerol · 脂解
  • malonyl-CoA — the three-carbon donor of fatty acid synthesis; it inhibits CPT-I · 丙二酰 CoA
  • MCAD deficiency — an inherited lack of medium-chain acyl-CoA dehydrogenase · MCAD 缺乏症
  • metabolism — the whole set of chemical reactions running in a cell · 代谢
  • methyl end — the end of a fatty acid opposite the carboxyl group, also called the ω end · 甲基端
  • mitochondrial matrix — the innermost compartment of the mitochondrion · 线粒体基质
  • mitochondrion — the double-membraned organelle where oxidative energy metabolism takes place · 线粒体
  • mutase — an enzyme that moves a group from one position to another within a molecule · 变位酶
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, which carries electrons to the respiratory chain · 还原型 NAD
  • ω-oxidation — oxidation of a fatty acid at its methyl end by cytochrome P450 enzymes · ω-氧化
  • odd-chain fatty acid — a fatty acid with an odd number of carbon atoms · 奇数碳脂肪酸
  • organelle — a membrane-bounded compartment inside a eukaryotic cell · 细胞器
  • oxidation — a reaction in which a molecule loses electrons, often as hydrogen atoms · 氧化
  • pathway — a fixed sequence of enzyme-catalysed reactions · 代谢途径
  • phenylacetic acid — benzene carrying a two-carbon carboxylic acid side chain · 苯乙酸
  • propionyl-CoA — the three-carbon acyl-CoA left by an odd chain · 丙酰 CoA
  • pyrophosphate — PPᵢ, two phosphates joined; its hydrolysis pulls a reaction forward · 焦磷酸
  • rate-limiting step — the slowest step of a pathway, which sets the speed of the whole path · 限速步
  • reduction — a reaction in which a molecule gains electrons or hydrogen · 还原
  • respiratory chain — the mitochondrial carriers that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）
  • saturated fatty acid — a fatty acid with no carbon-carbon double bonds · 饱和脂肪酸
  • succinyl-CoA — a four-carbon intermediate of the citric acid cycle · 琥珀酰 CoA
  • thioester — the high-energy bond between an acyl group and a thiol · 硫酯
  • thiolase — the enzyme that cuts the chain with coenzyme A and releases acetyl-CoA · 硫解酶
  • thiolysis — cleaving a bond with a thiol, here the -SH of coenzyme A · 硫解
  • trans — a double bond with the two chain segments on opposite sides · 反式
  • translocase — the inner-membrane carrier that exchanges acylcarnitine for free carnitine · 转位酶
  • transporter — a membrane protein that carries one particular substance across a membrane · 转运蛋白
  • triacylglycerol — glycerol esterified with three fatty acids; the storage form of fat · 甘油三酯（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat）
  • unsaturated fatty acid — a fatty acid containing one or more carbon-carbon double bonds · 不饱和脂肪酸
  • very-long-chain fatty acid — a fatty acid longer than about twenty-two carbons · 超长链脂肪酸
  • vitamin B12 — cobalamin, the cobalt vitamin needed by methylmalonyl-CoA mutase · 维生素 B₁₂
  • white matter — the myelin-rich part of the brain made of nerve fibres · 脑白质
  • X-linked adrenoleukodystrophy — an inherited failure of the peroxisomal transporter ABCD1 · X 连锁肾上腺脑白质营养不良

Fatty acid synthesis

⚠ 她点名模糊的那一条 · 四步，第一步就是把碳运出线粒体

  1. 什么时候跑 — 吃饱、胰岛素高的时候糖多到烧不完的时候，多余的碳要存成脂肪。胰岛素开这条路，胰高血糖素关它。⚠ 跟 β-氧化严格反相：不可能同时跑，因为丙二酰-CoA 会把氧化的门锁上。
  2. Citrate shuttle — 把碳运出线粒体acetyl-CoA 在线粒体里，合成在胞质，它过不了内膜。于是跟草酰乙酸缩合成柠檬酸坐转运体出来，胞质里 ATP-citrate lyase 花一个 ATP 再把它劈回 acetyl-CoA。⚠ 草酰乙酸回程走「苹果酸 → malic enzyme → 丙酮酸」，顺手造出一部分 NADPH，其余的从磷酸戊糖途径来。
  3. Acetyl-CoA carboxylation — 承诺步骤acetyl-CoA ＋ HCO₃⁻ ＋ ATP →[acetyl-CoA carboxylase|ABC：ATP · biotin · CO₂] malonyl-CoA限速步，调控全压在这个酶上：柠檬酸开它 · 棕榈酰-CoA 关它 · AMPK 磷酸化关它 · 胰岛素开它。⚠ 而且 malonyl-CoA 反手关掉 CPT-1——合成一开，氧化的门就锁上。
  4. Fatty acid synthase complex — 真正长链子的地方动物的 FAS 是一条多功能肽链，摆臂是 ACP（那条臂来自泛酸）。每轮四步：缩合（同时放掉一个 CO₂）→ 还原（NADPH）→ 脱水 → 再还原（NADPH），链子长两个碳。⚠ 必考的怪事：第 2 步加上的 CO₂ 在这里又放掉了，根本没进产物——它是用来买反应的，脱羧释放的能量推着缩合往前走，换来这一步不可逆。
  5. 后加工 — 加长和加双键FAS 只做到 C16 棕榈酸，转七轮之后硫酯酶把它切下来。更长的链和双键都在内质网另外加。⚠ 人做不出 C9 以后的双键，所以亚油酸和亚麻酸是必需脂肪酸。

Say this

Fatty acid synthesis happens in four stages. First, the carbon has to leave the mitochondrion. Acetyl-CoA cannot cross the inner membrane, so it comes out as citrate, and ATP-citrate lyase splits it back in the cytosol. The oxaloacetate returns as pyruvate, and that return trip makes some of the NADPH. Second is the committed step: acetyl-CoA carboxylase uses biotin and one ATP to make malonyl-CoA. Third is the fatty acid synthase complex. Each round is condensation, reduction, dehydration, reduction, it spends two NADPH, and the chain grows by two carbons. Seven rounds give palmitate. Fourth, anything longer or unsaturated is added afterwards in the ER. Overall: eight acetyl-CoA, seven ATP and fourteen NADPH per palmitate.

约 45 秒 · 总账 8 / 7 / 14 一定要说出来

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hodek、Martínek、Lipovová

① 脂肪酸合成是把吃不完的葡萄糖存起来的办法，所以只在饭后胰岛素高时跑 fed state · insulin

Fatty acid synthesis is how surplus glucose gets stored, so it runs after a meal, when insulin is high, mainly in the liver and adipose tissue; glucagon inhibits it.

胰岛素促进，胰高血糖素抑制。

② 乙酰 CoA 过不了线粒体内膜，所以它以柠檬酸的形式出来，再被劈回去 citrate shuttle

Acetyl-CoA cannot cross the mitochondrial inner membrane, so it leaves as citrate, and ATP-citrate lyase splits citrate back into acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate in the cytosol, where fatty acids are made.

这就是柠檬酸穿梭。

③ 全路的调控都压在乙酰 CoA 羧化酶上：它催化定向步，也是限速步 acetyl-CoA carboxylase · committed step

Acetyl-CoA carboxylase carries the regulation of the whole pathway, because it catalyses the committed and rate-limiting step: in the cytosol it uses biotin and ATP to turn acetyl-CoA into malonyl-CoA.

全路的调控都在这个酶上。

④ 一条逻辑记全：原料侧开，产物侧和能量不足关 regulation of ACC

Substrate activates, product and low energy stop it: citrate — which matters here a second time — and insulin activate acetyl-CoA carboxylase, while palmitoyl-CoA, glucagon and AMPK inhibit it, the last two by phosphorylation.

原料多 → 激活；产物多、能量少 → 抑制。

⑤ 丙二酰 CoA 顺手关掉 CPT-I，这就是防止合成和氧化同时空转的开关 malonyl-CoA inhibits CPT-I

The same malonyl-CoA is also the switch that prevents a futile cycle: it inhibits CPT-I, the enzyme that brings fatty acids into mitochondria, so a cell making fatty acids is not oxidising them.

接「脂肪酸氧化」。

⑥ 合成不是 β-氧化倒着跑，四处都不同：细胞质、ACP、NADPH、D 型 synthesis vs β-oxidation

Synthesis is not β-oxidation (also called fatty acid oxidation) run backwards, and four things differ: it is in the cytosol not the matrix, the chain is held on ACP not CoA, it uses NADPH not NAD⁺ and FAD, and its intermediates are D not L isomers.

地点 · 载体 · 辅酶 · 构型。

⑦ 肝和脂肪组织的磷酸戊糖途径特别活跃，就是为了给这条路供 NADPH NADPH from the pentose phosphate pathway

Liver and adipose tissue run the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway) actively for one reason: it supplies most of the NADPH this pathway needs. The rest comes from malic enzyme.

接「磷酸戊糖途径（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway）」。

⑧ 人没有能在第 9 碳以外引入双键的酶，所以亚油酸和亚麻酸必需；它们做出前列腺素 essential fatty acids · eicosanoids

Linoleic and linolenic acid are essential because humans cannot place double bonds beyond carbon nine. From them come eicosanoids such as prostaglandins, whose synthesis aspirin, a painkiller, blocks by inhibiting cyclooxygenase.

接「激素」。

⑨ 动物把全部活性装在一条肽链上（I 型），植物和细菌是一堆独立的酶（II 型） type I vs type II synthase

Animals carry all the activities on one multifunctional polypeptide, a type I synthase; plants and bacteria use type II, a set of separate enzymes, in plants located in the chloroplast.

动物一条肽链，植物细菌多个酶。

⑩ 书外：正因为细菌这套和人的不一样，它才能做药靶——异烟肼断的是分枝菌酸 isoniazid · mycolic acids

Because the bacterial system is unlike ours, it can be a drug target: isoniazid, a tuberculosis drug, blocks fatty-acid synthesis in Mycobacterium tuberculosis, so it cannot make the mycolic acids of its cell wall. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 细菌与人的酶不同 → 可以做药。

⑪ 书外：去饱和酶和 P450 共用同一个电子供体细胞色素 b5；双键是后加的 ⭐ Hodek · Martínek cytochrome b5

Cytochrome b5 is the link here: the same protein that interacts with cytochrome P450 gives electrons to the desaturases that add double bonds afterwards, in the endoplasmic reticulum. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · Martínek 做过 P450 与 b5 的对接。

⑫ 书外：链长由释放链的硫酯酶决定，所以换掉它就能定制链长 ⭐ Lipovová metabolic engineering

Chain length is decided by the thioesterase that releases the chain, so swapping it changes the product: plant thioesterases in E. coli give medium-chain fatty acids for biofuels. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 通往她的蛋白质工程。

陷阱问法

  • "Is it β-oxidation（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation） in reverse?" — No: different compartment, carrier, coenzyme and stereochemistry. · 不是倒着跑。
  • "What is the rate-limiting enzyme?" — Acetyl-CoA carboxylase, not fatty acid synthase. · 是 ACC。
  • "Can amino acids become fat?" — Yes, those that give acetyl-CoA. · 能，经乙酰 CoA。
  • "Why don't synthesis and oxidation run together?" — Malonyl-CoA inhibits CPT-I. · 丙二酰 CoA 抑制 CPT-I。

定义

  • ATP-citrate lyase — the cytosolic enzyme that splits citrate into acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · ATP-柠檬酸裂解酶
  • committed step — the first irreversible step unique to a pathway · 关键步
  • biotin — the vitamin that carries CO₂ in carboxylases · 生物素
  • malonyl-CoA — the three-carbon donor for chain growth · 丙二酰 CoA
  • AMPK — AMP-activated protein kinase, activated when ATP is low · AMP 激活的蛋白激酶
  • CPT-I — carnitine palmitoyltransferase（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） I, which moves fatty acids into mitochondria · 肉碱棕榈酰转移酶 I
  • ACP — acyl carrier protein, which holds the growing chain · 酰基载体蛋白
  • malic enzyme — converts malate to pyruvate and makes NADPH · 苹果酸酶
  • eicosanoids — signalling molecules made from twenty-carbon fatty acids · 二十碳类
  • cyclooxygenase — the enzyme that starts prostaglandin synthesis · 环氧合酶
  • mycolic acids — very long fatty acids of the mycobacterial cell wall · 分枝菌酸
  • desaturase — an enzyme that introduces a double bond into a fatty acid · 去饱和酶
  • thioesterase — the enzyme that releases the finished fatty acid · 硫酯酶

  • acetyl-CoA — an acetyl group carried on coenzyme A, the two-carbon unit that builds the chain · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • acetyl-CoA carboxylase — the biotin enzyme that makes malonyl-CoA from acetyl-CoA · 乙酰 CoA 羧化酶
  • adipose tissue — the fat-storing tissue of the body · 脂肪组织
  • aspirin — a painkiller that inhibits cyclooxygenase · 阿司匹林
  • biofuel — fuel made from biological material rather than from crude oil · 生物燃料

  • cell wall — the rigid layer outside a bacterial cell membrane · 细胞壁
  • chloroplast — the plant organelle in which photosynthesis takes place · 叶绿体

  • fat — triacylglycerol（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat）, the neutral lipid in which surplus carbon is stored · 脂肪
  • fatty acid — a long hydrocarbon chain ending in a carboxyl group · 脂肪酸
  • fatty acid synthase — the multi-activity enzyme that builds palmitate from acetyl-CoA and malonyl-CoA · 脂肪酸合酶
  • futile cycle — two opposite pathways running at the same time and wasting ATP · 无效循环
  • glucagon — the pancreatic hormone of fasting, which raises blood glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the main fuel circulating in blood · 葡萄糖
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • inhibitor — a molecule that lowers the rate of an enzyme reaction · 抑制剂
  • inner mitochondrial membrane — the impermeable membrane that holds the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain） · 线粒体内膜
  • insulin — the pancreatic hormone of the fed state, which lowers blood glucose · 胰岛素
  • irreversible — going in one direction only under the conditions inside the cell · 不可逆
  • isoniazid — a tuberculosis drug that blocks mycolic acid synthesis · 异烟肼
  • linoleic acid — an eighteen-carbon fatty acid with two double bonds; essential for humans · 亚油酸
  • linolenic acid — an eighteen-carbon fatty acid with three double bonds; essential for humans · 亚麻酸
  • membrane — a lipid bilayer with embedded proteins that separates two compartments · 膜
  • metabolic engineering — redesigning the pathways of a cell so that it makes a chosen product · 代谢工程
  • mitochondrion — the double-membraned organelle where oxidative energy metabolism takes place · 线粒体

  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid that accepts acetyl groups in the citric acid cycle · 草酰乙酸

  • pentose phosphate pathway — the cytosolic route that oxidises glucose-6-phosphate to make NADPH and ribose · 磷酸戊糖途径
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group to a protein, which usually changes its activity · 磷酸化
  • polypeptide — a single chain of amino acids joined by peptide bonds（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 多肽
  • product — the molecule an enzyme reaction produces · 产物
  • prostaglandin — an eicosanoid with a five-membered ring that acts locally, for example in inflammation · 前列腺素
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon acid that is the end product of glycolysis（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway） · 丙酮酸
  • rate-limiting step — the slowest step of a pathway, which sets the speed of the whole path · 限速步
  • reduction — a reaction in which a molecule gains electrons or hydrogen · 还原
  • regulation — control of how fast a pathway runs · 调控
  • stereochemistry — the three-dimensional arrangement of the atoms in a molecule · 立体化学
  • substrate — the molecule an enzyme acts on, which binds in its active site · 底物
  • tuberculosis — the infectious lung disease caused by Mycobacterium tuberculosis · 结核
  • type I fatty acid synthase — the animal and fungal form, with all activities on one large polypeptide · I 型脂肪酸合酶
  • type II fatty acid synthase — the bacterial and plant form, made of separate individual enzymes · II 型脂肪酸合酶

Triacylglycerol & phospholipid synthesis

⚠ 脂质合成的第二块 · 造好的脂肪酸接下来去哪儿 —— 一个岔路口决定储能还是造膜

  1. 什么时候跑 — 吃饱之后 胰岛素高的时候：糖多、能量够，多余的碳要存起来。 脂肪组织存成脂肪，肝把造好的脂肪打包成 VLDL 送出去。 ⚠ 跟 β-氧化正好反相：那条是空腹跑的，这条是饱食跑的。
  2. 两个原料先各自就位 脂肪酸要先活化成 acyl-CoA（跟氧化前一样，先做硫酯）。 骨架是 甘油-3-磷酸，它有两个来源：糖酵解的 DHAP 还原而来（大多数组织只能这样）， 或者甘油直接磷酸化（⚠ 只有肝和肾有甘油激酶，脂肪组织没有）。
  3. 接上两条脂肪酸 → 磷脂酸 甘油-3-磷酸的 C1 和 C2 各接一条脂肪酸，得到 磷脂酸（phosphatidic acid）。 ⚠ 这是整条路的岔路口，两边从这里分开。
  4. 岔路 A — 去掉磷酸，接第三条 → 甘油三酯（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat） 磷酸酶把 C3 上的磷酸切掉得到二酰甘油，再接第三条脂肪酸 → 三酰甘油。 完全非极性，所以能挤在一起成油滴，这就是为什么它适合储能。
  5. 岔路 B — 留着磷酸，接极性头 → 磷脂 磷酸上再接一个极性头基：胆碱、乙醇胺、丝氨酸、肌醇。 得到甘油磷脂，两亲性——一头亲水一头疏水，所以它才能排成双层膜。 ⚠ 储能要非极性，造膜要两亲性，同一个前体分两条路，就是为了这个差别。
  6. 另一条系统 — 鞘脂不走甘油 鞘脂的骨架是鞘氨醇，不是甘油，而且脂肪酸是用酰胺键挂上去的，不是酯键。 C1 上挂磷酸胆碱就是鞘磷脂，挂糖就是糖鞘脂（脑苷脂、神经节苷脂）。

Say this

Both storage fat and membrane phospholipids are built after a meal, when insulin is high, and they share the same beginning. The backbone is glycerol-3-phosphate, and two fatty acids are esterified onto it to give phosphatidic acid. That is the branch point. If the phosphate is removed and a third fatty acid added, the product is a triacylglycerol (also called triglyceride, neutral fat), which is completely non-polar and therefore good for storage. If the phosphate is kept and a polar head group such as choline or ethanolamine is attached, the product is a glycerophospholipid, which is amphipathic and therefore forms bilayers. Sphingolipids are a separate system: the backbone is sphingosine rather than glycerol, and the fatty acid is attached through an amide bond.

约 45 秒 · 「磷脂酸是岔路口」那一句是骨架

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① 游离脂肪酸本身就是去污剂，会溶解膜，所以必须中和成甘油三酯才能储存 why triacylglycerol

A free fatty acid is a detergent and would dissolve membranes, so cells neutralise it as triacylglycerol, which is uncharged and packs safely into lipid droplets.

中性 → 可以安全储存。

② 脂肪不溶于水，所以消化要先乳化再水解：胆汁酸乳化，胰脂肪酶水解，奥利司他抑制它 pancreatic lipase · orlistat

Dietary fat is not water-soluble, so bile acids must emulsify it before pancreatic lipase, the intestinal enzyme, can hydrolyse it. Orlistat, a weight-loss drug, inhibits that lipase.

接「胆固醇 → 胆汁酸」。

③ 看是哪种颗粒就知道脂肪的来源：乳糜微粒来自食物，VLDL 是肝自己造的 chylomicrons · VLDL

The particle tells you where the fat came from: chylomicrons are made by the intestinal cells from dietary fat, and VLDL by the liver from triacylglycerol it makes itself.

乳糜微粒 ＝ 食物来的，VLDL ＝ 肝造的。

④ 甘油三酯整个进不了细胞，必须先在毛细血管壁上被脂蛋白脂肪酶水解掉 lipoprotein lipase

Triacylglycerol cannot enter a cell as it is, so lipoprotein lipase on the capillary wall of muscle and adipose tissue hydrolyses it first and the fatty acids go in; insulin raises this enzyme in adipose tissue.

接「脂蛋白」。

⑤ 同一块脂肪，两个方向由相反的激素管：胰高血糖素和肾上腺素开，胰岛素关 lipolysis

The opposite direction is run by the opposite hormones: in fasting, adipose triglyceride lipase and hormone-sensitive lipase break the stored fat down, activated by glucagon and adrenaline and inhibited by insulin.

接「脂肪酸氧化」。

⑥ 书外：脂肪组织不只是仓库，它还是内分泌器官——瘦素向下丘脑报告存了多少脂肪 leptin

Adipose tissue is also an endocrine organ: leptin, the hormone it secretes, tells the hypothalamus how much fat is stored, and reduces appetite. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 脂肪组织也是内分泌器官。

⑦ UCP1 让质子绕过 ATP 合酶漏回基质，于是脂肪氧化的能量变成热而不是 ATP brown fat · UCP1

UCP1 (thermogenin), the protein of brown adipose tissue in newborns and hibernating animals, lets protons return to the matrix without ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase), so the energy of fat oxidation leaves as heat.

接「呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）」的解偶联。

⑧ 酒精性脂肪肝只经过一个中间量：NADH 升高，它同时促进合成、抑制氧化 ⭐ Hodek alcoholic fatty liver

Alcohol makes a fatty liver through one intermediate: alcohol dehydrogenase raises NADH in the liver, and a high NADH both favours triacylglycerol synthesis and inhibits fatty acid oxidation (also called β-oxidation).

乙醇代谢在 Hodek 的考纲里。

⑨ 膜磷脂也是信号的储备：磷脂酶 C 一刀把 PIP₂ 切出两个第二信使，走两个方向 phospholipase C

One cut gives two messengers: phospholipase C, switched on after a hormone binds its receptor, cleaves PIP₂ into IP₃, which releases calcium, and diacylglycerol, which activates protein kinase C.

接「激素信号」。

⑩ 书外：表面活性物质降低肺泡表面张力，肺泡才不会塌；早产儿还没造出来 lung surfactant

Surfactant lowers surface tension so the alveoli do not collapse; its main component is dipalmitoylphosphatidylcholine (also called lecithin), a phospholipid, and premature babies who lack it develop respiratory distress syndrome. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

⑪ 书外：溶酶体少一个酶，它的底物就在哪里堆起来——Tay–Sachs 是 GM2 堆在神经细胞 ⭐ Lipovová Tay–Sachs · hexosaminidase A

Sphingolipids are degraded in lysosomes, and when one enzyme is missing its substrate accumulates: Tay–Sachs disease, an inherited lysosomal disease, lacks β-hexosaminidase A, so the GM2 ganglioside builds up in nerve cells. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 氨基己糖苷酶是 Lipovová 研究的酶。

⑫ 书外：同一个酯键反应两种用途——洗涤剂里水解油污，工业上转酯化做生物柴油 ⭐ Lipovová industrial lipases · biodiesel

One ester reaction, two uses: lipases in laundry detergents hydrolyse fat stains, and biodiesel is made by transesterification of triacylglycerol with methanol. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 通往她的应用酶学。

⑬ 叶绿体类囊体膜主要不是磷脂而是糖脂；油料种子把甘油三酯存在油体里 plants

Plants differ twice: oil seeds store triacylglycerol in oil bodies, and chloroplast thylakoid membranes are made mainly of galactolipids, not phospholipids.

接「光合作用」。

陷阱问法

  • "Are fat and lipid the same?" — No, fat (triacylglycerol) is one class of lipid. · 脂肪只是脂质的一类。
  • "Is cholesterol a phospholipid?" — No, it is a sterol and contains no glycerol. · 不是，没有甘油。
  • "Why do phospholipids form bilayers but triacylglycerol forms droplets?" — Only phospholipids have a polar head group. · 只有磷脂有极性头。

定义

  • detergent — a molecule with a polar head and a hydrophobic tail that dissolves membranes · 去污剂
  • pancreatic lipase — the intestinal enzyme that digests dietary triacylglycerol · 胰脂肪酶
  • orlistat — a drug that inhibits pancreatic lipase · 奥利司他
  • chylomicron — the lipoprotein that carries dietary fat from the intestine · 乳糜微粒
  • VLDL — very-low-density lipoprotein, carrying triacylglycerol made in the liver · 极低密度脂蛋白
  • lipoprotein lipase — the capillary enzyme that hydrolyses triacylglycerol in lipoproteins · 脂蛋白脂肪酶
  • lipolysis — hydrolysis of stored triacylglycerol to fatty acids and glycerol · 脂解
  • leptin — a hormone from adipose tissue that reduces appetite · 瘦素
  • UCP1 / thermogenin — the proton channel of brown fat that produces heat · 解偶联蛋白 1
  • alcohol dehydrogenase — the liver enzyme that oxidises ethanol and produces NADH · 乙醇脱氢酶
  • IP₃ / diacylglycerol — the two second messengers made from PIP₂ · 三磷酸肌醇／二酰甘油
  • lung surfactant — the phospholipid layer that stops the lung alveoli collapsing · 肺表面活性物质
  • ganglioside — a sphingolipid with a sugar chain containing sialic acid · 神经节苷脂
  • transesterification — exchanging the alcohol part of an ester · 转酯化
  • galactolipid — a lipid whose polar head is galactose · 半乳糖脂
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acyl-CoA — a fatty acid joined to coenzyme A; the activated form used for esterification · 脂酰辅酶 A
  • adipose tissue — the fat-storing tissue, which is also an endocrine organ · 脂肪组织
  • adipose triglyceride lipase — the enzyme that takes the first fatty acid off stored triacylglycerol · 脂肪甘油三酯脂肪酶
  • adrenaline — the fast stress hormone that mobilises stored fuel · 肾上腺素
  • alveolus — one of the small air sacs of the lung where gas exchange happens · 肺泡
  • amide bond（= peptide bond = amide bond） — the bond formed between a carboxyl group and an amino group · 酰胺键
  • amphipathic — having both a water-loving and a water-repelling part in one molecule · 两亲性
  • ATP synthase — the inner-membrane enzyme that makes ATP from the proton gradient · ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase）
  • β-hexosaminidase A — the lysosomal enzyme that is missing in Tay-Sachs disease · β-己糖胺酶 A
  • bile — the liver fluid, containing bile acids, that is released into the intestine · 胆汁
  • bile acid — a cholesterol derivative secreted in bile that emulsifies dietary fat · 胆汁酸
  • biodiesel — fuel made by transesterifying fats or oils with methanol · 生物柴油
  • brown adipose tissue — fat tissue rich in mitochondria and UCP1 that produces heat · 棕色脂肪组织
  • calcium — the ion Ca²⁺, used inside cells as a signal · 钙离子
  • cholesterol — a four-ring sterol of animal membranes; it contains no glycerol · 胆固醇
  • choline — a small nitrogen-containing alcohol used as a phospholipid head group · 胆碱
  • DHAP — dihydroxyacetone phosphate, a glycolytic intermediate reduced to glycerol-3-phosphate · 磷酸二羟丙酮
  • digestion — breaking food down into molecules small enough to be absorbed · 消化
  • dipalmitoylphosphatidylcholine（= phosphatidylcholine = lecithin） — the main phospholipid of lung surfactant, a phosphatidylcholine with two palmitates · 二棕榈酰磷脂酰胆碱
  • emulsification — breaking fat into fine droplets dispersed through water · 乳化
  • endocrine organ — an organ that secretes hormones into the blood · 内分泌器官
  • ester — the compound formed when an acid reacts with an alcohol and water is lost · 酯
  • ester bond — the bond formed between an acid and an alcohol with loss of water · 酯键
  • esterification — joining an acid to an alcohol to form an ester · 酯化
  • ethanolamine — a small amino alcohol used as a phospholipid head group · 乙醇胺
  • fat — triacylglycerol, the neutral lipid in which animals store energy · 脂肪
  • fatty acid — a long hydrocarbon chain ending in a carboxyl group · 脂肪酸

  • free fatty acid — a fatty acid esterified to nothing, carried through blood on albumin · 游离脂肪酸
  • glucagon — the pancreatic hormone of fasting, which raises blood glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glycerol-3-phosphate — the phosphorylated three-carbon backbone on which glycerolipids are built · 甘油-3-磷酸
  • glycerol kinase — the enzyme that phosphorylates glycerol; liver and kidney have it, adipose tissue does not · 甘油激酶
  • glycerophospholipid — a lipid of glycerol, two fatty acids, a phosphate and a polar head group · 甘油磷脂
  • GM2 ganglioside — the ganglioside that accumulates in nerve cells in Tay-Sachs disease · GM2 神经节苷脂
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • hypothalamus — the brain region that controls appetite, temperature and hormone release · 下丘脑
  • insulin — the pancreatic hormone of the fed state, which lowers blood glucose · 胰岛素
  • lipid — a water-insoluble biological molecule such as a fat, a phospholipid or a sterol · 脂质
  • lipid bilayer — two sheets of phospholipid with the tails facing inwards; the basis of every membrane · 脂双层
  • lipid droplet — the cytoplasmic store of triacylglycerol inside a cell · 脂滴
  • lipoprotein — a particle of lipid and protein that carries lipids through the blood · 脂蛋白

  • methanol — the one-carbon alcohol, CH₃OH · 甲醇
  • mobilisation — releasing stored fuel into the blood so other tissues can use it · 动员
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺; ethanol metabolism raises it in the liver · 还原型 NAD
  • non-polar — having no separation of charge, so not mixing with water · 非极性
  • oil — a triacylglycerol that is liquid at room temperature, usually from a plant · 油
  • oil body — the triacylglycerol storage droplet of a plant seed · 油体
  • organelle — a membrane-bounded compartment inside a eukaryotic cell · 细胞器
  • phosphatidic acid — glycerol-3-phosphate carrying two fatty acids; the branch point of this pathway · 磷脂酸
  • phospholipase C — the enzyme that cleaves PIP₂ into IP₃ and diacylglycerol · 磷脂酶 C
  • PIP₂ — phosphatidylinositol 4,5-bisphosphate, the membrane lipid cut by phospholipase C · PIP₂
  • polar — having a partial separation of electric charge within the molecule · 极性
  • polar head group — the hydrophilic part of a phospholipid, such as choline or ethanolamine · 极性头基
  • protein kinase C — a kinase switched on by diacylglycerol together with calcium · 蛋白激酶 C
  • receptor — a protein that binds one particular signal molecule and passes the signal on · 受体
  • respiratory distress syndrome — breathing failure in premature babies who lack lung surfactant · 呼吸窘迫综合征
  • second messenger — a small molecule made inside the cell that relays a hormone's signal · 第二信使
  • sphingolipid — a lipid built on sphingosine instead of glycerol · 鞘脂
  • sphingomyelin — a sphingolipid carrying phosphocholine; abundant in myelin · 鞘磷脂
  • sphingosine — the long-chain amino alcohol that is the backbone of sphingolipids · 鞘氨醇
  • sterol — a lipid with four fused rings and a hydroxyl group · 固醇
  • surface tension — the force that makes a liquid surface contract · 表面张力
  • Tay-Sachs disease — an inherited lysosomal disease in which GM2 ganglioside builds up in nerve cells · 泰-萨克斯病
  • thylakoid — the internal membrane system of the chloroplast · 类囊体
  • triacylglycerol — glycerol esterified with three fatty acids; the storage form of fat · 甘油三酯

Cholesterol

⚠ 脂质合成的第三块 · 全部的碳都来自 acetyl-CoA

  1. 什么时候跑 — 细胞缺胆固醇的时候 膜要长、要做类固醇激素或胆汁酸的时候。吃进来的胆固醇多了，这条路就被关掉—— 典型的终产物反馈。⚠ 大部分在肝里进行，胞质和内质网。
  2. 三个 acetyl-CoA 拼成 HMG-CoA 2 acetyl-CoA →[thiolase] acetoacetyl-CoA →[HMG-CoA synthase|＋acetyl-CoA] HMG-CoA ⚠ 这两步跟酮体生成一模一样，差别只在地点：线粒体里做出来的去当酮体，胞质里做出来的去当胆固醇。 HMG-CoA 是这个岔路口。
  3. 限速步 — HMG-CoA 还原酶 HMG-CoA →[HMG-CoA reductase|2 NADPH] mevalonate 这是承诺步骤，也是全条路唯一值得记的酶。 关它的有三样：胆固醇本身（反馈）· AMPK 磷酸化（没能量别造）· 他汀。 ⚠ 他汀就是这个酶的竞争性抑制剂，全世界卖得最多的药之一。
  4. 甲羟戊酸 → 五碳单元 → 三十碳 → 环化 甲羟戊酸变成异戊烯基（5 碳），一段段接成 角鲨烯（30 碳），再环化成四环骨架， 最后修剪成胆固醇（27 碳）。⚠ 细节不用背，记住「五碳砖块 → 三十碳链 → 环化」这个形状就够。
  5. 往下还有三条路 胆固醇是类固醇激素、胆汁酸和维生素 D 的共同前体。 ⚠ 而在膜里它的角色是调节流动性——温度高时限制它、温度低时防止它冻住，两个方向的缓冲。

Say this

Every carbon of cholesterol comes from acetyl-CoA. Three of them are joined into HMG-CoA, and that intermediate is a branch point: made in the mitochondrion it goes to ketone bodies, made in the cytosol it goes to cholesterol. The committed and rate-limiting step is HMG-CoA reductase, which uses two NADPH to make mevalonate. It is inhibited by cholesterol itself, switched off by AMPK phosphorylation, and it is the target of the statins, which are competitive inhibitors of it. After that, five-carbon isoprene units are joined into squalene, which is cyclised into the four-ring sterol. Cholesterol then tunes membrane fluidity and is the precursor of steroid hormones, bile acids and vitamin D.

约 45 秒 · HMG-CoA 那个岔路口和他汀是两个必说

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Hudeček

① 每个动物细胞的膜都需要胆固醇，但血里多了就沉积成斑块——所以水平被严密调控 atherosclerosis

Every animal cell needs cholesterol for its membranes, but cholesterol in excess is deposited in artery walls as plaques, which is atherosclerosis. That is why its level is tightly regulated.

既必需，又不能多。

② 书外：方向决定好坏——LDL 从肝运到组织，HDL 把多余的运回肝 LDL · HDL

Direction is what makes one harmful and the other protective: LDL carries cholesterol from the liver to the tissues, HDL the excess back. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · LDL 高有害，HDL 高有益。

③ 书外：受体介导的内吞就是在 LDL 受体上弄清楚的；受体坏了就是家族性高胆固醇血症 LDL receptor

Receptor-mediated endocytosis was worked out on the LDL receptor: a defective receptor causes familial hypercholesterolaemia with early heart attacks, and Brown and Goldstein received the 1985 Nobel Prize for this. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 受体介导的内吞。

④ 他汀降血脂是绕了一圈的：抑制合成 → 肝细胞多造 LDL 受体 → 从血里多抓 LDL statins

Statins, the main cholesterol-lowering drugs, lower blood cholesterol indirectly, and that is the point: when HMG-CoA reductase is inhibited, liver cells make more LDL receptors and clear more LDL from the blood.

自己合成少 → 从血中摄取多。

⑤ 书外：他汀管合成，依折麦布管吸收——它堵的是肠道转运蛋白 NPC1L1 ezetimibe

Where statins act on synthesis, ezetimibe, another cholesterol-lowering drug, acts on absorption: it blocks the intestinal transporter NPC1L1. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 他汀管合成，依折麦布管吸收。

⑥ 固醇环打不开，胆固醇就烧不成 CO₂，所以只能转成胆汁酸排掉 bile acids

The sterol rings cannot be broken down to CO₂, so cholesterol cannot be burned and the only way out is conversion in the liver to bile acids, which in bile also emulsify dietary fat.

唯一的排出途径是胆汁。

⑦ 胆固醇的三个去向——胆汁酸、类固醇激素、活性维生素 D——第一步都是 P450 反应 ⭐ Hodek cytochrome P450

Cholesterol has three main fates and the first step of each is a cytochrome P450 reaction: to bile acids, to steroid hormones and to active vitamin D. P450 does more than metabolise drugs.

P450 不只代谢药物。

⑧ 真菌膜里是麦角固醇不是胆固醇，就这一点差别够做药：唑类抑制真菌那个 P450 ⭐ Hodek ergosterol · azoles

Fungi end the pathway at ergosterol, not cholesterol, and that difference is enough for a selective drug: azole antifungals such as fluconazole inhibit the fungal P450 enzyme that makes it. Plants make phytosterols such as sitosterol.

同一条路，不同物种，可以做药。

⑨ 胆固醇只是这条主干的一个分支：同样的异戊二烯单位还去做泛醌、多萜醇、Ras 的锚 isoprenoids

Cholesterol is only one branch: the isoprene units made before it also go to ubiquinone (also called coenzyme Q, CoQ), to dolichol, to the prenyl anchors of proteins such as Ras, and in plants to carotenoids.

胆固醇只是其中一个产物。

⑩ 胆固醇把膜流动性两头拉住：太流动时限制，太冷时阻止紧密堆积；测的物理量是转动快慢 ⭐ Hudeček fluorescence anisotropy

Cholesterol buffers fluidity both ways: it restrains a fluid membrane and stops a cold one packing tightly. Fluidity is read as the fluorescence anisotropy of the probe DPH — the less freely DPH rotates, the higher the anisotropy.

先说测的物理量：转动快慢。

⑪ 书外：合成路线先被阐明（Bloch 和 Lynen，1964 诺奖），第一个他汀是后来远藤章从青霉菌里找到的 discovery

The pathway itself was worked out by Konrad Bloch and Feodor Lynen, who received the 1964 Nobel Prize; the first statin, mevastatin, was found later by Akira Endo in the fungus Penicillium citrinum. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

陷阱问法

  • "Is cholesterol a fat?" — No, it is a sterol, an alcohol. · 不是脂肪，是固醇。
  • "Does all our cholesterol come from food?" — No, most is made in the body, mainly in the liver. · 大部分自己合成。
  • "Can cholesterol be used for energy?" — No, it is only excreted as bile acids. · 不能，只能排出。

定义

  • atherosclerosis — cholesterol-rich plaques in artery walls · 动脉粥样硬化
  • LDL / HDL — low- and high-density lipoproteins, the blood carriers of cholesterol · 低／高密度脂蛋白
  • LDL receptor — the cell-surface protein that binds LDL for uptake · LDL 受体
  • familial hypercholesterolaemia — an inherited defect of the LDL receptor · 家族性高胆固醇血症
  • statin — a drug that inhibits HMG-CoA reductase · 他汀
