  • ezetimibe — a drug that blocks intestinal cholesterol uptake · 依折麦布
  • bile acids — cholesterol derivatives that emulsify fat in the intestine · 胆汁酸
  • cytochrome P450 — a family of haem enzymes that insert an oxygen atom into molecules · 细胞色素 P450
  • ergosterol — the main sterol of fungi · 麦角固醇
  • isoprene unit — the five-carbon building unit of cholesterol and other isoprenoids · 异戊二烯单位
  • ubiquinone — the lipid electron carrier of the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain） · 泛醌（= ubiquinone = coenzyme Q = CoQ）
  • dolichol — a long isoprenoid that carries sugars for N-glycosylation · 多萜醇
  • fluorescence anisotropy — a measure of how freely a fluorescent molecule rotates · 荧光各向异性
  • DPH — diphenylhexatriene, a fluorescent probe that enters membranes · 二苯基己三烯
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • absorption — uptake of a substance from the gut into the body · 吸收
  • acetoacetyl-CoA — the four-carbon intermediate made by joining two acetyl-CoA · 乙酰乙酰 CoA
  • acetyl-CoA — an acetyl group carried on coenzyme A; every carbon of cholesterol comes from it · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • Akira Endo — the Japanese biochemist who found the first statin in a fungus · 远藤章
  • AMPK — AMP-activated protein kinase, switched on when cellular energy is low · AMP 激活的蛋白激酶
  • azole antifungal — a drug such as fluconazole that blocks the fungal P450 making ergosterol · 唑类抗真菌药
  • bile — the liver fluid, containing bile acids, that is released into the intestine · 胆汁
  • branch point — an intermediate from which two different pathways lead away · 岔路口
  • Brown and Goldstein — the two scientists who worked out the LDL receptor; 1985 Nobel Prize · 布朗与戈尔茨坦
  • carotenoid — a plant pigment built from isoprene units, coloured yellow to red · 类胡萝卜素
  • committed step — the first irreversible step that belongs to one pathway alone · 关键步
  • competitive inhibitor — an inhibitor that binds the active site and competes with the substrate · 竞争性抑制剂
  • cyclisation — closing an open chain into a ring · 环化
  • cytosol — the soluble part of the cytoplasm, outside the organelles · 胞质溶胶
  • emulsification — breaking fat into fine droplets dispersed through water · 乳化
  • endoplasmic reticulum — the membrane network where much of cholesterol synthesis takes place · 内质网
  • excretion — removal of a substance from the body · 排泄
  • feedback inhibition — the end product of a pathway inhibiting an enzyme near its start · 反馈抑制
  • Feodor Lynen — the German biochemist who shared the 1964 Nobel Prize for this pathway · 吕南
  • fluconazole — an azole antifungal drug · 氟康唑
  • HMG-CoA — 3-hydroxy-3-methylglutaryl-CoA, the six-carbon branch point intermediate · HMG-CoA
  • HMG-CoA reductase — the committed and rate-limiting enzyme, which makes mevalonate using two NADPH · HMG-CoA 还原酶
  • HMG-CoA synthase — the enzyme that adds acetyl-CoA to acetoacetyl-CoA to give HMG-CoA · HMG-CoA 合酶
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • isoprenoid — any compound built from five-carbon isoprene units · 类异戊二烯
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate, β-hydroxybutyrate and acetone, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • Konrad Bloch — the biochemist who worked out the cholesterol pathway; 1964 Nobel Prize · 布洛赫
  • lipid — a water-insoluble biological molecule such as a fat, a phospholipid or a sterol · 脂质
  • membrane — a lipid bilayer with embedded proteins that separates two compartments · 膜
  • membrane fluidity — how freely the lipids and proteins of a membrane can move · 膜流动性
  • mevalonate — the six-carbon product of HMG-CoA reductase · 甲羟戊酸
  • mevastatin — the first statin, isolated from Penicillium citrinum · 美伐他汀
  • mitochondrion — the organelle of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） and the respiratory chain · 线粒体
  • NADPH — the reduced coenzyme that supplies electrons for biosynthesis · 还原型 NADP
  • NPC1L1 — the intestinal transporter that takes up cholesterol; the target of ezetimibe · NPC1L1 转运蛋白
  • pathway — a fixed sequence of enzyme-catalysed reactions · 代谢途径
  • Penicillium citrinum — the mould from which mevastatin was isolated · 桔青霉
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group to a protein, which usually changes its activity · 磷酸化
  • phytosterol — a plant sterol, such as sitosterol · 植物固醇
  • plaque — a cholesterol-rich deposit in an artery wall · 斑块
  • precursor — a compound from which another substance is made · 前体
  • prenyl anchor — an isoprenoid chain attached to a protein to hold it at a membrane · 异戊烯锚
  • probe — a molecule added to a sample to report on its surroundings · 探针
  • Ras — a small signalling GTPase held at the membrane by a prenyl anchor · Ras 蛋白
  • rate-limiting step — the slowest step of a pathway, which sets the speed of the whole path · 限速步
  • receptor — a protein that binds one particular molecule and triggers a response · 受体
  • receptor-mediated endocytosis — uptake of a molecule bound to its surface receptor inside a coated vesicle · 受体介导的内吞
  • sitosterol — the commonest plant sterol · 谷甾醇
  • squalene — the thirty-carbon open-chain isoprenoid that is cyclised into the sterol rings · 角鲨烯
  • steroid hormone — a hormone made from cholesterol, such as cortisol or oestrogen · 类固醇激素
  • sterol — a lipid with four fused rings and a hydroxyl group · 固醇
  • thiolase — the enzyme that joins two acetyl-CoA into acetoacetyl-CoA · 硫解酶
  • transporter — a membrane protein that carries one particular substance across a membrane · 转运蛋白
  • vitamin D — the fat-soluble vitamin made in skin and activated to calcitriol · 维生素 D

Vitamins & coenzymes

⚠ 2026-09-27 她点名：完全没碰过 · 卡 31

Must say

The structural idea is one sentence: a water-soluble vitamin is the part of a coenzyme the body cannot make. So you do not memorise vitamins — you memorise which coenzyme each becomes, and what that coenzyme carries.

这一句是整章的钥匙。不是背维生素，是背「它变成哪个辅酶、那个辅酶搬什么」。

Numbers

B₁ thiamine → TPP (thiamine pyrophosphate), carries an aldehyde (the decarboxylations). B₂ riboflavin → FAD / FMN (flavin adenine dinucleotide / flavin mononucleotide), carries hydrogen. B₃ niacin → NAD⁺ / NADP⁺ (nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, and its phosphate), carries hydride. B₅ pantothenate → CoA (coenzyme A), carries acyl groups. B₆ pyridoxine → PLP (pyridoxal phosphate), carries amino groups. B₇ biotin carries CO₂. B₉ folate → THF (tetrahydrofolate), carries one-carbon units. B₁₂ cobalamin carries methyl groups. Vitamin C is the odd one out — not a coenzyme, but the reductant that keeps the iron of collagen hydroxylases working.

⚠ 两套名字都要认：考官可能说 B₁，也可能说 thiamine，还可能只说 TPP——同一样东西的三个叫法。
B₃ 还有个老名字 vitamin PP（也叫尼克酸／烟酰胺）；B₇ 偶尔被写成 vitamin H。
口诀 Naked Rats Take Pictures Before Party ＝ Niacin · Riboflavin · Thiamine · Pantothenate · Biotin · Pyridoxine。

Hook

Water-soluble ones are coenzyme parts; the fat-soluble ones, A D E K, are not. They act as a light receptor, a hormone, an antioxidant and a clotting cofactor. Deficiencies worth the name only: thiamine → beriberi, niacin → pellagra, vitamin C → scurvy (collagen cannot be hydroxylated), vitamin D → rickets, vitamin K → bleeding.

⚠ A D E K 脂溶性，其余水溶性 —— 这个分界本身就是一道题。

Say this

A water-soluble vitamin is the part of a coenzyme that the body cannot make, so each one is worth remembering as a pair: which coenzyme it becomes, and what that coenzyme carries. B one, thiamine, becomes TPP, thiamine pyrophosphate, which carries an aldehyde. B two, riboflavin, becomes FAD, which carries hydrogen. B three, niacin, becomes NAD-plus, and the split matters — NADH goes to the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain), NADPH goes to biosynthesis. B five, pantothenate, becomes coenzyme A, carrying acyl groups. B six, pyridoxine, becomes PLP, pyridoxal phosphate, carrying amino groups. B seven is biotin, which carries CO₂. B nine, folate, becomes tetrahydrofolate, carrying one-carbon units. And B twelve carries methyl groups. The fat-soluble ones, A, D, E and K, are not coenzyme parts, and because they are stored rather than excreted, A and D can be toxic in excess.

约 55 秒 · 「辅酶里身体造不出来的那一截」是开口第一句 · ⚠ 编号和名字一起说，两套都能接得上

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 4 位考官：Hudeček、Martínková、Ryšlavá、Hodek

① 整组维生素靠一条线索分开：溶于水还是溶于脂 water- vs fat-soluble

The one split that organises all vitamins is solubility. Vitamins must come from food. The B group and vitamin C are water-soluble, and most B vitamins become coenzymes; A, D, E and K are fat-soluble and mostly are not.

先分两组，再一个一个说。

② B1 缺了，卡住的是丙酮酸变乙酰 CoA 这一步 thiamine · beriberi

Thiamine, vitamin B1, matters because its coenzyme TPP is what lets pyruvate enter the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) as acetyl-CoA. Its deficiency causes beriberi, with nerve and heart damage, and Wernicke encephalopathy in alcoholics.

糖代谢卡住，神经和心脏最先受累。

③ 核黄素的名字来自它的颜色：拉丁语 flavus ＝ 黄 ⭐ Hudeček riboflavin · flavins

Riboflavin is named for its colour, from Latin flavus, yellow; flavins are fluorescent too, which is how they are measured. Vitamin B2 becomes FAD and FMN for redox reactions, and its lack causes angular cheilitis, cracks at the corners of the mouth.

黄色、会发荧光 → 可以用光谱测。

④ B3 特别的一点：身体能用色氨酸自己做出一部分 niacin · pellagra

Niacin is unusual among the B vitamins: the body can make some from tryptophan. Vitamin B3 becomes the redox coenzymes NAD⁺ and NADP⁺, and its deficiency causes pellagra — dermatitis, diarrhoea and dementia.

三个 D。

⑤ 泛酸的名字是希腊语「到处都有」，这也正是它很少缺的原因 pantothenic acid · coenzyme A

Pantothenic acid is named from the Greek for "everywhere", and that is also why deficiency is very rare. Vitamin B5 becomes coenzyme A, which carries acyl groups in fat and carbohydrate metabolism.

名字来自希腊语「到处」。

⑥ 每一次转氨都要 PLP ⭐ Martínková pyridoxine · PLP

Every transamination needs PLP. Pyridoxine, vitamin B6, becomes PLP, used in transamination, neurotransmitter synthesis and the first step of haem synthesis. Deficiency causes neuropathy and anaemia, and isoniazid, a tuberculosis drug, can cause it.

血红素从这一步开始 → Martínková。

⑦ 代谢里搬运 CO₂ 是生物素的活 biotin · avidin

In our metabolism, carrying CO₂ is biotin's job: biotin, vitamin B7, is the coenzyme of the carboxylases, such as acetyl-CoA carboxylase and pyruvate carboxylase. Deficiency is rare, but avidin, a protein of raw egg white, binds biotin and can cause it.

实验室的 biotin–streptavidin 是同一种结合。

⑧ 叶酸要在知道怀孕之前就补，因为神经管关得很早 folate · neural-tube defects

Folate has to be taken before pregnancy is known, because the neural tube closes very early. Vitamin B9 becomes tetrahydrofolate, which carries one-carbon units for DNA bases; its lack causes megaloblastic anaemia and neural-tube defects.

孕前补叶酸。

⑨ 药可以靠挡住一个维生素起作用 methotrexate

A drug can work by blocking a vitamin: methotrexate, an anticancer drug, inhibits dihydrofolate reductase and stops DNA synthesis in fast-dividing cells.

抗维生素也能当药。

⑩ 植物里没有 B12，它只由微生物制造 cobalamin · pernicious anaemia

No plant makes B12: cobalamin comes only from microorganisms, and is absorbed only with intrinsic factor from the stomach. It works with folate and in odd-chain fatty acid breakdown, and its lack causes pernicious anaemia and nerve damage.

素食者和胃有问题的人容易缺。

⑪ 坏血病其实是胶原蛋白的病 ⭐ Ryšlavá ascorbic acid · scurvy

Scurvy is a collagen disease. Vitamin C, ascorbic acid, is not a coenzyme but the reductant for prolyl hydroxylase, which stabilises collagen, and an antioxidant in plants; without it the gums bleed.

人不能自己合成 C，大多数动物能。

⑫ 脂溶性开始 · 维生素 A 一个分两用：看东西，和管基因 retinol · night blindness

Vitamin A, retinol, does two unrelated jobs: as retinal it is the light-absorbing part of rhodopsin, and as retinoic acid it regulates genes. Too little causes night blindness, too much causes birth defects.

视觉 ＋ 基因调控。

⑬ 维生素 D 其实是激素：皮肤自己造，P450 活化 ⭐ Hodek calciferol · rickets

Vitamin D is really a hormone: we make it in the skin by ultraviolet light, and P450 enzymes in the liver and kidney activate it to calcitriol. It increases calcium absorption, and its lack causes rickets in children and osteomalacia in adults.

其实像激素 · P450 活化 → Hodek。

⑭ 维生素 E 管的是水溶性抗氧化剂到不了的地方：膜里面 tocopherol · antioxidant

Vitamin E works where water-soluble antioxidants cannot reach: α-tocopherol sits inside the membrane and prevents lipid peroxidation. Deficiency is rare and causes neuropathy, and haemolytic anaemia in premature babies.

和维生素 C 一起抗氧化。

⑮ 凝血因子要先 γ-羧化才能结合钙，这一步靠维生素 K ⭐ Hodek vitamin K · warfarin

Clotting factors bind calcium only after γ-carboxylation, which needs vitamin K. Deficiency causes bleeding, so newborns receive an injection, and warfarin, an anticoagulant, blocks the recycling of vitamin K.

抗凝药 ＝ 抗维生素 K。

⑯ 会吃到中毒的只有脂溶性那一组 storage and toxicity

Only the fat-soluble ones can be overdosed. Excess water-soluble vitamins are excreted in urine, while fat-soluble vitamins are stored in liver and fat, so A and D can become toxic.

B12 是例外，肝里能存几年。

⑰ 书外：vitamine 这个名字来自一个错误 —— 以为它们都是胺 discovery

The name came from a mistake: Casimir Funk coined "vitamine" in 1912, thinking these factors were amines. Christiaan Eijkman found that chickens fed polished rice developed a beriberi-like disease, and received the 1929 Nobel Prize. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

陷阱问法

  • "Do vitamins provide energy?" — No, they help the enzymes that release energy. · 不供能。
  • "Is every vitamin a coenzyme?" — No; most B vitamins are, but A, C and D are not. · A、C、D 不是辅酶。
  • "Is vitamin D really a vitamin?" — With enough sunlight we make it, so it acts like a hormone. · 晒太阳能自己合成。
  • "Why do vegans need B12 supplements?" — Plants contain no B12. · 植物里没有。

定义

  • coenzyme — a small organic molecule that carries a group or electrons for an enzyme · 辅酶
  • TPP — thiamine pyrophosphate, the coenzyme form of B1 · 焦磷酸硫胺素
  • Wernicke encephalopathy — brain damage from thiamine deficiency, mainly in alcoholics · 韦尼克脑病
  • flavins — FAD and FMN, made from riboflavin · 黄素
  • PLP — pyridoxal phosphate, the coenzyme form of B6 · 磷酸吡哆醛
  • avidin — an egg-white protein that binds biotin tightly · 抗生物素蛋白
  • tetrahydrofolate — the coenzyme form of folate, carrying one-carbon units · 四氢叶酸
  • megaloblastic anaemia — anaemia with large immature red cells from slow DNA synthesis · 巨幼细胞贫血
  • neural-tube defect — failure of the embryonic spinal cord or brain to close · 神经管缺陷
  • dihydrofolate reductase — the enzyme that regenerates tetrahydrofolate · 二氢叶酸还原酶
  • intrinsic factor — a stomach protein needed to absorb B12 · 内因子
  • prolyl hydroxylase — the enzyme that hydroxylates proline in collagen · 脯氨酰羟化酶
  • rhodopsin — the light receptor of rod cells, containing retinal · 视紫红质
  • calcitriol — the active hormone form of vitamin D · 骨化三醇
  • osteomalacia — soft bones in adults from lack of vitamin D · 骨软化症
  • lipid peroxidation — oxidative damage to membrane fatty acids · 脂质过氧化
  • γ-carboxylation — adding a carboxyl group to glutamate so a protein binds calcium · γ-羧化
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • absorption — uptake of a substance from the gut into the body · 吸收
  • acyl group — the R-CO- group left when a carboxylic acid loses its hydroxyl · 酰基
  • aldehyde — a compound carrying a -CHO group · 醛
  • amino acid — a molecule with both an amino group and a carboxyl group; the unit of proteins · 氨基酸
  • amino group — the -NH₂ group, transferred by pyridoxal phosphate · 氨基
  • anaemia — too little haemoglobin or too few red blood cells · 贫血
  • angular cheilitis — cracks at the corners of the mouth, a sign of riboflavin deficiency · 口角炎
  • antioxidant — a substance that prevents oxidative damage by reacting with radicals itself · 抗氧化剂
  • ascorbic acid — vitamin C; a water-soluble reductant rather than a coenzyme, which humans cannot make · 抗坏血酸（维生素 C）
  • beriberi — thiamine deficiency, with nerve and heart damage · 脚气病
  • biotin — vitamin B7, the coenzyme that carries CO₂ for the carboxylases · 生物素（B₇）
  • calciferol — vitamin D; made in skin by ultraviolet light and activated to calcitriol · 钙化醇（维生素 D）
  • carboxyl group — the -COOH group that makes a compound an acid · 羧基
  • carboxylase — an enzyme that attaches CO₂ to its substrate · 羧化酶
  • Casimir Funk — the Polish biochemist who coined the word "vitamine" in 1912 · 冯克
  • Christiaan Eijkman — the Dutch physician whose polished-rice experiments led to thiamine; 1929 Nobel Prize · 艾克曼
  • clotting factor — a blood protein of the coagulation cascade · 凝血因子
  • cobalamin — vitamin B12; the cobalt-containing vitamin that carries methyl groups · 钴胺素（B₁₂）
  • coenzyme A — the coenzyme made from pantothenate that carries acyl groups as thioesters · 辅酶 A
  • cofactor — a non-protein component that an enzyme needs in order to work · 辅因子
  • collagen — the triple-helical structural protein of skin, bone and blood vessels · 胶原蛋白
  • deficiency — a shortage of a nutrient large enough to cause disease · 缺乏症
  • excretion — removal of a substance from the body, for vitamins mostly in urine · 排泄
  • FAD — flavin adenine dinucleotide, the riboflavin coenzyme that carries hydrogen · 黄素腺嘌呤二核苷酸
  • fat-soluble — dissolving in lipid, so stored in liver and fat rather than excreted · 脂溶性
  • flavus — Latin for yellow, the root of the names riboflavin and flavin · 拉丁语「黄」
  • fluorescence — light emitted by a molecule immediately after it absorbs light · 荧光
  • FMN — flavin mononucleotide, the smaller riboflavin coenzyme · 黄素单核苷酸
  • folate — vitamin B9; converted to tetrahydrofolate, which carries one-carbon units · 叶酸（B₉）
  • gene regulation — control of how much of a gene is transcribed · 基因调控
  • haem synthesis — the pathway that builds the iron-porphyrin group of haemoglobin · 血红素合成
  • haemolytic anaemia — anaemia caused by destruction of red blood cells · 溶血性贫血
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • hydride — a hydrogen atom together with two electrons, H⁻, transferred by NAD⁺ · 氢负离子
  • isoniazid — a tuberculosis drug that can cause vitamin B6 deficiency · 异烟肼
  • methotrexate — an anticancer drug that inhibits dihydrofolate reductase · 甲氨蝶呤
  • methyl group — the -CH₃ group, transferred by vitamin B12 · 甲基
  • microorganism — a bacterium, archaeon or other organism too small to see · 微生物
  • NAD⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, the niacin coenzyme that accepts a hydride · 烟酰胺腺嘌呤二核苷酸
  • NADP⁺ — the phosphorylated form of NAD⁺; its reduced form supplies biosynthesis · NADP⁺
  • neuropathy — disease of the peripheral nerves · 神经病变
  • neurotransmitter — a molecule released by a nerve cell to signal to the next cell · 神经递质
  • niacin — vitamin B3; converted to NAD⁺ and NADP⁺, and partly made from tryptophan · 烟酸（B₃）
  • night blindness — poor vision in dim light, caused by vitamin A deficiency · 夜盲症
  • one-carbon unit — a single-carbon group such as methyl or formyl, carried by tetrahydrofolate · 一碳单位
  • P450 enzyme — cytochrome P450, a family of haem enzymes that insert an oxygen atom into molecules · 细胞色素 P450
  • pantothenic acid — vitamin B5; the part of coenzyme A that the body cannot make · 泛酸（B₅）
  • pellagra — niacin deficiency: dermatitis, diarrhoea and dementia · 糙皮病
  • pernicious anaemia — anaemia from vitamin B12 deficiency, usually through lack of intrinsic factor · 恶性贫血
  • pyridoxine — vitamin B6; converted to pyridoxal phosphate, which carries amino groups · 吡哆醇（B₆）
  • redox reaction — a reaction in which electrons pass from one molecule to another · 氧化还原反应
  • reductant — a substance that gives electrons to another and is itself oxidised · 还原剂
  • retinal — the aldehyde form of vitamin A, the light-absorbing part of rhodopsin · 视黄醛
  • retinoic acid — the acid form of vitamin A, which regulates gene transcription · 视黄酸
  • retinol — vitamin A; the fat-soluble vitamin of vision and gene regulation · 视黄醇（维生素 A）
  • riboflavin — vitamin B2; converted to FAD and FMN, which carry hydrogen · 核黄素（B₂）
  • rickets — vitamin D deficiency in children, with soft and deformed bones · 佝偻病
  • scurvy — vitamin C deficiency; collagen cannot be hydroxylated, so the gums bleed · 坏血病
  • solubility — how well a substance dissolves in a given liquid · 溶解度
  • thiamine — vitamin B1; converted to thiamine pyrophosphate, which carries an aldehyde · 硫胺素（B₁）
  • tocopherol — vitamin E; the fat-soluble antioxidant that sits inside the membrane · 生育酚（维生素 E）
  • toxicity — harm caused by too much of a substance · 毒性
  • transamination — transfer of an amino group from an amino acid to a keto acid · 转氨
  • tryptophan — the aromatic amino acid from which the body can make some niacin · 色氨酸
  • tuberculosis — the lung infection caused by Mycobacterium tuberculosis · 结核
  • ultraviolet light — light of wavelength shorter than visible violet · 紫外线
  • vitamin — an organic compound needed in small amounts that the body cannot make, so it must come from food · 维生素
  • vitamin H — an old name for biotin · 维生素 H
  • vitamin K — the fat-soluble vitamin needed for γ-carboxylation of clotting factors · 维生素 K
  • vitamin PP — an old name for niacin, from "pellagra-preventing" · 维生素 PP
  • warfarin — an anticoagulant drug that blocks the recycling of vitamin K · 华法林
  • water-soluble — dissolving in water, so any excess is excreted in the urine · 水溶性

Who carries what

七个基团载体 —— 把上面那张维生素表反过来查

Must say

Every activated group travels on a dedicated carrier. Acyl on CoA · CO₂ on biotin · amino on PLP · one carbon on tetrahydrofolate · methyl on SAM · aldehyde on TPP · sugar on UDP.

Can Bears Play Tennis? Some Try Upstairs.

Numbers

Two electron carriers, and the split is the whole point: NADH goes to the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) to make ATP; NADPH goes to biosynthesis.

Hook

Several of these carriers contain a nucleotide that takes no part in the chemistry — it is a handle for the enzyme to grip, not cargo.

Say this

Activated groups do not travel alone — each one has a dedicated carrier, and remembering the pairs is faster than remembering the pathways. Acyl groups travel on CoA (coenzyme A), CO₂ on biotin, amino groups on PLP (pyridoxal phosphate), one-carbon units on THF (tetrahydrofolate), methyl groups on SAM (S-adenosylmethionine), aldehydes on TPP (thiamine pyrophosphate), and sugars on UDP (uridine diphosphate). For electrons there are two pools and the split is the whole point: NADH goes to the respiratory chain to make ATP, and NADPH goes to biosynthesis. ⚠ Several of these carriers contain a nucleotide that takes no part in the chemistry at all — it is a handle for the enzyme to grip, not cargo.

约 40 秒 · 「NADH 烧、NADPH 造」那一句最常用

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Ryšlavá、Hudeček

① 最容易答反的一条：能量不在键里，而在产物比 ATP 更稳定 phosphoanhydride bonds

The energy is not stored in the bond. ATP's three phosphates are joined by phosphoanhydride bonds, and hydrolysing one releases much energy because the products are more stable than ATP.

能量来自产物更稳定。

② 「为什么产物更稳定」正好三条，三条都要能说出来 charge, resonance, hydration

There are three reasons, and each can be named: the negative charges repel each other less, the released phosphate is stabilised by resonance, and the products are better hydrated.

电荷排斥 · 共振 · 水合。

③ ATP 能当通货，恰恰因为它的转移势不是最高的：高的给它，它给低的 intermediate group-transfer potential

What makes ATP the cell's energy currency is that its phosphate group-transfer potential is high but not the highest: it takes phosphate from phosphoenolpyruvate and 1,3-bisphosphoglycerate, and gives phosphate to glucose and other acceptors.

这是 ATP 能当能量通货的原因。

④ ATP 是被不断周转的，不是被储存的，所以细胞里只有很少一点 ATP is turned over, not stored

ATP is turned over rather than stored: it is made and used continuously, the cell keeps only a small amount, and long-term energy is stored as fat and glycogen.

所以吃 ATP 没有用。

⑤ 化学相同、碱基不同，而每一个都接着一条你已经学过的通路 other nucleoside triphosphates

Other nucleoside triphosphates do the same chemistry with a different base, each in its own pathway. UTP activates sugars as UDP-glucose, GTP works in protein synthesis, and CTP activates lipid head groups.

接糖原合成、翻译、磷脂合成。

⑥ 多出来的那个磷酸不做任何化学，它的全部作用就是让酶认出是哪一种 the extra phosphate

The extra phosphate on NADPH takes no part in the redox chemistry. Its only role is to let enzymes distinguish NADPH from NADH, so the cell can hold two separate pools.

化学相同，被不同的酶识别。

⑦ 正因为认得出来，细胞才能让 NAD⁺ 多数保持氧化态，专供分解代谢 NAD⁺ in catabolism

Being able to tell them apart lets the cell keep NAD⁺ mostly oxidised, ready to accept electrons in breakdown, and the NADH formed then goes to the respiratory chain to make ATP.

分解 → NADH → 呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）。

⑧ 同一时刻、相反的状态：NADPH 多数是还原态，随时给电子 NADPH in biosynthesis and defence

At the same time, and in the opposite state, NADPH is kept mostly reduced, ready to donate electrons for fatty-acid and cholesterol synthesis and to keep glutathione reduced.

合成 ＋ 抗氧化。

⑨ NADPH 的三个来源正好各接一个主题：PPP、苹果酸酶、光反应 ⭐ Ryšlavá sources of NADPH

There are three sources, each a topic of its own: the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway), malic enzyme, and in plants the light reactions of photosynthesis; NADP-malic enzyme is one of the enzymes Ryšlavá studies.

接 PPP、脂肪合成、光合作用。

⑩ 只有还原型在 340 nm 有吸收，氧化型没有——这一条差别就够跟踪反应 ⭐ Hudeček absorbance at 340 nm

The reduced forms absorb light at 340 nm and the oxidised forms do not, and that single difference is what lets any enzyme using them be followed in a spectrophotometer.

Hudeček 做光谱学。

⑪ 书外：肌肉里最快的一路 ATP 来源，比糖酵解还快，只够头几秒 phosphocreatine

Phosphocreatine is the fastest source of ATP in muscle, faster than glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway): creatine kinase moves its phosphate to ADP in the first seconds of exercise. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the energy stored in the bond?" — No, it comes from the products being more stable than ATP. · 来自产物更稳定。
  • "Why does the cell need two electron carriers?" — One carrier cannot be mostly oxidised and mostly reduced at the same time. · 一种载体不能同时多数氧化又多数还原。
  • "Does NADPH go to the respiratory chain?" — No, it is used for biosynthesis and antioxidant defence. · 不进呼吸链。

定义

  • phosphoanhydride bond — the bond between two phosphate groups · 磷酸酐键
  • resonance — electrons spread over several atoms, which stabilises a molecule · 共振
  • hydration — being surrounded by water molecules · 水合
  • group-transfer potential — how readily a molecule donates a group such as phosphate · 基团转移势
  • catabolism — breakdown of molecules that releases energy · 分解代谢
  • glutathione — a small thiol-containing peptide that removes peroxides · 谷胱甘肽
  • malic enzyme — converts malate to pyruvate and produces NADPH · 苹果酸酶
  • phosphocreatine — muscle compound that regenerates ATP from ADP · 磷酸肌酸
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 1,3-bisphosphoglycerate — the acyl phosphate of glycolysis, whose phosphate transfer potential is higher than ATP's · 1,3-二磷酸甘油酸
  • absorbance — the fraction of light a solution takes up at a given wavelength · 吸光度
  • activated group — a chemical group attached to a carrier so that it can be transferred without further energy · 活化基团
  • acyl group — the R–CO– group left when a carboxylic acid loses its hydroxyl · 酰基
  • ATP — adenosine triphosphate, an adenosine carrying three phosphates, the cell's energy currency · 三磷酸腺苷
  • biosynthesis — the enzymatic building of larger molecules from smaller ones · 生物合成
  • biotin — vitamin B₇, the coenzyme that carries CO₂ for carboxylases · 生物素
  • carrier — a molecule that binds a group or electrons and hands them to another reaction · 载体
  • coenzyme A — the thiol coenzyme, made from vitamin B₅, that carries acyl groups as thioesters · 辅酶 A
  • creatine kinase — the muscle enzyme that transfers the phosphate of phosphocreatine to ADP · 肌酸激酶
  • CTP — cytidine triphosphate, the nucleotide that activates the head groups of phospholipids · 胞苷三磷酸
  • electron carrier — a molecule that is alternately reduced and reoxidised as it passes electrons on · 电子载体
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the cell's main circulating fuel · 葡萄糖
  • glycogen — a branched polymer of glucose, the storage form of sugar in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • glycolysis — the ten-step cytosolic pathway from glucose to pyruvate · 糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）
  • GTP — guanosine triphosphate, the nucleotide used in protein synthesis and in signalling · 鸟苷三磷酸
  • hydrolysis — splitting a bond by adding water across it · 水解
  • light reactions — the photosynthetic reactions of the thylakoid membrane that make ATP, NADPH and O₂ · 光反应
  • NAD⁺ — nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide, the oxidised electron carrier of breakdown reactions · 氧化型 NAD
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺, carrying two electrons · 还原型 NAD
  • NADPH — the reduced form of NADP⁺, the electron donor used for building molecules · 还原型 NADP
  • nucleoside triphosphate — a base and sugar carrying three phosphates, such as ATP, GTP, CTP or UTP · 核苷三磷酸
  • nucleotide — a base joined to a sugar and to one or more phosphate groups · 核苷酸
  • oxidation — loss of electrons by a molecule, often as loss of hydrogen · 氧化
  • pentose phosphate pathway — the cytosolic pathway making NADPH and ribose-5-phosphate from glucose-6-phosphate · 磷酸戊糖途径（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway）
  • phosphoenolpyruvate — the glycolytic intermediate with the highest phosphate transfer potential in the cell · 磷酸烯醇式丙酮酸
  • pyridoxal phosphate — PLP, the coenzyme from vitamin B₆ that carries amino groups in transaminases · 磷酸吡哆醛
  • redox reaction — a reaction in which electrons pass from one molecule to another · 氧化还原反应
  • reduction — gain of electrons by a molecule, the opposite of oxidation · 还原
  • respiratory chain — the carriers in the inner mitochondrial membrane that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons · 呼吸链
  • S-adenosylmethionine — SAM, the cell's general donor of methyl groups · S-腺苷甲硫氨酸
  • spectrophotometer — an instrument that measures how much light a solution absorbs · 分光光度计
  • tetrahydrofolate — THF, the coenzyme made from folate that carries one-carbon units · 四氢叶酸
  • thiamine pyrophosphate — TPP, the coenzyme from vitamin B₁ that carries two-carbon aldehyde units · 硫胺素焦磷酸
  • turnover — being made and broken down continuously, so the amount present stays small · 周转
  • UDP — uridine diphosphate, the carrier on which sugars are activated for transfer · 尿苷二磷酸
  • UDP-glucose — glucose activated on UDP, the donor used to build glycogen · UDP-葡萄糖
  • UTP — uridine triphosphate, the nucleotide from which UDP-sugars are made · 尿苷三磷酸

Ketone bodies

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 肝内生成、肝外利用

  1. 什么时候造 — acetyl-CoA 多到循环吃不下饥饿或糖尿病时脂肪大量分解，草酰乙酸又被抽去做糖异生，于是 acetyl-CoA 进不了柠檬酸循环，只能改道。
  2. 生成（肝线粒体）2 acetyl-CoA →[thiolase] acetoacetyl-CoA →[HMG-CoA synthase|限速] HMG-CoA →[HMG-CoA lyase] acetoacetate乙酰乙酸再被还原成 β-羟丁酸（血里最多的那个），或者自发脱羧成丙酮。⚠ 丙酮是废气不是燃料，从呼吸里出去。
  3. 利用（肝外）β-hydroxybutyrate →[BDH|NAD⁺] acetoacetate →[thiophorase (SCOT)|借 succinyl-CoA 的 CoA] acetoacetyl-CoA →[thiolase] 2 acetyl-CoA⚠ 肝自己用不了，因为肝没有 thiophorase——这一句解释了为什么肝造了却全送走。
  4. 谁在吃心和肌肉平时就吃；脑正常只吃葡萄糖，长期饥饿才改用酮体。⚠ 红细胞永远不能用——没有线粒体。

Say this

Ketone bodies are made in the liver and used outside it. They are made when acetyl-CoA is produced faster than the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) can take it, in starvation and in diabetes. There are three: acetoacetate, β-hydroxybutyrate and acetone, but only the first two are fuel — acetone is waste and is breathed out. The committed enzyme is HMG-CoA synthase, in the liver mitochondrion. The tissues that use them convert them back to acetyl-CoA with thiophorase, and the liver cannot use them itself precisely because it lacks that enzyme. The heart and muscle use them routinely; the brain only after prolonged starvation, and red cells never, because they have no mitochondria.

约 40 秒 · 「肝没有 thiophorase」是这题最值钱的一句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Hudeček

① 酮体是把脂肪改装成水溶性的办法：不用白蛋白运，脑才用得上 why ketone bodies exist

Ketone bodies exist to make fat usable by the brain: they are water-soluble and travel in blood without albumin, and in fasting the liver converts the acetyl-CoA of fatty acids into them.

脂肪 → 脑能用的形式。

② 长期饥饿能活下来靠这一步：脑改烧酮体，葡萄糖需求下降，肌肉蛋白就不必被拆 protein sparing

This is what makes long fasting survivable: the brain takes much of its energy from ketone bodies, so less glucose is needed from gluconeogenesis and less muscle protein is broken down.

省葡萄糖，也省肌肉蛋白。

③ 酮体和胆固醇共用起点 HMG-CoA，分岔靠地点：线粒体里出酮体，细胞质里出胆固醇 HMG-CoA branch point

HMG-CoA is a branch point shared with cholesterol synthesis, and the compartment decides the product: in the mitochondrion it gives ketone bodies, in the cytosol cholesterol.

接「胆固醇」。

④ 亮氨酸和赖氨酸是仅有的两个纯生酮氨基酸——它们的碳永远到不了葡萄糖 ketogenic amino acids

Leucine and lysine are the only two purely ketogenic amino acids: their carbons can reach ketone bodies or fat, but never glucose.

接「氨基酸代谢」。

⑤ 区别在于还剩不剩胰岛素这个刹车：饥饿时低但还在，1 型糖尿病完全没有 ketosis vs ketoacidosis

The difference is whether any insulin is left to restrain lipolysis. In fasting some remains and mild ketosis is normal; in untreated type 1 diabetes there is none, so ketone bodies rise until blood pH falls — diabetic ketoacidosis.

区别在于有没有胰岛素。

⑥ 书外：酮症酸中毒通常在血糖很高时才想到，SGLT2 抑制剂偏偏在血糖几乎正常时引起它 SGLT2 inhibitors

Ketoacidosis is usually looked for only when glucose is high, but SGLT2 inhibitors, diabetes drugs such as empagliflozin, can cause it with nearly normal blood glucose. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 血糖不高也可能酮症酸中毒。

⑦ 三个酮体里丙酮是唯一不当燃料的：它自发生成，直接从肺呼出去 acetone on the breath

Acetone is the one ketone body that is not a fuel: it forms spontaneously from acetoacetate, is breathed out, and gives the breath its fruity smell in ketoacidosis.

丙酮不是燃料，从肺排出。

⑧ 尿试纸的硝普盐反应看得见乙酰乙酸，看不见 β-羟丁酸，所以要用血酮仪 how it is measured

Urine test strips use the nitroprusside reaction, which sees acetoacetate but not β-hydroxybutyrate — the one that predominates in ketoacidosis — so blood meters measure that instead.

尿测乙酰乙酸，血测 β-羟丁酸。

⑨ NADH 在 340 nm 有吸收而 NAD⁺ 没有，所有 NAD 相关的酶测定都靠这个差 ⭐ Hudeček NADH absorbance at 340 nm

The assay works because NADH absorbs at 340 nm and NAD⁺ does not: β-hydroxybutyrate dehydrogenase converts β-hydroxybutyrate to acetoacetate and reduces NAD⁺, so the rise in absorbance is the measurement.

先说测的物理量：340 nm 吸光度。

⑩ 书外：生酮饮食是把饥饿的代谢状态当药用，治药物无效的儿童癫痫 ketogenic diet

The ketogenic diet uses a metabolic state as a treatment: a very low-carbohydrate diet keeps ketone bodies high and reduces seizures in children with drug-resistant epilepsy. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外。

⑪ 书外：β-羟丁酸不只是燃料，还是信号——抑制组蛋白去乙酰化酶，所以饥饿能改变基因表达 histone deacetylases

β-Hydroxybutyrate is not only a fuel but a signal: it inhibits histone deacetylases, so fasting itself changes gene expression. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

书外 · 点到为止。

陷阱问法

  • "Are all three ketone bodies ketones?" — No, β-hydroxybutyrate is a hydroxy acid, not a ketone. · β-羟丁酸不是酮。
  • "Does the liver use ketone bodies?" — No, it lacks thiophorase. · 肝不用。
  • "Is ketosis always a disease?" — No, it is normal in fasting; ketoacidosis is the disease. · 酮症 ≠ 酮症酸中毒。
  • "Can ketone bodies become glucose?" — No. · 不能。

定义

  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate, β-hydroxybutyrate and acetone · 酮体
  • albumin — the blood protein that carries free fatty acids · 白蛋白
  • HMG-CoA — the six-carbon intermediate shared by ketone body and cholesterol synthesis · HMG-CoA
  • ketogenic — able to form ketone bodies or fat but not glucose · 生酮的
  • ketosis — raised ketone bodies in blood · 酮症
  • ketoacidosis — ketone bodies high enough to lower blood pH · 酮症酸中毒
  • SGLT2 inhibitor — a diabetes drug that makes the kidney excrete glucose · SGLT2 抑制剂
  • nitroprusside — the reagent on urine strips that reacts with acetoacetate · 硝普盐
  • absorbance — the amount of light a sample absorbs at one wavelength · 吸光度
  • histone deacetylase — an enzyme that removes acetyl groups from histones · 组蛋白去乙酰化酶
  • thiophorase — the enzyme outside the liver that activates acetoacetate with CoA · 硫激酶（SCOT）
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetoacetate — the first ketone body formed, a four-carbon keto acid · 乙酰乙酸
  • acetoacetyl-CoA — the four-carbon intermediate made by joining two acetyl-CoA · 乙酰乙酰 CoA
  • acetone — the smallest ketone; it forms spontaneously from acetoacetate and is breathed out · 丙酮
  • acetyl-CoA — an acetyl group carried on coenzyme A, the two-carbon product of fatty acid oxidation（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation） · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • assay — a measurement of how much of a substance is present or how fast a reaction runs · 测定
  • β-hydroxybutyrate — the most abundant ketone body in blood; chemically a hydroxy acid, not a ketone · β-羟丁酸
  • β-hydroxybutyrate dehydrogenase — the NAD enzyme that interconverts β-hydroxybutyrate and acetoacetate · β-羟丁酸脱氢酶
  • cholesterol — a four-ring sterol; its synthesis shares the HMG-CoA branch point · 胆固醇
  • citric acid cycle — the mitochondrial cycle that oxidises acetyl-CoA to carbon dioxide · 柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）
  • coenzyme A — the thiol carrier that binds acyl groups as thioesters · 辅酶 A
  • compartment — a space inside the cell separated from the rest by a membrane · 区室
  • cytosol — the soluble part of the cytoplasm, outside the organelles · 胞质溶胶
  • decarboxylation — removal of a carboxyl group as carbon dioxide · 脱羧
  • diabetic ketoacidosis — ketone bodies high enough to lower blood pH, typically in type 1 diabetes · 糖尿病酮症酸中毒
  • drug-resistant epilepsy — epilepsy whose seizures are not controlled by the usual medicines · 难治性癫痫
  • empagliflozin — an SGLT2 inhibitor used to treat diabetes · 恩格列净
  • fasting — going without food, so the body lives on its own stores · 空腹
  • fatty acid — a long hydrocarbon chain ending in a carboxyl group · 脂肪酸
  • fuel — a molecule the cell oxidises in order to obtain energy · 燃料
  • gene expression — the making of RNA and then protein from a gene · 基因表达
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the main fuel circulating in blood · 葡萄糖
  • HMG-CoA lyase — the enzyme that cleaves HMG-CoA into acetoacetate and acetyl-CoA · HMG-CoA 裂解酶
  • HMG-CoA synthase — the committed and rate-limiting enzyme of ketone body synthesis · HMG-CoA 合酶
  • hydroxy acid — a compound carrying both a hydroxyl and a carboxyl group · 羟基酸
  • insulin — the pancreatic hormone of the fed state; it restrains lipolysis · 胰岛素
  • ketogenic diet — a very low-carbohydrate diet that keeps ketone bodies high · 生酮饮食
  • ketone — a compound with a carbonyl group between two carbon atoms · 酮
  • leucine — a branched-chain amino acid; one of the two purely ketogenic ones · 亮氨酸
  • lipolysis — hydrolysis of stored triacylglycerol（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat） into fatty acids and glycerol · 脂解
  • lysine — a basic amino acid; one of the two purely ketogenic ones · 赖氨酸
  • mitochondrion — the double-membraned organelle where oxidative energy metabolism takes place · 线粒体
  • NAD⁺ — the oxidised coenzyme that accepts a hydride in dehydrogenase reactions · 氧化型 NAD
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺; it absorbs light at 340 nm and NAD⁺ does not · 还原型 NAD
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid that accepts acetyl-CoA into the citric acid cycle · 草酰乙酸
  • pH — a measure of how acidic or alkaline a solution is · pH 值
  • protein sparing — using another fuel so that body protein is not broken down for glucose · 节省蛋白
  • SCOT — succinyl-CoA:3-oxoacid CoA transferase, the full name of thiophorase · SCOT
  • seizure — a burst of abnormal electrical activity in the brain · 癫痫发作
  • starvation — prolonged lack of food, with the body's fuel stores being used up · 饥饿
  • succinyl-CoA — the citric acid cycle intermediate that lends its coenzyme A to acetoacetate · 琥珀酰 CoA
  • thiolase — the enzyme that joins or splits acetyl units carried on coenzyme A · 硫解酶
  • type 1 diabetes — diabetes in which the pancreas makes no insulin at all · 1 型糖尿病
  • water-soluble — dissolving in water, so able to travel in blood without a carrier protein · 水溶性
  • wavelength — the distance between two peaks of a light wave, measured in nanometres · 波长

Gluconeogenesis

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 七步共用，四个酶绕过三道坎

  1. 什么时候跑 — 空腹超过几小时、饥饿、剧烈运动之后肝糖原只够撑十几个小时，之后血糖全靠这条路。胰高血糖素和皮质醇开它，胰岛素关它。⚠ 为什么非做不可：脑只烧葡萄糖，红细胞只能烧葡萄糖——它们停不得。
  2. 第一道坎 — 丙酮酸 → PEP，要两个酶两步走pyruvate →[pyruvate carboxylase|ABC · 线粒体] oxaloacetate →[PEP carboxykinase|GTP · −CO₂] PEP⚠ 先加一个 CO₂ 再把它拿掉，跟脂肪酸合成那一步是同一个把戏：花钱买不可逆。而且 acetyl-CoA 开启 pyruvate carboxylase——脂肪够烧了就去造糖。
  3. 中间七步 — 直接借糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的酶倒着跑这七步本来就近平衡，浓度一变方向就反，不需要另外的酶。
  4. 第二道坎 — 果糖-1,6-二磷酸酶fructose-1,6-bisP →[fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase|水解，不是逆激酶] fructose-6-P⚠ 被果糖-2,6-二磷酸关掉，而同一个分子开启 PFK-1——一个分子管住正反两条路。
  5. 第三道坎 — 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶glucose-6-P →[glucose-6-phosphatase|肝 · 肾] glucose⚠ 只有肝和肾有这个酶，所以只有它们能把葡萄糖放进血里。肌肉没有。

Say this

Gluconeogenesis makes glucose from lactate, glycerol and glucogenic amino acids, mainly in the liver and kidney. Seven of the steps are simply glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) running backwards, because those steps are near equilibrium. Three steps are irreversible and need four different enzymes. Pyruvate is carboxylated to oxaloacetate by pyruvate carboxylase, using biotin and ATP, and then PEP carboxykinase spends GTP to make phosphoenolpyruvate. Fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase and glucose-6-phosphatase then hydrolyse the two phosphates off. The cost is four ATP, two GTP and two NADH per glucose, and what that cost buys is direction.

约 40 秒 · 四个酶的名字 ＋ 4/2/2 这个代价

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Ryšlavá

① 糖酵解只有三步不可逆，所以糖异生只在这三处换酶 bypass reactions

Gluconeogenesis needs different enzymes at only three points, because only three steps of glycolysis are irreversible: hexokinase, phosphofructokinase-1 and pyruvate kinase. The rest of the path is shared.

三个不可逆的酶，三处绕道。

② 两条路用不同的酶，为的是能分别开关 reciprocal regulation

The point of different enzymes is control. The two pathways can be switched on and off separately, so the cell does not run both at once and waste ATP in a futile cycle.

接「调控」主题。

③ 开关不由细胞自己定，由激素定 hormones

Hormones decide this, not the cell: glucagon and cortisol turn gluconeogenesis on in fasting, and insulin turns it off after a meal.

谁下令。

④ 三种原料各来自一个组织 lactate, amino acids, glycerol

Each starting material comes from a different tissue: lactate from muscle and red blood cells, alanine and other amino acids from muscle protein, and glycerol from fat tissue.

每种原料来自一个器官。

⑤ 脂肪不能变成葡萄糖，只有里面的甘油能 fatty acids are not a source

Animals cannot turn fat into glucose: glycerol is the only part of fat they can use, because the fatty acids give only acetyl-CoA, which cannot become glucose.

常考。

⑥ 糖异生之所以必要，是因为肝糖原会用完 glycogen first

Gluconeogenesis is needed because liver glycogen is finite: the liver uses glycogen first, and when glycogen runs low, gluconeogenesis becomes the main source of blood glucose.

接「糖原」主题。

⑦ 用氨基酸做糖，剩下的氮要处理，所以尿素循环加快 ketone bodies · urea cycle

Using amino acids for glucose leaves their nitrogen behind, so the urea cycle (also called ornithine cycle, Krebs–Henseleit cycle) runs faster; at the same time the liver makes ketone bodies from fatty acids.

接「酮体」「氨基酸」两个主题。

⑧ 能做糖异生的只有肝和肾，因为要把磷酸去掉 liver and kidney

Only an organ with glucose-6-phosphatase can release glucose, and that means the liver and the kidney; in long starvation the kidney contributes more.

只有这两个器官有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。

⑨ 书外：牛的糖异生不是应急，是常态供应 ruminants

In cows gluconeogenesis is not a fasting pathway but the normal supply: rumen microbes ferment dietary sugar to propionate, and the liver makes most glucose from propionate. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑩ 空腹喝酒会低血糖：乙醇产生的 NADH 抑制糖异生 ⭐ Hodek ethanol · hypoglycaemia

Drinking on an empty stomach can cause hypoglycaemia, because alcohol dehydrogenase in the liver produces a lot of NADH, and that NADH inhibits gluconeogenesis. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

Hodek 考纲里有乙醇代谢。

⑪ 植物一个酶完成动物要两个酶的事 ⭐ Ryšlavá pyruvate phosphate dikinase

In plants one enzyme does what animals need two for. Pyruvate phosphate dikinase makes PEP from pyruvate directly, and C4 plants use it to regenerate the PEP for PEP carboxylase.

她研究的酶之一，接光合作用。

⑫ 书外：2 型糖尿病不只是吸收不了糖，肝还在继续造糖 type 2 diabetes

Type 2 diabetes is not only poor glucose uptake: the liver responds poorly to insulin and keeps making glucose when blood glucose is already high. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Is it just glycolysis in reverse?" — Only partly; most steps are shared, but the three irreversible steps use different enzymes. · 三步用不同的酶。
  • "Can muscle raise blood glucose?" — No; it lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, so it sends lactate or alanine to the liver. · 肌肉没有葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。
  • "Why does the body need it?" — The brain and red blood cells need glucose, and liver glycogen runs out. · 脑和红细胞要葡萄糖。

定义

  • bypass reaction — a different enzyme step used instead of an irreversible glycolytic step · 绕道反应
  • futile cycle — two opposite pathways running at once, wasting ATP · 无效循环
  • reciprocal regulation — one pathway switched on while the opposite one is switched off · 相互调控
  • hypoglycaemia — blood glucose that is too low · 低血糖
  • propionate — a three-carbon acid made by rumen microbes · 丙酸
  • pyruvate phosphate dikinase — a plant enzyme making PEP directly from pyruvate · 丙酮酸磷酸双激酶
  • type 2 diabetes — diabetes where tissues respond poorly to insulin · 2 型糖尿病
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetyl-CoA — the two-carbon product of fatty acid oxidation（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）; it cannot become glucose · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • adipose tissue — the fat-storing tissue, the source of the glycerol used here · 脂肪组织
  • alanine — the amino acid that muscle sends to the liver as a glucose precursor · 丙氨酸
  • alcohol dehydrogenase — the liver enzyme that oxidises ethanol and produces NADH · 乙醇脱氢酶
  • amino acid — a molecule with both an amino group and a carboxyl group; the unit of proteins · 氨基酸
  • ATP — adenosine triphosphate, the cell's general energy currency · 三磷酸腺苷
  • biotin — the vitamin that carries CO₂ in carboxylases such as pyruvate carboxylase · 生物素
  • C4 plants — plants that fix CO₂ first into a four-carbon acid before the Calvin cycle（= Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions） · C4 植物
  • carboxylation — attaching CO₂ to a molecule · 羧化
  • cortisol — the adrenal steroid hormone of prolonged stress and fasting · 皮质醇
  • Embden-Meyerhof pathway — another name for glycolysis · 糖酵解途径
  • equilibrium — the state in which forward and reverse reactions run at the same rate · 平衡
  • ethanol — the alcohol of drinks, oxidised in the liver by alcohol dehydrogenase · 乙醇
  • fermentation — breakdown of sugar by microbes without oxygen · 发酵
  • fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase — the enzyme that hydrolyses fructose-1,6-bisphosphate to fructose-6-phosphate · 果糖-1,6-二磷酸酶
  • fructose-2,6-bisphosphate — the regulator that activates PFK-1 and inhibits fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase · 果糖-2,6-二磷酸
  • glucagon — the pancreatic hormone of fasting; it switches gluconeogenesis on · 胰高血糖素
  • glucogenic — able to give carbons that end up in glucose · 生糖的
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the main fuel circulating in blood · 葡萄糖
  • glucose-6-phosphatase — the liver and kidney enzyme that frees glucose from glucose-6-phosphate · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶
  • glycerol — the three-carbon backbone released when fat is hydrolysed; the only part of fat giving glucose · 甘油
  • glycogen — the branched glucose polymer stored in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • glycolysis — the cytosolic pathway that breaks glucose down to pyruvate · 糖酵解
  • GTP — guanosine triphosphate, a nucleotide used as an energy source like ATP · 三磷酸鸟苷
  • hexokinase — the enzyme that phosphorylates glucose to glucose-6-phosphate · 己糖激酶
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • hydrolysis — cutting a bond by adding a molecule of water across it · 水解
  • insulin — the pancreatic hormone of the fed state; it switches gluconeogenesis off · 胰岛素
  • irreversible — going in one direction only under the conditions inside the cell · 不可逆
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate, β-hydroxybutyrate and acetone, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • kinase — an enzyme that transfers a phosphate group from ATP onto something else · 激酶
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid made when pyruvate is reduced without oxygen · 乳酸
  • microbe — a bacterium, archaeon or other organism too small to see · 微生物
  • NADH — the reduced form of NAD⁺; too much of it inhibits gluconeogenesis · 还原型 NAD
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid made by carboxylating pyruvate · 草酰乙酸
  • PEP carboxykinase — the enzyme that makes phosphoenolpyruvate from oxaloacetate, spending GTP · PEP 羧激酶
  • PEP carboxylase — the plant enzyme that fixes CO₂ onto phosphoenolpyruvate · PEP 羧化酶
  • phosphatase — an enzyme that removes a phosphate group by hydrolysis · 磷酸酶
  • phosphate group — the -PO₃²⁻ group attached to a molecule · 磷酸基
  • phosphoenolpyruvate — the three-carbon high-energy phosphate ester, written PEP · 磷酸烯醇式丙酮酸
  • phosphofructokinase-1 — the rate-limiting kinase of glycolysis, written PFK-1 · 磷酸果糖激酶-1
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon acid that is the end product of glycolysis · 丙酮酸
  • pyruvate carboxylase — the mitochondrial biotin enzyme that carboxylates pyruvate to oxaloacetate · 丙酮酸羧化酶
  • pyruvate kinase — the last and irreversible enzyme of glycolysis · 丙酮酸激酶
  • rumen — the first stomach of a cow, where microbes ferment the food · 瘤胃
  • starvation — prolonged lack of food, with the body's fuel stores being used up · 饥饿
  • tissue — a group of similar cells doing one job together · 组织
  • urea cycle — the liver cycle that turns the nitrogen of amino acids into urea · 尿素循环（= urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle）

Glycogen

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 合成和分解各四步，共用一套开关

  1. 什么时候跑 — 饭后存、空腹取饭后胰岛素高 → 存；空腹或运动、胰高血糖素和肾上腺素高 → 取。⚠ 肝的糖原是给全身的（维持血糖），肌肉的糖原只给自己（供自己收缩）——两个器官存的是同一样东西，用途完全不同。
  2. 合成 — 先活化，再接上去glucose-6-P →[phosphoglucomutase] glucose-1-P →[UDP-glucose pyrophosphorylase|＋UTP] UDP-glucose →[glycogen synthase|α-1,4] glycogen⚠ 葡萄糖不能直接接上去，必须先做成 UDP-葡萄糖。分支由分支酶做成 α-1,6。
  3. 分解 — 细胞内是磷酸解，不是水解glycogen →[glycogen phosphorylase|＋Pᵢ · α-1,4] glucose-1-P →[phosphoglucomutase] glucose-6-P⚠ 用磷酸而不是水去切，所以出来的是磷酸糖，省掉一次激酶。分支点由脱支酶处理，那里放出的是游离葡萄糖。⚠ 消化道里才是水解（淀粉酶），这个二分是考点。
  4. 出口 — 只有肝能放糖进血glucose-6-P →[glucose-6-phosphatase|只有肝和肾] glucose⚠ 肌肉没有这个酶，所以肌糖原只给肌肉自己用。
  5. 调控 — 一套级联，两个相反的结果磷酸化之后：磷酸化酶开（b→a），合成酶关（a→b）。所以肾上腺素和胰高血糖素一来，拆的开、造的关，绝不会同时跑——这就是避免无效循环。

Say this

Glycogen synthesis needs the glucose activated first, as UDP-glucose. Then glycogen synthase makes the α-1,4 chain and a branching enzyme makes the α-1,6 branches. Breakdown inside the cell is not hydrolysis but phosphorolysis: glycogen phosphorylase uses inorganic phosphate, so the product is already glucose-1-phosphate. In the digestive tract it is hydrolysis, by amylases. Only the liver and kidney have glucose-6-phosphatase, so muscle glycogen can never raise blood glucose. And the two directions share one cascade: phosphorylation activates the phosphorylase and inactivates the synthase, so the cell never runs both at once.

约 40 秒 · 磷酸解 vs 水解、肌肉没有磷酸酶，两句必说

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Lipovová

① 一个糖原分子只算一个渗透颗粒，却装着上千个葡萄糖 osmotic pressure

One glycogen molecule counts as a single particle for osmotic pressure, although it holds thousands of glucose units; the same glucose left free would draw water into the cell.

存成大分子，不吸水。

② 分支之所以重要，是因为末端多、分解就快 branches

Branching is what makes glycogen fast to mobilise: its many α-1,6 branches give many chain ends, and glycogen phosphorylase works on many ends at the same time.

分支多 ＝ 分解快。

③ 糖原合酶只会接长链、不会起头，所以每个糖原都从一个蛋白开始 glycogenin

Glycogen synthase can only lengthen a chain, so every glycogen molecule begins on glycogenin, a small protein that adds the first glucose units to itself.

起点是一个蛋白。

④ 用磷酸切而不用水切，是为了把葡萄糖留在细胞里 phosphorolysis vs hydrolysis

Cutting with phosphate instead of water is what keeps the glucose inside. Glycogen phosphorylase gives glucose-1-phosphate, which saves an ATP and cannot leave the cell; in the gut, amylase cuts with water and gives free glucose that can be absorbed.

细胞内：磷酸解。肠道：水解。

⑤ 淀粉的消化在嘴里就开始了，而且两个淀粉酶都只切 α-1,4 salivary and pancreatic amylase

Digestion of starch begins already in the mouth, with salivary α-amylase, and continues with pancreatic α-amylase in the small intestine; both cut only α-1,4 bonds.

嘴里一次，小肠一次。

⑥ 最后一刀切在小肠细胞表面，因为只有单糖能进去 brush-border enzymes

Only single sugars can be absorbed, so the last cut happens on the surface of the intestinal cells. Maltase, sucrase-isomaltase and lactase release them, and the isomaltase part cuts the α-1,6 branch points.

只有单糖能被吸收。

⑦ 书外：乳糖不耐受是成年人的常态，不是少数人的病 lactose intolerance

Lactose intolerance is the normal adult state: most adults make much less lactase, so lactose reaches the colon, where bacteria ferment it and cause gas and diarrhoea. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑧ 同样是葡萄糖，只差一个键的方向，人就消化不了 cellulose

One bond makes the difference: cellulose is also a glucose polymer, but its bonds are β-1,4, and humans have no enzyme that cuts them.

同样是葡萄糖，键不同。

⑨ 植物解决同一个问题用的是淀粉：键相同，分支更少 starch

Plants solve the same storage problem with starch, which has the same α-1,4 and α-1,6 bonds as glycogen, but fewer branches.

接光合作用主题。

⑩ 第二信使这个概念，就是从糖原分解这条路上来的 discovery

The whole idea of a second messenger came out of this pathway. Earl Sutherland discovered cAMP while studying how adrenaline activates glycogen phosphorylase; Carl and Gerty Cori received a Nobel Prize for glycogen breakdown.

「怎么发现的」那一问。

⑪ 书外：缺酶的是哪个器官，决定症状是什么 glycogen storage diseases

Which organ lacks the enzyme decides the symptom. In von Gierke disease the liver lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, so fasting blood glucose falls; in McArdle disease muscle lacks glycogen phosphorylase, so exercise causes cramps. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

肝型：空腹低血糖。肌型：运动抽筋。

⑫ 糖苷酶的定义就是用水切糖与糖之间的键 ⭐ Lipovová glycosidases

What makes an enzyme a glycosidase is hydrolysing the bond between sugars with water. Amylase and lactase do that, and industry uses α-amylase to turn starch into glucose syrup.

她研究糖苷酶。

陷阱问法

  • "Is glycogen phosphorylase a glycosidase?" — No; it uses phosphate, not water. · 不是，它用磷酸不用水。
  • "Why can we not digest cellulose?" — Its bonds are β-1,4, and we have no enzyme for them. · β 键，没有酶。
  • "Why is glucose made into UDP-glucose first?" — Glucose must be activated before glycogen synthase can add it to the chain. · 先活化才能接上。

定义

  • osmotic pressure — the pull of water towards the side with more dissolved particles · 渗透压
  • glycogenin — the protein on which a glycogen molecule is built · 糖原蛋白
  • phosphorolysis — cutting a bond with phosphate instead of water · 磷酸解
  • hydrolysis — cutting a bond with water · 水解
  • brush border — the surface of the intestinal cells where the last digestive enzymes sit · 刷状缘
  • glycosidase — an enzyme that hydrolyses the bond between sugars · 糖苷酶
  • second messenger — a small molecule inside the cell, such as cAMP, that carries a hormone's signal · 第二信使
  • glycogen storage disease — an inherited lack of one enzyme of glycogen metabolism · 糖原贮积病
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • α-1,4 bond — the glycosidic link that joins glucose units along a straight chain · α-1,4 键
  • α-1,6 bond — the glycosidic link at a branch point of glycogen or starch · α-1,6 键
  • α-amylase — an enzyme that hydrolyses α-1,4 bonds in starch and glycogen · α-淀粉酶
  • absorption — uptake of digested molecules from the gut into the body · 吸收
  • adrenaline — the fast stress hormone that switches glycogen breakdown on · 肾上腺素
  • β-1,4 bond — the glycosidic link of cellulose, which humans have no enzyme to cut · β-1,4 键
  • branch point — the place on a chain where a side chain is attached · 分支点
  • branching enzyme — the enzyme that moves a piece of chain across to create an α-1,6 branch · 分支酶
  • cAMP — cyclic AMP, the second messenger made inside the cell after a hormone binds · 环腺苷酸
  • cascade — a chain of enzymes each activating the next, so a small signal becomes large · 级联
  • cellulose — a glucose polymer with β-1,4 bonds; the main structural material of plant walls · 纤维素
  • Cori — Carl and Gerty Cori, who worked out glycogen breakdown and received a Nobel Prize · 科里夫妇
  • debranching enzyme — the enzyme that clears an α-1,6 branch point and releases free glucose · 脱支酶
  • digestion — breaking food down into molecules small enough to be absorbed · 消化
  • disaccharide — a sugar built from two monosaccharide units · 二糖
  • fermentation — breakdown of sugar by microbes without oxygen · 发酵
  • galactose — a six-carbon sugar; with glucose it makes lactose · 半乳糖
  • glucagon — the pancreatic hormone of fasting, which switches glycogen breakdown on · 胰高血糖素
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the main fuel circulating in blood · 葡萄糖
  • glucose-1-phosphate — the phosphorylated glucose released by glycogen phosphorylase · 葡萄糖-1-磷酸
  • glucose-6-phosphatase — the liver and kidney enzyme that frees glucose from glucose-6-phosphate · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶
  • glucose-6-phosphate — glucose phosphorylated on carbon six; charged, so it cannot leave the cell · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸
  • glycogen — the branched α-glucose polymer stored in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • glycogen phosphorylase — the enzyme that cuts glycogen with phosphate to give glucose-1-phosphate · 糖原磷酸化酶
  • glycogen synthase — the enzyme that adds glucose from UDP-glucose onto an α-1,4 chain · 糖原合酶
  • glycosidic bond — the bond that joins one sugar unit to another · 糖苷键
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on distant cells · 激素
  • inherited disease — a disease caused by a mutation passed on from a parent · 遗传病
  • inorganic phosphate — free phosphate ion, written Pᵢ · 无机磷酸
  • insulin — the pancreatic hormone of the fed state, which switches glycogen storage on · 胰岛素
  • lactase — the brush-border enzyme that splits lactose into glucose and galactose · 乳糖酶
  • lactose — the disaccharide of milk, made of glucose and galactose · 乳糖
  • lactose intolerance — the normal adult fall in lactase, so lactose is fermented in the colon · 乳糖不耐受
  • maltase — the intestinal enzyme that splits maltose into two glucose units · 麦芽糖酶
  • maltose — the disaccharide of two glucose units · 麦芽糖
  • McArdle disease — an inherited lack of muscle glycogen phosphorylase, causing cramps on exercise · 麦卡德尔病
  • mobilisation — releasing a stored fuel so that it can be used · 动员
  • monosaccharide — a single sugar unit, such as glucose or galactose · 单糖
  • pancreatic α-amylase — the starch-digesting enzyme secreted by the pancreas into the small intestine · 胰 α-淀粉酶
  • phosphoglucomutase — the enzyme that interconverts glucose-1-phosphate and glucose-6-phosphate · 磷酸葡萄糖变位酶
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group to a protein, which usually changes its activity · 磷酸化
  • polymer — a large molecule built from many repeating units · 聚合物
  • polysaccharide — a polymer of many sugar units joined by glycosidic bonds · 多糖
  • salivary α-amylase — the starch-digesting enzyme of saliva, which begins digestion in the mouth · 唾液 α-淀粉酶
  • starch — the plant storage polymer of glucose, with the same bonds as glycogen but fewer branches · 淀粉
  • sucrase-isomaltase — the brush-border enzyme that splits sucrose, its isomaltase part cutting α-1,6 points · 蔗糖酶-异麦芽糖酶
  • sucrose — the disaccharide of glucose and fructose; ordinary table sugar · 蔗糖
  • Sutherland — Earl Sutherland, who discovered cyclic AMP and the idea of a second messenger · 萨瑟兰
  • UDP-glucose — glucose activated by attachment to UDP; the donor for glycogen synthesis · UDP-葡萄糖
  • UDP-glucose pyrophosphorylase — the enzyme that makes UDP-glucose from glucose-1-phosphate and UTP · UDP-葡萄糖焦磷酸化酶
  • UTP — uridine triphosphate, the nucleotide that activates glucose for glycogen synthesis · 尿苷三磷酸
  • von Gierke disease — an inherited lack of liver glucose-6-phosphatase, causing fasting hypoglycaemia · 冯·吉尔克病

Amino acid breakdown & the urea cycle

⚠ 没碰过的一条 · 氮和碳分开处理

  1. 什么时候跑 — 一直在跑，而且吃高蛋白或饥饿时更快氨基酸没法储存——多出来的只能烧掉或转成糖和脂肪，而氮必须处理掉。⚠ 饥饿时肌肉蛋白被拆来做糖异生，氮的负担反而更重。游离氨有神经毒性，所以这条路不能停。
  2. 摘氨基 — 转氨，再集中脱氨各种氨基酸把氨基交给 α-酮戊二酸，变成谷氨酸（转氨酶，辅酶 PLP，来自 B6）；谷氨酸再由谷氨酸脱氢酶放出游离氨。这两步合起来叫联合脱氨。
  3. 把氨运到肝 — 两条运输线肌肉走丙氨酸-葡萄糖循环，脑和其他组织走谷氨酰胺。⚠ 游离氨有毒，绝不在血里裸奔。
  4. 尿素循环（= urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle） — 两步在线粒体，三步在胞质NH₄⁺ ＋ HCO₃⁻ →[CPS I|2 ATP · 限速] carbamoyl phosphate →[ornithine transcarbamoylase] citrullinecitrulline →[argininosuccinate synthetase|＋aspartate · ATP→AMP] argininosuccinate →[argininosuccinate lyase|−fumarate] arginine →[arginase] urea ＋ ornithine⚠ 两个氮来路不同：一个是游离氨，一个由天冬氨酸带进来。CPS I 要 N-乙酰谷氨酸才开工——有活干才开机。
  5. 碳骨架去哪剩下的碳架按去向分两类：生糖的进丙酮酸或柠檬酸循环的中间物，生酮的变 acetyl-CoA。⚠ 纯生酮只有两个：Leu 和 Lys。

Say this

Amino acid breakdown separates the nitrogen from the carbon skeleton. The nitrogen is collected by transamination onto glutamate — the transaminases all need pyridoxal phosphate, vitamin B6 — and then released as free ammonia by glutamate dehydrogenase. Because ammonia is toxic it travels to the liver as alanine from muscle or as glutamine from other tissues. In the liver the urea cycle (also called ornithine cycle, Krebs–Henseleit cycle) runs with its first two steps in the mitochondrion and the last three in the cytosol. The control enzyme is carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I, switched on by N-acetylglutamate. The two nitrogens of urea come from different places: one from free ammonia, one from aspartate. It costs four ATP per urea, although the summary equation writes three, because one ATP is broken all the way to AMP.

约 50 秒 · 两个氮的来路 ＋ 4 对 3 那个说破

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Ryšlavá、Martínková、Lipovová

①「必需」的意思只是碳骨架我们自己做不出来 essential vs non-essential

Amino acids are also made, not only broken down. Essential means only that we cannot build the carbon skeleton: nine amino acids are essential and must be eaten, the others we make ourselves. The synthesis side is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

口诀 PVT TIM HaLL（Phe Val Thr Trp Ile Met His Leu Lys）。合成这半是书外。

② 氮几乎都从谷氨酸这一个口进来 glutamate dehydrogenase · glutamine synthetase

Nearly all nitrogen enters through glutamate. Glutamate dehydrogenase adds ammonia to α-ketoglutarate, glutamine synthetase adds a second one to make glutamine, and transamination passes it on to the other amino acids.

跟分解走的是同一个中转站，方向相反。

③ 空气里几乎都是氮，可是植物一点也用不上 ⭐ Ryšlavá GS/GOGAT · nitrogen fixation

The air is mostly nitrogen, and no plant can use it: only nitrogenase, in some bacteria, turns nitrogen gas into ammonia — nitrogen fixation. Plants and bacteria take ammonia up mainly through GS/GOGAT, glutamine synthetase plus glutamate synthase.

固氮在她的关键词里。

④ 二十个碳骨架只出自几个中心中间物，所以能分家族 families

Only a few intermediates give the carbon skeletons of all twenty amino acids: glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway), the citric acid cycle (also called TCA cycle, tricarboxylic acid cycle, Krebs cycle) and the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway). That is why they are grouped into families by their starting molecule.

按出发的中间物分家族。

⑤ 草酰乙酸这一家里有三个必需氨基酸：Met、Thr、Lys α-ketoglutarate · oxaloacetate families

Three essential ones come from a single family: methionine, threonine and lysine, made from oxaloacetate through aspartate, together with asparagine. From α-ketoglutarate come glutamate, glutamine, proline and arginine.

这两个都是柠檬酸循环（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle）的中间物。

⑥ 三个支链氨基酸都在丙酮酸这一家 pyruvate · 3-phosphoglycerate families

The branched-chain amino acids sit in one family: from pyruvate come alanine, valine, leucine and isoleucine; from 3-phosphoglycerate come serine, glycine and cysteine.

这两个都是糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）的中间物。

⑦ 做核苷酸用的一碳单位，主要从这一步来 serine → glycine

This is a main source of the one-carbon units for nucleotides: serine hydroxymethyltransferase takes one carbon off serine, which leaves glycine, and hands that carbon to tetrahydrofolate.

同事问过、你卡住过的那一题。

⑧ 草甘膦能杀草不伤人，是因为莽草酸途径人根本没有 shikimate pathway · glyphosate

Glyphosate kills plants and spares us because the shikimate pathway exists only in plants and microbes. It makes phenylalanine, tyrosine and tryptophan from PEP and erythrose-4-phosphate, and glyphosate blocks its enzyme EPSP synthase.

草甘膦：植物有这条路，人没有。

⑨ 酪氨酸之所以算非必需，全靠一个酶；酶坏了就是苯丙酮尿症 histidine · tyrosine · PKU

Tyrosine is non-essential only because phenylalanine hydroxylase makes it from phenylalanine; when that enzyme is missing, phenylalanine builds up, and this is phenylketonuria. Histidine is made from ribose-5-phosphate.

新生儿都筛查苯丙酮尿症。

⑩ 几个信号分子其实就是一个氨基酸改一改 precursors

Several signalling molecules are single amino acids modified: tyrosine becomes adrenaline and dopamine, tryptophan becomes serotonin, and histidine becomes histamine.

接激素主题。

⑪ 一氧化氮是气体做的信号分子，原料是精氨酸 ⭐ Martínková nitric oxide

Nitric oxide is a signal molecule that is a gas, made from arginine by nitric oxide synthase. Haem proteins sense it by binding it to their iron.

她研究血红素怎样感受气体。

⑫ 尿素循环的开关在第一步，信号就是刚吃下去的那顿蛋白 committed step

Back to breakdown: the urea cycle is controlled at its first, committed step, and the signal is the meal itself. Carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I works only when N-acetylglutamate is present, and N-acetylglutamate rises after a protein-rich meal. The cycle itself runs ornithine, carbamoyl phosphate, citrulline, aspartate, argininosuccinate, fumarate, arginine, urea: “One Cold Cat Ate A Fat Angry Uncle.”

蛋白吃得多 → N-乙酰谷氨酸多 → 第一步开。「鸟 瓜 精琥 精 尿」。

⑬ 排什么含氮废物，由能用多少水决定 ammonia · uric acid · urea

How much water an animal has decides what it excretes. Fish release ammonia straight into the water, birds and reptiles excrete uric acid, which needs almost none, and mammals excrete urea.

排什么，和能用多少水有关。

⑭ 书外：天冬酰胺酶是把营养拿走的药 ⭐ Lipovová asparaginase

Asparaginase treats acute lymphoblastic leukaemia by taking a nutrient away. It breaks down the asparagine in the blood, and these leukaemia cells cannot make enough of their own. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

她做过天冬酰胺酶的项目。

陷阱问法

  • "Where does glycine come from?" — From serine, by serine hydroxymethyltransferase, which passes one carbon to tetrahydrofolate. · 丝氨酸去一个碳。
  • "Why does glyphosate not harm us?" — Humans do not have the shikimate pathway. · 人没有这条路。
  • "Is tyrosine essential?" — No; we make it from phenylalanine, except in phenylketonuria. · 不是，由苯丙氨酸做。
  • "Which step of the urea cycle is regulated?" — The first one, carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I, switched on by N-acetylglutamate. · 第一步。

定义

  • essential amino acid — an amino acid we cannot make and must eat · 必需氨基酸
  • transamination — moving an amino group from an amino acid to a keto acid · 转氨
  • GS/GOGAT — glutamine synthetase plus glutamate synthase, the main ammonia uptake route in plants and bacteria · 谷氨酰胺合成酶/谷氨酸合酶途径
  • nitrogen fixation — turning nitrogen gas into ammonia, by nitrogenase in some bacteria · 固氮
  • tetrahydrofolate — the folate coenzyme that carries one-carbon units · 四氢叶酸
  • shikimate pathway — the plant and microbial route to phenylalanine, tyrosine and tryptophan · 莽草酸途径
  • phenylketonuria — an inherited lack of phenylalanine hydroxylase · 苯丙酮尿症
  • committed step — the first irreversible step unique to a pathway · 定向步骤
  • N-acetylglutamate — the molecule that activates carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I · N-乙酰谷氨酸
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acute lymphoblastic leukaemia — a cancer of the immature cells of the lymphoid line, commonest in children · 急性淋巴细胞白血病
  • adrenaline — a hormone and neurotransmitter made from tyrosine and released in stress · 肾上腺素
  • alanine — the amino acid with a methyl side chain · 丙氨酸
  • α-ketoglutarate — a five-carbon keto acid of the citric acid cycle; the usual acceptor of amino groups · α-酮戊二酸
  • amino acid — a molecule carrying an amino group and a carboxyl group on the same carbon, the unit from which proteins are built · 氨基酸
  • amino group — the –NH₂ group, basic because its nitrogen takes up a proton · 氨基
  • ammonia — NH₃, the free nitrogen compound released by deamination; toxic to the nervous system · 氨
  • arginase — the enzyme that hydrolyses arginine into urea and ornithine · 精氨酸酶
  • arginine — the amino acid with a guanidinium side chain, the most basic of the twenty · 精氨酸
  • argininosuccinate — a urea cycle intermediate formed from citrulline and aspartate · 精氨酸代琥珀酸
  • argininosuccinate lyase — the enzyme that splits argininosuccinate into arginine and fumarate · 精氨酸代琥珀酸裂解酶
  • argininosuccinate synthetase — the enzyme that joins citrulline to aspartate at the cost of ATP · 精氨酸代琥珀酸合成酶
  • asparaginase — an enzyme, used as a drug, that hydrolyses asparagine to aspartate and ammonia · 天冬酰胺酶
  • asparagine — the amino acid whose side chain is the amide of aspartate · 天冬酰胺
  • aspartate — the amino acid with a carboxylate side chain, the anion of aspartic acid · 天冬氨酸
  • branched-chain amino acids — valine, leucine and isoleucine, whose side chains are branched hydrocarbons · 支链氨基酸
  • carbamoyl phosphate — the activated compound made from ammonia and bicarbonate that opens the urea cycle · 氨甲酰磷酸
  • carbamoyl phosphate synthetase I — the mitochondrial enzyme that makes carbamoyl phosphate, spending two ATP · 氨甲酰磷酸合成酶 I
  • carbon skeleton — what remains of an amino acid once the amino group has been taken off · 碳骨架
  • citrulline — an amino acid that never appears in proteins, an intermediate of the urea cycle · 瓜氨酸
  • coenzyme — a small non-protein molecule that an enzyme needs bound to it in order to work · 辅酶
  • dopamine — a neurotransmitter made from tyrosine, and the precursor of adrenaline · 多巴胺
  • EPSP synthase — 5-enolpyruvylshikimate-3-phosphate synthase, the shikimate pathway enzyme that glyphosate inhibits · EPSP 合酶
  • erythrose-4-phosphate — a four-carbon sugar phosphate from the pentose phosphate pathway（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway） · 4-磷酸赤藓糖
  • fumarate — a four-carbon dicarboxylic acid, released by argininosuccinate lyase and also an intermediate of the citric acid cycle · 延胡索酸
  • glucogenic — said of an amino acid whose carbon skeleton can be converted into glucose · 生糖的
  • glutamate — the amino acid with a three-carbon acidic side chain, the anion of glutamic acid · 谷氨酸
  • glutamate dehydrogenase — the enzyme that releases the amino group of glutamate as free ammonia · 谷氨酸脱氢酶
  • glutamine — the amino acid whose side chain is the amide of glutamate · 谷氨酰胺
  • glutamine synthetase — the enzyme that attaches ammonia to glutamate to give glutamine · 谷氨酰胺合成酶
  • glycine — the smallest amino acid, with a single hydrogen as its side chain · 甘氨酸
  • glyphosate — a herbicide that inhibits EPSP synthase of the shikimate pathway · 草甘膦
  • haem protein — a protein carrying a haem group, an iron-porphyrin, as its prosthetic group · 血红素蛋白
  • histamine — a signalling compound formed by decarboxylating histidine · 组胺
  • histidine — the amino acid with an imidazole side chain, the only one that changes charge near pH 7 · 组氨酸
  • intermediate — a compound made at one step of a pathway and used up at the next · 中间物
  • keto acid — an acid whose chain also carries a ketone group, usually next to the carboxyl · 酮酸
  • ketogenic — said of an amino acid whose carbon skeleton gives only acetyl-CoA or ketone bodies · 生酮的
  • nitric oxide — NO, a gas that acts as a signal molecule between cells · 一氧化氮
  • nitric oxide synthase — the enzyme that makes nitric oxide from arginine · 一氧化氮合酶
  • nitrogen — the element N, carried in biology as amino groups, amides and the bases of nucleotides · 氮
  • nitrogenase — the bacterial enzyme that reduces nitrogen gas to ammonia · 固氮酶
  • one-carbon unit — a single carbon group carried on a coenzyme and used in biosynthesis · 一碳单位
  • ornithine — a five-carbon amino acid, not found in proteins, that carries the urea cycle round · 鸟氨酸
  • ornithine transcarbamoylase — the enzyme that joins carbamoyl phosphate to ornithine, giving citrulline · 鸟氨酸转氨甲酰酶
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid of the citric acid cycle, and the precursor of aspartate · 草酰乙酸
  • pathway — an ordered set of enzyme-catalysed reactions turning one compound into another · 代谢途径
  • PEP — phosphoenolpyruvate, the three-carbon intermediate of glycolysis with a high phosphate transfer potential · 磷酸烯醇式丙酮酸
  • phenylalanine hydroxylase — the liver enzyme that converts phenylalanine into tyrosine · 苯丙氨酸羟化酶
  • precursor — a compound that is converted into another one further along · 前体
  • pyridoxal phosphate — PLP, the coenzyme derived from vitamin B6 that every transaminase needs · 磷酸吡哆醛
  • pyruvate — the three-carbon keto acid at the end of glycolysis · 丙酮酸
  • ribose-5-phosphate — the five-carbon sugar phosphate from the pentose phosphate pathway · 5-磷酸核糖
  • serine hydroxymethyltransferase — the enzyme that takes one carbon from serine onto tetrahydrofolate, leaving glycine · 丝氨酸羟甲基转移酶
  • serotonin — a neurotransmitter made from tryptophan by hydroxylation and decarboxylation · 血清素
  • signal molecule — a compound released by one cell that changes the behaviour of another · 信号分子
  • tyrosine — the aromatic amino acid with a phenol side chain · 酪氨酸
  • urea — the soluble nitrogen waste product of mammals, two nitrogens on a single carbon · 尿素
  • urea cycle — the liver pathway that turns ammonia and aspartate nitrogen into urea · 尿素循环
  • uric acid — a purine, almost insoluble, the nitrogen waste product of birds and reptiles · 尿酸

Photosynthesis

卡 27 · 28 · 两半在两个隔间，跟线粒体「膜上发电、旁边花钱」同一个故事

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有光的时候（而固碳白天一直在跑） 定义先行：把辐射能生物性地转化成化学能（书自己的定义）。 光同时驱动两件事：把一个供体氧化、把 CO₂ 还原成碳水化合物。 H₂D ＋ A →[pigments] H₂A ＋ D 绿色植物里供体是水，所以才放氧。
  2. 光反应 · 电子链 — 在类囊体膜上 PS Ⅱ 吸光，电子被打出去，留下的空穴靠裂解水补上 —— 氧就是这么来的。 电子经 PQ → cyt b₆f（泵质子进类囊体腔）→ PC → PS Ⅰ， 在 PS Ⅰ 再吸一次光，经 Fd → FNR 把 NADP⁺ 还原成 NADPH。 口诀 Two Quick Boys Climb One Fir, Fetch Nuts。 ⚠ 二号在一号前面干活 —— 编号按发现顺序，不按工作顺序。这句本身是考点。
  3. 光合磷酸化 — 同一台机器，方向相反 质子被泵进类囊体腔，再经 ATP 合酶流回基质做 ATP。 ⚠ 跟线粒体是同一种酶、同一个原理，只是质子梯度的方向反过来（这里是腔里酸）。 ⚠ 环式电子传递：电子从 Fd 绕回 b₆f，只产 ATP 不产 NADPH —— 细胞用它来调两者的比例。
  4. 固碳 · Calvin 循环（= Calvin cycle = Calvin–Benson cycle = dark reactions = light-independent reactions） — 在 stroma 三步 Cows Run Round：羧化 → 还原 → 再生。 rubisco 把 CO₂ 接到核酮糖-1,5-二磷酸（5 碳）上，六碳产物立刻裂成 两个 3-磷酸甘油酸（3 碳） —— C3 这个名字就是这么来的。 ⚠ NADPH 只花在「还原」，ATP 花在「还原」和「再生」。 rubisco 是地球上丰度最高的酶。
  5. rubisco 的毛病 — 它还会抓氧 名字里的 oxygenase 不是白叫的：O₂ 会跟 CO₂ 抢同一个活性位点， 抓到氧就走光呼吸，白白浪费能量和碳。温度高、气孔关的时候更严重。
  6. 两种对策 — C4 分地方，CAM 分时间 都是先用 PEP 羧化酶把 CO₂ 抓住（它不认氧），浓缩之后再交给 rubisco。 C4：叶肉细胞抓，运到维管束鞘细胞放 —— 分地方。 CAM：夜里开气孔抓、存成苹果酸，白天关气孔放 —— 分时间。 ⚠ 三者只动固碳这一半，光反应完全一样。⚠ CAM 书里没有，要标 general。
  7. 总式和那个正号 12 H₂O ＋ 6 CO₂ →[light · pigments] C₆H₁₂O₆ ＋ 6 O₂ ＋ 6 H₂O ΔG°′ ＝ +2867 kJ/mol。⚠ 正号 ＝ 吸能，这就是为什么非要有光； 说成负号等于把光合说成了呼吸。 ⚠ 书写十二个水而不是六个，就是为了说明氧来自水，不是 CO₂。

Say this

Photosynthesis is the biological conversion of radiant energy into chemical energy, and it happens in two halves in two places. The light reactions run in the thylakoid membrane: photosystem II absorbs light, and the electron hole it leaves is filled by splitting water, which is where the oxygen comes from. The electrons pass through plastoquinone, cytochrome b₆f, plastocyanin and photosystem I to ferredoxin and NADP reductase, giving NADPH, while the protons pumped into the lumen drive an ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase) — the same machine as in the mitochondrion. The fixation of CO₂ is the Calvin cycle (also called Calvin–Benson cycle, dark reactions, light-independent reactions), and it runs in the stroma in three stages: carboxylation by rubisco, reduction, and regeneration, and it spends exactly the ATP and NADPH the light reactions made. The old name "dark reactions" is misleading: they run in daylight, they just do not use light directly. The first stable product is a three-carbon acid, 3-phosphoglycerate, which is why ordinary plants are called C3 plants. Rubisco also grabs oxygen, which wastes carbon in photorespiration, so some plants first capture CO₂ with PEP carboxylase, which ignores oxygen, and only then hand it to rubisco: C4 plants separate the two steps in space — mesophyll cells capture, bundle-sheath cells release — and CAM plants separate them in time — they capture at night and release by day with the stomata shut. The light reactions are the same in all three. ⚠ The oxygen comes from water, not from carbon dioxide, and my textbook makes that point by writing twelve waters rather than six.

约 90 秒 · 「氧来自水」和「二号在一号前面」是两个必说；Calvin 循环点名和 C3/C4/CAM 是 09-29 补的

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Ryšlavá、Martínková、Hudeček

① 叶子是绿的，恰恰因为绿光是叶绿素不吸收的那一段 chlorophyll absorbs blue and red

Leaves are green because green is the light chlorophyll does not use: it absorbs mainly blue and red light and reflects the green.

吸蓝、吸红、反射绿。

② 类胡萝卜素同时干两件事：补吸光，和防强光 carotenoids

Carotenoids, the yellow and orange pigments, do two jobs: they absorb light that chlorophyll misses, and they protect the leaf against excess light. In autumn, when chlorophyll is broken down, their colour becomes visible.

辅助色素 ＋ 保护。秋天叶子变黄就是它。

③ 一大群色素里只有一对叶绿素真的做化学，其余只负责收集 antenna · reaction centre

Of all these pigments, only one chlorophyll pair per photosystem does chemistry. The rest are the antenna: they absorb light and pass the energy to that reaction centre, where the electron is released.

像卫星天线把信号汇到一个接收点。

④ 一个光子不够把电子从水送到 NADPH，所以要吸两次光 Z-scheme

One photon cannot lift an electron from water all the way to NADPH. So light is needed twice, at photosystem II and at photosystem I, and the energy diagram has the shape of the letter Z. The carriers in order are PQ, cytochrome b₆f, PC, photosystem I, ferredoxin, FNR, NADPH: “Pink Bear Pays Police For Free Noodles.”

两个光系统串联。

⑤ 环式光合磷酸化补的是一个缺口：ATP 不够，NADPH 够 cyclic photophosphorylation

Cyclic photophosphorylation exists to fix a mismatch: the Calvin cycle spends ATP in two of its three stages — carboxylation, reduction and regeneration — but NADPH only in the reduction. So electrons from photosystem I sometimes flow back to cytochrome b₆f instead of to NADP⁺, which makes extra ATP only.

用来调 ATP 和 NADPH 的比例。

⑥ 光呼吸是在做无用功：耗 ATP，还放掉已经固定的 CO₂ photorespiration

Photorespiration undoes work already done. Rubisco sometimes binds oxygen instead of CO₂, and recovering the product costs ATP and releases CO₂ that was already fixed, across the chloroplast, peroxisome and mitochondrion.

高温、干旱、气孔关闭时更严重。

⑦ rubisco 慢、又分不清 CO₂ 和 O₂，植物只能靠数量补 —— 所以它最多 slow and abundant

Rubisco is very slow and discriminates poorly between CO₂ and O₂, so plants compensate with quantity, and that is why it is the most abundant enzyme on Earth. The speed trade-off is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

慢、不专一 → 靠数量。

⑧ 三个特征就是内共生的证据：双层膜、自己的 DNA、自己的核糖体 endosymbiosis

Three features are the evidence for endosymbiosis. Chloroplasts and mitochondria both have two membranes, their own DNA and their own ribosomes, because both were once bacteria: chloroplasts a cyanobacterium, mitochondria an α-proteobacterium.

两者都用 ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase），只是质子梯度方向相反。

⑨ 我们呼吸的氧，最初是废物，而且是毒 the oxygen atmosphere

The oxygen we breathe began as a waste product and a poison. Photosynthesis is its only source, and when it first accumulated, billions of years ago, it was toxic to most organisms.

氧最初是毒。
