⑩ 光太多不只是浪费，是危险：多余的电子被交给氧 ⭐ Ryšlavá reactive oxygen species

Too much light is dangerous, not just wasted. Electrons the Calvin cycle cannot use pass to oxygen and form reactive oxygen species, such as superoxide and hydrogen peroxide, which superoxide dismutase, catalase and peroxidases remove.

她研究植物胁迫和抗氧化酶。

⑪ 叶绿素和血红素是同一个环，只换了中心金属 ⭐ Martínková porphyrin: Mg vs Fe

Chlorophyll and haem are the same ring with a different metal: both are porphyrins, with magnesium in chlorophyll and iron in haem.

同一种环，中心金属不同。

⑫ 两个问题，两种测法：有多少用吸光度，好不好用荧光 ⭐ Hudeček absorbance · fluorescence

Two different questions, two different measurements: absorbance tells how much chlorophyll there is, and chlorophyll fluorescence tells how well photosystem II is working. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

先说测的是什么物理量。

陷阱问法

  • "Why are leaves green?" — Chlorophyll absorbs blue and red light and reflects green. · 反射绿光。
  • "Do plants respire?" — Yes, day and night; they have mitochondria. · 植物也一直在呼吸。
  • "Is photosynthesis respiration in reverse?" — Only the overall equation; the pathways, enzymes and compartments are different. · 只有总式是反的。

定义

  • carotenoid — a yellow or orange accessory pigment that also protects against excess light · 类胡萝卜素
  • antenna — pigments that absorb light and pass the energy to the reaction centre · 天线色素
  • reaction centre — the chlorophyll pair where light energy releases an electron · 反应中心
  • Z-scheme — the energy diagram of photosystem II and photosystem I in series · Z 图
  • cyclic photophosphorylation — electrons returning from photosystem I to cytochrome b₆f, making ATP only · 环式光合磷酸化
  • photorespiration — rubisco using O₂ instead of CO₂, which wastes energy and fixed carbon · 光呼吸
  • endosymbiosis — a bacterium living inside another cell and becoming an organelle · 内共生
  • reactive oxygen species — highly reactive forms of oxygen, such as superoxide and hydrogen peroxide · 活性氧
  • porphyrin — the ring structure of haem and chlorophyll · 卟啉
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 3-phosphoglycerate — a three-carbon acid carrying one phosphate, the first stable product of CO₂ fixation · 3-磷酸甘油酸
  • absorbance — a measure of how much light a sample removes from a beam at one wavelength · 吸光度
  • α-proteobacterium — a member of the bacterial group from which mitochondria descend · α-变形菌
  • ATP synthase — the membrane enzyme that makes ATP as protons flow back down their gradient through it · ATP 合酶
  • bundle-sheath cell — the inner leaf cell of a C4 plant, where CO₂ is released again for rubisco · 维管束鞘细胞
  • C3 plant — a plant in which the first product of CO₂ fixation is a three-carbon acid · C3 植物
  • C4 plant — a plant that fixes CO₂ first in one cell type and releases it for rubisco in another · C4 植物
  • Calvin cycle — the pathway in the stroma that fixes CO₂ into sugar in three stages · 卡尔文循环
  • CAM plant — crassulacean acid metabolism: a plant that takes CO₂ up at night and uses it by day · 景天酸代谢植物
  • carbon fixation — converting inorganic CO₂ into an organic molecule · 固碳
  • carboxylation — attaching CO₂ to a molecule, so adding one carbon and one carboxyl group · 羧化
  • catalase — the enzyme that breaks hydrogen peroxide down into water and oxygen · 过氧化氢酶
  • chlorophyll — the green pigment of photosynthesis, a porphyrin ring holding a magnesium ion · 叶绿素
  • chlorophyll fluorescence — light re-emitted by chlorophyll, measured to see how well photosystem II is working · 叶绿素荧光
  • chloroplast — the plant organelle with two outer membranes in which photosynthesis takes place · 叶绿体
  • cyanobacterium — a photosynthetic bacterium that releases oxygen; the ancestor of the chloroplast · 蓝细菌
  • cytochrome b₆f — the thylakoid complex passing electrons from plastoquinone to plastocyanin and pumping protons as it does · 细胞色素 b₆f 复合体
  • dark reactions — the older name for the Calvin cycle, meaning reactions that do not use light directly, not ones needing darkness · 暗反应
  • ΔG°′ — the standard free-energy change of a reaction at pH 7; positive means energy must be supplied · 标准自由能变
  • electron — the negatively charged particle that is handed on in every oxidation and reduction · 电子
  • electron acceptor — the substance that takes up electrons in a redox reaction and is thereby reduced · 电子受体
  • electron donor — the substance that gives electrons away in a redox reaction and is thereby oxidised · 电子供体
  • ferredoxin — the small iron–sulfur protein that accepts electrons from photosystem I · 铁氧还蛋白
  • FNR — ferredoxin–NADP⁺ reductase, the enzyme that passes those electrons on to NADP⁺ · 铁氧还蛋白-NADP⁺ 还原酶
  • haem — the iron-holding porphyrin group of haemoglobin and of the cytochromes · 血红素
  • hydrogen peroxide — H₂O₂, a reactive oxygen compound, removed by catalase · 过氧化氢
  • light reactions — the light-driven half of photosynthesis, run in the thylakoid membrane · 光反应
  • mesophyll cell — the ordinary photosynthetic cell of the leaf interior · 叶肉细胞
  • NADPH — the reduced form of nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide phosphate, which supplies electrons for biosynthesis · 还原型辅酶 II
  • oxidation — loss of electrons from a substance · 氧化
  • PEP carboxylase — the enzyme that attaches CO₂ to phosphoenolpyruvate and does not react with oxygen · 磷酸烯醇式丙酮酸羧化酶
  • peroxidase — an enzyme that uses hydrogen peroxide to oxidise some other substrate · 过氧化物酶
  • peroxisome — the organelle holding catalase, and the middle part of photorespiration · 过氧化物酶体
  • photon — a single quantum of light, its energy set by its wavelength · 光子
  • photosystem I — the thylakoid complex whose absorbed light drives electrons on to ferredoxin · 光系统 I
  • photosystem II — the thylakoid complex that absorbs light and refills its electron hole by splitting water · 光系统 II
  • pigment — a compound that looks coloured because it absorbs only some wavelengths of visible light · 色素
  • plastocyanin — the small blue copper protein that carries electrons to photosystem I · 质体蓝素
  • plastoquinone — the mobile lipid-soluble quinone that carries electrons and protons within the thylakoid membrane · 质体醌
  • proton gradient — a difference in proton concentration, and so in charge, across a membrane · 质子梯度
  • reduction — gain of electrons by a substance · 还原
  • ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate — the five-carbon sugar with two phosphates that accepts CO₂ in the Calvin cycle · 1,5-二磷酸核酮糖
  • rubisco — ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate carboxylase/oxygenase, the enzyme that fixes CO₂ · 核酮糖二磷酸羧化酶/加氧酶
  • stoma — an adjustable pore in the leaf surface through which gases enter and leave; plural stomata · 气孔
  • stroma — the soluble interior of the chloroplast, outside the thylakoids · 叶绿体基质
  • superoxide — O₂⁻·, the radical formed when oxygen takes up a single electron · 超氧阴离子
  • superoxide dismutase — the enzyme converting superoxide into hydrogen peroxide and oxygen · 超氧化物歧化酶
  • thylakoid — a flattened membrane sac inside the chloroplast, holding the light reactions · 类囊体
  • thylakoid lumen — the enclosed space inside a thylakoid, into which protons are pumped · 类囊体腔

Metabolic regulation

卡 32 · 四问

Must say

Four questions: where control sits, how it acts, what the cell senses, who gives the order. Control sits only at the steps that are far from equilibrium, because only there does the enzyme set the net flux.

Numbers

A reaction is near equilibrium when Q and K′eq are within one or two orders of magnitude — true of more than half the enzymes of glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway).

Hook

Three cross-pathway signals are worth more than any list: citrate shuts down glycolysis and opens fat synthesis; malonyl-CoA shuts down fat oxidation; acetyl-CoA shuts down PDH and opens gluconeogenesis.

Say this

I would answer this as four questions: where control sits, how it acts, what the cell senses, and who gives the order. Where: only at steps that are far from equilibrium, because at a near-equilibrium step the forward and reverse rates are both large, so adding enzyme speeds up both and the net flux barely moves. In practice a reaction is near equilibrium when Q and K′eq are within one or two orders of magnitude, and that is true of more than half the enzymes of glycolysis. How: slowly by changing the amount of enzyme, or quickly by changing the activity of enzyme that already exists — allosterically, covalently, by an inhibitor, or by a regulatory protein — and these are not mutually exclusive. What it senses: not absolute amounts but ratios — ATP to ADP, NADH to NAD-plus — and each ratio is a permit. Who: inside a cell those signals are enough; between tissues it is hormones.

约 55 秒 · 开口先报「四个问题」，结构就在你手里

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Martínková、Lipovová、Hodek

① 调控回答的是一个问题：现在需要多少 homeostasis

Regulation answers one question: how much is needed now. It saves the cell energy and keeps ATP and blood glucose stable, and keeping the internal state stable is called homeostasis.

为什么要调：省能量 ＋ 保持稳定。

② 有两套控制，是因为快慢不同：几秒 vs 几小时 time scales

Two kinds of control exist because they work at different speeds: changing an enzyme's activity takes seconds to minutes, and changing its amount through gene expression takes hours.

快调活性，慢调数量。

③ 反馈抑制放在第一步，为的是中间物一个都不白做 feedback inhibition

Feedback inhibition sits at the first committed step so that nothing is made that is not needed: the end product inhibits the first step of its own pathway.

关在第一步，不关在中间。

④ 方向相反的两条路，放在不同区室就互不干扰 compartmentation

Two opposite pathways cannot interfere if they are kept apart: fatty acid synthesis runs in the cytosol and fatty acid oxidation (also called β-oxidation) in the mitochondria, which is compartmentation.

分开放，就不会同时跑。

⑤ 合成的第一个中间物，本身就是氧化的关闭信号 malonyl-CoA

The first intermediate of fatty acid synthesis is itself what switches oxidation off. Malonyl-CoA inhibits carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1), so new fatty acids are not carried into the mitochondria and oxidised.

合成一开，氧化就关。

⑥ 激素进不进细胞，决定它怎么起作用 second messengers

Whether a hormone enters the cell decides how it acts. Glucagon and adrenaline stay outside and act through the second messenger cAMP, while steroid hormones such as cortisol enter and change gene expression. There are four such cascades: the cyclase, the phosphoinositide, the receptor kinase and the nucleus route, which is C-P-R-N.

两条路：第二信使，或直接进核。

⑦ 这里只有胰岛素说「存起来」，另两个都说「放出来」 insulin vs glucagon

Insulin is the one signal here for storage: it means the fed state, while glucagon and adrenaline mean fasting or stress and release glucose and fatty acids.

一对相反的命令。

⑧ 同一个反应可以按器官各调各的 isozymes

The same reaction can be tuned tissue by tissue with isozymes (also called isoenzyme): liver uses glucokinase and muscle uses hexokinase to phosphorylate glucose.

接「器官分工」主题。

⑨ 别构：不碰活性中心，信号照样传到那里 ⭐ Martínková allostery

Allosteric control is how a signal reaches the active site without touching it. The regulator binds at another site and changes the enzyme's conformation; haem sensor proteins work on the same principle.

她的框架：一个位点结合，构象变化传到另一处。

⑩ 底物浓度低于 Km 时，不需要调节剂，供应本身就是调控 ⭐ Lipovová Km

An enzyme working below its Km is regulated by nothing but supply: the rate rises as the substrate concentration rises, with no regulator involved.

接酶动力学。

⑪ 肝对外来物的回答，是多造一些酶 ⭐ Hodek enzyme induction

The liver answers a foreign compound by making more enzyme: drugs such as phenobarbital induce the synthesis of more cytochrome P450 enzymes.

「改数量」的例子，通往他的主场。

⑫ 书外：细胞读的是 AMP，不是 ATP AMP-activated protein kinase

AMP is the signal, not ATP: when ATP falls, AMP rises and activates AMP-activated protein kinase, which turns catabolism on and synthesis off. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑬ 这条级联最后落在磷酸化上 adenylate cyclase cascade

The adenylate cyclase cascade ends in phosphorylation. The hormone stays outside and binds a receptor on the surface, the level of cyclic AMP rises inside, and cyclic AMP activates a protein kinase that phosphorylates serine and threonine residues of the target proteins.

激素不进细胞，第二信使替它进去。

⑭ 放大效应是很小的信号就够用的原因 amplification

Amplification is the reason a very small signal is enough. Forming a second messenger amplifies it: one hormone molecule leads to many molecules of cyclic AMP, and each activated kinase phosphorylates many enzyme molecules.

很小的信号，很大的回答。

⑮ 别构只是结合，共价修饰是真的改了化学结构 covalent modification

Unlike an allosteric regulator, which only binds, covalent modification changes the enzyme chemically, most often by phosphorylation. A phosphatase takes the group off again, so the switch works in both directions.

别构靠结合，共价修饰靠改化学结构。

⑯ 书外 · general：凋亡与别的细胞死亡不同，不引起炎症 apoptosis

Apoptosis, programmed cell death, differs from other cell death in causing no inflammation: the cell dismantles itself in an ordered way and neighbours take up the fragments. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

有程序，而且不引起炎症。

陷阱问法

  • "Is the slowest step the only control point?" — No; control is usually shared by several steps. · 控制分摊在几步上。
  • "Is allosteric inhibition the same as competitive?" — No; an allosteric regulator binds at a different site. · 结合位点不同。
  • "Why regulate the first committed step?" — So that no energy is spent on intermediates that are not needed. · 不浪费中间物。

定义

  • homeostasis — keeping the internal state stable · 稳态
  • feedback inhibition — an end product inhibiting an early step of its own pathway · 反馈抑制
  • committed step — the first irreversible step unique to a pathway · 定向步骤
  • compartmentation — separating pathways into different parts of the cell · 区室化
  • second messenger — a small molecule inside the cell, such as cAMP, that carries a hormone's signal · 第二信使
  • isozyme — a different form of an enzyme that catalyses the same reaction · 同工酶（= isoenzyme = isozyme）
  • allosteric — acting at a site other than the active site by changing conformation · 别构
  • Km — the substrate concentration giving half the maximum rate · 米氏常数（= Km = Michaelis constant）
  • enzyme induction — increased synthesis of an enzyme in response to a signal · 酶诱导
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group held on coenzyme A by a thioester bond · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • active site — the pocket of an enzyme in which the substrate binds and the reaction takes place · 活性中心
  • adenylate cyclase — the membrane enzyme that makes cyclic AMP from ATP · 腺苷酸环化酶
  • adrenaline — the hormone of the adrenal medulla, released in stress, which mobilises glucose and fatty acids · 肾上腺素
  • AMP — adenosine monophosphate: adenine, ribose and a single phosphate · 腺苷一磷酸
  • AMP-activated protein kinase — AMPK, the kinase switched on by rising AMP, which turns catabolism on and synthesis off · AMP 活化蛋白激酶
  • amplification — one signal molecule causing many active molecules at each following step · 放大
  • apoptosis — programmed cell death, in which the cell dismantles itself in order and without inflammation · 细胞凋亡
  • β-oxidation — the mitochondrial pathway that shortens a fatty acid two carbons at a time · β-氧化（= β-oxidation = fatty acid oxidation）
  • cAMP — cyclic AMP, an AMP whose phosphate closes a ring onto the ribose; it acts as a second messenger · 环腺苷酸
  • carnitine acyltransferase I（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） — CPT-1, the enzyme that moves a fatty acyl group onto carnitine so it can enter the mitochondrion · 肉碱脂酰转移酶 I
  • cascade — a series of activation steps in which each activated component activates the next · 级联
  • catabolism — the breaking down of molecules, which releases energy · 分解代谢
  • citrate — the six-carbon tricarboxylic acid that is the first intermediate of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） · 柠檬酸
  • concentration — the amount of a substance present per unit volume of solution · 浓度
  • conformation — the three-dimensional shape a molecule takes up without any covalent bond being broken · 构象
  • cortisol — the main human glucocorticoid, a steroid hormone of the adrenal cortex · 皮质醇
  • covalent modification — changing an enzyme's activity by attaching or removing a chemical group · 共价修饰
  • cytochrome P450 — a family of haem enzymes, chiefly of the liver, that oxidise drugs and other foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450
  • cytosol — the soluble part of the cytoplasm, outside all the organelles · 胞质溶胶
  • equilibrium — the state in which forward and reverse rates are equal, so nothing changes overall · 平衡
  • far from equilibrium — said of a reaction whose actual ratio of products to reactants is far from the equilibrium one, so it runs one way · 远离平衡
  • flux — the net rate at which material passes through a pathway · 通量
  • gene expression — making the RNA, and then the protein, that a gene codes for · 基因表达
  • glucagon — the peptide hormone of the pancreatic α cells, which raises blood glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glucokinase — the liver isozyme of hexokinase, with a high Km for glucose · 葡糖激酶
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from precursors that are not carbohydrates · 糖异生
  • hexokinase — the enzyme that puts a phosphate on carbon 6 of glucose and other hexoses · 己糖激酶
  • hormone — a signal molecule released by one tissue that acts on cells elsewhere in the body · 激素
  • inflammation — the local response to injury or infection, with swelling, redness and incoming immune cells · 炎症
  • inhibitor — a substance that binds an enzyme and lowers the rate of its reaction · 抑制剂
  • insulin — the pancreatic peptide hormone of the fed state, which drives uptake and storage · 胰岛素
  • K′eq — the equilibrium constant of a reaction under standard biochemical conditions · 平衡常数
  • malonyl-CoA — the three-carbon donor of fatty acid synthesis, made by carboxylating acetyl-CoA · 丙二酰辅酶 A
  • metabolism — the whole set of chemical reactions that a cell carries out · 代谢
  • order of magnitude — a factor of ten · 数量级
  • pathway — an ordered set of enzyme-catalysed reactions turning one compound into another · 代谢途径
  • phenobarbital — a barbiturate drug, used as a sedative and anticonvulsant · 苯巴比妥
  • phosphatase — an enzyme that hydrolyses a phosphate group off its substrate · 磷酸酶
  • phosphoinositide — a membrane phospholipid with an inositol head group, whose fragments act as second messengers · 磷酸肌醇
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group, usually taken from ATP · 磷酸化
  • protein kinase — an enzyme that transfers a phosphate from ATP onto a protein · 蛋白激酶
  • pyruvate dehydrogenase — PDH, the enzyme complex converting pyruvate into acetyl-CoA and CO₂ · 丙酮酸脱氢酶
  • Q — the mass action ratio: the ratio of products to reactants actually present at a given moment · 质量作用比
  • receptor — a protein that binds a specific signal molecule and passes the message on · 受体
  • receptor kinase — a receptor that is itself a protein kinase, switched on when its ligand binds · 受体激酶
  • regulatory protein — a protein that binds an enzyme or a gene and changes how active it is · 调节蛋白
  • signal — a molecule whose concentration a cell reads in order to change what it is doing · 信号
  • steroid hormone — a lipid-soluble hormone built from cholesterol, which enters the cell and alters gene expression · 甾体激素
  • substrate — the molecule an enzyme acts on, which binds in the active site · 底物
  • tissue — a group of similar cells doing one job together · 组织

Who eats what

器官分工 —— 常被当作串场题

Must say

The liver is the switchboard: after a meal it stores, in starvation it releases glycogen, runs gluconeogenesis and makes ketone bodies for everyone else.

Numbers

The brain normally burns only glucose and takes about 20% of the oxygen; only in prolonged starvation does it switch to ketone bodies.

Hook

Red cells have no mitochondria — glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) only, and that is also why they depend on the pentose phosphate pathway (also called hexose monophosphate shunt, phosphogluconate pathway) for NADPH.

Say this

The tissues do not all eat the same thing, and the differences are the point. The liver is the switchboard: after a meal it stores glycogen and makes fat; in starvation it releases glucose, runs gluconeogenesis, and makes ketone bodies for everyone else — though it cannot use them itself. The brain normally burns only glucose and takes about a fifth of the body’s oxygen; only after prolonged starvation does it switch partly to ketone bodies. Red cells have no mitochondria, so they depend entirely on glycolysis, and that is also why they depend on the pentose phosphate pathway for NADPH. Muscle keeps its glycogen for itself, because it lacks glucose-6-phosphatase, and in hard exercise it sends lactate to the liver through the Cori cycle (also called lactic acid cycle).

约 45 秒 · 这一段是串场题，四个器官各一句

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Martínková

① 整条时间线只护一样东西：血糖。先肝糖原，再糖异生，最后靠酮体 fed → fasting → starvation

The whole sequence protects one thing: blood glucose. After a meal fuel is stored; in the first hours of fasting the liver uses glycogen, then gluconeogenesis; in long starvation ketone bodies take over, so less muscle protein is broken down.

时间线一句话。

② 脂肪组织不只是仓库，它还是内分泌器官 adipose tissue

Adipose tissue is not only a store: it holds triacylglycerol (also called triglyceride, neutral fat), releases fatty acids and glycerol in fasting, and also secretes the hormone leptin. Leptin is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

脂肪组织也分泌激素。

③ 心脏不能停，所以它几乎全靠有氧，血里有什么都能烧 heart

The heart never rests, so it is almost entirely aerobic, with very many mitochondria. It burns mainly fatty acids, and also lactate and ketone bodies.

什么都能烧，主要是脂肪酸。

④ 红白之分来自肌红蛋白和线粒体：红的做有氧，白的做糖酵解 slow vs fast fibres

Skeletal muscle has two fibre types, and the colour comes from myoglobin and mitochondria. Slow red fibres work oxidatively for long activity; fast white fibres use glycolysis for short, intense effort.

红肌耐力，白肌爆发。

⑤ 最快的一路 ATP 来源，因为只需要一步酶促转移 creatine phosphate

Creatine phosphate is the fastest source of ATP because only one step is needed: creatine kinase moves its phosphate straight onto ADP in the first seconds of effort.

最快的 ATP 来源。

⑥ 这两个循环里付 ATP 的是肝，不是肌肉 Cori cycle · glucose–alanine cycle

In both cycles the liver pays the ATP cost: working muscle sends lactate and alanine to the liver, and the liver turns them into glucose and sends it back.

Cori 循环（= Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle） ＋ 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环（= glucose–alanine cycle = Cahill cycle）。

⑦ 除了肝，只有肾能往血里放葡萄糖；它还用氨管酸碱 kidney

Apart from the liver, the kidney is the other organ that can release glucose into the blood, in long starvation. It also puts ammonia from glutamine into the urine to help control acid–base balance.

第二个能放葡萄糖的器官。

⑧ 唯一造酮体却用不了的器官是肝，因为它缺那个 CoA 转移酶 ketone bodies

The liver is the one organ that makes ketone bodies and cannot use them, because it lacks succinyl-CoA:3-ketoacid CoA transferase, so all of them go to other organs.

肝做，给别的器官用。

⑨ 药物之所以在肝代谢，是因为肠吸收的东西先到肝 ⭐ Hodek xenobiotic metabolism

Drugs, ethanol and other xenobiotics are processed in the liver, mostly by cytochrome P450 enzymes, because what is absorbed from the gut reaches the liver first.

通往他的主场。

⑩ 红细胞用糖酵解的一条旁路来管氧的投放 ⭐ Martínková 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate

Red blood cells control oxygen delivery with a side branch of glycolysis: 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate (also called 2,3-BPG, 2,3-DPG) lowers the oxygen affinity of haemoglobin, so oxygen is released in the tissues.

代谢和血红蛋白在这里接上。

⑪ 书外：褐色脂肪要的产物是热，不是 ATP brown fat · UCP1

Brown adipose tissue makes heat instead of ATP: uncoupling protein 1, also called thermogenin, lets protons return across the inner mitochondrial membrane without passing ATP synthase (also called Complex V, F₀F₁-ATPase). That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑫ 血糖明明很高，肝却在按饥饿干活——因为没有胰岛素 diabetic ketoacidosis

In type 1 diabetes the liver behaves as in starvation although blood glucose is high, because insulin is absent. It makes so many ketone bodies that the blood turns acidic, which is diabetic ketoacidosis.

把前面的饥饿时间线反过来用。

陷阱问法

  • "Why does the brain not use fatty acids?" — Fatty acids cross the blood–brain barrier poorly. · 过不了血脑屏障。
  • "Why do red cells not use ketone bodies?" — They have no mitochondria. · 没有线粒体。
  • "Why does muscle not release its glycogen as glucose?" — It lacks glucose-6-phosphatase. · 缺葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶。

定义

  • triacylglycerol — the storage form of fat, three fatty acids on glycerol · 甘油三酯（= triacylglycerol = triglyceride = neutral fat）
  • leptin — a hormone from adipose tissue that reports how much fat is stored · 瘦素
  • creatine phosphate — a high-energy phosphate compound that regenerates ATP in muscle · 磷酸肌酸
  • acid–base balance — keeping blood pH in its normal range · 酸碱平衡
  • xenobiotic — a foreign compound, such as a drug or poison · 外源物
  • 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate（= 2,3-BPG = 2,3-bisphosphoglycerate = 2,3-DPG） — a red-cell molecule that lowers haemoglobin's oxygen affinity · 2,3-二磷酸甘油酸
  • uncoupling protein 1 — a protein in brown fat that lets protons return without making ATP · 解偶联蛋白 1
  • diabetic ketoacidosis — acidic blood caused by too many ketone bodies when insulin is missing · 糖尿病酮症酸中毒
  • blood–brain barrier — the tight wall of brain capillaries that keeps many molecules out · 血脑屏障
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • adipose tissue — the tissue that stores fat as triacylglycerol and releases fatty acids · 脂肪组织
  • aerobic — taking place in the presence of oxygen · 有氧的
  • affinity — how tightly a protein holds the molecule it binds · 亲和力
  • alanine — an amino acid whose carbon skeleton is pyruvate · 丙氨酸
  • ammonia — NH₃, the toxic nitrogen released when amino acids are broken down · 氨
  • ATP synthase — the enzyme that makes ATP from ADP and phosphate as protons flow back through it · ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase）
  • brown adipose tissue — fat tissue full of mitochondria that produces heat instead of ATP · 褐色脂肪组织
  • Cahill cycle — another name for the glucose–alanine cycle · 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环的别名
  • Cori cycle — muscle sends lactate to the liver, which returns it as glucose · Cori 循环
  • creatine kinase — the muscle enzyme that transfers the phosphate of creatine phosphate to ADP · 肌酸激酶
  • cytochrome P450 — the haem enzymes, mainly in the liver, that oxidise drugs and foreign compounds · 细胞色素 P450
  • fast white fibre — a muscle fibre with few mitochondria, relying on glycolysis for short intense effort · 快肌白纤维
  • fasting — the metabolic state hours after a meal, when glucagon dominates and stores are released · 空腹状态
  • fatty acid — a long hydrocarbon chain with a carboxyl group at one end · 脂肪酸
  • gluconeogenesis — making glucose from non-sugar precursors · 糖异生
  • glucose-6-phosphatase — the enzyme that removes the phosphate from glucose-6-phosphate so glucose can leave the cell · 葡萄糖-6-磷酸酶
  • glucose–alanine cycle — muscle sends amino nitrogen to the liver as alanine and receives glucose back · 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环
  • glutamine — the amide of glutamate, the non-toxic form in which ammonia travels in the blood · 谷氨酰胺
  • glycerol — the three-carbon alcohol that forms the backbone of fats · 甘油
  • glycogen — a branched polymer of glucose, the storage form of sugar in liver and muscle · 糖原
  • glycolysis — the ten-step cytosolic pathway from glucose to pyruvate · 糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）
  • haemoglobin — the four-subunit haem protein of red cells that carries oxygen in the blood · 血红蛋白
  • hormone — a signalling molecule released into the blood that acts on cells elsewhere · 激素
  • insulin — the hormone released after a meal that promotes uptake and storage of fuel · 胰岛素
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid formed by reducing pyruvate · 乳酸
  • metabolism — the whole set of chemical reactions a cell uses to obtain energy and build molecules · 代谢
  • mitochondrion — the double-membraned organelle that houses the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） and the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain） · 线粒体
  • muscle fibre — one long multinucleate muscle cell · 肌纤维
  • myoglobin — the single-chain haem protein of muscle that binds oxygen and gives red muscle its colour · 肌红蛋白
  • NADPH — the reduced form of NADP⁺, the electron donor used for building molecules and for antioxidant defence · 还原型 NADP
  • pentose phosphate pathway — the cytosolic pathway making NADPH and ribose-5-phosphate from glucose-6-phosphate · 磷酸戊糖途径（= pentose phosphate pathway = hexose monophosphate shunt = phosphogluconate pathway）
  • slow red fibre — a muscle fibre rich in mitochondria and myoglobin, working oxidatively for sustained activity · 慢肌红纤维
  • starvation — prolonged lack of food, after which ketone bodies become a main fuel · 饥饿
  • succinyl-CoA:3-ketoacid CoA transferase — the enzyme that activates acetoacetate with coenzyme A; the liver lacks it · 琥珀酰 CoA 转移酶
  • thermogenin — another name for uncoupling protein 1, the proton-leak protein of brown fat · 解偶联蛋白
  • tissue — a group of similar cells carrying out the same function · 组织
  • type 1 diabetes — diabetes in which the pancreas makes no insulin · 1 型糖尿病

Membranes and transport

卡 x-mem · 先说「为什么必须半透」，四条过膜的路就自己出来了

  1. 膜是什么做的 — 四个数都是书印的 蛋白 ＋ 脂，重量比 1:4 到 3:1；糖 0,5–10%，一定挂在糖蛋白或糖脂上；双层厚 3,5–4,0 nm。 ⚠ 「生物膜」不是一种材料，是一种做法 —— 脂双层到处一样， 各张膜真正的差别在上面插了多少蛋白、插了哪些蛋白（p198）。
  2. 为什么会自己排成双层 — 不是脂互相吸引 是水把它们挤到一起：疏水效应，而书明说它不是一种键（完整的一段在蛋白质的键那一题，这里只借用）。 一条尾巴的两亲分子堆成球，两条尾巴的堆成片 —— 磷脂有两条尾巴，所以它排出来的是片。 片一封口，就是脂质体。
  3. 流动镶嵌模型 ＋ 膜蛋白两类 ＋ 胆固醇 膜的组分一直在动：蛋白能转、能在膜平面里横着走，但蛋白不翻转（脂会）。 外周蛋白只碰极性头，盐就能洗下来；整合蛋白扎进尾巴堆里，要去垢剂才下来。 胆固醇插在磷脂之间缓冲流动性（合成那条在胆固醇那一题）。 书还按功能分了一次：结构蛋白，加上运输 · 催化 · 受体三类动态蛋白。
  4. 为什么必须「半透」— 两个极端都不行 全不透 → 细胞被完全隔离，营养进不来、产物出不去；全透 → 什么都能在任意两处之间乱跑，等于没隔开。 ⚠ 两个极端自然都不允许，所以膜是选择性通透的。 这段论证的好处是它自己就回答了「为什么需要运输蛋白」—— 不是细胞想控制，是物理上只剩这一条路。
  5. 被动 · 简单扩散 — 顺梯度、不花能量、不要载体 书给了三条路：直接溶进脂双层 · 从偶发的孔里钻过 · 走整合蛋白做成的通道。 能这样自己过去的只有三样：脂溶性物质、水、一部分离子。 ⚠ 反过来说就是下面几条路存在的理由：带电的、极性大的、分子大的，全都过不去。 ⚠ 水还另有一条蛋白通道：水通道蛋白，书的年表里就印着（结构 1999）。
  6. 被动 · 易化扩散 — 要蛋白，但不要能量 载体通常是整合蛋白，有专一结合位点。关键三条：专一 · 可饱和 · 可抑制， ⚠ 所以书自己说载体「行为像一个酶」，可以用酶动力学的方程描述 —— 可饱和就意味着有一个半饱和浓度，而半饱和浓度就是 Km 的定义。 例子：GLUT 运葡萄糖，只顺梯度走。
  7. 主动运输 — 初级花 ATP，次级花别人建好的梯度 永远逆着浓度梯度走。初级：书叫它们 ATPase 泵，标准例子是钠钾 ATP 酶 —— 一个 ATP 送 3 个 Na⁺ 出、2 个 K⁺ 进，⚠ 个数不相等，所以这个泵是生电的。 次级：SGLT 让 Na⁺ 顺梯度下落，付的就是葡萄糖逆梯度上行的账。 ⚠ 「次级」不是「次要」，是用的二手能量 —— 最后还是 ATP 付的账，只是隔了一手。 三个方向词：uniport 一个 · symport 同向 · antiport 反向。
  8. 太大的走大块运输 — 胞吞与胞吐 大分子和整颗粒子过不了双层，只能把膜局部重新搭一遍：进来是 endocytosis，出去是 exocytosis； 显微镜下看得见的两种是 pinocytosis 和 phagocytosis。 分泌的蛋白走的是那条现成的路：信号肽 → 内质网 → 高尔基体 → 分泌囊泡 → 胞吐。 还有一类是基团转位：载体本身带酶活性，运的时候顺手把底物磷酸化，于是它再也出不去了。
  9. 膜对离子不透 —— 所以梯度才立得住 质子带电，双层中间明显非极性，它自己过不去。 所以泵出去的就留在那儿，回来只有一条路：ATP 合成酶。 ⚠ 同一条道理撑起区室化：每个区室能维持自己的 pH 和离子浓度， 而书说这种非平衡的离子分布本身就是一种能量储存形式（p017）—— 「细胞把能量存在 ATP 里」只讲了一半，另一半存在两个房间的差里。

Say this

A biological membrane is a lipid bilayer with proteins in it, three and a half to four nanometres thick. Nothing holds the lipids to each other: the hydrophobic effect arranges them, and a phospholipid with two tails packs into a sheet rather than into a droplet. In the fluid mosaic model lipids and proteins keep moving in the plane of the membrane. Peripheral proteins touch only the polar heads, integral proteins are sunk among the tails, and cholesterol buffers the fluidity. ⚠ The textbook argues from both extremes: an impermeable membrane would isolate the cell completely, a freely permeable one would separate nothing, so membranes are selectively permeable. There are four routes across. Simple diffusion needs no protein and carries lipophilic substances, water and some ions. Facilitated diffusion needs a channel or a carrier, and it is specific, saturable and inhibitable, so the carrier behaves like an enzyme. Active transport always goes against the gradient: primary transport spends ATP, as the sodium-potassium ATPase does, and secondary transport spends a gradient that primary transport built earlier. Anything larger needs endocytosis or exocytosis, which rebuild the membrane locally. And because the bilayer is impermeable to ions, a proton gradient stays where it is pumped, which is what the respiratory chain (also called electron transport chain) and the compartments of metabolism both rest on.

约 55 秒 · 「两个极端都不行，所以必须选择性通透」是这一段的因果

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背

① 双层里一个共价键都没有，却能自己排出来——因为磷脂两亲 amphipathic · self-assembly

The bilayer forms by itself, and the textbook says the interactions between heads and between tails are exclusively noncovalent. That works because a phospholipid is amphipathic: one polar head, two nonpolar tails.

没有东西粘住它，是水不肯让它散开。

② 书外 · general：尾巴的条数决定堆成什么——一条堆成胶束，两条堆成双层，封闭的双层是脂质体 micelle · bilayer · liposome

The number of tails decides the shape: a single-tailed molecule, such as a soap, packs into a micelle with the tails inside; a two-tailed one packs into a bilayer. A closed bilayer vesicle is a liposome. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

形状决定堆法：球还是片。

③ 流动镶嵌模型里蛋白只在膜平面内转和滑，从不翻面，而脂会翻 fluid mosaic model

In the fluid mosaic model everything moves, but not in every direction: a protein can rotate and travel sideways in the plane, and does not flip across the membrane, although lipids do.

脂会翻转，蛋白不会。

④ 分外周和整合蛋白的判据是「用什么才洗得下来」：简单处理，还是去垢剂 peripheral · integral proteins

The two classes are told apart by what releases them. Peripheral proteins touch only the polar heads, and the textbook says a relatively simple procedure releases them. Integral proteins sit among the tails by hydrophobic interaction, so a detergent is needed.

判据是一个实验事实，不是一个定义。

⑤ 运输、催化、受体这三类动态蛋白，书说每一种细胞上都有 membrane protein functions

What the textbook stresses is that three dynamic kinds are present in every type of cell: transport proteins, catalytic proteins and receptor proteins. The other division is the structural proteins.

三类一报，运输、呼吸链（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）、激素三道题的门都开了。

⑥ 胆固醇是双向缓冲，不是单向让膜变硬：热时不让太软，冷时不让太硬 cholesterol · fluidity

Cholesterol buffers fluidity in both directions, not in one only: sitting between the phospholipids, it keeps the membrane from becoming too fluid when warm and too rigid when cold.

是缓冲，不是单向地让膜变硬。

⑦ 两个极端自然都不允许：完全不透进不来营养，完全通透就什么都拦不住 selectively permeable

Neither extreme is allowed in nature. If membranes were impermeable, nutrition could not enter and products could not leave; if they were freely permeable, any substance could move between any two regions.

这段论证自己回答了「为什么需要运输蛋白」。

⑧ 简单扩散不花能量，所以只能顺着梯度走；书给三条路：直接穿脂、偶发的孔、整合蛋白的通道 simple diffusion

Simple diffusion spends no energy, so it can only follow the gradient. The textbook gives three routes: straight through the bilayer, through random pores, or through a channel made by an integral protein.

能自己过去的只有三样：脂溶性、水、一部分离子。

⑨ 水通道蛋白的本事是让水极快通过，却把离子和质子挡在外面 aquaporin

What makes the aquaporin remarkable is that its narrow pore passes water very fast while keeping ions and protons out. It is an integral membrane protein, and the textbook's timeline dates its structure to 1999.

同一张年表上还有钾通道的空间结构。

⑩ 载体的行为像酶，所以书说可以直接用酶动力学的方程来描述它 facilitated diffusion · carrier

A carrier behaves like an enzyme, so the textbook says it can be described by the equations of enzyme kinetics. It has a specific binding site, and the process is specific, saturable and inhibitable.

可饱和 → 有半饱和浓度 → 那就是 Km。

⑪ 离子的两种专门载体，书给的区别只有一条：离子载体不是蛋白，离子通道是蛋白 ionophore · ion channel

The two specialised carriers for ions differ in one thing only: an ionophore is not a protein, while an ion channel is a membrane protein.

一个是小分子，一个是蛋白。

⑫ 一个 ATP 送三个钠出去、两个钾进来，两边都逆着梯度——初级主动运输的标准例子 sodium-potassium ATPase

One ATP moves three sodium ions out and two potassium ions in, both against their gradients. The sodium-potassium ATPase is generally the standard example of primary active transport; the textbook names the class, ATPase pumps, but no example.

类别是书上的，名字是通用的。

⑬ 书外 · general：三出两进不相等，所以泵是生电的，膜电位就建在它维持的梯度上；乌本苷抑制它 electrogenic · ouabain

Because the counts are unequal the pump is electrogenic: it builds a charge difference as well as a concentration one, and the membrane potential rests on the gradients it maintains. Ouabain, a cardiac glycoside, inhibits it. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

生电 ＝ 同时立起电荷差。

⑭ 次级主动运输不直接用 ATP，花的是初级泵先建好的梯度 secondary active transport · SGLT

Secondary active transport uses no ATP directly: it spends a concentration gradient of another substance, one that primary transport built earlier. Generally the SGLT carrier is the example: sodium running down its gradient pays for glucose moving up its own.

二手能量，最后还是 ATP 付的账。

⑮ 三个词的前缀本身就是答案：uni 一个，sym 同向，anti 反向 uniport · symport · antiport

The prefixes carry the meaning: uniport moves one substance, symport moves two or more the same way, and antiport moves them in opposite directions.

uni 一个 · sym 同向 · anti 反向。

⑯ 基团转位是唯一在运输途中把底物改掉的方式，因为载体本身就是酶 group translocation

Group translocation is the only transport in which the cargo is chemically changed on the way, because the carrier itself has an enzyme activity. The textbook names phosphorylation and hydrolysis, with sugars, amino acids and peptides as the cargo.

糖一进来就被磷酸化，于是再也出不去。

⑰ 只有胞吞胞吐要把膜局部重搭一遍，也只有它们能运大分子和整颗粒子 endocytosis · exocytosis

Whole particles and bulk material cannot cross a membrane at all, so they move by rebuilding the membrane locally: endocytosis inwards and exocytosis outwards. The forms large enough to see under a microscope are pinocytosis and phagocytosis.

唯一一条要重搭膜的路。

⑱ 每个分泌蛋白走的都是同一条固定的路，终点就是胞吐；分拣发生在高尔基体 secretory pathway · Golgi apparatus

Every secreted protein follows the same fixed route, ending in exocytosis: signal peptide (also called signal sequence), endoplasmic reticulum, Golgi apparatus, secretory vesicle. The Golgi is where the sugar chains are finished and each protein is sorted to its destination.

高尔基体既是加工台，也是分拣台。

⑲ 溶酶体是酸性的，降解酶也只在酸里工作，胞吞进来的东西就在那里被分解 lysosome

The lysosome is the acidic compartment holding the degradative enzymes, which work at acid pH, and material taken in by endocytosis is broken down there. Its enzymes arrive from the Golgi, carrying the mannose-6-phosphate tag.

酸性 ＋ 降解酶 ＋ 胞吞的终点。

⑳ 质子带电，过不去双层的非极性中间层——呼吸链的质子梯度和区室化共用这一条 impermeable to ions · compartment

A proton carries a charge, so it cannot cross the nonpolar middle of a bilayer by itself. That single fact is why a proton gradient stays where it is pumped, and why each compartment can hold its own pH and ion concentrations.

呼吸链和区室化共用这一条地基。

陷阱问法

  • "What is the difference between osmosis and chemiosmosis?" — Osmosis is water crossing a membrane towards the higher solute concentration; chemiosmosis is Mitchell's coupling of the proton gradient to ATP synthesis. · 同一个词根，两回事。
  • "Is simple diffusion really protein-free?" — The textbook lists an integral-protein channel among its routes, so judge by behaviour: a carrier is specific, saturable and inhibitable, a pore is none of these. · 用行为判，不用结构判。
  • "Does secondary active transport use ATP?" — Yes, indirectly; the gradient it spends was built by a primary pump. · 隔了一手。
  • "Why can water cross at all, if it is polar?" — It is very small and uncharged, and it also has aquaporins. · 小、不带电。
  • "Which transport can be saturated?" — Anything using a carrier, so facilitated diffusion and active transport, but not simple diffusion. · 有载体才会饱和。

定义

  • amphipathic — having a polar part and a nonpolar part in one molecule · 两亲性
  • micelle — a ball of single-tailed amphipathic molecules, tails inside · 胶束
  • liposome — a closed vesicle made of a lipid bilayer · 脂质体
  • fluid mosaic model — lipids and proteins moving in the plane of the membrane · 流动镶嵌模型
  • selectively permeable — some substances cross freely, others not at all · 选择性通透（半透）
  • ionophore — a carrier for ions that is not a protein · 离子载体
  • aquaporin — an integral membrane protein forming a water channel · 水通道蛋白
  • electrogenic — moving unequal charge, so it builds a voltage · 生电的
  • uniport · symport · antiport — one substance · two the same way · two in opposite directions · 单向 · 同向 · 反向
  • group translocation — transport in which the cargo is chemically changed on the way · 基团转位
  • endocytosis · exocytosis — taking material in · releasing material out, by rebuilding the membrane · 胞吞 · 胞吐
  • pinocytosis · phagocytosis — endocytosis of fluid · endocytosis of particles · 胞饮 · 吞噬
  • osmosis — water crossing a membrane towards the higher solute concentration · 渗透
  • lysosome — the acidic compartment holding the degradative enzymes · 溶酶体
  • compartment — a space whose exchange with the others is limited · 区室
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • active transport — moving a substance against its gradient, which can only be done by spending energy · 主动运输
  • ATP — adenosine triphosphate, the nucleotide whose phosphate transfers supply the energy for cell work · 三磷酸腺苷
  • ATP synthase — the membrane enzyme that makes ATP as protons flow back down their gradient through it · ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase）
  • ATPase pump — a membrane protein that hydrolyses ATP and uses that energy to move a solute uphill · ATP 酶泵
  • carrier — a transport protein that binds its cargo at a specific site and changes shape to release it on the other side · 载体
  • channel — a transport protein forming a pore through which a substance passes down its gradient · 通道
  • chemiosmosis — the coupling of a proton gradient across a membrane to ATP synthesis; it concerns protons and energy, not the movement of water · 化学渗透
  • cholesterol — the animal sterol that sits between the phospholipids and buffers membrane fluidity · 胆固醇
  • detergent — an amphipathic molecule that surrounds lipids and membrane proteins and takes them into solution · 去垢剂
  • diffusion — the net movement of a substance from higher to lower concentration by random thermal motion · 扩散
  • endoplasmic reticulum — the network of membrane sacs where secreted and membrane proteins enter and fold · 内质网
  • facilitated diffusion — movement down a gradient through a channel or carrier, spending no energy · 易化扩散
  • GLUT — glucose transporter, the family of carriers that move glucose down its gradient · 葡萄糖转运蛋白
  • glycolipid — a lipid carrying a covalently attached sugar chain · 糖脂
  • glycoprotein — a protein carrying covalently attached sugar chains · 糖蛋白
  • Golgi apparatus — the stack of membrane sacs that finishes sugar chains and sorts proteins to their destinations · 高尔基体
  • gradient — a difference in concentration, or in charge, between two places · 梯度
  • hydrophobic effect — the pushing together of nonpolar groups by water, which gains disorder when they are not separately surrounded · 疏水效应
  • integral protein — a membrane protein sunk among the lipid tails, released only by a detergent · 整合蛋白
  • ion — an atom or molecule carrying a net electric charge · 离子
  • ion channel — a membrane protein forming a pore selective for one kind of ion · 离子通道
  • Km — the substrate concentration at which an enzyme, or a carrier, works at half its maximum rate · 米氏常数（= Km = Michaelis constant）
  • lipid bilayer — two sheets of lipid lying tail to tail, with the polar heads facing the water on both sides · 脂双层
  • mannose-6-phosphate — the sugar tag added in the Golgi that sends an enzyme to the lysosome · 甘露糖-6-磷酸
  • membrane — a lipid bilayer with proteins in it, separating two aqueous spaces · 生物膜
  • membrane potential — the difference in electrical charge across a membrane · 膜电位
  • organelle — a membrane-bounded structure inside a eukaryotic cell that does one particular job · 细胞器
  • ouabain — a cardiac glycoside that inhibits the sodium-potassium ATPase · 哇巴因
  • peripheral protein — a membrane protein touching only the polar heads, released by a relatively simple treatment · 外周蛋白
  • permeability — how readily a given substance passes through a membrane · 通透性
  • pH — the negative logarithm of the hydrogen ion concentration, a measure of acidity · pH
  • phospholipid — a lipid with a phosphate-containing polar head and two nonpolar tails · 磷脂
  • polar · nonpolar — having an uneven distribution of charge, so mixing with water · having none, so avoiding it · 极性 · 非极性
  • primary active transport — uphill transport driven directly by the hydrolysis of ATP · 初级主动运输
  • proton gradient — a difference in proton concentration, and so in charge, across a membrane · 质子梯度
  • pump — a transport protein that spends energy to move a substance against its gradient · 泵
  • respiratory chain — the mitochondrial carriers that pass electrons to oxygen and pump protons as they do · 呼吸链
  • saturable — reaching a maximum rate once every binding site is occupied · 可饱和
  • secondary active transport — uphill transport paid for by the gradient of another substance, built earlier by a pump · 次级主动运输
  • secretory vesicle — a membrane sac carrying protein from the Golgi to the cell surface · 分泌囊泡
  • SGLT — sodium–glucose cotransporter, which carries glucose uphill using the sodium gradient · 钠-葡萄糖共转运蛋白
  • signal peptide — a short sequence at the start of a protein chain that directs it to the endoplasmic reticulum · 信号肽（= signal peptide = signal sequence）
  • simple diffusion — diffusion across a membrane with no transport protein and no energy · 简单扩散
  • sodium-potassium ATPase — the pump that moves three sodium ions out and two potassium ions in for each ATP · 钠钾 ATP 酶
  • solute — the substance dissolved in a solvent · 溶质
  • transport — the movement of a substance across a membrane from one compartment to another · 运输
  • vesicle — a small closed sac of membrane inside a cell · 囊泡

Post-translational modification

卡 13 · 06 · 基因只写了序列，功能是修饰出来的（⚠ 这一节原来一页纸上没有）

  1. 什么时候跑 — 翻译当中和翻译之后 一条刚合成的肽链还不是成品。折叠、切割、加基团之后才有功能。 ⚠ 一个基因因此能给出好几种不同的蛋白。
  2. 信号肽（= signal peptide = signal sequence） — 先决定这条蛋白去哪儿 N 端一段 15–30 个氨基酸，中间是一段疏水核心，它就是邮寄地址。 SRP 在翻译刚开头就认出它，把核糖体暂停一下，拖到 ER 膜上的受体那儿， 新生肽链一边合成一边穿过转运通道进 ER 腔（共翻译转运），进去之后信号肽酶把它切掉。 ⚠ 没有信号肽的蛋白就留在胞质里 —— 有没有这一段，决定了这条蛋白的一生走哪条路。 ⚠ 你自己会用到：想让蛋白分泌到周质或培养基，就在前面接一段信号肽（pelB、OmpA 这些）。
  3. N-糖基化 — 在 ER 开始，在 Golgi 完成 ⚠ 正因为蛋白是这样一边合成一边进 ER 的，糖基转移酶才能趁它还没折叠起来就下手 —— 上一步和这一步是连着的，不是两件事。 ⚠ 核心糖链是预先造好的，挂在一个叫 多萜醇（dolichol）的脂类载体上等着。 蛋白正在进入 ER 的时候（共翻译），这条现成的糖链认出序列 Asn-X-Ser/Thr（⚠ X 可以是任何氨基酸，但不能是脯氨酸）， 整条转移到天冬酰胺侧链的酰胺氮上。 ⚠ N 就是从 Asn 来的 —— 接在氮上。之后在 Golgi 里被修剪和加工成最终形态。
  4. O-糖基化 — 在 Golgi，规矩完全不同 接在 Ser 或 Thr 的羟基氧上，一个糖一个糖地加， ⚠ 没有共识序列，也不用脂载体 —— 跟 N-糖基化的三处差别正好成对比： 接在氮 vs 接在氧 · 整条转移 vs 逐个添加 · 有序列要求 vs 没有。
  5. 磷酸化 — 最常见的开关 加在 Ser · Thr · Tyr 的羟基上（⚠ 三个都有 −OH，这就是为什么是它们）。 激酶加上、磷酸酶拿掉，可逆，所以适合当开关。 ⚠ 记那两个反向的例子：糖原磷酸化酶磷酸化后开，PDH 磷酸化后关。
  6. 其余几种，各有各的用处 乙酰化（组蛋白，中和赖氨酸的正电，染色质松开）· 甲基化（组蛋白和 DNA，调控）· 泛素化（降解的标签，送去蛋白酶体）· 羟化（胶原的脯氨酸和赖氨酸，⚠ 需要维生素 C，缺了就是坏血病）· γ-羧化（凝血因子的谷氨酸，⚠ 需要维生素 K）· 蛋白水解激活（酶原切成活性酶，如胰蛋白酶原 → 胰蛋白酶，不可逆）。
  7. 为什么这跟你的实验有关 ⚠ 大肠杆菌不做糖基化，也不太会做二硫键。 所以要表达一个需要糖基化才有功能的真核蛋白，就得换宿主 （酵母、昆虫细胞、哺乳动物细胞）。这是选表达系统时第一个要问的问题。

Say this

A newly made polypeptide is not yet a finished protein, and post-translational modification is why one gene can give several different products. Where a protein is modified depends first on where it is sent, and that is decided by a signal peptide (also called signal sequence): a short hydrophobic stretch at the N-terminus that is recognised by SRP as soon as it emerges, so the ribosome is brought to the ER membrane and the chain is threaded into the ER as it is being made. The signal peptide is then cut off. And that is exactly why N-glycosylation can be co-translational. N-glycosylation begins in the ER and is completed in the Golgi. The core sugar chain is pre-assembled on a lipid carrier called dolichol, and while the protein is still entering the ER that whole chain is transferred onto the amide nitrogen of an asparagine, at the sequence Asn–X–Ser or Thr, where X can be anything except proline. O-glycosylation is different: it happens in the Golgi, the sugars are added one at a time onto the hydroxyl oxygen of serine or threonine, and there is no consensus sequence. The most common regulatory modification is phosphorylation, on the hydroxyls of serine, threonine and tyrosine, and it is reversible, which is what makes it a switch. ⚠ And practically: E. coli does not glycosylate, so a protein that needs its sugars has to be expressed in a eukaryotic host.

约 55 秒 · 「预先造好挂在多萜醇上」和「X 不能是脯氨酸」是两个细节分

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 4 位考官：Martínková、Šulc、Lipovová、Hodek

① 要在几秒内反应就不能靠造新蛋白，只能改手上已有的那个 speed of regulation

A cell that must respond in seconds cannot make a new protein: it modifies one it already has, for example by phosphorylation, and most modifications are reversible.

快，而且多数可逆。

② 胞质是还原的，内质网腔是氧化的，所以二硫键只在内质网里成 disulfide bonds in the ER

The cytosol is reducing and the ER lumen is oxidising, so disulfide bonds, links between two cysteines, form in secreted proteins such as antibodies and insulin, helped by protein disulfide isomerase.

分泌蛋白多二硫键，胞质蛋白少。

③ 糖链还被拿来当折叠的检查手段：calnexin 抓着不放，过不了关就退回去降解 ER quality control · ERAD

The sugar chain doubles as a folding check: calnexin, an ER membrane protein, holds an N-linked glycoprotein until it folds, and proteins that fail are sent back to the cytosol and degraded — ERAD.

糖链也用来查折叠。

④ 降解是不可逆的，所以细胞周期靠它只能往前走：cyclin 就是这样被清掉的 ubiquitin · proteasome

Degradation cannot be undone, which is how the cell cycle moves only forwards: a chain of ubiquitin, a small protein, is attached to lysine residues and the proteasome destroys the tagged protein — this is how cyclins are removed.

接细胞周期。

⑤ 切一刀是不可逆的，所以危险的活性可以先造好，等到了地方再开 proteolytic activation

Cutting switches a protein on permanently, so a dangerous activity can be built in advance and released only where it is wanted: trypsinogen is cut by enteropeptidase in the intestine, so the pancreas does not digest itself. Proinsulin gives insulin and C-peptide, prothrombin gives thrombin in clotting.

切一刀才有活性，不可逆。

⑥ 这里磷酸要走两步：先落在组氨酸上，再传到天冬氨酸上 —— 两个都不是丝/苏/酪 ⭐ Martínková histidine kinase

In bacterial two-component systems the phosphate moves twice, and onto neither serine, threonine nor tyrosine: a histidine kinase phosphorylates its own histidine and then passes the phosphate to an aspartate of a response regulator.

Martínková 的 AfGcHK 就是这样的血红素激酶。

⑦ 没有那条脂链，Ras 就留在胞质里，信号根本传不出去 lipidation · farnesylation

Without its lipid, Ras stays in the cytosol and cannot signal: a farnesyl group holds it on the inner face of the plasma membrane, and GPI anchors hold other proteins on the outer face.

修饰决定蛋白在哪儿。

⑧ 每种修饰增加一个固定质量，质谱能测出来，基因序列看不出来 ⭐ Šulc mass spectrometry

None of these modifications is visible in the gene sequence, but each adds a defined mass, so mass spectrometry detects them — phosphorylation, for example, adds about 80 daltons.

通往 Šulc。

⑨ 选酵母不选大肠杆菌，买到的就是这两样：二硫键和糖基化 —— 但糖型不是人的 ⭐ Lipovová yeast display · host choice

Choosing yeast over E. coli buys two things: in yeast display my protein, fused to the cell-wall protein Aga2, travels through the ER to the surface, so it can form disulfide bonds and be glycosylated — though with far more mannose than a human cell adds.

选宿主是 Lipovová 的问题。

⑩ 第二相加基团的目的只有一个：让它溶于水，好排出去 ⭐ Hodek phase II conjugation

The point of phase II is solubility: the liver attaches glucuronic acid, sulfate or glutathione to a foreign compound, which makes it water-soluble and so excretable.

Hodek 的第二相。

⑪ 书外：少一个基团，一整套酶就被分泌出细胞，而不是进溶酶体 —— I-cell 病 mannose-6-phosphate

One missing group sends enzymes out of the cell instead of into the lysosome: mannose-6-phosphate, added in the Golgi, is that signal, and in I-cell disease it is absent, so undigested material builds up. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does E. coli glycosylate?" — Essentially no. · 基本不做。
  • "Why do cytosolic proteins rarely have disulfides?" — The cytosol is reducing. · 胞质是还原环境。
  • "Is proteolytic activation reversible?" — No, the cut is permanent. · 不可逆。

定义

  • protein disulfide isomerase — an ER enzyme that forms and rearranges disulfide bonds · 蛋白二硫键异构酶
  • calnexin — an ER membrane protein that binds glycoproteins until they fold · 钙联蛋白
  • ERAD — ER-associated degradation of misfolded proteins · 内质网相关降解
  • proteasome — a large protease complex that degrades ubiquitin-tagged proteins · 蛋白酶体
  • response regulator — the protein that receives the phosphate in a two-component system · 响应调节蛋白
  • farnesyl group — a 15-carbon lipid chain attached to some proteins · 法尼基
  • GPI anchor — a glycolipid that fixes a protein to the outer membrane surface · GPI 锚
  • xenobiotic — a foreign compound, such as a drug · 外源物
  • lysosome — the digestive organelle of the cell · 溶酶体
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetylation — attaching an acetyl group, most often to the amino group of a lysine side chain · 乙酰化
  • Aga2 — the yeast cell-wall protein used as the fusion partner that carries a displayed protein to the surface · Aga2
  • antibody — a protein of the immune system that binds one specific target and nothing else · 抗体
  • asparagine — the amino acid whose side chain ends in an amide; its nitrogen is the attachment point of N-glycosylation · 天冬酰胺
  • aspartate — the amino acid with a carboxylate side chain, the anion of aspartic acid · 天冬氨酸
  • cell cycle — the ordered sequence in which a cell grows, copies its DNA and divides · 细胞周期
  • chromatin — DNA together with the histones and other proteins that package it in the nucleus · 染色质
  • clotting — sealing a wound by converting soluble fibrinogen into an insoluble fibrin network · 凝血
  • collagen — the fibrous structural protein of connective tissue, a triple helix rich in hydroxyproline · 胶原蛋白
  • conjugation — attaching a polar group such as glucuronic acid, sulfate or glutathione onto a compound · 结合反应
  • consensus sequence — the short sequence pattern that an enzyme recognises in its substrates · 共识序列
  • co-translational translocation — threading a protein chain into the endoplasmic reticulum while it is still being made · 共翻译转运
  • C-peptide — the middle stretch of proinsulin, joining the two chains and cut out when insulin is made · C 肽
  • cyclin — a regulatory protein of the cell cycle that is destroyed to let the cycle move on · 细胞周期蛋白
  • cysteine — the amino acid with a thiol side chain, the only one that forms disulfide bonds · 半胱氨酸
  • cytosol — the soluble part of the cytoplasm, outside all the organelles · 胞质溶胶
  • dalton — the unit of molecular mass, close to the mass of one hydrogen atom · 道尔顿
  • degradation — breaking a molecule down into its parts · 降解
  • disulfide bond — a covalent link between the sulfur atoms of two cysteine side chains · 二硫键
  • dolichol — an isoprenoid alcohol in the ER membrane, on which the core sugar chain is built before transfer · 多萜醇
  • E. coli — Escherichia coli, the gut bacterium used as the standard host for protein expression · 大肠杆菌
  • endoplasmic reticulum — the network of membrane sacs where secreted proteins enter, fold and are first glycosylated · 内质网
  • enteropeptidase — the enzyme of the small-intestine lining that cuts trypsinogen into trypsin · 肠激酶
  • eukaryote — an organism whose cells have a nucleus and membrane-bounded organelles · 真核生物
  • expression — producing a protein from its gene in a chosen cell · 表达
  • fibrinogen · fibrin — the soluble blood protein · the insoluble threads it becomes when thrombin cuts it · 纤维蛋白原 · 纤维蛋白
  • folding — the process by which a new protein chain takes up its defined three-dimensional shape · 折叠
  • γ-carboxylation — adding a second carboxyl group to a glutamate side chain, which needs vitamin K · γ-羧化
  • glucuronic acid — the sugar acid formed by oxidising carbon 6 of glucose, attached to compounds to make them soluble · 葡萄糖醛酸
  • glutathione — the tripeptide of glutamate, cysteine and glycine, used in conjugation and in thiol redox control · 谷胱甘肽
  • glycan — a chain of sugars joined together, here the one carried by a glycoprotein · 聚糖
  • glycosylation — attaching sugar chains covalently to a protein · 糖基化
  • histidine kinase — a bacterial kinase that phosphorylates a histidine of its own chain · 组氨酸激酶
  • histone — a small, strongly basic protein around which DNA is wound in chromatin · 组蛋白
  • host — the organism or cell line in which a recombinant protein is produced · 宿主
  • hydroxylation — adding a hydroxyl group to a side chain; in collagen it needs vitamin C · 羟化
  • I-cell disease — an inherited disease in which lysosomal enzymes lack the mannose-6-phosphate tag and are secreted instead · I-细胞病
  • insulin — the pancreatic peptide hormone of the fed state, two chains held together by disulfide bonds · 胰岛素
  • lumen — the enclosed internal space of an organelle or a vesicle · 腔
  • lysine — the amino acid whose side chain ends in an amino group, positively charged at pH 7 · 赖氨酸
  • mannose — a six-carbon sugar, the epimer of glucose at carbon 2 · 甘露糖
  • mannose-6-phosphate — the tag added in the Golgi that sends an enzyme to the lysosome · 甘露糖-6-磷酸
  • mass spectrometry — a method that separates ionised molecules and fragments by their mass-to-charge ratio · 质谱
  • methylation — attaching a methyl group, for example to DNA or to a histone side chain · 甲基化
  • N-glycosylation — attaching a sugar chain to the amide nitrogen of an asparagine side chain · N-糖基化
  • N-terminus — the end of a protein chain carrying a free amino group, and the end made first · N 端
  • O-glycosylation — attaching sugars to the hydroxyl oxygen of a serine or threonine side chain · O-糖基化
  • phase II — the liver's conjugation reactions, which attach a polar group to a foreign compound · 第二相
  • phosphorylation — attaching a phosphate group, usually taken from ATP · 磷酸化
  • polypeptide — a chain of amino acids joined by peptide bonds（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 多肽
  • post-translational modification — a covalent change made to a protein after its chain has been synthesised · 翻译后修饰
  • proinsulin — the single-chain precursor of insulin, still containing the C-peptide · 胰岛素原
  • protease — an enzyme that hydrolyses peptide bonds in a protein · 蛋白酶
  • proteolytic activation — switching a protein on by cutting it, a change that cannot be undone · 蛋白水解激活
  • prothrombin — the inactive precursor of thrombin, circulating in the blood · 凝血酶原（= zymogen = proenzyme）
  • Ras — a small GTP-binding signalling protein, held at the membrane by an attached lipid · Ras 蛋白
  • reducing · oxidising environment — one that keeps thiol groups reduced · one in which disulfide bonds can form · 还原环境 · 氧化环境
  • residue — one amino acid unit within a protein chain · 残基
  • ribosome — the RNA and protein machine that builds a protein chain from a messenger RNA · 核糖体
  • scurvy — the disease of vitamin C deficiency, in which collagen is defective · 坏血病
  • serine · threonine · tyrosine — the three amino acids with a hydroxyl in the side chain, and so the three that can be phosphorylated · 丝氨酸 · 苏氨酸 · 酪氨酸
  • signal peptidase — the enzyme at the ER membrane that cuts the signal peptide off · 信号肽酶
  • signal peptide — a short sequence at the start of a chain, with a hydrophobic core, that directs the protein to the ER · 信号肽
  • solubility — how much of a substance will dissolve in a given solvent · 溶解度
  • SRP — signal recognition particle, which binds the emerging signal peptide and brings the ribosome to the ER membrane · 信号识别颗粒
  • thrombin — the protease that cuts fibrinogen to fibrin in clotting · 凝血酶
  • translocon — the protein channel in the ER membrane through which the new chain passes · 转运子
  • trypsin — the intestinal protease that cuts a chain after lysine and arginine residues · 胰蛋白酶
  • trypsinogen — the inactive precursor of trypsin, secreted by the pancreas · 胰蛋白酶原
  • two-component system — a bacterial signalling pair, a histidine kinase and a response regulator · 双组分系统
  • ubiquitin — a small protein attached in chains to mark another protein for destruction · 泛素
  • vitamin C — ascorbic acid, needed for the hydroxylation of collagen · 维生素 C
  • vitamin K — the fat-soluble vitamin needed for γ-carboxylation of the clotting factors · 维生素 K
  • yeast display — showing a protein on the yeast surface by fusing it to a cell-wall protein · 酵母展示
  • zymogen — an inactive enzyme precursor, switched on by being cut · 酶原

Shuttles & inter-organ cycles

⚠ 散在各条路里、单独看都很小，但合起来只有七个 —— 它们回答的是同一个问题：膜挡住了这个分子，细胞怎么把它弄过去

  1. 总规矩 — 过不去就换个身份 膜不让某个分子过，细胞的办法从来不是硬闯，而是把它变成另一个能过的分子，到对面再变回来。 ⚠ 七个穿梭全是这一招，区别只在换成了什么、以及回程怎么走。
  2. 柠檬酸穿梭 — 把 acetyl-CoA 运出线粒体 acetyl-CoA 过不了内膜 → 跟草酰乙酸缩合成柠檬酸坐转运体出来 → 胞质里 ATP-citrate lyase 劈回来。 ⚠ 回程顺手造 NADPH（草酰乙酸 → 苹果酸 → malic enzyme → 丙酮酸）。 给脂肪酸合成用的。
  3. 肉碱穿梭 — 把长链脂酰基运进线粒体 方向正好反过来。CPT-1 把酰基换到肉碱上 → translocase 换进去 → CPT-2 换回 CoA。 ⚠ CPT-1 是 β-氧化的限速门，被丙二酰-CoA 关掉 —— 所以这两个穿梭是互锁的：一个开，另一个就关。
  4. 苹果酸-天冬氨酸穿梭 — 把胞质的 NADH 运进去（肝、心） NADH 本身过不去 → 在胞质把草酰乙酸还原成苹果酸进去 → 在基质再氧化回去， 在里面交出一个 NADH。⚠ 值 2.5 个 ATP。
  5. 甘油磷酸穿梭 — 同样的事，但便宜一档（肌肉、脑） 把氢交给 DHAP 变成甘油-3-磷酸，在膜上交给 FAD → 出来的是 FADH₂。 ⚠ 只值 1.5 个 ATP。 ⚠ 这两条的差别，就是「一个葡萄糖 30 还是 32」的全部答案。
  6. Cori 循环（= Cori cycle = lactic acid cycle） — 把乳酸从肌肉搬到肝（器官之间） 肌肉缺氧产乳酸 → 血 → 肝做糖异生变回葡萄糖 → 血 → 回肌肉。 ⚠ 这不是省能量，是转移负担：肝花 6 个 ATP 造出的葡萄糖，肌肉烧了只得 2 个。 肌肉把账单寄给了肝。
  7. 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环（= glucose–alanine cycle = Cahill cycle） — 同一条路，顺便把氮也捎上 肌肉里丙酮酸接上一个氨基变成丙氨酸再送去肝； 肝把氨基摘下来进尿素循环，碳架去糖异生。 ⚠ 一趟车运两样东西：Cori 循环只运碳，这一条碳和氮一起运。
  8. 尿素循环（= urea cycle = ornithine cycle = Krebs–Henseleit cycle）那一对进出 — 跨两个区室 瓜氨酸要从线粒体出到胞质，鸟氨酸要从胞质回线粒体。 ⚠ 所以尿素循环「两步在线粒体、三步在胞质」不是随口一说，中间真有一对转运体在跑。

Say this

Several pathways are split across a membrane, and the cell always solves that the same way: it converts the molecule into something that can cross, and converts it back on the other side. Acetyl-CoA leaves the mitochondrion as citrate for fatty acid synthesis, and the return leg also generates NADPH. Fatty acyl groups go the other way on carnitine, and carnitine acyltransferase I (also called carnitine palmitoyltransferase I, CPT-1) is the rate-limiting step, inhibited by malonyl-CoA — so the two shuttles are interlocked. Cytosolic NADH cannot cross either: the malate–aspartate shuttle delivers NADH and is worth 2.5 ATP, while the glycerol phosphate shuttle delivers FADH₂ and is worth only 1.5, which is why a glucose is worth thirty or thirty-two depending on the tissue. Between organs, the Cori cycle (also called lactic acid cycle) sends lactate from muscle to the liver and glucose back, and the glucose–alanine cycle (also called Cahill cycle) does the same journey but carries the amino nitrogen as well.

约 55 秒 · 「过不去就换个身份」是这一整节的那句总纲

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 1 位考官：Ryšlavá

① 所有穿梭的存在理由只有一条：内膜要守住质子梯度，就几乎什么都不放过 impermeable inner membrane

Every shuttle exists for one reason: the inner mitochondrial membrane must hold the proton gradient, so it is almost impermeable, while the outer membrane has porins and lets small molecules through.

内膜不通，外膜通。

② 运输本身也要花掉一部分梯度：ATP 换 ADP 出去，磷酸带着一个质子进来 ADP/ATP translocase

Transport itself costs part of the gradient: ATP leaves the matrix on the ADP/ATP translocase in exchange for ADP, and phosphate enters together with a proton.

所以现代 ATP 数是 2.5 和 1.5 这样的小数。

③ 叶绿体运出去的是磷酸丙糖，不是葡萄糖，而且一出一进把磷酸换回来 ⭐ Ryšlavá triose phosphate/phosphate translocator

Chloroplasts export sugar as triose phosphate, not as glucose, on the triose phosphate/phosphate translocator, taking in one phosphate for each one exported, which returns phosphate to the stroma.

一出一进。

④ 在 C4 植物里，苹果酸当的是 CO₂ 的运输形式，放 CO₂ 的正是同一个苹果酸酶 ⭐ Ryšlavá C4 · malic enzyme

In C4 plants malate is the transport form of CO₂: PEP carboxylase fixes CO₂ in mesophyll cells, and in bundle-sheath cells malic enzyme releases it — the same malic enzyme that makes NADPH.

PEPC 和苹果酸酶是 Ryšlavá 的酶。

⑤ Cori 循环安静时也在转，因为红细胞没有线粒体，永远停在乳酸 red blood cells

The Cori cycle runs even at rest, because red blood cells have no mitochondria and must always end glycolysis (also called Embden–Meyerhof pathway) with lactate.

不只是运动时。

⑥ 唯一一个造酮体却用不了酮体的器官是肝，因为它缺 thiophorase ketone bodies

The liver is the one tissue that makes ketone bodies and cannot use them, because it lacks thiophorase, the enzyme that activates acetoacetate; in fasting it sends them to brain and muscle.

肝只出不用。

⑦ 氨有毒，不能自由地在血里走，所以多数组织把它装成谷氨酰胺再运 glutamine

Free ammonia is toxic, so it does not travel free. Besides alanine from muscle, most tissues send it to the liver and kidney as glutamine, made by glutamine synthetase.

接尿素循环。

⑧ 丙二酰 CoA 是那个开关，它挡住 CPT-1，不让合成和氧化同时跑 glucagon · insulin

Malonyl-CoA is the switch that stops synthesis and oxidation running at once: in fasting glucagon inhibits acetyl-CoA carboxylase, malonyl-CoA falls and CPT-1 is released; after a meal insulin does the opposite.

接激素调控。

⑨ 发作的时机就是线索：一饿、久练就发，那正是必须烧长链脂肪酸的时候 carnitine deficiency

Symptoms typically appear on fasting or long exercise, exactly when long-chain fatty acids must be oxidised: if carnitine or CPT is deficient they cannot enter, giving muscle weakness and hypoglycaemia.

病。

⑩ 书外：甘油磷酸穿梭产 ATP 少但快，昆虫飞行肌是标准例子 insect flight muscle

The glycerol phosphate shuttle gives less ATP but works faster, and insect flight muscle, where it is especially active, is the standard example. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Can NADH cross the inner membrane?" — No, only its electrons are carried across by a shuttle. · 只运电子。
  • "Does the Cori cycle save energy?" — No, the liver spends more ATP than the muscle gains. · 不省能量。
  • "Why is the outer membrane permeable?" — It contains porins. · 有孔蛋白。

定义

  • proton gradient — the H⁺ difference across the inner membrane that drives ATP synthase（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase） · 质子梯度
  • porin — a channel protein in the outer membrane · 孔蛋白
  • triose phosphate — a three-carbon sugar phosphate, the main sugar exported from chloroplasts · 磷酸丙糖
  • C4 plant — a plant that first fixes CO₂ into a four-carbon acid in mesophyll cells · C4 植物
  • thiophorase — the enzyme that activates acetoacetate with CoA, absent in liver · 琥珀酰 CoA 转移酶
  • CPT-1 — carnitine palmitoyltransferase（= carnitine acyltransferase I = carnitine palmitoyltransferase I = CPT-1） I, the controlled entry step of fatty acids into mitochondria · 肉碱棕榈酰转移酶 I
  • hypoglycaemia — low blood glucose · 低血糖
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • acetoacetate — one of the two main ketone bodies, a four-carbon keto acid made in the liver · 乙酰乙酸
  • acetyl-CoA — a two-carbon acetyl group joined by a thioester bond to coenzyme A · 乙酰辅酶 A
  • acetyl-CoA carboxylase — the biotin enzyme that adds CO₂ to acetyl-CoA to give malonyl-CoA · 乙酰 CoA 羧化酶
  • ADP/ATP translocase — the inner-membrane carrier that exchanges matrix ATP for cytosolic ADP · ADP/ATP 转位酶
  • alanine — an amino acid whose carbon skeleton is pyruvate · 丙氨酸
  • ammonia — NH₃, the toxic nitrogen released when amino acids are broken down · 氨
  • ATP-citrate lyase — the cytosolic enzyme that splits citrate back into acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · ATP-柠檬酸裂解酶
  • bundle-sheath cell — the inner leaf cell of a C4 plant, where the CO₂ is released again and fixed · 维管束鞘细胞
  • Cahill cycle — another name for the glucose–alanine cycle · 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环的别名
  • carnitine — the small molecule that carries a fatty acyl group across the inner mitochondrial membrane · 肉碱
  • citrate — the six-carbon tricarboxylic acid formed from acetyl-CoA and oxaloacetate · 柠檬酸
  • citrulline — the urea cycle intermediate made in the matrix from ornithine and carbamoyl phosphate · 瓜氨酸
  • compartment — a separate space in the cell bounded by membrane, such as the cytosol or the matrix · 区室
  • Cori cycle — muscle sends lactate to the liver, which returns it as glucose · Cori 循环
  • CPT-2 — the enzyme on the matrix side that puts the acyl group back from carnitine onto coenzyme A · CPT-2
  • deficiency — too little of an enzyme or nutrient present for the reaction to run normally · 缺乏
  • dihydroxyacetone phosphate — DHAP, the three-carbon ketose phosphate of glycolysis · 磷酸二羟丙酮
  • FAD — flavin adenine dinucleotide, a tightly bound coenzyme from vitamin B₂ that carries two electrons · 黄素腺嘌呤二核苷酸
  • FADH₂ — the reduced form of FAD · 还原型 FAD
  • glucagon — the hormone released in fasting that makes the liver release glucose · 胰高血糖素
  • glucose–alanine cycle — muscle sends amino nitrogen to the liver as alanine and receives glucose back · 葡萄糖-丙氨酸循环
  • glutamine — the amide of glutamate, the non-toxic form in which ammonia travels in the blood · 谷氨酰胺
  • glutamine synthetase — the enzyme that attaches ammonia to glutamate to make glutamine, using ATP · 谷氨酰胺合成酶
  • glycerol-3-phosphate — glycerol carrying a phosphate, the carrier in the glycerol phosphate shuttle · 甘油-3-磷酸
  • glycerol phosphate shuttle — carries the electrons of cytosolic NADH into the mitochondrion as FADH₂ · 甘油磷酸穿梭
  • glycolysis — the ten-step cytosolic pathway from glucose to pyruvate · 糖酵解（= glycolysis = Embden–Meyerhof pathway）
  • inner mitochondrial membrane — the folded inner membrane that holds the respiratory chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain） and ATP synthase · 线粒体内膜
  • insulin — the hormone released after a meal that promotes uptake and storage of fuel · 胰岛素
  • ketone bodies — acetoacetate and β-hydroxybutyrate, made by the liver from acetyl-CoA · 酮体
  • lactate — the three-carbon acid formed by reducing pyruvate · 乳酸
  • lactic acid cycle — another name for the Cori cycle · Cori 循环的别名
  • malate — the four-carbon hydroxy acid of the citric acid cycle（= citric acid cycle = TCA cycle = tricarboxylic acid cycle = Krebs cycle） · 苹果酸
  • malate–aspartate shuttle — carries the electrons of cytosolic NADH into the matrix as NADH · 苹果酸-天冬氨酸穿梭
  • malic enzyme — converts malate to pyruvate and produces NADPH · 苹果酸酶
  • malonyl-CoA — the three-carbon product of acetyl-CoA carboxylase, the first intermediate of fatty acid synthesis · 丙二酰 CoA
  • mesophyll cell — the outer photosynthetic leaf cell, where a C4 plant first fixes CO₂ · 叶肉细胞
  • NADPH — the reduced form of NADP⁺, the electron donor used for building molecules · 还原型 NADP
  • ornithine — the amino acid of the urea cycle that is regenerated at the end of each turn · 鸟氨酸
  • oxaloacetate — the four-carbon acid that accepts the acetyl group at the start of the citric acid cycle · 草酰乙酸
  • PEP carboxylase — the plant enzyme that adds CO₂ to phosphoenolpyruvate to give oxaloacetate · PEP 羧化酶
  • rate-limiting step — the slowest, regulated step, which sets how fast the whole pathway runs · 限速步骤
  • shuttle — a pair of reactions that carries a group or electrons across a membrane the molecule itself cannot cross · 穿梭
  • stroma — the soluble interior of the chloroplast, outside the thylakoids · 叶绿体基质
  • translocase — a membrane protein that carries one specific molecule from one side to the other · 转位酶
  • triose phosphate/phosphate translocator — the chloroplast carrier exporting a triose phosphate in exchange for inorganic phosphate · 磷酸丙糖/磷酸转运体
  • urea cycle — the liver pathway that converts ammonia into urea · 尿素循环

Central dogma

Replication & repair

卡 16 · 一条连续、一条断成片，都因为只能 5′→3′

  1. 什么时候跑 — S 期，而且只跑一次 半保留：每条新双链都是一条老链配一条新链。 ⚠ 新链永远 5′→3′ 延伸 —— 后面所有的麻烦都是这一条规矩逼出来的。
  2. 开链 — 三个零件先上 解旋酶把双链拉开（烧 ATP）· SSB 撑住单链不让它回粘 · 拓扑异构酶在前面把拧紧的超螺旋切开再接上（不然越解越拧死）。
  3. 引物 — 聚合酶不会从零开始 DNA 聚合酶只能往已有的 3′-OH 上接，所以要 primase 先做一小段 RNA 引物。 ⚠ 用 RNA 当引物是故意的：它后面会被整段换掉，等于把最容易出错的开头部分丢弃。
  4. 延伸 — 领头连续，随从断成冈崎片段 模板反平行，而新链只能 5′→3′，所以一条能顺着叉一直走（领头链）， 另一条只能倒着一段一段做（随从链）。原核主力是 pol Ⅲ： α 亚基聚合 · ε 亚基 3′→5′ 校对 · β 亚基是滑动夹（把酶箍在模板上，决定持续合成能力）。
  5. 收尾 — 换引物、封口 pol Ⅰ 用它的 5′→3′ 外切酶把 RNA 引物切掉、同时用聚合酶活性补上 DNA； 连接酶把缺口接成磷酸二酯键。
  6. 保真 — 三道关，各降几个数量级 碱基配对约 10⁻⁴–10⁻⁵ → 校对再降 2–3 个数量级 → 错配修复再降。 ⚠ 错配修复怎么认出哪条是新链？细菌靠 GATC 的半甲基化（新链还没来得及甲基化）， 真核靠新链上的缺口。
  7. 修复五类 — Don't Be Nervous, Mum's Driving 直接逆转（光复活酶拆 UV 二聚体）· BER（糖苷酶挖单个碱基 → AP 位点 → AP 内切酶）· NER（大块损伤两侧各一刀；⚠ 坏了得着色性干皮病）· MMR · 双链断裂（HR 用姐妹染色单体、经 Holliday junction，准；NHEJ 直接接，快但出错）。 ⚠ 书上只有半句（p079：pol Ⅰ 切除 UV 嘧啶二聚体再补缺口）—— 那就是切除修复，书只是没给名字。先说书那句，再挂通用名。
  8. 真核多出来的麻烦 — 末端 线性染色体的随从链末端，引物一拆就缺一小段，每复制一次短一点。 端粒是那段可牺牲的重复序列，端粒酶（自带 RNA 模板的逆转录酶）能补回去 —— 大多数体细胞不表达它，干细胞和癌细胞表达。

Say this

Replication is semiconservative and semidiscontinuous, and the reason for the second one is that the new strand can only grow 5′ to 3′. Helicase opens the duplex, single-strand binding protein holds it open, and topoisomerase relieves the supercoiling ahead of the fork. Because DNA polymerase cannot start from nothing, primase lays down a short RNA primer. Pol III then extends — continuously on the leading strand, in Okazaki fragments on the lagging strand — with its ε subunit proofreading and its β subunit acting as a sliding clamp. Pol I replaces the primers and ligase seals the nicks. Accuracy comes in three layers: base pairing, proofreading, and mismatch repair, and mismatch repair knows which strand is new because the new strand is not yet methylated.

约 55 秒 · 「只能 5′→3′，所以随从链断成片」是这段的因果

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① DNA 里出现尿嘧啶一定是损伤：胞嘧啶脱氨就变成它，所以 DNA 用胸腺嘧啶 why thymine

Uracil in DNA is always damage, because cytosine can deaminate to uracil. That is why DNA uses thymine instead, and why uracil-DNA glycosylase removes any uracil it finds.

接碱基切除修复。

② 起点的数目是被长度逼出来的：细菌一个 oriC 就够，真核染色体太长，每条要很多个 one origin vs many

Origin number follows from length: a bacterial chromosome is circular with one origin, oriC, but eukaryotic chromosomes are far too long for one, so each has many, used once per cell cycle.

环形没有末端问题。

③ 之所以能证明半保留，是因为一代之后只出现一条中间密度的带 Meselson–Stahl

What proves replication is semiconservative is one band of intermediate density: Meselson and Stahl grew bacteria in ¹⁵N, moved them to ¹⁴N, and after one generation DNA lay between the two in a caesium chloride gradient.

「怎么知道的」。

④ PCR 就是复制换掉三个部件：加热代替解旋酶，合成引物代替引物酶，Taq 代替聚合酶 PCR

PCR is replication with three parts swapped out: heat separates the strands instead of helicase, synthetic DNA primers replace primase, and heat-stable Taq polymerase extends them.

接「克隆与 PCR」。

⑤ 同一招既用来读 DNA 也用来治感染：缺 3′-OH，链就停在那里 AZT · acyclovir

Chain termination both reads DNA and treats infection: AZT against HIV and acyclovir against herpes are nucleoside analogues that lack a usable 3′-OH, exactly like the ddNTPs in Sanger sequencing.

和 Sanger 测序同一原理。

⑥ 同一类酶出两种药：打细菌的那个治感染，打人自己的那个治癌 ciprofloxacin · etoposide

One enzyme family, two drugs: ciprofloxacin, a quinolone, inhibits bacterial DNA gyrase (also called DNA gyrase, topoisomerase II (bacteria)) to treat infection, and etoposide inhibits human topoisomerase II to kill cancer cells.

接抗生素和抗癌药。

⑦ 苯并芘要等细胞想给它解毒时才变得危险：P450 把它做成环氧化物，结合上 DNA ⭐ Hodek benzo[a]pyrene · DNA adduct

Benzo[a]pyrene, from tobacco smoke, becomes dangerous only once the cell tries to detoxify it: cytochrome P450 turns it into an epoxide that forms a DNA adduct, and a mutation follows if nucleotide excision repair misses it.

通往 Hodek。

⑧ 病毒只能在细胞里复制，因为它就只有核酸加衣壳，有的外面再加一层宿主的膜 virus · capsid · envelope

A virus can replicate only inside a host cell, because it is no more than a genome of DNA or RNA inside a protein capsid, in some viruses inside a lipid envelope taken from the host membrane.

离开细胞不能复制。

⑨ 宿主细胞既没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶也没有逆转录酶，病毒得自带 —— 所以这两个是药靶 DNA · RNA · retrovirus

Host cells have neither an RNA-dependent RNA polymerase nor a reverse transcriptase, so a virus that needs one brings it. RNA viruses such as influenza carry the first; retroviruses such as HIV carry the second plus integrase; only DNA viruses such as herpesviruses can borrow the host enzyme.

宿主细胞没有后两种酶。

⑩ 大多数 RNA 病毒的聚合酶没有校对，所以突变快；冠状病毒有校对 no proofreading

Because most RNA-dependent RNA polymerases have no proofreading, RNA viruses mutate fast; coronaviruses are the exception, with a proofreading exonuclease, which is why their genome can be about thirty thousand nucleotides long.

保真度限制基因组长度。

⑪ 溶原我自己天天在用：BL21(DE3) 里那个 DE3 就是一个 λ 原噬菌体 lytic vs lysogenic

I keep a lysogen in the lab: the DE3 in BL21(DE3) is a λ prophage carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene. A bacteriophage either replicates and lyses the cell, or integrates into the chromosome as a prophage, which is lysogeny.

接我自己的表达菌株。

⑫ 易错 PCR 做出来的库并不是真随机：转换比颠换多 ⭐ Lipovová error-prone PCR

Error-prone PCR — Taq with extra manganese and unequal dNTPs — makes a random mutant library, but not an unbiased one: it favours transitions over transversions.

Lipovová 出过这道题。

⑬ 书外：CRISPR 本来是细菌的免疫记忆 —— 存一段噬菌体 DNA，下次照着它切 CRISPR–Cas

CRISPR is a bacterial immune memory: short phage DNA sequences are stored in the CRISPR locus, and Cas9 uses RNA copies of them to cut the same phage on the next infection. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑭ 书外 · general：同样只换一个碱基，后果可以完全不同 —— 同义、错义、无义 silent · missense · nonsense

One base changed can have three quite different outcomes. It is silent when the amino acid is unchanged, missense when a different amino acid goes in, and nonsense when a stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon) appears and the chain ends early. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

同义 · 错义 · 无义。

⑮ 书外 · general：移码通常比点突变坏得多，因为读框整个移位 frameshift

A frameshift usually damages a protein far more than a point mutation does. It comes from an insertion or a deletion that is not a multiple of three, so every codon after it is misread. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

一个点错一个位置，移码错后面全部。

⑯ 书外 · general：一个碱基的三种替换里两种是颠换，实际却是转换更常见 transition · transversion

Two of the three possible substitutions at any base are transversions, a purine exchanged for a pyrimidine, and yet transitions — purine for purine, or pyrimidine for pyrimidine — are the commoner kind. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

A↔G、C↔T 是转换，其余是颠换。

⑰ 书外 · general：密码子的第三位最不要紧 third base · degeneracy

The third base of a codon matters least, because the code is degenerate and wobble pairing is allowed at that position. A change there is often silent. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

简并和摆动都落在第三位。

陷阱问法

  • "Is a virus alive?" — It cannot replicate outside a host cell. · 离开细胞不能复制。
  • "Does proofreading mean no mutations?" — No, far fewer. · 只是少很多。
  • "Why not use Taq for cloning?" — It has no 3′→5′ proofreading. · 不校对。

定义

  • deamination — loss of an amino group; turns cytosine into uracil · 脱氨
  • oriC — the single origin of replication in E. coli · 大肠杆菌复制起点
  • nucleoside analogue — a drug that resembles a nucleoside and is built into DNA · 核苷类似物
  • DNA gyrase — the bacterial topoisomerase that relieves supercoiling · DNA 旋转酶（= DNA gyrase = topoisomerase II (bacteria)）
  • DNA adduct — a chemical bound covalently to DNA · DNA 加合物
  • capsid — the protein shell of a virus · 衣壳
  • envelope — a lipid membrane around some viruses, taken from the host · 包膜
  • integrase — the retroviral enzyme that inserts viral DNA into the host genome · 整合酶
  • prophage — a phage genome integrated in the host chromosome · 原噬菌体
  • transition — a purine-to-purine or pyrimidine-to-pyrimidine mutation · 转换
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 3′ end — the end of a nucleic acid chain carrying a free hydroxyl on the sugar's 3′ carbon · 3′ 端
  • 5′ end — the end of a nucleic acid chain carrying the phosphate on the sugar's 5′ carbon · 5′ 端
  • acyclovir — an antiviral nucleoside analogue, used against herpesviruses, that stops the DNA chain · 阿昔洛韦
  • antiparallel — of the two strands of a duplex: they run in opposite directions · 反向平行
  • AP site — a position in DNA where the base has been removed but the sugar and phosphate remain · 无碱基位点
  • AZT — azidothymidine, a nucleoside analogue used against HIV that terminates the DNA chain · 叠氮胸苷
  • bacteriophage — a virus that infects bacteria · 噬菌体
  • base excision repair — repair in which a glycosylase removes one damaged base and the gap is refilled · 碱基切除修复
  • base pair — two complementary bases held across the duplex by hydrogen bonds: A with T, G with C · 碱基对
