  • benzo[a]pyrene — a polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon from tobacco smoke, mutagenic after P450 activation · 苯并芘
  • Cas9 — the CRISPR enzyme that cuts double-stranded DNA at a site chosen by a guide RNA · Cas9 核酸酶
  • cell cycle — the ordered sequence a cell passes through between one division and the next · 细胞周期
  • chromosome — one long DNA molecule with its bound proteins, carrying many genes · 染色体
  • ciprofloxacin — a quinolone antibiotic that inhibits bacterial DNA gyrase · 环丙沙星
  • coronavirus — an enveloped positive-strand RNA virus whose polymerase has a proofreading exonuclease · 冠状病毒
  • CRISPR — a bacterial locus that stores short phage sequences and serves as immune memory · CRISPR 位点
  • cytochrome P450 — a haem enzyme family that oxidises foreign compounds in the first phase of detoxification · 细胞色素 P450
  • cytosine — a pyrimidine base of DNA and RNA, which pairs with guanine · 胞嘧啶
  • ddNTP — a dideoxynucleoside triphosphate; it has no 3′-OH, so the chain stops where it goes in · 双脱氧核苷三磷酸
  • DE3 — a λ prophage in the E. coli chromosome carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene · DE3 溶原
  • degenerate — of the genetic code: several codons can specify the same amino acid · 简并的
  • deletion — loss of one or more bases from a sequence · 缺失
  • direct reversal — repair that undoes the chemical damage itself, without cutting the strand · 直接逆转
  • DNA — deoxyribonucleic acid, the double-stranded nucleotide polymer that stores genetic information · 脱氧核糖核酸
  • DNA ligase — the enzyme that seals a nick by forming a phosphodiester bond · DNA 连接酶
  • DNA polymerase I — the bacterial polymerase that removes RNA primers and fills the gap with DNA · DNA 聚合酶 I
  • DNA polymerase III — the main replicative DNA polymerase of bacteria · DNA 聚合酶 III
  • DNA repair — enzymatic correction of damage or of mispaired bases in DNA · DNA 修复
  • double helix — the two complementary DNA strands wound round each other · 双螺旋
  • double-strand break — a cut through both strands of the DNA duplex · 双链断裂
  • epoxide — a reactive three-membered ring made of two carbons and one oxygen · 环氧化物
  • error-prone PCR — PCR run under conditions that raise the error rate, to make a mutant library · 易错 PCR
  • etoposide — an anticancer drug that inhibits human topoisomerase II · 依托泊苷
  • frameshift — an insertion or deletion not a multiple of three, so every later codon is misread · 移码
  • GATC — the sequence E. coli methylates on adenine; the newly made strand is briefly unmethylated · GATC 位点
  • genome — the complete set of genetic material of an organism or virus · 基因组
  • helicase — the enzyme that separates the two DNA strands at the fork, using ATP · 解旋酶
  • Holliday junction — a four-armed DNA structure formed when two duplexes exchange strands · Holliday 交叉
  • homologous recombination — accurate repair of a double-strand break using the sister chromatid as template · 同源重组
  • insertion — addition of one or more bases into a sequence · 插入
  • lagging strand — the new strand that has to be made backwards, in short separate fragments · 后随链
  • leading strand — the new strand that is made continuously towards the fork · 领头链
  • lesion — a chemically damaged place in the DNA · 损伤
  • library — a large collection of different DNA sequences or protein variants held together · 文库
  • lysogeny — the state in which a phage genome sits integrated and is copied with the host chromosome · 溶原
  • lytic cycle — the route in which a phage replicates and bursts the cell to release progeny · 裂解周期
  • Meselson and Stahl — they showed in 1958, by labelling with ¹⁵N, that replication is semiconservative · 证明半保留复制
  • methylation — addition of a methyl group; on E. coli DNA it marks the older strand · 甲基化
  • mismatch repair — repair that corrects base-pairing errors left behind after replication · 错配修复
  • missense — a base change that puts a different amino acid into the protein · 错义
  • mutation — a permanent change in the base sequence of DNA · 突变
  • nick — a break in one strand of a DNA duplex · 缺口
  • non-homologous end joining — direct rejoining of a double-strand break with no template; fast but error-prone · 非同源末端连接
  • nonsense — a base change that creates a stop codon（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon）, so the chain ends early · 无义
  • nucleoside — a base joined to a sugar, with no phosphate · 核苷
  • nucleotide — a base joined to a sugar and to one or more phosphates; the unit of DNA and RNA · 核苷酸
  • nucleotide excision repair — repair that cuts the strand either side of a bulky lesion and replaces the piece · 核苷酸切除修复
  • Okazaki fragment — a short piece of new DNA on the lagging strand, each begun from its own RNA primer · 冈崎片段
  • origin of replication — the sequence at which the strands are first opened and replication begins · 复制起点
  • PCR — polymerase chain reaction: cycles of denaturation, annealing and extension that amplify a region · 聚合酶链式反应
  • phosphodiester bond — the link from the 3′ carbon of one nucleotide to the 5′ phosphate of the next · 磷酸二酯键
  • photolyase — the enzyme that uses light energy to split a UV-induced pyrimidine dimer · 光复活酶
  • point mutation — a change of a single base · 点突变
  • primase — the enzyme that lays down the short RNA primer replication starts from · 引物酶
  • processivity — how many nucleotides a polymerase adds before it lets go of the template · 持续合成能力
  • proofreading — removal of a wrongly added nucleotide by the polymerase's own 3′→5′ exonuclease · 校对
  • purine — the two-ringed bases, adenine and guanine · 嘌呤
  • pyrimidine — the single-ringed bases, cytosine, thymine and uracil · 嘧啶
  • pyrimidine dimer — two neighbouring pyrimidines joined covalently by UV light · 嘧啶二聚体
  • quinolone — the antibiotic class, including ciprofloxacin, that targets bacterial topoisomerases · 喹诺酮类
  • replication — copying a DNA molecule so that two identical duplexes result · 复制
  • replication fork — the Y-shaped point at which the strands are separated and copied · 复制叉
  • retrovirus — an RNA virus that copies its genome into DNA and inserts it into the host genome · 逆转录病毒
  • reverse transcriptase — an enzyme that makes DNA from an RNA template · 逆转录酶
  • RNA — ribonucleic acid, a usually single-stranded nucleotide polymer with ribose and uracil · 核糖核酸
  • RNA primer — the short piece of RNA a polymerase extends from, replaced by DNA afterwards · RNA 引物
  • RNA-dependent RNA polymerase — an enzyme that copies RNA from an RNA template; host cells have none · RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶
  • S phase — the part of the cell cycle in which the DNA is replicated, once only · S 期
  • Sanger sequencing — sequencing by chain termination with ddNTPs, reading the fragments by size · Sanger 测序
  • semiconservative — each daughter duplex keeps one old strand and one newly made one · 半保留
  • semidiscontinuous — one new strand is made continuously, the other in separate fragments · 半不连续
  • silent — a base change that leaves the amino acid unchanged · 同义突变
  • single-strand binding protein — a protein that coats the separated strands and stops them re-annealing · 单链结合蛋白
  • sister chromatid — one of the two identical copies of a chromosome, still joined after replication · 姐妹染色单体
  • sliding clamp — a ring-shaped subunit that holds the polymerase on the template · 滑动夹
  • somatic cell — any cell of the body other than a germ cell · 体细胞
  • stem cell — a cell that can keep dividing and give rise to more specialised cell types · 干细胞
  • strand — one of the two polynucleotide chains of a duplex, with a 5′ and a 3′ end · 链
  • supercoiling — the extra twisting of a closed DNA duplex above or below its relaxed state · 超螺旋
  • Taq polymerase — the heat-stable DNA polymerase of Thermus aquaticus, used in PCR; it does not proofread · Taq 聚合酶
  • telomerase — a reverse transcriptase carrying its own RNA template, which lengthens telomeres · 端粒酶
  • telomere — the repeated, expendable sequence at the end of a linear chromosome · 端粒
  • template — the strand that is read, whose sequence dictates the new strand made against it · 模板
  • thymine — the pyrimidine base used in DNA but not RNA, which pairs with adenine · 胸腺嘧啶
  • topoisomerase — an enzyme that cuts DNA and reseals it to change supercoiling; type II cuts both strands · 拓扑异构酶
  • transversion — a mutation that exchanges a purine for a pyrimidine, or the reverse · 颠换
  • uracil — the pyrimidine base used in RNA in place of thymine · 尿嘧啶
  • uracil-DNA glycosylase — the enzyme that removes uracil from DNA and starts base excision repair · 尿嘧啶-DNA 糖苷酶
  • virus — a genome of DNA or RNA in a protein coat, able to replicate only inside a living cell · 病毒
  • wobble — the looser base pairing allowed at the third position of a codon · 摆动
  • xeroderma pigmentosum — the disease caused by defective nucleotide excision repair · 着色性干皮病

Viruses

横向卡 x-vir · 先问「基因组是什么形态、到 mRNA 要走几步」，这一句定了它必须自带什么酶

  1. 定义 — 核酸装在蛋白外壳里，只能在活细胞内复制 衣壳是包住基因组的蛋白外壳；有的病毒外面还有一层包膜，是从宿主的膜上带走的脂膜，病毒的表面蛋白插在上面。 它缺三样：核糖体、代谢酶、产能机制，所以自己既造不出蛋白也造不出 ATP。 ⚠ 「算不算生命」的答案是有争议，通常说成处于生命边缘的实体。别说「最小的生物」。
  2. 分类 — Baltimore 七类，判据只有一句 判据：基因组是什么形态，以及从它到 mRNA 要走几步。 七类：双链 DNA · 单链 DNA · 双链 RNA · 正链 RNA · 负链 RNA · 经过 DNA 的单链 RNA · 经过 RNA 的双链 DNA。 最后两类是逆转录的。⚠ 不按长相、不按宿主、不按疾病分 —— 先把判据说出口。
  3. 复制周期 — 六步，只在第四步分家 吸附受体 → 进入 → 脱壳 → 复制与表达 → 组装 → 释放（裂解或出芽）。 七类的差别只落在第四步：基因组形态决定这一步必须干什么。 吸附那一步决定宿主范围，因为受体决定哪些细胞能被感染。
  4. 正链与负链 — 基因组本身能不能当 mRNA 读 正链：序列和方向跟 mRNA 一样，宿主核糖体直接翻译，所以裸基因组就有感染性。 负链：是信使的互补链，必须先被抄成正链；宿主细胞没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，所以颗粒里必须自带。 同一个判据，两个相反的后果 —— 这是最好的一问一答。
  5. 逆转录病毒 — RNA 先变成 DNA，再插进宿主基因组 HIV 这类逆转录病毒用逆转录酶把 RNA 基因组抄成 DNA，再由整合酶插进宿主染色体。 宿主细胞既没有逆转录酶，也没有整合酶。 这一类顺带把中心法则补完：RNA → DNA 是原始版本里没有的流向。
  6. 突变快 — 大多数 RNA 病毒的聚合酶不校对 没有校对，每复制一次错很多，所以基因组长度被保真度封住。 冠状病毒是例外：聚合酶复合体里有带外切酶活性的校对模块，基因组能到约三万个核苷酸，是已知 RNA 病毒里最长的。 ⚠ 有校对不等于不变异，只是每个碱基出错少。
  7. 噬菌体 — 感染细菌的病毒，两条路 裂解周期：劫持宿主机器造噬菌体、组装、裂解细胞放出子代。 溶原周期：基因组整合进细菌染色体成为原噬菌体，跟着被动复制，受到胁迫信号再转入裂解。两条路都能走的叫温和噬菌体，λ 是典型。 BL21(DE3) 里的 DE3 就是一个 λ 原噬菌体 —— 接我自己的表达菌株。
  8. 负链病毒自带的那台机器 — 真底物不是裸 RNA 负链基因组读不了，宿主细胞又没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，所以颗粒里必须预先装好自己那一个。 那个酶是一条很大的蛋白（常记作 L），而且它对裸露的 RNA 活性很低：真底物是核糖核蛋白——基因组被核蛋白整根包起来之后的形态。 L 还要靠一个搭档亚基（很多科里是磷蛋白）才能接近模板。为什么要包起来：包着的基因组不会被宿主的先天免疫传感器当成裸露的外来 RNA 认出来，也不容易被降解；代价是聚合酶得边走边把外壳掀开再盖回去。
  9. 负链病毒的两个花招 — 抢帽与转录梯度 抢帽：有些分节段的负链病毒不自己造帽子，而是从宿主的前体 mRNA 上切下一段带帽的短片段当引物起头 —— 所以病毒信使最前面那几个碱基来自宿主。（冠状病毒走的是另一条路：自己带甲基转移酶造帽。） 转录梯度：聚合酶只从基因组一端的单一位点进入，每过一个基因间隔区都有一定概率掉下来，概率会累积。 所以离入口近的基因转录得多，远的少 —— 表达量由基因排列的顺序决定。⚠ 这里没有任何调控，梯度只是几何的后果。
  10. 冠状病毒的因果链 — 有校对 → 基因组能最长 → 才需要一套新办法 冠状病毒的每一个特点都从一条因果链推下来，说的时候先给因果再给名字： 聚合酶复合体里有一个带外切酶活性的校对模块 → 突破了「保真度封住长度」这个上限，基因组能到约三万个核苷酸 → 基因组这么长，有用的基因离头部很远，就必须有一套高效表达下游基因的办法 → 那个办法就是不连续转录。 ⚠ 校对不等于修复：校对发生在合成的当时，由聚合酶那一侧自己完成；修复是事后另一套酶去改。
  11. 一个移码位点 — 一条信使读出两条多聚蛋白，比例固定 基因组前三分之二编码两条多聚蛋白。第一条读到一个终止密码子就停；但它上游有一段滑动序列，下游有一个 RNA 假结。 假结挡住核糖体、造出张力，于是一部分核糖体往回退一个核苷酸、换到新的读框，直接读过那个终止密码子，读出更长的第二条。效率大约三成。 ⚠ 这不是移码突变：移码突变是插入或缺失造成的错误，这里是基因组自带的机制，而且比例固定——那正是病毒控制两组蛋白用量的办法。
  12. 不连续转录 — 一整套嵌套信使，共用同一段前导序列 为什么需要它：真核核糖体基本只翻译一条信使最前面那个阅读框，所以要表达靠后的基因，就得做一条以那个基因开头的信使。 怎么做到：合成负链时，聚合酶走到两个基因之间的调控序列处发生模板跳跃，跳到基因组一端那段共享的前导序列上接着延伸。 在不同的间隔处各跳一次，就得到一整套彼此嵌套、末端都带着同一段前导的信使；在哪个间隔跳，决定哪个基因排在最前面。 ⚠ 这里的「前导序列」和操纵子衰减子的前导同名不同物，被问到主动说破。
  13. 双膜囊泡 — 把复制现场关起来 复制不是在胞质里裸着做的：几个病毒的跨膜蛋白把宿主的膜掰成双膜囊泡，复制在囊泡内部进行。 目的是藏起来：复制中间体里有双链 RNA，而胞质里的先天免疫传感器认的正是双链 RNA——正常细胞质里不该有长的双链 RNA，所以它是一个干净的「外来」信号。 囊泡上有专门的孔道把新合成的 RNA 运到胞质去。⚠ 这不是细胞原来就有的细胞器：脂来自宿主，形状是病毒搭的。
  14. ⭐ 两类关键蛋白（一）结构蛋白 — 被装进颗粒的那四个 先把分界说清楚：结构蛋白是被包装进病毒颗粒里、随颗粒一起出去的那些；非结构蛋白不进颗粒，是在被感染的细胞里现做出来、负责把基因组抄出来的那些。 结构蛋白四个： 刺突 S——结合受体、驱动融合； 膜蛋白 M——数量最多，决定颗粒的形状； 包膜蛋白 E——很小，管出芽； 核蛋白 N——把基因组结合成核糖核蛋白（跟负链病毒那一格是同一个道理：包起来才藏得住）。 N 还值得多一句：它有内在无序区，能发生液液相分离，一般认为这既跟基因组包装有关，也跟拮抗先天免疫有关。⚠ 无序不等于没功能，那正是它能结合很多不同伙伴的原因。
  15. ⭐ 两类关键蛋白（二）非结构蛋白 — 干活的那一组，按功能记六样 按功能记，别按编号记： ① 聚合酶核心（合成 RNA）· ② 两个病毒蛋白酶（把多聚蛋白切开）· ③ 解旋酶（拆开前方的二级结构）· ④ 带外切酶活性的校对模块 · ⑤ 甲基转移酶（给信使加帽）· ⑥ 核酸内切酶。它们组装成转录复制复合体。 蛋白酶那一条要说出后半句：正因为能自己切开，才可以把所有东西编成两条长多聚蛋白——用少数几个阅读框装很多蛋白（和酶原、propeptide 是同一个逻辑）。 ⚠ 解旋酶是单独一个亚基，不是聚合酶的一个结构域。加帽要紧是因为没帽子的信使在真核细胞里基本不被翻译，而且帽子让它看起来像宿主自己的信使。 第三组是辅助蛋白：不搭颗粒、也不直接合成 RNA，主要拮抗干扰素通路、干扰免疫信号；很多在培养皿里不是复制必需的，但决定病毒在真实宿主里能不能站住。
  16. 刺突 — 一类融合蛋白，两半分工，还有一道构象的门 刺突是三聚体，两半各管一件事：S1 结合受体，S2 把病毒膜和细胞膜融合。 受体结合结构域在 S1 上，有关闭和开放两种构象，只有开放的那个能碰到受体——所以结合这一步是被构象变化把着门的（接别构和 Kd：表观亲和力里混着这个平衡）。 S1 与 S2 交界处有一个蛋白酶切位点：在细胞表面就被切开，就在表面融合；进了内体才被切，就跟内体膜融合——切在哪儿，就在哪儿融合。不同冠状病毒用不同的受体，受体本身就是宿主范围的决定因素。
  17. ⭐ ACE2 是什么 —— 它本来是一个酶，跟病毒毫无关系 ACE2 ＝ 血管紧张素转化酶 2，长在细胞膜上，朝着细胞外面。 它的本职工作是管血压：血管紧张素 Ⅱ 升血压，ACE2 把它降解掉，所以 ACE2 是降压的那一头。 ⚠ ACE 和 ACE2 是两个不同的酶，方向相反：ACE 造出血管紧张素 Ⅱ，ACE2 拆掉它。 两者是同源蛋白，但降压药里的 ACE 抑制剂不抑制 ACE2——这一句最容易被问住。
  18. ⭐ 病毒怎么用它 —— 受体只是一块认得出的表面，跟酶活性无关 刺突结合的是 ACE2 的外表面，不是它的活性位点，所以病毒不需要 ACE2 的酶活性， 抑制这个酶也挡不住感染。这一句是落点：受体对病毒来说只是一块认得出的表面，这个蛋白自己干什么活，病毒不关心。 ACE2 长在肺、肠、肾、心和血管上，所以感染不只是呼吸道的事。 不同物种的 ACE2 有小差别，结合的强弱就不一样，所以它是宿主范围的决定因素之一。

Say this

A virus is a piece of nucleic acid inside a protein capsid, in some viruses wrapped in a lipid envelope taken from the host membrane. It has no ribosomes, no metabolism and no way to make energy, so it can replicate only inside a living cell. Viruses are grouped by the Baltimore classification: by what the genome is made of, and how many steps separate it from messenger RNA. That gives seven classes. Every virus runs the same six steps: attachment to a receptor, entry, uncoating, replication and expression, assembly, and release. The classes differ only in step four. A positive-strand RNA genome is read directly by the ribosome, so the naked genome is infectious. A negative-strand genome must be copied first, and because the host has no RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, the particle carries its own. Retroviruses, such as HIV, use reverse transcriptase to make DNA, and integrase inserts that DNA into the host genome.

约 60 秒 · 两个必说点：分类的判据是「到 mRNA 要走几步」；宿主没有 RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶，所以负链病毒必须自带

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Ryšlavá、Hudeček

① 一旦整合就清不掉了：原病毒跟着染色体一起复制，每个子细胞都带着它 provirus

Once the retroviral DNA integrates, the infection cannot be cleared: this provirus is copied with the host chromosome at every division, so every daughter cell carries it.

和噬菌体的「原噬菌体」是同一个思路。

② 大多数 RNA 病毒的聚合酶不校对，所以突变快；冠状病毒有校对 no proofreading

Most RNA-dependent RNA polymerases have no proofreading, so RNA viruses mutate fast. Coronaviruses are the exception: a proofreading exonuclease lets their genome reach about thirty thousand nucleotides.

保真度封住基因组长度。

③ 因为群体本来就是一团序列，抗药的变体在用药之前往往就已经在里面了 quasispecies

Because an RNA virus population is a cloud of related sequences, a quasispecies, resistant variants are often present before any drug is given, and selection acts on the whole cloud.

群体的单位是分布，不是一条序列。

④ 抗病毒药能有选择性，是因为靶子在宿主身上根本没有对应物 acyclovir · AZT · protease inhibitors

Antiviral drugs are selective because their targets have no host counterpart: acyclovir against herpesviruses and AZT against HIV stop the viral DNA chain, and HIV protease inhibitors block cutting of the viral polyprotein; that one is general knowledge.

AZT、阿昔洛韦和 Sanger 测序同一原理。

⑤ 书外：奥司他韦打的是最后一步 —— 新病毒黏在原来那个细胞上出不去 oseltamivir · neuraminidase

Oseltamivir blocks the last step of the cycle: without neuraminidase, new influenza particles stay stuck to the cell that made them. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

打的是第六步「释放」。

⑥ 书外：疫苗买的是时间 —— 让免疫系统在病毒到来之前先见过抗原 vaccines

A vaccine buys time: the immune system meets the antigen before the infection, as a killed or weakened virus, a viral protein, or an mRNA encoding that protein. That is general knowledge.

一句话够了。

⑦ 植物没有抗体，所以被感染后的回应是代谢上的 —— 这正是她的主场 ⭐ Ryšlavá potato virus Y

Plants have no antibodies, so an infected plant answers with its metabolism, which is what Ryšlavá's group studies in tobacco. Potato virus Y, a positive-strand RNA virus, makes one long polyprotein that its own proteases cut; that is general knowledge.

接她的主场：逆境下代谢怎么变。

⑧ 这个实验之所以成立，是因为噬菌体自己把蛋白和 DNA 分开了：只注基因组，衣壳留外面 bacteriophage · Hershey–Chase

The Hershey–Chase experiment worked because the phage separates protein from DNA itself: a bacteriophage attaches to a specific surface receptor and injects only its genome, while the capsid stays outside.

证明遗传物质是 DNA，不是蛋白。

⑨ 细菌不靠繁殖也能拿到新基因，转导就是由病毒代办的那一条 transduction

Bacteria can acquire genes without reproducing, and transduction is the route a virus carries out: a phage takes host DNA from one bacterium into another.

另外两条：转化、接合。

⑩ pET 质粒单独放进普通大肠杆菌是死的 —— 宿主没有能读 T7 启动子的酶 λ DE3

A pET plasmid alone makes nothing, because no E. coli enzyme reads a T7 promoter: DE3 is a λ prophage in the chromosome carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene under a lacUV5 promoter.

没有 DE3，pET 质粒就不表达。

⑪ 表型和基因型被绑在同一个颗粒上，所以筛到哪个就能直接把它测出来 phage display

Phage display ties a protein to its own gene, so whatever is selected can be sequenced: the protein is fused to an M13 coat protein such as pIII, its DNA inside the same particle.

蛋白和基因连在一起，筛到就能读出来。

⑫ 书外：病毒载体留下的只有「进细胞」这套本领，别的全拿掉 viral vectors

What is kept in a viral vector is the entry machinery and nothing else: adenoviruses and lentiviruses with their disease genes removed deliver genes into human cells for gene therapy and vaccines. That is general knowledge.

病毒的进入本领被拿来用。

⑬ 书外：CRISPR 本来是细菌的免疫记忆 —— 存一段噬菌体 DNA，下次照着它切 CRISPR–Cas

CRISPR is a bacterial immune memory: short phage DNA sequences are stored in the CRISPR locus, and Cas9 uses RNA copies of them to cut the same phage on the next infection. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑭ 模板从来不是裸 RNA：负链病毒的聚合酶抄的是核糖核蛋白 ribonucleoprotein template

The template is never naked RNA: the polymerase of a negative-strand virus, one very large protein called L, copies the ribonucleoprotein — the genome wrapped along its whole length in nucleoprotein.

包起来才不被当成外来 RNA。

⑮ 这里一点调控都没有，基因的排列顺序本身就定了蛋白的量 transcription gradient

There is no regulation here at all: the polymerase enters at one end and can fall off at every junction, so gene order itself sets how much of each protein is made.

没有调控，只是几何的后果。

⑯ 大约三成核糖体换框，比例就此固定 —— 这是控制两条多聚蛋白用量的办法 programmed frameshift

About thirty per cent of ribosomes change frame, which fixes the ratio: a slippery sequence and a downstream RNA pseudoknot make one message give both polyproteins in a set proportion.

比例固定，是控制用量的办法。

⑰ 真核核糖体只读最前面那个阅读框，所以每个基因都得有自己的信使 —— 嵌套那一套就是这么来的 discontinuous transcription · shared leader

Because a eukaryotic ribosome reads only the first open reading frame, each gene needs its own message: the polymerase jumps at a junction onto one shared leader sequence, making a nested set.

真核核糖体只读最前面那个阅读框。

⑱ 双膜囊泡的用处就是藏：双链 RNA 一露出来，胞质里的传感器立刻认得 double-membrane vesicles

The purpose of the compartment is concealment: viral membrane proteins bend host membranes into double-membrane vesicles, and replicating inside them keeps the double-stranded intermediates away from the cytoplasmic sensors that recognise them.

脂是宿主的，形状是病毒的。

⑲ 「结构蛋白」的定义就是被包进颗粒里的那些 —— 一共四个 spike · membrane protein · envelope protein

Structural means packaged into the particle, and there are four: the spike binds the receptor and drives fusion, the membrane protein is the most abundant and sets the particle's shape, the envelope protein works in budding.

结构蛋白＝被包装进颗粒的那些。

⑳ 书外 · general：无序区不等于没功能 —— 核蛋白正是靠它做相分离 nucleoprotein

Disordered does not mean functionless: the nucleoprotein, the fourth structural protein, binds the genome into ribonucleoprotein, and its disordered regions allow phase separation linked to packaging and to blocking innate immunity. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

无序不等于没功能。

㉑ 非结构蛋白就是那张药靶清单：聚合酶、两个蛋白酶、解旋酶、校对外切酶、加帽甲基转移酶、内切酶 non-structural proteins

The non-structural proteins are where the antiviral drugs aim: the polymerase core, two proteases, a helicase, the proofreading exonuclease module, capping methyltransferases and an endonuclease.

按功能记，别按编号记。

㉒ 没有蛋白酶，多聚蛋白这条路根本走不通 —— 切开之后才组装成转录复制复合体 replication–transcription complex

Without the proteases the polyprotein strategy could not work at all: they cut the long chains apart, and the pieces then assemble into the replication and transcription complex.

解旋酶是单独的亚基，不是聚合酶的结构域。

㉓ 辅助蛋白在培养皿里看着可以不要，在体内才看得出它们在干什么 accessory proteins

The accessory proteins look dispensable because many are not needed for replication in culture, but they interfere with the interferon pathway and with immune signalling, which shows only in an animal.

皿里看不出，体内才看得出。

㉔ 结合这一步被构象把着门：受体结合结构域只有在「开」的状态才碰得到受体 S1 · S2 · receptor-binding domain

Binding is gated by conformation: the spike is a trimer of two halves, S1 binding the receptor and S2 fusing the membranes, and only the open state of the receptor-binding domain can reach the receptor.

结合被构象把着门。

㉕ 书外 · general：在哪儿被切，就在哪儿融合 —— 细胞表面，还是内体里 cleavage site · entry route

Which protease cuts at the S1–S2 boundary decides where fusion happens: at the cell surface, or inside an endosome after uptake. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

切在哪儿，就在哪儿融合。

㉖ ⭐ 「病毒受体」不是 ACE2 的本职 —— 它先是一个管血压的酶 angiotensin-converting enzyme 2

Being a receptor is not what ACE2 is for: angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 is an enzyme on the outer surface of the cell membrane, and its normal job is controlling blood pressure.

病毒之前它就在那儿，管血压。

㉗ 同源，方向相反 —— 而且 ACE 抑制剂并不抑制 ACE2 ACE vs ACE2

ACE and ACE2 are homologues with opposite effects: ACE makes angiotensin II, which raises blood pressure, ACE2 breaks angiotensin II down again, and ACE inhibitors do not inhibit ACE2.

同源、反向。最常见的陷阱。

㉘ 结论先说：抑制这个酶挡不住感染 —— 刺突结合的是 ACE2 的外表面，不是活性位点 receptor is a surface

Inhibiting the enzyme does not block infection: the receptor-binding domain of the spike binds the outer surface of ACE2, away from its active site, so the virus does not need the enzyme activity at all.

受体 ＝ 认得出的表面，不是功能。

㉙ 这病之所以不只是呼吸道的事，是因为受体本来就不只长在呼吸道 tissue distribution

The infection is not only respiratory because the receptor is not: ACE2 is present on lung alveolar cells, intestinal epithelium, kidney, heart and blood vessels.

受体在哪儿，病毒就能到哪儿。

㉚ 宿主范围其实是一个亲和力问题，可以用 Kd 量出来 ⭐ Hudeček affinity and host range

Host range is an affinity question, and it can be measured: small differences in the ACE2 sequence between species change how tightly the spike binds, as a dissociation constant, and so, in part, which species a coronavirus can infect.

接 Kd 那张卡：宿主范围是一个亲和力问题。

㉛ 书外 · general：两种疗法其实是同一件事 —— 把那一个接触面从两边分别挡住 decoy · antibodies

Both treatments cover the same one contact surface, from opposite sides: soluble ACE2 binds virus particles before they reach a cell, and monoclonal antibodies cover that surface on the spike. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

知道机制就知道怎么挡。

㉜ 书外 · general：先有酶，三年后才知道它被病毒用 —— 2000 年发现，2003 年才认出是受体 discovery

The enzyme came first, the receptor three years later: ACE2 was found in 2000 as a homologue of ACE, and only in 2003 shown to be the receptor for the first SARS coronavirus; SARS-CoV-2 uses the same one. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

先有酶，后知道它被病毒用。

陷阱问法

  • "Is a virus alive?" — Disputed; it has no ribosomes, no metabolism and no energy supply, so it sits at the edge of life. · 有争议，别说「最小的生物」。
  • "Does negative-strand mean damaged?" — No; it is the complement of the message and needs one copying step. · 只是多走一步。
  • "Does proofreading mean coronaviruses do not mutate?" — No; fewer errors per base, but the genome is long and copies are many. · 少，不是没有。
  • "Is programmed frameshifting a frameshift mutation?" — No; it is built into the sequence and makes two proteins in a fixed ratio. · 机制，不是错误。

定义

  • capsid — the protein shell around the viral genome · 衣壳
  • envelope — a lipid membrane around some viruses, taken from the host · 包膜
  • RNA-dependent RNA polymerase — an enzyme that copies RNA from an RNA template; host cells lack it · RNA 依赖的 RNA 聚合酶
  • reverse transcriptase — an enzyme that makes DNA from an RNA template · 逆转录酶
  • integrase — the retroviral enzyme that inserts viral DNA into the host genome · 整合酶
  • provirus — retroviral DNA integrated in the host chromosome · 原病毒
  • prophage — a phage genome integrated in the host chromosome · 原噬菌体
  • temperate phage — a phage that can enter either the lytic or the lysogenic cycle · 温和噬菌体
  • quasispecies — a virus population as a cloud of related sequences · 准种
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 5′ cap — the modified nucleotide at the 5′ end of an mRNA; without it a eukaryotic message is barely translated · 5′ 帽
  • accessory protein — a viral protein that is not packaged and not needed in culture, but blocks host immune signalling · 辅助蛋白
  • ACE — angiotensin-converting enzyme, which makes angiotensin II and so raises blood pressure · 血管紧张素转化酶
  • ACE inhibitor — a blood-pressure drug that blocks ACE; it does not block ACE2 · ACE 抑制剂
  • ACE2 — angiotensin-converting enzyme 2, a membrane enzyme that breaks angiotensin II down · ACE2
  • active site — the pocket of an enzyme where the substrate binds and is converted · 活性位点
  • acyclovir — a nucleoside analogue used against herpesviruses that ends the viral DNA chain · 阿昔洛韦
  • adenovirus — a non-enveloped DNA virus, used with its disease genes removed as a gene-delivery vector · 腺病毒
  • affinity — how tightly a protein and its ligand bind to each other · 亲和力
  • angiotensin II — a peptide hormone that raises blood pressure · 血管紧张素 II
  • antibody — an immune protein that binds one specific target on a pathogen · 抗体
  • antigen — the part of a pathogen that the immune system recognises · 抗原
  • assembly — the step in which new genomes and proteins are put together into particles · 组装
  • attachment — the first step of infection, in which the virus binds a receptor on the cell surface · 吸附
  • AZT — azidothymidine, a nucleoside analogue against HIV that ends the DNA chain · 叠氮胸苷
  • bacteriophage — a virus that infects bacteria · 噬菌体
  • Baltimore classification — grouping viruses by what the genome is made of and how many steps separate it from mRNA · 巴尔的摩分类
  • budding — release of an enveloped virus by wrapping itself in host membrane as it leaves · 出芽
  • cap snatching — taking a capped piece of a host pre-mRNA to use as a primer for viral transcription · 抢帽
  • capping — addition of the modified cap nucleotide to the 5′ end of an mRNA · 加帽
  • Cas9 — the CRISPR enzyme that cuts double-stranded DNA at a site chosen by a guide RNA · Cas9
  • cell — the smallest unit able to live and reproduce on its own, bounded by a membrane · 细胞
  • central dogma — the flow of sequence information from DNA to RNA to protein · 中心法则
  • cleavage site — the place in a protein sequence at which a protease cuts · 切割位点
  • codon — three consecutive mRNA bases, specifying one amino acid or a stop · 密码子
  • conformation — one of the shapes a protein can take up without any covalent bond being broken · 构象
  • conjugation — transfer of DNA between two bacteria through direct contact · 接合
  • coronavirus — an enveloped positive-strand RNA virus whose polymerase proofreads, so its genome can be very long · 冠状病毒
  • CRISPR — a bacterial locus that stores short phage sequences and serves as immune memory · CRISPR
  • culture — cells or microorganisms grown in the laboratory in a controlled medium · 培养物
  • cytoplasm — everything inside the cell membrane apart from the nucleus · 细胞质
  • DE3 — a λ prophage in the BL21 chromosome carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene · DE3
  • discontinuous transcription — making a nested set of mRNAs by jumping template onto one shared leader sequence · 不连续转录
  • disordered region — a part of a protein with no fixed structure, which lets it bind many different partners · 无序区
  • dissociation constant — the ligand concentration at which half the binding sites are filled; lower means tighter · 解离常数
  • domain — a part of a protein that folds and works as a unit on its own · 结构域
  • double-membrane vesicle — a compartment bent out of host membranes, inside which coronavirus RNA is replicated · 双膜囊泡
  • double-stranded RNA — the replication intermediate that cytoplasmic immune sensors read as foreign · 双链 RNA
  • drug target — the molecule a drug is designed to bind and block · 药靶
  • endonuclease — a nuclease that cuts within a nucleic acid chain · 核酸内切酶
  • endosome — a membrane vesicle formed when material is taken into the cell · 内体
  • envelope protein — the smallest coronavirus structural protein, E, which works in budding · 包膜蛋白 E
  • epithelium — the sheet of cells lining a surface or a cavity of the body · 上皮
  • exonuclease — a nuclease that removes nucleotides one at a time from an end · 核酸外切酶
  • fusion — the joining of the viral membrane with a cell membrane, which lets the genome in · 膜融合
  • gene — a stretch of nucleic acid specifying one product, either a protein or a functional RNA · 基因
  • gene junction — the sequence between two genes at which the polymerase can fall off or jump · 基因间隔区
  • gene therapy — treating disease by delivering a gene into a patient's cells · 基因治疗
  • genome — the complete set of genetic material of an organism or a virus · 基因组
  • helicase — an enzyme that uses ATP to unwind double-stranded nucleic acid ahead of the polymerase · 解旋酶
  • Hershey–Chase experiment — the 1952 phage experiment showing that DNA, not protein, is the genetic material · 赫尔希-蔡斯实验
  • HIV — human immunodeficiency virus, the retrovirus that causes AIDS · 人类免疫缺陷病毒
  • HIV protease inhibitor — a drug that blocks the viral protease, so the polyprotein is never cut apart · HIV 蛋白酶抑制剂
  • homologue — a protein related to another by descent from a common ancestor · 同源蛋白
  • hormone — a molecule released by one tissue that acts on cells elsewhere in the body · 激素
  • host — the organism or cell that a virus enters and uses in order to replicate · 宿主
  • host range — the set of species and cell types a virus is able to infect · 宿主范围
  • infection — the entry and multiplication of a pathogen inside a host · 感染
  • innate immune sensor — a host protein that recognises a general feature of pathogens, such as double-stranded RNA · 先天免疫传感器
  • integration — insertion of a viral or phage genome into the host chromosome · 整合
  • interferon — a protein released by infected cells that puts neighbouring cells into an antiviral state · 干扰素
  • L protein — the very large polymerase protein carried inside negative-strand RNA virus particles · L 蛋白
  • lacUV5 promoter — a lac promoter variant that works without cAMP and CAP（= CAP = CRP = catabolite activator protein = cAMP receptor protein）, driving T7 polymerase in DE3 · lacUV5 启动子
  • λ phage — the classic temperate phage of E. coli, able to take either the lytic or the lysogenic route · λ 噬菌体
  • leader sequence — the short sequence from one end of the coronavirus genome carried by every subgenomic mRNA · 前导序列
  • lentivirus — a genus of retroviruses, including HIV, used as a vector that integrates into the genome · 慢病毒
  • ligand — the molecule that binds a protein at a specific site · 配体
  • lipid — a water-insoluble biological molecule, such as a fat or a membrane phospholipid · 脂质
  • liquid–liquid phase separation — the forming of a concentrated protein-rich droplet separate from the solution around it · 液液相分离
  • lysis — bursting of the host cell, which releases the new virus particles · 裂解
  • lysogenic cycle — the route in which the phage genome integrates and is copied with the host chromosome · 溶原周期
  • lytic cycle — the route in which a phage replicates, assembles and bursts the cell · 裂解周期
  • M13 — a filamentous phage of E. coli, whose coat protein pIII is used for phage display · M13 噬菌体
  • membrane — the lipid bilayer, with proteins set in it, that encloses a cell or a compartment · 膜
  • membrane protein — the most abundant coronavirus structural protein, M, which sets the particle's shape · 膜蛋白 M
  • methyltransferase — an enzyme that transfers a methyl group; in coronaviruses it builds the mRNA cap · 甲基转移酶
  • monoclonal antibody — an antibody from a single cell clone, so every copy binds the same site · 单克隆抗体
  • mutation — a permanent change in the base sequence of a genome · 突变
  • negative-strand — of a genome: complementary to the message, so it must be copied before it can be read · 负链
  • neuraminidase — the influenza surface enzyme that cuts sialic acid so new particles can leave the cell · 神经氨酸酶
  • non-structural protein — a viral protein made in the infected cell but not packaged into the particle · 非结构蛋白
  • nucleoprotein — the structural protein, N, that coats the genome along its whole length · 核蛋白
  • nucleotide — a base joined to a sugar and to one or more phosphates; the unit of DNA and RNA · 核苷酸
  • open reading frame — a run of codons from a start codon to a stop codon that can be translated · 开放阅读框
  • Oseltamivir — a drug that inhibits influenza neuraminidase, so new particles stay stuck to the cell · 奥司他韦
  • pathogen — an organism or virus that causes disease · 病原体
  • pET plasmid — an expression plasmid in which the inserted gene sits under a T7 promoter · pET 质粒
  • phage display — showing a protein on a phage coat protein, so it stays tied to its own gene inside · 噬菌体展示
  • phosphoprotein — the partner subunit many negative-strand viruses need in order to bring L onto the template · 磷蛋白
  • plasmid — a small circular DNA that replicates independently of the chromosome · 质粒
  • polymerase core — the subunit of the viral complex that actually synthesises the RNA · 聚合酶核心
  • polyprotein — one long chain holding several proteins, which proteases then cut apart · 多聚蛋白
  • positive-strand — of a genome: the same sequence as the message, so the ribosome reads it directly · 正链
  • Potato virus Y — a positive-strand RNA plant virus that makes one polyprotein, cut by its own proteases · 马铃薯 Y 病毒
  • pre-mRNA — the primary transcript, before capping, splicing and tailing have been finished · 前体信使 RNA
  • programmed frameshift — a shift of reading frame built into the sequence, giving two proteins in a fixed ratio · 程序性移码
  • proofreading — removal of a wrongly added nucleotide at the moment of synthesis, by the polymerase itself · 校对
  • protease — an enzyme that cuts peptide bonds（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 蛋白酶
  • pseudoknot — an RNA fold in which a loop pairs with a sequence outside the stem · 假结
  • receptor — a cell protein that binds one specific molecule and lets the cell respond to it · 受体
  • receptor-binding domain — the part of the spike S1 subunit that makes contact with the receptor · 受体结合结构域
  • replication — copying the genome, so that each new particle can be given one · 复制
  • replication–transcription complex — the assembly of coronavirus non-structural proteins that copies the genome · 转录复制复合体
  • retrovirus — an RNA virus that copies its genome into DNA and inserts it into the host chromosome · 逆转录病毒
  • ribonucleoprotein — a complex of RNA with protein; it is the real template for a negative-strand polymerase · 核糖核蛋白
  • ribosome — the RNA and protein particle that reads the mRNA and makes the peptide bonds · 核糖体
  • S1 — the outer half of the spike, which binds the receptor · S1 亚基
  • S2 — the inner half of the spike, which fuses the viral and the cell membrane · S2 亚基
  • SARS-CoV-2 — the coronavirus that causes COVID-19; it uses ACE2 as its receptor · 新冠病毒
  • secondary structure — the local folded shapes of a chain, such as a stem and loop in RNA · 二级结构
  • segmented genome — a genome split into several separate nucleic acid molecules inside one particle · 分节段基因组
  • selective toxicity — harming the pathogen and not the host, by aiming at something the host does not have · 选择性毒性
  • sequence — the order of the bases in a nucleic acid, or of the residues in a protein · 序列
  • slippery sequence — the sequence at which the ribosome slides back one nucleotide into a new reading frame · 滑动序列
  • soluble ACE2 — the outer part of the receptor given as a drug, binding virus before it reaches a cell · 可溶性 ACE2
  • species — a group of organisms that breed together; the basic unit of biological classification · 物种
  • spike — the coronavirus surface trimer, S, that binds the receptor and drives fusion · 刺突蛋白
  • stop codon — UAA, UAG or UGA, at which the ribosome stops and releases the chain · 终止密码子（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon）
  • strand — one of the nucleic acid chains, with a 5′ and a 3′ end · 链
  • structural protein — a viral protein that is packaged into the particle and leaves the cell with it · 结构蛋白
  • subgenomic mRNA — a shorter coronavirus message that begins at a gene further down the genome · 亚基因组信使
  • subunit — one of the separate chains that assemble into a working protein · 亚基
  • T7 RNA polymerase — a single-subunit phage enzyme that transcribes only from T7 promoters · T7 RNA 聚合酶
  • template — the strand that is read, whose sequence dictates the new strand made against it · 模板
  • template jumping — the polymerase leaving one sequence and carrying on from another one · 模板跳跃
  • tissue — a group of similar cells doing one job together in the body · 组织
  • transcription — copying a nucleic acid sequence into RNA · 转录
  • transcription gradient — expression falling with distance, because the polymerase can drop off at every junction · 转录梯度
  • transduction — transfer of host DNA from one bacterium to another inside a phage particle · 转导
  • transformation — uptake of free DNA from the surroundings by a bacterium · 转化
  • translation — making a protein by reading the codons of an mRNA · 翻译
  • trimer — a protein built from three identical subunits · 三聚体
  • uncoating — release of the genome from the capsid once the virus is inside the cell · 脱壳
  • vaccine — a preparation that shows the immune system an antigen before any infection · 疫苗
  • viral vector — a virus with its disease genes removed, kept only for its ability to enter cells · 病毒载体
  • virus particle — one complete assembled virus: the genome together with its protein coat · 病毒颗粒

Transcription

横向卡 x-trans · 不需要引物，这是跟复制最大的差别

  1. 什么时候跑 — 需要这个基因的产物时 ⚠ 转录不需要引物，RNA 聚合酶能从零开始。以 DNA 的模板链（反义链）为模板， 所以新 RNA 的序列跟编码链一致，只是 T 换成 U。
  2. 起始 — σ 亚基认门牌 原核全酶是 α₂ββ′σ。⚠ σ 不参与催化，它唯一的任务是认出启动子、定下起点。 原核启动子认的是两段：−10 区（TATAAT，又叫 Pribnow box）和 −35 区。 认准之后聚合酶把 DNA 撑开一个小泡，开始合成； 起始一旦迈出第一步，σ 就脱落，剩下核心酶 α₂ββ′ 负责延伸。
  3. 延伸 — 读 3′→5′，长 5′→3′ 核心酶沿模板链走，模板读的方向是 3′→5′，所以新 RNA 是 5′→3′ 长的（跟 DNA 复制同一个方向规则）。 前面边走边解链，后面边走边让 DNA 回粘 —— 泡跟着酶一起往前挪。
  4. 终止 — 两种办法让酶掉下来 不依赖 ρ 的：RNA 自己折成富含 GC 的发夹，后面跟一串 U；发夹把酶卡住，A–U 配对最弱，一松就掉。 依赖 ρ 的：ρ 是一个解旋酶，沿 RNA 追上聚合酶，把 RNA 从模板上拽下来。
  5. 真核 — 三套聚合酶各管一摊 Pol Ⅰ → rRNA（在核仁）· Pol Ⅱ → mRNA（最常考）· Pol Ⅲ → tRNA 和 5S rRNA。 ⚠ 记法「一老二新三转运」。鹅膏蕈碱专门毒 Pol Ⅱ。
  6. 加工 — 真核 mRNA 要做三件事 5′ 加帽（m⁷G，防外切酶降解 ＋ 帮核糖体识别）· 3′ 加 polyA 尾（不依赖模板，维持稳定）· 剪接（剪接体切掉内含子、拼上外显子）。 ⚠ 可变剪接让一个基因能出好几种蛋白 —— 这是真核复杂性的来源之一。
  7. 原核对真核，一句话对比 原核：转录和翻译同时进行（没有核膜隔开），mRNA 不加工、多顺反子。 真核：先在核里转录和加工，再运出去翻译，单顺反子。
  8. ⭐ 核糖体是在核仁里造出来的 —— 核仁不是细胞器，是一个工地 核仁 nucleolus 没有膜，它只是「正在大量转录 rRNA 基因的那一块染色质」—— 基因在哪儿，核仁就长在哪儿，转录停了它就散掉。 Pol Ⅰ 在这里转录出一条大的前体 rRNA，切开之后给出 18S、5.8S、28S 三条； ⚠ 5S 是个例外，它由 Pol Ⅲ 在核仁外面转录，再运进来。 核糖体蛋白则是在胞质里翻译出来的，再运回核仁跟 rRNA 装配， 两个亚基分别装好、分别经核孔出去，到了胞质才合体开始工作。 ⚠ 落点：核糖体的两半是分开造、分开运、用的时候才合上的。 这也是为什么 rRNA 占细胞里全部 RNA 的绝大部分——一个细胞要的核糖体太多了。

Say this

Transcription needs no primer, which is the main difference from replication. It reads the template strand (also called antisense strand, non-coding strand), so the RNA matches the coding strand (also called sense strand, non-template strand) with U in place of T. In bacteria the holoenzyme is α₂ββ′σ, and the sigma subunit does not catalyse anything — its only job is to recognise the promoter, and it falls off once initiation has happened. The process has three stages. In initiation, sigma finds the promoter — in bacteria the −10 box (also called Pribnow box), TATAAT, and the −35 box — and the polymerase opens a short bubble of DNA. In elongation, the core enzyme reads the template 3′ to 5′, so the RNA grows 5′ to 3′, unwinding the DNA in front and letting it close again behind. Termination is either rho-independent — the RNA folds into a GC-rich hairpin followed by a run of U, and the weak A–U pairs let go — or rho-dependent, where the rho helicase chases the polymerase and pulls the RNA off. Eukaryotes have three polymerases: I makes rRNA, II makes mRNA, and III makes tRNA and 5S. Eukaryotic mRNA is then processed in three ways: a 5′ cap, a poly-A tail, and splicing out the introns (also called intervening sequence). And in bacteria, because there is no nucleus, transcription and translation happen at the same time.

约 75 秒 · 「不需要引物」和「σ 认门牌然后脱落」是两个抓手；起始 → 延伸 → 终止三段是 09-29 补的

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Martínková

① RNA 出错不遗传，所以 RNA 聚合酶不需要像 DNA 聚合酶那么准 fidelity

RNA polymerase has lower fidelity than DNA polymerase because an error in RNA affects only one short-lived copy and is not inherited.

跟复制对比。

② 抗生素多半打细胞壁或翻译，利福平少见地打转录 —— 靶子是细菌自己的 RNA 聚合酶 rifampicin

Rifampicin is unusual among antibiotics in attacking transcription: it inhibits the bacterial RNA polymerase and not ours, which makes it a main drug against tuberculosis.

选择性。

③ 一个选择性一个不选择：α-鹅膏蕈碱只掐 Pol Ⅱ，放线菌素 D 结合 DNA 把转录全掐掉 α-amanitin

α-Amanitin, the toxin of the death cap mushroom, Amanita phalloides, is selective: it blocks polymerase II, mRNA synthesis stops, and the liver is damaged first. Actinomycin D, an antibiotic, is not selective — it binds DNA and blocks all transcription.

α-鹅膏蕈碱只抑制 Pol Ⅱ。

④ Pol Ⅱ 自己认不出启动子，要 TFIID 先坐上 TATA 框把它放上去 general transcription factors

Unlike bacterial sigma, polymerase II cannot recognise a promoter itself: general transcription factors, starting with TFIID on the TATA box (also called Goldberg–Hogness box), place it there, and distant enhancers add to transcription through DNA looping.

接「基因调控」。

⑤ 书外 · general：这些位点的名字就是发现者的名字 —— ρ 1969、Shine–Dalgarno 1974、Pribnow 1975、足迹法 1978、SV40 增强子 1981 how the sites were found

Several of these names are the people who found them; the history is general knowledge, outside my textbook. Roberts found rho in 1969 as the factor needed for termination in the test tube.

Shine and Dalgarno noticed in 1974 that the 3′ end of 16S rRNA is complementary to sequences before start codons. Pribnow found TATAAT in 1975 in the protected region of many promoters.

DNase footprinting, from 1978, shows where a protein sits on DNA. Enhancers were found in the SV40 virus in 1981.

足迹法：蛋白坐着的那段 DNA 不被 DNase 切，胶上留下一块空白。

⑥ 乙酰化把赖氨酸的正电荷去掉，组蛋白抓 DNA 就没那么紧，染色质才打开 histone acetylation

Acetylation removes the positive charge from histone lysines, so the histones bind DNA less tightly, the chromatin opens, and transcription can start; the enzymes are histone acetyltransferases.

接表观遗传。

⑦ 真核和古菌更近的一条证据就在聚合酶上：古菌的那个像 Pol Ⅱ archaea

One piece of evidence that eukaryotes are closer to archaea is the polymerase itself: the archaeal enzyme resembles eukaryotic polymerase II, while bacteria have only one.

三域进化的证据。

⑧ T7 聚合酶一条链就干完全部活，而且只认 T7 启动子 —— 所以它几乎只转我的基因 T7 RNA polymerase

T7 RNA polymerase transcribes almost nothing but my gene: a single-subunit enzyme from phage T7, it recognises only the T7 promoter, and it is very fast.

接我自己的表达系统。

⑨ 细菌不会剪接，所以真核基因不能从基因组 DNA 直接表达 —— 必须绕 mRNA 一圈 reverse transcriptase · cDNA

Bacteria cannot splice, so a eukaryotic gene cannot be expressed from genomic DNA: I use reverse transcriptase to copy the mRNA into cDNA, which has the introns already removed.

遗传信息也能从 RNA 流回 DNA。

⑩ RT-qPCR 只能测你事先选好的基因，RNA-seq 把所有转录本一起数出来 RT-qPCR · RNA-seq

To measure transcription, RNA is reverse-transcribed first. RT-qPCR (also called real-time PCR (≠ RT-PCR)) then counts the genes I chose in advance; RNA-seq counts all transcripts at once.

「怎么测」。

⑪ 苯并芘自己诱导出那个把它活化成致癌物的酶 —— 经过 AhR ⭐ Hodek AhR · P450 induction

Benzo[a]pyrene induces the very enzyme that activates it: with dioxin it binds the aryl hydrocarbon receptor, AhR, which switches on the gene for cytochrome P450 1A1.

Hodek 定量的正是 P450 1A1。

⑫ 二组分系统的终点就是转录：响应调节蛋白本身就是转录因子 ⭐ Martínková response regulator

The response regulator of a bacterial two-component system is itself a transcription factor: once phosphorylated, OmpR for example binds DNA and switches genes on or off.

通往 Martínková。

⑬ 书外：RNA 世界的遗迹现在还在工作 —— 核糖体形成肽键靠的是 rRNA RNA world

RNA can both store information and catalyse reactions — the peptidyl transferase of the ribosome is rRNA — so early life may have used RNA alone, with DNA and proteins added later. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑭ ⭐ 核仁根本不是有膜的细胞器，只是正在大量转录 rRNA 的那块染色质，核糖体在那里造 nucleolus

The nucleolus, where ribosomes are assembled, is not a membrane-bounded organelle at all. It is simply the region where the ribosomal RNA genes are being transcribed heavily, and it disperses when transcription stops.

核仁没有膜，是工地不是细胞器。

⑮ ⭐ 5S 是唯一的例外：Pol Ⅰ 的大前体切出 18S、5.8S、28S，5S 由 Pol Ⅲ 在核仁外做 pre-rRNA processing

The 5S ribosomal RNA is the one made separately: RNA polymerase I makes a single large precursor, the 45S pre-rRNA, cut into the 18S, 5.8S and 28S, while 5S is made outside the nucleolus by RNA polymerase III and imported.

三条一起切出来，5S 单独。

⑯ ⭐ 核糖体蛋白要走一个来回：胞质里造，运进核仁装配，再分开运出来 ribosome assembly

The ribosomal proteins make a round trip: translated in the cytoplasm, imported into the nucleolus, where each subunit is assembled separately. The two subunits then leave through the nuclear pores independently and join only in the cytoplasm when translation begins.

分开造、分开运、用时才合上。

陷阱问法

  • "Why does rifampicin not harm us?" — Our RNA polymerases are different. · 聚合酶不同。
  • "Is most cellular RNA mRNA?" — No, most is ribosomal RNA. · 大部分是 rRNA。
  • "Why use cDNA for expression in E. coli?" — Bacteria cannot splice out introns. · 细菌不会剪接。

定义

  • general transcription factors — proteins such as TFIID that polymerase II needs at every promoter · 通用转录因子
  • TATA box — a promoter sequence about 25 bases upstream of the start in many eukaryotic genes · TATA 框（= TATA box = Goldberg–Hogness box）
  • enhancer — a DNA site, often far from the gene, that increases transcription · 增强子
  • histone acetyltransferase — an enzyme that adds acetyl groups to histone lysines · 组蛋白乙酰转移酶
  • DNase footprinting — finding a protein's binding site as the DNA stretch it protects from DNase · DNase 足迹法
  • archaea — single-celled organisms without a nucleus, distinct from bacteria · 古菌
  • cDNA — DNA copied from mRNA, without introns · 互补 DNA
  • RT-qPCR（= qPCR = real-time PCR (≠ RT-PCR)） — reverse transcription followed by quantitative PCR · 逆转录定量 PCR
  • AhR — aryl hydrocarbon receptor, a transcription factor activated by aromatic pollutants · 芳烃受体
  • ribozyme — an RNA molecule with catalytic activity · 核酶
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 3′ end — the end of a nucleic acid chain carrying a free hydroxyl on the sugar's 3′ carbon · 3′ 端
  • 5′ cap — an m⁷G added to the 5′ end of eukaryotic mRNA; it blocks exonucleases and helps ribosomes bind · 5′ 帽
  • 5′ end — the end of a nucleic acid chain carrying the phosphate on the sugar's 5′ carbon · 5′ 端
  • 5S rRNA — the small ribosomal RNA, made by polymerase III outside the nucleolus and then imported · 5S rRNA
  • 16S rRNA — the RNA of the bacterial small ribosomal subunit · 16S rRNA
  • α-amanitin — the toxin of the death cap mushroom, Amanita phalloides, which inhibits polymerase II · α-鹅膏蕈碱
  • Actinomycin D — an antibiotic that binds DNA and blocks all transcription, in any organism · 放线菌素 D
  • alternative splicing — joining different combinations of exons from one gene, giving several proteins · 可变剪接
  • antibiotic — a compound that kills bacteria or stops them growing · 抗生素
  • benzo[a]pyrene — a polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon from tobacco smoke, activated by P450 into a carcinogen · 苯并芘
  • catalysis — the speeding of a reaction by something that is not used up in it · 催化
  • chromatin — DNA together with its histones, as it exists in the nucleus · 染色质
  • coding strand — the DNA strand with the same sequence as the RNA, carrying T where the RNA has U · 编码链（= coding strand = sense strand = non-template strand）
  • complementary — of two sequences: each base on one is matched by its pairing partner on the other · 互补
  • core enzyme — bacterial RNA polymerase α₂ββ′ without sigma, which carries out elongation · 核心酶
  • cytochrome P450 1A1 — the P450 form induced by aromatic hydrocarbons, which activates benzo[a]pyrene · P450 1A1
  • cytoplasm — everything inside the cell membrane apart from the nucleus · 细胞质
  • dioxin — a persistent aromatic pollutant that binds the aryl hydrocarbon receptor · 二恶英
  • DNA looping — bending of the DNA so that two distant sites are brought next to each other · DNA 成环
  • downstream — towards the 3′ end, relative to a chosen point on the DNA or RNA · 下游

  • eukaryote — an organism whose cells have a nucleus and other membrane-bounded compartments · 真核生物
  • exon — the part of a gene that is kept in the mature mRNA · 外显子
  • fidelity — how accurately an enzyme copies its template · 保真度
  • gene — a stretch of DNA specifying one product, either a protein or a functional RNA · 基因
  • genomic DNA — the chromosomal DNA of a cell, with its introns still in place · 基因组 DNA
  • histone — one of the small basic proteins that DNA is wound around in the nucleus · 组蛋白

  • holoenzyme — the complete bacterial RNA polymerase: core enzyme plus the sigma subunit · 全酶
  • induction — a rise in the transcription of a gene in answer to a signal · 诱导

  • kinase — an enzyme that transfers a phosphate group onto its substrate · 激酶

  • monocistronic — of an mRNA: carrying one gene only, as in eukaryotes · 单顺反子
  • mRNA — messenger RNA, the transcript that carries a gene's sequence to the ribosome · 信使 RNA
  • m⁷G — 7-methylguanosine, the modified nucleotide that forms the mRNA cap · 7-甲基鸟苷
  • nuclear pore — a channel through the nuclear envelope that RNA and proteins pass through · 核孔
  • nucleolus — the region where rRNA genes are transcribed and ribosomes assembled; it has no membrane · 核仁
  • nucleus — the membrane-bounded compartment holding a eukaryotic cell's chromosomes · 细胞核
  • OmpR — an E. coli response regulator that binds DNA once it has been phosphorylated · OmpR
  • organelle — a distinct compartment inside a eukaryotic cell, usually bounded by a membrane · 细胞器
  • peptidyl transferase — the ribosome activity that forms the peptide bond（= peptide bond = amide bond）; it resides in the rRNA · 肽酰转移酶
  • phosphorylation — addition of a phosphate group to a protein, usually changing its activity · 磷酸化
  • poly-A tail — a run of adenines added to the 3′ end of eukaryotic mRNA without any template · 多聚 A 尾

  • Pribnow box（= −10 box = Pribnow box） — the −10 sequence TATAAT of a bacterial promoter, read by sigma along with the −35 sequence · Pribnow 框
  • primer — a short nucleic acid with a free 3′-OH that a polymerase can extend from · 引物
  • prokaryote — an organism whose cell has no nucleus · 原核生物

  • reverse transcriptase — an enzyme that makes DNA from an RNA template · 逆转录酶
  • rho — a helicase that runs along the RNA, catches the polymerase and pulls the RNA off · ρ 因子
  • rho-dependent termination — termination in which the rho protein strips the RNA off the template · 依赖 ρ 的终止
  • rho-independent termination — termination by a GC-rich hairpin followed by a run of U, needing no protein · 不依赖 ρ 的终止
  • Rifampicin — an antibiotic that inhibits bacterial RNA polymerase; a main drug against tuberculosis · 利福平
  • RNA polymerase I — the eukaryotic polymerase that makes the large rRNA precursor, in the nucleolus · RNA 聚合酶 I
  • RNA polymerase II — the eukaryotic polymerase that makes mRNA · RNA 聚合酶 II
  • RNA polymerase III — the eukaryotic polymerase that makes tRNA and 5S rRNA · RNA 聚合酶 III
  • RNA world — the idea that early life used RNA alone, both to hold information and to catalyse · RNA 世界
  • RNA-seq — sequencing all the RNA of a sample, so every transcript is counted at once · 转录组测序
  • Roberts — he identified rho, in 1969, as the factor needed for termination in the test tube · 发现 ρ 因子
  • rRNA — ribosomal RNA, the RNA of the ribosome and the largest part of the RNA in a cell · 核糖体 RNA
  • selective toxicity — harming the pathogen and not the host, by aiming at a difference between them · 选择性毒性
  • sequence — the order of the bases in a nucleic acid, or of the residues in a protein · 序列

  • termination — the stage in which the polymerase releases the RNA and leaves the DNA · 终止
  • terminator — the sequence at which transcription stops and the RNA is let go · 终止子

  • transcription — copying a DNA sequence into RNA · 转录
  • transcription bubble — the short stretch of unwound DNA that travels along with the polymerase · 转录泡
  • transcription factor — a protein that binds DNA and changes how much a gene is transcribed · 转录因子
  • translation — making a protein by reading the codons of an mRNA · 翻译
  • tRNA — transfer RNA, the adaptor that carries an amino acid and reads the codon · 转运 RNA
  • tuberculosis — the lung disease caused by Mycobacterium tuberculosis · 结核病
  • two-component system — a bacterial signalling pair: a membrane sensor kinase and a response regulator · 二组分系统
  • upstream — towards the 5′ end, relative to a chosen point on the DNA or RNA · 上游

Translation

横向卡 x-trans · 每个残基烧四个高能键，贵得有道理

  1. 什么时候跑 — 有 mRNA 就跑 遗传密码的四个性质：简并性（一个氨基酸可以有好几个密码子，让很多突变不致命）· 通用性（从病毒到人共用一套）· 摆动性 · 无标点、不重叠。 起始 AUG（甲硫氨酸），终止 UAA · UAG · UGA（不编码任何氨基酸）。
  2. 活化 — 先把氨基酸装到 tRNA 上 amino acid ＋ tRNA ＋ ATP →[aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase] aminoacyl-tRNA ＋ AMP ＋ PPᵢ ⚠ ATP 断成 AMP，所以这一步就花掉 2 个高能键。 ⚠ 这个酶是密码正确性的真正把关人：它必须同时认对氨基酸和tRNA， 认错了后面核糖体发现不了。
  3. 起始 — 小亚基先找到起点 原核靠 mRNA 上的 Shine-Dalgarno 序列跟 16S rRNA 配对定位； 真核靠 5′ 帽，小亚基从帽子开始扫描到第一个 AUG。 然后大亚基合上，起始 tRNA 坐进 P 位。
  4. 延伸 — 三个动作循环 进位：EF-Tu 护送氨酰-tRNA 进 A 位，花 1 GTP → 成肽：肽酰转移酶把 P 位的肽链接到 A 位的氨基酸上（⚠ 这个活性在 rRNA 上，核糖体是核酶，不花 GTP）→ 转位：EF-G 推着核糖体沿 mRNA 走一个密码子，再花 1 GTP。
  5. 摆动 — 为什么 tRNA 不用 61 种 tRNA 反密码子的第 1 位跟密码子的第 3 位配对时可以松一点： C 只配 G · A 只配 U · U 配 A 或 G · G 配 C 或 U · 次黄嘌呤 I 能配 A、C、U。 ⚠ 所以一个 tRNA 能认好几个密码子，细胞不需要 61 种。
  6. 终止 — 没有 tRNA 认终止密码子（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon） 释放因子（蛋白质，不是 tRNA）进 A 位，让肽酰转移酶把肽链水解下来。
  7. 能量账 — 每个残基 4 个高能键 活化 2（ATP→AMP）＋ 进位 1 GTP ＋ 转位 1 GTP ＝ 4。 ⚠ 为什么这么贵：翻译是全细胞最耗能的过程之一，代价买的是精确 —— 一个错的氨基酸可能毁掉整条蛋白。

Say this

Translation starts at AUG and stops at UAA, UAG or UGA, and the code is degenerate, universal and read with wobble. Wobble means the pairing at the third position of the codon is loose: the first base of the anticodon can pair with more than one base — G pairs with C or U, and inosine pairs with A, C or U — so one tRNA reads several codons, and the cell needs far fewer than 61 tRNAs. The amino acid is first attached to its tRNA by an aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase, and because the ATP goes to AMP that costs two high-energy bonds. Elongation is then three steps repeated: EF-Tu (also called eEF1A in eukaryotes) brings the tRNA into the A site using one GTP, peptidyl transferase makes the bond — and that activity is in the rRNA, so the ribosome is a ribozyme — and EF-G (also called eEF2 in eukaryotes) translocates using another GTP. So each residue costs four high-energy phosphate bonds, and what that buys is accuracy. Termination is done by release factors, which are proteins, not tRNAs.

约 60 秒 · 「4 个高能键」和「核糖体是核酶」是两个必说；摆动那两句是 09-29 补的

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Lipovová、Šulc

① 整整几类抗生素就靠这一个差别：细菌核糖体 70S，人的 80S 70S vs 80S

Whole classes of antibiotics rest on one difference: bacterial ribosomes are 70S and ours are 80S, so the drug blocks their translation and not ours.

选择性毒性。

② 机制是从亚基推出来的：30S 管读码，所以出错读、挡 tRNA；50S 管成键和出口通道 streptomycin · tetracycline · chloramphenicol · erythromycin

Mechanism follows from subunit: on the 30S, which reads the message, streptomycin causes misreading and tetracycline blocks tRNA entry; on the 50S, chloramphenicol blocks peptidyl transferase and erythromycin the exit tunnel.

两个小亚基，两个大亚基。

③ 这些抗生素在人身上的副作用来源是内共生：线粒体的核糖体还像细菌的 mitochondrial ribosomes

These antibiotics have side effects in us because of endosymbiosis: mitochondria still have bacteria-like ribosomes, so chloramphenicol suppresses the bone marrow and aminoglycosides damage hearing.

内共生的证据。

④ 这两个反过来打真核，而且两个都是酶：改掉 eEF2，或从 28S rRNA 上拔掉一个腺嘌呤 diphtheria toxin · ricin

Diphtheria toxin and ricin attack our own translation, and both act as enzymes: diphtheria toxin inactivates the translocation factor eEF2 by ADP-ribosylation, and ricin, from castor beans, removes an adenine from the 28S rRNA.

反过来打真核。

⑤ 嘌呤霉素连我们自己的核糖体一起打，所以它的抗性基因能用来筛哺乳动物细胞 puromycin

Puromycin blocks our ribosomes as well as bacterial ones, which is why its resistance gene selects mammalian cells: it resembles the end of an aminoacyl-tRNA, joins the growing chain, and releases it early.

接「筛选标记」。

⑥ 一个甲酰基就够免疫系统分出敌我：细菌用甲酰甲硫氨酸起始 fMet

One formyl group lets our immune system tell bacteria from us: bacterial translation starts with N-formylmethionine, and neutrophils recognise formylated peptides.

又一处原核真核差别。

⑦ 链还没造完就开始折叠，所以才需要分子伴侣；一条 mRNA 上同时挂很多核糖体 polysome · chaperones

A chain begins to fold before it is finished, which is why chaperones (also called heat shock protein (many of them)) such as DnaK and GroEL in bacteria are needed; and many ribosomes read one mRNA at once, as a polysome.

接「蛋白折叠」。

⑧ 蛋白在哪儿造，是它自己的序列说的：N 端一段疏水信号肽被 SRP 认出来 signal peptide · SRP

Where a protein is made is decided by its own sequence: SRP binds the hydrophobic signal peptide (also called signal sequence) at the N-terminus and brings the ribosome to the ER membrane, so secreted proteins are made there.

接「翻译后修饰」。

⑨ 一种碱基进去，一种氨基酸出来 —— 第一个密码子就是这样读出来的 poly-U · Nirenberg

One kind of base in, one kind of amino acid out: in 1961 Nirenberg and Matthaei added synthetic poly-U to a cell-free (also called cell-free protein synthesis, CFPS, in vitro transcription–translation) E. coli extract and got polyphenylalanine, so UUU codes for phenylalanine.

无细胞（= cell-free protein synthesis = CFPS = in vitro transcription–translation）体系，今天也用来表达蛋白。

⑩ 密码子是通用的，tRNA 的库存不是 —— 人的基因在大肠杆菌里会卡在 AGG、AGA ⭐ Lipovová codon bias · Rosetta

The code is universal but the tRNA pool is not: a human gene in E. coli can stall at rare codons such as AGG and AGA for arginine, so I use Rosetta strains, which supply them, or a codon-optimised gene.

接表达菌株。

⑪ 密码表上有一个空位可以借：三个终止密码子里把 UAG 拿走，塞进第 21 种氨基酸 ⭐ Šulc amber suppression · ncAA

The genetic code has a slot to spare: the amber stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon) UAG can be reassigned, and an engineered tRNA and synthetase pair inserts a non-canonical amino acid there, such as a photo-cross-linker for mass spectrometry.

通往 Šulc 的交联质谱。

⑫ 书外：换掉一个碱基一次解决两个问题 —— 免疫反应降下来，翻译还升上去 N1-methylpseudouridine

Replacing uridine with N1-methylpseudouridine solves two problems at once in an mRNA vaccine: the innate immune response falls and translation rises. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

⑬ 核糖体离不开镁：太少亚基散架，太多读错码 Mg2+ and the ribosome

The ribosome needs magnesium ions because ribosomal RNA is one long negative backbone, and the magnesium screens that charge so the RNA can fold tightly and the two subunits can hold together. ⚠ Both directions go wrong. With too little magnesium the 70S ribosome falls apart into its 30S and 50S subunits and translation stops; with too much, it reads the message inaccurately and lets wrong amino acids through. tRNA needs magnesium for the same reason — its L shape does not hold without it.

同一个道理也是 PCR 的镁：太少不工作，太多不准确。转化用 Ca2+ 抓 DNA 也是这条 —— 正二价离子中和磷酸的负电。

⑭ S 是沉降系数，不是重量，所以 30S ＋ 50S ＝ 70S，不是 80S why S values do not add up

An S number is a sedimentation coefficient: how fast the particle travels down the tube in an ultracentrifuge. The sample is layered on a sucrose gradient, spun, and stopped before anything reaches the bottom; the tube is then collected in fractions and read at 260 nm, and each peak is one kind of particle. ⚠ Because a bigger particle also meets more drag, doubling the mass does not double the speed — S depends on shape and compactness as well as mass, so the numbers do not add up. That is why the two subunits, 30S and 50S, make a 70S ribosome and not an 80S one.

同一个实验直接看得见镁的作用：高镁缓冲液出一个 70S 峰，低镁出 30S 和 50S 两个峰。⚠ 45S 前体、5S、5.8S 这些名字全是沉降系数。别和等密度离心混 —— 那个甩到平衡、只按密度分，用氯化铯，是 Meselson–Stahl 那一套。

陷阱问法

  • "Why do these antibiotics spare us?" — Our cytosolic ribosomes are 80S. · 核糖体不同。
  • "Then why side effects?" — Mitochondrial ribosomes resemble bacterial ones. · 线粒体核糖体像细菌。
  • "Is peptidyl transferase a protein?" — No, it is the rRNA. · 是 rRNA。

定义

  • S — the sedimentation coefficient: how fast a particle moves down a centrifuge tube, which depends on its shape as well as its mass, so S values do not add up · 沉降系数，不可相加
  • aminoglycoside — the antibiotic class that includes streptomycin and kanamycin · 氨基糖苷类
  • eEF2 — the eukaryotic elongation factor that moves the ribosome along the mRNA · 真核延伸因子 2
  • polysome — one mRNA with many ribosomes · 多聚核糖体
  • chaperone — a protein that helps other proteins fold · 分子伴侣（= chaperone = heat shock protein (many of them)）
  • SRP — signal recognition particle, which brings ribosomes to the ER · 信号识别颗粒
  • codon bias — an organism's preference among synonymous codons · 密码子偏好
  • non-canonical amino acid — an amino acid outside the standard twenty · 非天然氨基酸
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • sucrose gradient centrifugation — a run in which the sample is layered on a sucrose gradient and stopped early, so particles separate by how fast they sediment · 蔗糖梯度离心
  • 5′ cap — the modified nucleotide at the 5′ end of eukaryotic mRNA, where the small subunit starts scanning · 5′ 帽
  • 28S rRNA — the large ribosomal RNA of the eukaryotic large subunit · 28S rRNA
  • 30S subunit — the small bacterial ribosomal subunit, which holds the mRNA and reads the codons · 30S 小亚基
  • 50S subunit — the large bacterial ribosomal subunit, in which the peptide bond is made · 50S 大亚基
  • 70S — the bacterial ribosome: a 30S subunit together with a 50S subunit · 70S 核糖体
  • 80S — the eukaryotic cytosolic ribosome: a 40S subunit together with a 60S subunit · 80S 核糖体
  • A site — the ribosome site that an incoming aminoacyl-tRNA enters · A 位
  • ADP-ribosylation — transfer of an ADP-ribose group from NAD⁺ onto a protein, changing its activity · ADP-核糖基化
  • amber codon — the stop codon UAG · 琥珀密码子
  • amber suppression — reading through UAG with an engineered tRNA, so an amino acid goes in instead · 琥珀抑制
  • aminoacyl-tRNA — a tRNA with its amino acid already attached, ready for the ribosome · 氨酰-tRNA
  • aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase — the enzyme that attaches the correct amino acid to the correct tRNA, using ATP · 氨酰-tRNA 合成酶
  • AMP — adenosine monophosphate, ATP with two of its phosphates removed · 一磷酸腺苷
  • antibiotic — a compound that kills bacteria or stops them growing · 抗生素
  • anticodon — the three tRNA bases that pair with a codon on the mRNA · 反密码子
  • AUG — the start codon, which also codes for methionine · 起始密码子
  • cell-free protein synthesis — making protein in a cell extract instead of inside living cells · 无细胞蛋白合成
  • chloramphenicol — an antibiotic that blocks peptidyl transferase on the 50S subunit · 氯霉素
  • codon — three consecutive mRNA bases, specifying one amino acid or a stop · 密码子
  • codon-optimised — of a gene: rewritten with the synonymous codons the host uses most often · 密码子优化
  • degeneracy — the property that several codons can specify the same amino acid · 简并性
  • Diphtheria toxin — a bacterial toxin that inactivates eEF2 by ADP-ribosylation, so translation stops · 白喉毒素
  • DnaK — the bacterial Hsp70 chaperone that holds a newly made chain while it folds · DnaK
  • EF-G（= EF-G = eEF2 in eukaryotes） — the bacterial elongation factor that moves the ribosome one codon along, using GTP · 延伸因子 G
  • EF-Tu（= EF-Tu = eEF1A in eukaryotes） — the bacterial elongation factor that delivers aminoacyl-tRNA into the A site, using GTP · 延伸因子 Tu
  • elongation — the repeated three-step cycle that adds one residue to the growing chain · 延伸
  • endosymbiosis — the origin of mitochondria from a bacterium taken up inside another cell · 内共生
  • ER membrane — the membrane of the endoplasmic reticulum, where secreted proteins are made · 内质网膜
  • erythromycin — a macrolide antibiotic that blocks the exit tunnel of the 50S subunit · 红霉素
  • exit tunnel — the channel in the large subunit through which the new chain leaves the ribosome · 出口通道
  • expression strain — an E. coli strain bred to make large amounts of a chosen protein · 表达菌株
  • formylated peptide — a peptide beginning with N-formylmethionine, which neutrophils read as bacterial · 甲酰化肽
  • genetic code — the rules by which each codon specifies one amino acid or a stop · 遗传密码
  • GroEL — the bacterial chaperone barrel that encloses an unfolded protein so it can refold; GroES is its lid · GroEL
  • GTP — guanosine triphosphate, the nucleotide whose hydrolysis drives the elongation steps · 三磷酸鸟苷
  • high-energy bond — a bond whose hydrolysis releases a large amount of free energy, as in ATP · 高能键
  • hydrolysis — cleavage of a bond by the addition of water · 水解
  • hydrophobic — of a group or a sequence: not mixing with water · 疏水的
  • initiation — the stage in which the ribosome is placed on the start codon and assembled · 起始
  • innate immune response — the fast, non-specific arm of immunity, which recognises general features of pathogens · 先天免疫应答
  • inosine — a modified nucleoside at the first anticodon position, able to pair with A, C or U · 肌苷
  • mass spectrometry — a method that identifies molecules by measuring their mass-to-charge ratio · 质谱
  • mitochondrial ribosome — the ribosome inside a mitochondrion, which still resembles a bacterial one · 线粒体核糖体
  • mitochondrion — the membrane-bounded organelle in which respiration and most ATP synthesis happen · 线粒体
  • mRNA — messenger RNA, the transcript whose codons the ribosome reads · 信使 RNA
  • mRNA vaccine — a vaccine made of mRNA encoding an antigen, which the person's own cells translate · mRNA 疫苗
  • N1-methylpseudouridine — a modified uridine used in place of uridine in mRNA vaccines · N1-甲基假尿苷
  • nascent chain — the polypeptide while it is still being made and still held by the ribosome · 新生肽链
  • neutrophil — a white blood cell belonging to the innate immune response · 中性粒细胞
  • N-formylmethionine — the modified methionine with which every bacterial protein begins · 甲酰甲硫氨酸
  • Nirenberg and Matthaei — they read the first codon in 1961, adding poly-U to a cell-free E. coli extract · 破译第一个密码子
  • N-terminus — the end of a polypeptide carrying a free amino group; it is made first · N 端
  • P site — the ribosome site holding the tRNA that carries the growing chain · P 位
  • peptide bond — the amide link from the carboxyl group of one amino acid to the amino group of the next · 肽键（= peptide bond = amide bond）
  • peptidyl transferase — the ribosome activity that forms the peptide bond; it resides in the rRNA · 肽酰转移酶
  • photo-cross-linker — a chemical group that forms a covalent bond to whatever is beside it when illuminated · 光交联剂
  • polypeptide — a chain of amino acids joined by peptide bonds · 多肽
  • polyphenylalanine — a chain made only of phenylalanine residues · 多聚苯丙氨酸
  • poly-U — a synthetic RNA made only of uridines · 多聚尿苷酸
  • protein folding — the settling of a new chain into the defined three-dimensional shape it works in · 蛋白折叠
  • Puromycin — a drug resembling the end of an aminoacyl-tRNA; it joins the chain and releases it early · 嘌呤霉素
  • pyrophosphate — two phosphate groups joined together, released when ATP is cleaved to AMP · 焦磷酸
  • rare codon — a codon whose tRNA is scarce in the host, where the ribosome can stall · 稀有密码子
  • reading frame — which of the three possible groupings of the bases into codons is being read · 阅读框
  • release factor — a protein that enters the A site at a stop codon and frees the finished chain · 释放因子
  • resistance gene — a gene that lets a cell survive a drug, used to select the cells carrying a construct · 抗性基因
  • ribosome — the RNA and protein particle that reads the mRNA and makes the peptide bonds · 核糖体
  • ribozyme — an RNA molecule that catalyses a reaction · 核酶
  • ricin — a toxin of castor beans that removes an adenine from 28S rRNA, stopping translation · 蓖麻毒素
  • Rosetta — an E. coli expression strain that supplies the tRNAs for rare codons · Rosetta 菌株
  • scanning — the eukaryotic search along the mRNA from the cap to the first AUG · 扫描
  • selective toxicity — harming the pathogen and not the host, by aiming at a difference between them · 选择性毒性
  • Shine–Dalgarno（= Shine–Dalgarno sequence = ribosome binding site） sequence — the purine-rich bacterial sequence before a start codon, which pairs with 16S rRNA · SD 序列
  • signal peptide — a short hydrophobic sequence at the N-terminus that directs a protein to the ER · 信号肽（= signal peptide = signal sequence）
  • stop codon — UAA, UAG or UGA; no tRNA reads them and they code for no amino acid · 终止密码子
  • strain — a particular genetic line of a bacterium, kept and grown in the laboratory · 菌株
  • streptomycin — an aminoglycoside antibiotic that makes the 30S subunit misread the message · 链霉素
  • subunit — one of the separate particles or chains that assemble into a working whole · 亚基
  • termination — the stage in which the finished chain is cut free and the ribosome comes apart · 终止
  • tetracycline — an antibiotic that stops aminoacyl-tRNA entering the A site on the 30S subunit · 四环素
  • translation — making a protein by reading the codons of an mRNA · 翻译
  • translocation — the elongation step in which the ribosome moves one codon along the mRNA · 转位
  • tRNA — transfer RNA, the adaptor that carries an amino acid and reads the codon · 转运 RNA
  • universal — of the genetic code: the same one is used from viruses to humans · 通用性
  • wobble — the looser pairing allowed at the third base of a codon, so one tRNA reads several · 摆动

The lac operon

卡 12

Must say

Two switches, and both must be right. The repressor is the negative one, cAMP–CAP (also called CRP, catabolite activator protein, cAMP receptor protein) is the positive one.

Numbers

Three states: no lactose → off; lactose and glucose → barely on, because glucose keeps cAMP low; lactose and no glucose → fully on.

Hook

Glucose lowers cAMP, so the cell will not bother with lactose while there is glucose. That is catabolite repression.

Say this

The lac operon has two switches, and both have to be right. The repressor is the negative one: without lactose it sits on the operator and blocks the polymerase. cAMP with CAP is the positive one: it recruits the polymerase, but only when cAMP is high. So there are three states. With no lactose, the repressor is bound and the operon is off. With lactose and glucose, the repressor comes off, but glucose keeps cAMP low, so there is only weak basal transcription. With lactose and no glucose, the repressor is off and cAMP is high, so the operon is fully on. ⚠ That last effect of glucose is called catabolite repression — the cell will not bother with lactose while there is glucose to eat.

约 40 秒 · 「两个开关都要对」是这题的形状

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Martínková、Lipovová

① 控制的方向是从代谢的方向来的：分解代谢由底物开，合成代谢由产物关 inducible vs repressible

Control direction follows the pathway: the catabolic lac operon is inducible, switched on by its substrate; the anabolic trp operon is repressible, switched off by its product.

lac 对 trp。

② trp 阻遏蛋白光靠自己结合不上 DNA，要色氨酸来当辅阻遏物 —— 于是它关掉造自己的基因 corepressor

The trp repressor cannot bind DNA on its own: it needs tryptophan as a corepressor, so the amino acid switches off the very genes that make it.

色氨酸 ＝ 辅阻遏物。

③ 衰减只有细菌玩得起，因为它们边转录边翻译：核糖体跑得快，终止发夹就形成 attenuation

Attenuation is possible only because bacteria translate while they transcribe: when tryptophan is plentiful, ribosomes translate the leader peptide quickly and a terminator hairpin forms, so transcription stops early.

接「转录」和「翻译」。

④ 这一种调控一个蛋白都不用：mRNA 自己结合代谢物，自己改形状 riboswitch

Regulation here needs no protein at all: a riboswitch, a region of an mRNA, binds a metabolite such as S-adenosylmethionine, changes structure, and either stops transcription or hides the ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence).

不需要蛋白的调控。

⑤ 书外 · general：生长曲线上那个停顿，就是一次调控决定被看见了 diauxic growth · Monod

A pause in the growth curve shows a regulatory decision: Jacques Monod saw bacteria on glucose plus lactose use the glucose first, stop, then switch — diauxic growth. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

两段生长 ＝ 分解代谢物阻遏。

⑥ 书外 · general：顺式反式这条判据能不看见任何东西就分出「DNA 位点」和「可扩散的蛋白」—— Jacob 和 Monod 1961，1965 年诺贝尔奖 Jacob · Monod · cis vs trans

The cis–trans test tells a DNA site from a diffusible protein without seeing either, and Jacob and Monod invented it on lac in 1961, using constitutive mutants, which never switched off, and partial diploids carrying a second lac region.

A mutation acting only in cis marked a DNA site, the operator; one acting in trans marked a diffusible protein, the LacI repressor. They shared the 1965 Nobel Prize with Lwoff. That is also general knowledge.

顺式 ＝ 只管同一条 DNA 上的基因；反式 ＝ 能跑到另一条 DNA 上去管。

⑦ 信号本身不用进细胞：膜上的感受激酶把磷酸往里传，响应调节蛋白去管基因 ⭐ Martínková two-component systems

In a two-component system the signal never has to enter the cell: a membrane sensor kinase such as EnvZ passes a phosphate inwards to a response regulator such as OmpR, which controls the genes.

Martínková 的血红素组氨酸激酶也属于这一类。

⑧ 真核没有操纵子，所以信息落在组合里：转录因子、增强子、染色质状态一起说话 eukaryotic regulation

Eukaryotes mostly lack operons, so the information is in the combination: each gene has its own promoter, controlled by a set of transcription factors, enhancers, and histone acetylation and DNA methylation.

组合控制。

⑨ IPTG 好用是因为它不会被吃掉，开关就一直开着 ⭐ Lipovová IPTG · DE3

IPTG works because it is not metabolised, so the switch stays on: in BL21(DE3) the T7 RNA polymerase gene sits under a lac-controlled promoter, and IPTG binds LacI to release it.

接我自己的表达。

⑩ 自诱导培养基不用人守着：葡萄糖一用完，乳糖自己把表达打开 catabolite repression in the lab

Auto-induction media switch themselves on: they contain glucose and lactose, and expression starts when the glucose runs out. Glucose alone in the medium lowers leaky expression — both are catabolite repression.

书上的原理，台上的用法。

⑪ 要挑的是白菌落 —— 「没有颜色」才是好结果 blue–white screening

In blue–white screening the colony I want is the colourless one: an insert in the cloning site disrupts lacZ, so the cell cannot cut X-gal and the colony stays white.

克隆筛选。

⑫ 书外：两个阻遏蛋白互相压住对方，电路就有了记忆 genetic toggle switch

Two repressors each blocking the other give a circuit with memory — in synthetic biology that is the genetic toggle switch, built from the lac repressor and others. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

主动标「书外」。

陷阱问法

  • "Does lactose itself bind the repressor?" — No, allolactose made from it does. · 是别乳糖。
  • "Is CAP（= CAP = CRP = catabolite activator protein = cAMP receptor protein） an activator or a repressor?" — An activator. · 激活物。
  • "Why is IPTG better than lactose?" — It is not metabolised. · 不被代谢。

定义

  • corepressor — a small molecule that must bind a repressor to activate it · 辅阻遏物
  • attenuation — early termination of transcription controlled by translation of a leader · 衰减作用
  • riboswitch — an mRNA region that binds a metabolite and controls its own gene · 核糖开关
  • cis-acting — affecting only genes on the same DNA molecule, as a DNA site does · 顺式作用
  • trans-acting — affecting genes on any DNA molecule, as a diffusible protein does · 反式作用
  • partial diploid — a bacterium carrying a second copy of a region, usually on a plasmid · 部分二倍体
  • diauxic growth — two growth phases when bacteria use one sugar after another · 二次生长
  • sensor kinase — a membrane histidine kinase that detects a signal · 感受激酶
  • leaky expression — expression without inducer · 本底表达
  • X-gal — a colourless compound that turns blue when cut by β-galactosidase · X-gal
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • allolactose — the isomer of lactose made by β-galactosidase; it is what actually binds the repressor · 别乳糖
  • anabolic — of a pathway: building larger molecules up, and using energy to do it · 合成代谢的
  • Auto-induction medium — a medium holding both glucose and lactose, which starts expression when the glucose runs out · 自诱导培养基
  • β-galactosidase — the enzyme encoded by lacZ, which cuts lactose into glucose and galactose · β-半乳糖苷酶
  • BL21(DE3) — the standard E. coli expression strain, carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene on a λ prophage · BL21(DE3)
  • blue–white screening — picking colonies by colour: an insert disrupts lacZ, so recombinant colonies stay white · 蓝白筛选
  • cAMP — cyclic AMP, a signalling nucleotide that is high in E. coli when glucose is scarce · 环腺苷酸
  • CAP — catabolite activator protein, which with cAMP binds near the promoter and recruits the polymerase · CAP 激活蛋白
  • catabolic — of a pathway: breaking molecules down and releasing energy · 分解代谢的
  • catabolite repression — the effect by which glucose keeps cAMP low, so other sugar operons stay weakly expressed · 分解代谢物阻遏
  • cis–trans test — telling a DNA site from a diffusible protein by whether a mutation acts on its own DNA copy only · 顺反测验
  • colony — the visible clump of cells that grows on a plate from one original cell · 菌落
  • constitutive mutant — a mutant that expresses the operon all the time, with or without inducer · 组成型突变体
  • DNA methylation — addition of methyl groups to DNA, which usually lowers transcription of that gene · DNA 甲基化
  • enhancer — a DNA site, often far from the gene, that increases its transcription · 增强子
  • EnvZ — the membrane sensor kinase of the E. coli EnvZ–OmpR two-component system · EnvZ
  • galactose — a six-carbon sugar, differing from glucose at one carbon; one of the two halves of lactose · 半乳糖
  • gene — a stretch of DNA specifying one product, either a protein or a functional RNA · 基因
  • gene expression — the making of a gene's RNA and protein product · 基因表达
  • gene regulation — control of how much of a gene's product the cell makes · 基因调控
  • genetic toggle switch — a synthetic circuit of two repressors each blocking the other, which gives it memory · 基因拨动开关
  • glucose — the six-carbon sugar that is the cell's main fuel and its preferred one · 葡萄糖
  • growth curve — a plot of the density of a culture against time · 生长曲线
  • histone — one of the small basic proteins that DNA is wound around in the nucleus · 组蛋白
  • histone acetylation — addition of acetyl groups to histone lysines, which opens the chromatin · 组蛋白乙酰化
  • inducer — the small molecule that switches an operon on · 诱导物
  • inducible — of an operon: switched on by the substrate of its own pathway · 可诱导的
  • insert — the DNA fragment that is put into a vector · 插入片段
  • IPTG — isopropyl thiogalactoside, a lactose analogue that induces the operon and is not metabolised · IPTG
  • Jacob and Monod — they worked out lac regulation in 1961 and shared the 1965 Nobel Prize with Lwoff · 提出操纵子模型
  • lac repressor — the protein, encoded by lacI, that sits on the operator until allolactose binds it · lac 阻遏蛋白
  • lacI — the gene encoding the lac repressor · lacI 基因
  • lactose — the disaccharide of glucose and galactose found in milk · 乳糖
  • lacZ — the gene encoding β-galactosidase · lacZ 基因
  • leader peptide — the short peptide encoded ahead of the trp genes, whose translation speed sets attenuation · 前导肽
  • Lwoff — André Lwoff, who worked on lysogeny and shared the 1965 Nobel Prize with Jacob and Monod · 共享 1965 诺贝尔奖
  • medium — the mixture of nutrients, liquid or solid, in which a culture is grown · 培养基
  • metabolite — a small molecule that takes part in metabolism · 代谢物
  • mRNA — messenger RNA, the transcript that carries a gene's sequence to the ribosome · 信使 RNA
  • mutant — an organism carrying a change in its DNA sequence · 突变体
  • mutation — a permanent change in the base sequence of DNA · 突变
  • OmpR — the response regulator phosphorylated by EnvZ, which then binds DNA · OmpR
  • operator — the DNA site beside the promoter at which a repressor binds · 操纵基因
