  • operon — a group of genes transcribed together from one promoter, as a single mRNA · 操纵子
  • phosphorylation — addition of a phosphate group to a protein, usually changing its activity · 磷酸化
  • plasmid — a small circular DNA that replicates independently of the chromosome · 质粒
  • product — the molecule an enzyme or a pathway makes · 产物
  • promoter — the DNA sequence where RNA polymerase binds to begin transcription · 启动子
  • repressible — of an operon: switched off by the product of its own pathway · 可阻遏的
  • repressor — a protein that binds DNA and blocks transcription of the genes behind it · 阻遏蛋白
  • response regulator — the second protein of a two-component system, usually itself a transcription factor · 响应调节蛋白
  • ribosome binding site（= Shine–Dalgarno sequence = ribosome binding site） — the mRNA sequence where the small subunit is placed, just before the start codon · 核糖体结合位点
  • S-adenosylmethionine — the cell's main methyl donor, and the metabolite some riboswitches bind · S-腺苷甲硫氨酸
  • substrate — the molecule an enzyme acts on, which binds in its active site · 底物
  • synthetic biology — building new genetic circuits and organisms out of defined parts · 合成生物学
  • T7 RNA polymerase — a single-subunit phage enzyme that transcribes only from T7 promoters · T7 RNA 聚合酶
  • terminator hairpin — an RNA hairpin whose forming makes the polymerase stop early · 终止子发夹
  • transcription — copying a DNA sequence into RNA · 转录
  • transcription factor — a protein that binds DNA and changes how much a gene is transcribed · 转录因子
  • trp operon — the operon for making tryptophan, switched off when tryptophan is plentiful · trp 操纵子
  • tryptophan — an aromatic amino acid; it acts as the corepressor of the trp repressor · 色氨酸
  • two-component system — a bacterial signalling pair: a membrane sensor kinase and a response regulator · 二组分系统

Cloning, PCR & sequencing

卡 pe-clone · taq · 你自己天天做的那一套

  1. 为什么要克隆 — 要拿到很多份同一个基因 把目的基因放进一个能在细菌里自主复制的载体，细菌一分裂就替你复制一遍。
  2. 质粒三件套 Ori（复制起点，能自主复制）· 选择标记（抗性基因，在平板上毒死没转进去的）· MCS（多克隆位点，一小段集中了很多独特酶切位点）。
  3. 把基因放进去 — 两条路 传统：限制性内切酶切出黏末端，连接酶接上。 无切法：restriction-free cloning / Gibson —— 用带同源臂的引物把插入片段变成一条大引物， ⚠ 不需要合适的酶切位点，这是它最大的好处。
  4. 送进细胞 ＋ 挑出来 转化（化学感受态 ＋ 热激）或电穿孔。 铺抗生素平板 → 只有带质粒的长得出来 → 挑单克隆 → 测序确认。
  5. PCR — 三步一轮，指数放大 变性 → 退火 → 延伸。⚠ Taq 没有 3′→5′ 校对，Kapa 和 Pfu 有 —— 要克隆、要表达的，必须用有校对的那种，不然错配会被一路放大。
  6. Sanger 测序 — 靠一个缺掉的羟基 反应里掺一点 ddNTP：它的糖环 3′ 位是 −H 而不是 −OH。 一旦被掺进去，下一个磷酸二酯键再也建不起来，链当场停住。 不同长度的片段按大小分开，就读出了序列。

Say this

A cloning vector needs three things: an origin of replication so it copies itself in the host, a selectable marker so that only the cells that took it up survive, and a multiple cloning site. The insert can be put in with restriction enzymes and ligase, or by restriction-free cloning, where the insert itself is used as a large primer, which avoids needing suitable restriction sites at all. PCR is denaturation, annealing and extension, and Taq has no 3′→5′ proofreading while Kapa and Pfu do, so anything that will be cloned should be amplified with a proofreading polymerase. Sanger sequencing works by chain termination with ddNTPs: the 3′ position is a hydrogen instead of a hydroxyl, so no further phosphodiester bond can form.

约 45 秒 · 这一段是你的主场，说得比别人细是加分

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Hudeček、Lipovová、Hodek

① 质粒能当工具，是因为它不靠染色体、自己复制 plasmid

A plasmid replicates independently of the chromosome, which is what makes it a tool: whatever I put on it is copied too. It is a small circular bacterial DNA, often carrying antibiotic resistance.

plasmid ＝ 染色体外的小环 DNA。

② 书外 · general：1973 年的 pSC101 实验是重组 DNA 的起点；plasmid 这个名字是 Lederberg 1952 年起的 discovery

Recombinant DNA begins with one experiment: in 1973 Cohen, Chang, Boyer and Helling cut pSC101 with EcoRI, joined foreign DNA into it, transformed E. coli, and the inserted gene was expressed. Joshua Lederberg had coined the word plasmid in 1952, and R factors, plasmids carrying several antibiotic resistances, already moved between bacteria by conjugation. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

⚠ 这是 Herbert Boyer，不是 ATP 合酶（= ATP synthase = Complex V = F₀F₁-ATPase）那个 Paul Boyer。

③ 克隆载体要的是拷贝数，不是蛋白：高拷贝起点、选择标记、多克隆位点 cloning vector · pUC19

A cloning vector is built for copy number, not for protein: it needs a usually high-copy origin of replication, a selection marker such as ampicillin resistance, and a multiple cloning site. pUC19 is the standard example, and its lacZα gene gives blue–white screening.

目的：多拷贝、存起来。不需要表达。

④ 表达载体 ＝ 克隆载体 ＋ 翻译要用的那一整套 expression vector · pET

An expression vector is a cloning vector plus everything transcription and translation need: a strong, usually inducible promoter, a ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence), a start codon, often a His-tag, and a transcription terminator. pET vectors are the usual example, and they are lower copy than cloning vectors.

克隆载体是仓库，表达载体是工厂。

⑤ 强和紧是两个互相冲突的性质，毒性蛋白要的是紧，不是强 promoter · strength vs tightness

Strength and tightness are two different properties of a promoter and they usually conflict: strength is how much it expresses after induction, tightness how little it leaks before induction. A promoter itself is the DNA sequence where RNA polymerase binds and starts transcription.

强 ≠ 紧。毒性蛋白要紧的。

⑥ tac 和 trc 是 trp 与 lac 的杂合体，所以比 lac 强；lac 本身中等，而且是最漏的 lac · tac · trc

tac and trc are hybrids of the trp and lac promoters, which is why they are stronger than lac, and IPTG induces them as it does lac. The lac promoter itself is only moderate in strength, and it is the leakiest of the standard set.

lac 中等最漏；tac/trc 比 lac 强。

⑦ T7 最强，但宿主自己的聚合酶根本不认它 —— 所以没有 (DE3) 的菌株什么都不表达 T7 · BL21(DE3)

Only T7 RNA polymerase reads the T7 promoter, so a pET plasmid makes nothing in an ordinary E. coli. The host must be a strain such as BL21(DE3), which carries the T7 polymerase gene under an IPTG-inducible lacUV5 promoter. T7, from phage T7, is the strongest of this set.

两级：IPTG → 造 T7 聚合酶 → 转录我的基因。

⑧ araBAD 是最紧的那一个，而且水平能按阿拉伯糖的剂量调，不只是开关 araBAD · constitutive

The araBAD promoter is the tightest, the opposite choice to T7. It is induced by arabinose, and the level can be tuned by the arabinose dose rather than merely switched. A constitutive promoter, by contrast, is always on and cannot be switched at all.

T7 最强，araBAD 最紧，lac 最漏。

⑨ GAL1 和 AOX1 借的是宿主自己的代谢基因，所以诱导剂就是那条途径的底物；CMV 来自病毒 CMV · GAL1 · AOX1

Two of these are borrowed from the host's own metabolic genes, so the inducer is that pathway's substrate: GAL1 is induced by galactose in yeast, and AOX1, from alcohol oxidase, by methanol in Pichia pastoris. CMV, from cytomegalovirus, is instead strong and constitutive in mammalian cells.

GAL1 就是我酵母展示里用的开关。

⑩ 元件只是一段序列，意义来自认它的分子：启动子由 RNA 聚合酶认，操纵基因由阻遏蛋白认 genetic elements · promoter · operator

A genetic element is just a stretch of sequence; its meaning comes from what recognises it. The promoter is recognised by RNA polymerase, through the σ factor in bacteria and through TBP and other transcription factors in eukaryotes. The operator is recognised by a repressor, such as LacI.

问「这个元件是什么」，就答「谁认它」。

⑪ 增强子跟启动子的差别是：位置无所谓，方向也无所谓 enhancer

Unlike a promoter, an enhancer works at a distance from the gene and in either orientation. It is recognised by activator proteins.

远、方向无所谓。

⑫ 这两个元件要先被转录出来才起作用 —— 它们工作在 RNA 上 terminator · Shine–Dalgarno

Two elements are the exceptions, because they only work once transcribed, as RNA and not as DNA. The terminator works as an RNA hairpin followed by a run of U; rho-dependent terminators use the rho protein. The ribosome binding site, or Shine–Dalgarno sequence, pairs with 16S rRNA.

先转录出来，才起作用。

⑬ 前两个是「被认出来的位点」，抗性基因却是一整个要被翻译成蛋白的基因 origin · MCS · resistance gene

Two of these are sites and one is not. The origin of the E. coli chromosome, oriC, is recognised by the initiator protein DnaA, and the multiple cloning site by restriction enzymes. The resistance gene is a whole gene that must be translated, for example into β-lactamase, which destroys ampicillin.

抗性基因要被表达成蛋白才有用。

⑭ 书外 · general：「转化」这个词就是从 Griffith 1928 年那个实验来的 —— 死细菌把无害菌变成了致死菌 transformation · Griffith · Avery

The word transformation comes from Griffith's 1928 experiment: live harmless R pneumococci plus heat-killed virulent S pneumococci killed mice, so something from the dead cells had transformed R. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge. My textbook's timeline on p009 records Avery, 1944: genetic information is transferred by DNA. The finished vector enters bacteria the same way.

S 有荚膜，致死；R 没有。

⑮ 吸光度之所以能定量，是因为它跟浓度成正比：A ＝ ε·c·l ⭐ Hudeček Beer–Lambert law

Absorbance can be quantitative only because it is linear in concentration: the Beer–Lambert (also called Beer–Lambert law, Beer's law) law, A = ε·c·l says absorbance equals the extinction coefficient times concentration times path length. It fails at high concentration.

Hudeček：说方法先说测的是什么物理量。

⑯ 两个波长属于两种不同的分子 —— 260 nm 是碱基，280 nm 是色氨酸和酪氨酸 —— 所以比值才报得了纯度 A260 · A280 · purity ratios

On the NanoDrop the two wavelengths belong to different molecules — 260 nm the bases, 280 nm tryptophan and tyrosine — which is the reason a ratio reports purity. A260/A280 near 1.8 means clean DNA and near 2.0 clean RNA. A low A260/A230 means guanidine salts or phenol were carried over.

吸光度分不清 DNA 和 RNA，所以要报比值。

⑰ OD600 叫「光密度」不叫「吸光度」：光是被散射出去的，测的是浊度；数值随仪器不同 OD600 · turbidity

OD600 is called an optical density and not an absorbance: used before induction to follow the culture, it reports scattering. Cells scatter light out of the beam, so it measures turbidity. 600 nm is chosen because medium and cells absorb little there, and the value depends on the instrument.

散射，不是吸收。

⑱ IMAC 认的是几个组氨酸侧链，不是折叠 —— 所以尿素里的包涵体也能纯化 ⭐ Lipovová His-tag · IMAC · Ni-NTA

IMAC, immobilised metal affinity chromatography, binds a run of histidine side chains rather than a folded surface, so it also works on inclusion bodies dissolved in urea. Those histidines coordinate nickel held by Ni-NTA, and free imidazole competes for the nickel and elutes the protein.

咪唑就是组氨酸的侧链本身。

⑲ 凝胶按大小分开的前提只有一条：单位质量电荷一致。DNA 天生就有，蛋白要 SDS 给 agarose gel · SDS-PAGE

A gel separates by size only if the charge per unit mass is uniform. DNA has that for free in an agarose gel, one phosphate per nucleotide; protein does not, so SDS unfolds the chain and imposes it. A reducing agent such as DTT breaks disulfide bonds.

DNA 天生电荷均匀，蛋白要 SDS 给。

⑳ Western blot 一定要转膜，因为让分离成为可能的那些胶孔，对抗体来说太细了 Western blot

A Western blot (also called immunoblot) needs the transfer step because antibodies cannot enter the gel pores. It starts from SDS-PAGE: the bands are moved to a nitrocellulose or PVDF membrane, blocked, and probed with a primary antibody and a labelled secondary antibody.

凝胶管分开，抗体管认出。

㉑ 「酶联」这两个字讲的是放大：一个结合上去的酶能把很多底物变色 ⭐ Hodek ELISA · IgY

ELISA is enzyme-linked because the enzyme amplifies: one bound antibody turns over many substrate molecules into colour. In a sandwich ELISA a capture and a detection antibody bind two different epitopes on the plate, which is what makes it specific. Hodek uses chicken IgY from egg yolk as such probes.

抗体管特异性，酶管信号。

㉒ 酵母展示的关键是一个细胞 ＝ 一个变体：蛋白和编码它的基因不会走散 yeast display · FACS

Yeast display, my own method, keeps each variant in the same cell as its gene, so sorted cells can be regrown and sequenced. The protein is shown on the yeast surface as a fusion with Aga2, switched on by GAL1; cells are sorted by FACS, and affinity is measured on the cells by titrating labelled antigen.

一个细胞 ＝ 一个变体。

陷阱问法

  • "Does a T7 promoter work in any E. coli?" — No, only in a host that makes T7 RNA polymerase, such as BL21(DE3). · 要有 T7 聚合酶。
  • "Is OD600 an absorbance?" — No, it is light scattered by cells. · 是散射。
  • "Can A260 tell DNA from RNA?" — No, both absorb there; the ratios help, a dye assay is specific. · 分不清。
  • "Is a terminator recognised as DNA?" — No, it acts as RNA, like the Shine–Dalgarno（= Shine–Dalgarno sequence = ribosome binding site） sequence. · 在 RNA 上起作用。

定义

  • IPTG — a lactose analogue that binds LacI but is not metabolised · 异丙基硫代半乳糖苷
  • σ factor — the bacterial RNA polymerase subunit that recognises the promoter · σ 因子
  • operator — the DNA site bound by a repressor · 操纵基因
  • enhancer — a DNA site bound by activators, working at a distance and in either orientation · 增强子
  • Shine–Dalgarno sequence — the ribosome binding site that pairs with 16S rRNA · SD 序列
  • DnaA — the bacterial protein that starts replication at oriC · 起始蛋白
  • β-lactamase — the enzyme that destroys ampicillin · β-内酰胺酶
  • extinction coefficient — ε, how strongly a substance absorbs at one wavelength · 消光系数
  • turbidity — cloudiness caused by light scattering · 浊度
  • Ni-NTA — nickel held by nitrilotriacetic acid on a resin · 镍柱
  • epitope — the part of an antigen an antibody binds · 表位
  • FACS — fluorescence-activated cell sorting · 荧光激活细胞分选
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 16S rRNA — the ribosomal RNA of the bacterial small subunit, which base-pairs with the ribosome binding site · 细菌小亚基 rRNA
  • A260/A230 — the ratio of absorbance at 260 and at 230 nanometres, which falls when guanidine salts or phenol are carried over · 残留盐与酚的比值
  • A260/A280 — the ratio of absorbance at 260 and 280 nanometres, near 1.8 for clean DNA and 2.0 for clean RNA · 核酸纯度比值
  • absorbance — the logarithm of the light entering a sample divided by the light leaving it, a number with no units · 吸光度
  • activator protein — a protein that binds an enhancer or a nearby site and raises transcription from a promoter · 激活蛋白
  • affinity — how tightly two molecules bind, reported as the dissociation constant of the complex · 亲和力
  • Aga2 — the small yeast cell-wall protein a displayed protein is fused to, so that it is shown on the yeast surface · 酵母展示的锚定蛋白
  • agar plate — a dish of medium solidified with agar, on which single cells grow into separate colonies · 琼脂平板
  • agarose gel — a gel of agarose in which DNA is separated by size in an electric field · 琼脂糖凝胶
  • ampicillin — a beta-lactam antibiotic, the usual selection agent for a plasmid carrying beta-lactamase · 氨苄青霉素
  • annealing — the PCR step in which the primers bind their complementary sequences on the template · 退火
  • antibiotic — a compound that kills bacteria or stops them growing, used here to kill every cell without the plasmid · 抗生素
  • antibody — a protein made by the immune system that binds one particular shape on one particular molecule · 抗体
  • antigen — the molecule an antibody binds · 抗原
  • AOX1 — the promoter of the alcohol oxidase gene of Pichia pastoris, induced by methanol · 甲醇诱导启动子
  • araBAD promoter — the promoter of the E. coli arabinose operon, the tightest of the standard expression promoters · araBAD 启动子
  • arabinose — the five-carbon sugar that induces the araBAD promoter · 阿拉伯糖
  • assay — a procedure that measures how much of something is present, or whether it is active · 测定方法
  • Avery — Oswald Avery, who showed in 1944 that the material transferring genetic information is DNA · 证明遗传物质是 DNA
  • base — the nitrogen-containing ring of a nucleotide, and the part of DNA that absorbs at 260 nanometres · 碱基
  • Beer–Lambert（= Beer–Lambert law = Beer's law） law — absorbance equals the extinction coefficient times concentration times path length · 朗伯-比尔定律
  • BL21(DE3) — an E. coli expression strain carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene in its chromosome · 常用表达菌株
  • blocking — covering the free surface of a blot with irrelevant protein, so the antibody binds only its target · 封闭
  • blue–white screening — colonies with an insert interrupting lacZα stay white, while empty ones turn blue · 蓝白斑筛选
  • charge-to-mass ratio — a molecule's charge divided by its mass, which must be uniform before a gel can separate by size · 电荷质量比
  • clone — a population of cells or DNA molecules all descended from one original · 克隆株
  • cloning — putting one DNA fragment into a vector so that a host cell makes many identical copies of it · 克隆
  • cloning vector — a plasmid built for copy number, carrying an origin, a selection marker and a cloning site · 克隆载体
  • CMV promoter — a cytomegalovirus promoter, strong and constitutive in mammalian cells · 哺乳细胞强启动子
  • Cohen and Boyer — Stanley Cohen and Herbert Boyer, whose 1973 experiment produced the first recombinant DNA plasmid · 重组 DNA 的起点
  • colony — a visible mass of cells on a plate, all grown from one cell · 菌落
  • competent cells — bacteria treated so that they can take up DNA from the solution around them · 感受态细胞
  • concentration — the amount of a substance per unit volume of solution · 浓度
  • conjugation — direct transfer of a plasmid from one bacterium to another through a pilus · 接合
  • constitutive promoter — a promoter that is always on and cannot be switched · 组成型启动子
  • copy number — how many copies of a plasmid one cell holds, set by its origin of replication · 拷贝数
  • culture — cells growing in liquid medium or on a plate under controlled conditions · 培养物
  • ddNTP — a dideoxynucleotide, whose sugar carries a hydrogen instead of the 3′ hydroxyl, so the chain stops · 双脱氧核苷酸
  • denaturation — in PCR, the heating step that separates the two strands of the template · 变性
  • detergent — a molecule with a water-soluble head and a fat-soluble tail, which dissolves membranes and unfolds proteins · 去污剂
  • disulfide bond — a covalent bond between the sulfur atoms of two cysteine side chains · 二硫键
  • DTT — dithiothreitol, a reducing agent that breaks disulfide bonds · 二硫苏糖醇
  • EcoRI — a restriction enzyme from E. coli that cuts GAATTC and leaves a single-stranded overhang · 限制性内切酶 EcoRI
  • electroporation — a brief electric pulse that makes the membrane permeable, so DNA can enter · 电穿孔
  • ELISA — enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay, an antibody assay in which a bound enzyme generates the colour · 酶联免疫吸附试验
  • elution — changing the conditions so that the bound molecule leaves the resin and comes off the column · 洗脱
  • expression vector — a cloning vector with a promoter, ribosome binding site, start codon and terminator added · 表达载体
  • extension — the PCR step in which the polymerase builds the new strand from each primer · 延伸
  • fluorescent dye assay — quantifying nucleic acid with a dye that fluoresces only once bound, so it is specific for one kind · 荧光染料法
  • fusion protein — one protein chain made from two genes joined in frame, so both parts are translated together · 融合蛋白
  • GAL1 — a yeast promoter taken from the galactose pathway and induced by galactose · 半乳糖诱导启动子
  • galactose — the sugar that induces the GAL1 promoter in yeast · 半乳糖
  • gel — a porous solid network through which molecules are driven and sieved by size · 凝胶
  • gene — a stretch of DNA carrying the information for one RNA or one protein · 基因
  • genetic element — a defined stretch of sequence with a function, such as a promoter or an operator · 遗传元件
  • Gibson assembly — joining DNA fragments through long overlapping ends in a single isothermal reaction · Gibson 组装
  • Griffith — Frederick Griffith, whose 1928 experiment with pneumococci first showed transformation · 转化现象的发现者
  • His-tag — a short run of histidines added to a protein so that it binds a nickel column · 组氨酸标签
  • host — the cell in which the vector is kept and the protein is made · 宿主
  • IgY — the main antibody class of birds, obtained from egg yolk · 禽类卵黄抗体
  • IMAC — immobilised metal affinity chromatography, in which a metal ion fixed on the resin binds histidine side chains · 固定化金属亲和层析
  • imidazole — the ring compound that is the histidine side chain itself, used to compete a His-tag off nickel · 咪唑
  • inclusion body — an aggregate of misfolded recombinant protein inside the cell · 包涵体
  • inducer — the small molecule added to switch an inducible promoter on · 诱导剂
  • induction — adding that molecule to start transcription of the cloned gene · 诱导
  • insert — the DNA fragment put into the vector · 插入片段
  • Kapa — a commercial DNA polymerase with proofreading, used for high-fidelity PCR · 高保真聚合酶
  • lac promoter — the promoter of the E. coli lactose operon, moderate in strength and the leakiest of the standard set · lac 启动子
  • LacI — the repressor protein that sits on the lac operator until IPTG binds it · lac 阻遏蛋白
  • lacUV5 — a mutant form of the lac promoter, used in DE3 strains to drive the T7 polymerase gene · lacUV5 启动子
  • lacZα — the fragment of the beta-galactosidase gene on pUC vectors whose interruption gives a white colony · lacZα 片段
  • leakiness — how much a promoter transcribes before any inducer has been added · 本底渗漏
  • Lederberg — Joshua Lederberg, who coined the word plasmid in 1952 · 「质粒」一词的提出者
  • ligase — DNA ligase, the enzyme that seals a nick and so joins insert to vector · 连接酶
  • ligation — the reaction in which ligase closes the breaks between insert and vector · 连接反应
  • methanol — the alcohol that induces the AOX1 promoter in Pichia pastoris · 甲醇
  • multiple cloning site — a short stretch containing many unique restriction sites, for inserting a gene · 多克隆位点
  • NanoDrop — a spectrophotometer that reads a microlitre drop held between two surfaces, without a cuvette · 微量分光光度计
  • nitrocellulose — one of the two membrane materials protein is transferred onto in a Western blot（= Western blot = immunoblot） · 硝酸纤维素膜
  • OD600 — optical density at 600 nanometres, the turbidity reading used to follow a bacterial culture · 菌液浊度读数
  • optical density — a reading of light lost from the beam, including light scattered as well as absorbed · 光密度
  • oriC — the single origin of replication of the E. coli chromosome · 大肠杆菌染色体复制起点
  • origin of replication — the sequence where replication starts, letting a plasmid copy itself and setting its copy number · 复制起点
  • path length — the distance the light travels through the sample, the l in A = ε·c·l · 光程
  • PCR — polymerase chain reaction, exponential amplification of a chosen DNA region by repeated cycles of three temperatures · 聚合酶链式反应
  • pET — the standard family of E. coli expression vectors, built around a T7 promoter · pET 载体
  • Pfu — a DNA polymerase from Pyrococcus furiosus, which has 3′→5′ proofreading · 高保真聚合酶
  • Pichia pastoris — a yeast that can grow on methanol, used as an expression host · 毕赤酵母
  • plasmid — a small circular DNA that replicates independently of the chromosome · 质粒
  • primary antibody — the antibody that binds the target protein itself on a blot or plate · 一抗
  • primer — a short single-stranded DNA that pairs with the template and gives the polymerase a 3′ end to extend · 引物
  • promoter — the DNA sequence where RNA polymerase binds and begins transcription · 启动子
  • proofreading — the 3′→5′ exonuclease activity that removes a base the polymerase has just added wrongly · 校对活性
  • pSC101 — the low-copy E. coli plasmid used in the 1973 recombinant DNA experiment · pSC101 质粒
  • pUC19 — the standard high-copy cloning vector, carrying ampicillin resistance and lacZα · pUC19 载体
  • purity — the fraction of the total material in the sample that is the molecule I want · 纯度
  • PVDF — polyvinylidene difluoride, the other membrane material used for Western blotting · PVDF 膜
  • R factor — a plasmid carrying several antibiotic resistance genes and able to move between bacteria · 抗性因子
  • reducing agent — a compound that donates electrons to another, here to break disulfide bonds · 还原剂
  • resin — the solid beads packed in a column, carrying the groups that bind the sample · 层析介质
  • resistance gene — a whole gene on the vector that must be translated, whose product lets only transformed cells grow · 抗性基因
  • restriction enzyme — an enzyme that cuts double-stranded DNA at one specific short sequence · 限制性内切酶
  • restriction-free cloning — cloning in which the insert itself serves as a large primer, so no restriction site is needed · 无酶切克隆
  • rho protein — the bacterial protein that releases the transcript at rho-dependent terminators · rho 因子
  • RNA polymerase — the enzyme that transcribes a DNA template into RNA · RNA 聚合酶
  • S and R pneumococci — the capsulated virulent and the uncapsulated harmless forms of the pneumococcus in Griffith's experiment · 有荚膜致死型与无荚膜无害型
  • sandwich ELISA — an ELISA in which a capture and a detection antibody bind two different epitopes of the same molecule · 夹心 ELISA
  • Sanger sequencing — sequencing by chain termination with ddNTPs, reading the sequence off the fragment lengths · Sanger 测序
  • screening — testing every candidate one by one and keeping the ones that pass · 筛选
  • SDS — sodium dodecyl sulfate, a detergent that unfolds proteins and gives them a uniform negative charge · 十二烷基硫酸钠
  • SDS-PAGE — polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis in SDS, which separates proteins by molecular weight alone · SDS 聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳
  • secondary antibody — a labelled antibody that binds the primary antibody and carries the signal · 二抗
  • selectable marker — a gene on the vector that lets only the cells holding it survive a condition killing the rest · 选择标记
  • selection — imposing a condition under which only the wanted cells survive, so nothing has to be tested · 选择
  • start codon — the AUG at which translation begins · 起始密码子
  • sticky end — a short single-stranded overhang left by a restriction enzyme, which pairs with a matching overhang · 黏末端
  • strain — one genetically defined line of a bacterial species, differing from others in named mutations or added genes · 菌株
  • strength — of a promoter, how much transcript it gives after induction · 启动子强度
  • T7 promoter — a promoter read only by T7 RNA polymerase, the strongest of the standard set · T7 启动子
  • T7 RNA polymerase — the single-subunit polymerase of phage T7, which transcribes only from T7 promoters · T7 RNA 聚合酶
  • tac promoter — a hybrid of the trp and lac promoters, stronger than lac and induced by IPTG · tac 启动子
  • Taq — the thermostable DNA polymerase of Thermus aquaticus, which has no 3′→5′ proofreading · Taq 聚合酶
  • TBP — TATA-binding protein, the eukaryotic factor that binds the core promoter · TATA 结合蛋白
  • template — the strand the polymerase reads to build the complementary new strand · 模板
  • tightness — of a promoter, how little transcript it gives before induction · 启动子紧密性
  • titration — adding a reagent in a series of known amounts and reading the response at each one · 滴定
  • transcription terminator — the sequence that, once transcribed, makes RNA polymerase release the transcript · 转录终止子
  • transfer — in blotting, moving the separated bands out of the gel onto a membrane · 转膜
  • transformation — the uptake of free DNA from outside by a bacterial cell · 转化
  • trc promoter — another trp–lac hybrid promoter, very close to tac in strength and induction · trc 启动子
  • urea — a compound that unfolds proteins at high concentration, used to dissolve inclusion bodies · 尿素
  • variant — one member of a library, differing from the others in sequence · 变体
  • vector — the DNA molecule that carries my insert into a cell and is replicated there · 载体
  • wavelength — the distance between two peaks of the light wave, which decides what a molecule absorbs · 波长
  • Western blot — proteins separated by SDS-PAGE, transferred to a membrane and detected with antibodies · 蛋白质印迹
  • yeast display — showing each protein variant on the surface of the yeast cell that carries its gene · 酵母展示

From the main app

卡 14 · mb-int · mb-ko · mb-post · x-ph · 主 app 里有、这一页原来没有的五块

  1. pH — 一个事实，四种读法 pH = −log₁₀[H⁺]，一个单位就是十倍，刻度反着走 —— 越酸，pH 越小。 中性是 7，是因为 25 °C 时 K_w = [H⁺][OH⁻] = 10⁻¹⁴，两个离子成对生出来，各是 10⁻⁷ M。不是谁挑的中点。 真正干活的那个数是 pKa：pH 等于 pKa 时，那个基团正好半解离。 滴定曲线、缓冲液、等电点、酶的 pH 最适 —— 这四样是同一个事实的四种读法，剩下的只是代数。 缓冲液 ＝ 一个弱酸加它自己的共轭碱，管用范围 pKa ± 1；⚠ 范围由 pKa 定，容量由浓度定，这是两件事。 ⚠ 书从头到尾没有定义过 pH，也没有任何一节讲缓冲 —— 这两块被问出处就直说「通用知识」。 书真正印出来的是甘氨酸曲线那三个数 2,4 / 9,8 / 6,1（p023）、pI = (pK₁ + pK₂)/2（p024）、pH 最适那一节和那张表（p058 p116）。
  2. 送进去 — 四个词，两个问题 transformation · electroporation · transduction · transfection 说的是同一件事：把外源核酸送进一个活细胞。 差别只在两个问题上：进的是哪种细胞、谁把它带过膜。 transformation ＝ 细菌自己从溶液里吸裸 DNA，前提是它处在 competent 状态； transduction ＝ 病毒当车，细菌那边是噬菌体； transfection ＝ 送进真核细胞，定义就这么多，不暗示任何机制。 ⚠ electroporation 不在这张清单上 —— 它回答的是「你怎么做的」。所以电转进大肠杆菌仍然是一次 transformation。 落锤那两句：The method did not change. The destination did. ⚠ 而且命名有一半是约定：酵母是真核，按定义该叫转染，全世界却都说 yeast transformation。 ⚠ 最后一个陷阱：癌症生物学里 transformation 指正常细胞变成恶性的，而书里 transformation 唯一的生物学用法正是这个（p182）。
  3. 证明接触 — 八种方法，八种读出 两个分子有没有接触，显微镜分辨不出来。所以每一种方法都在接触的下游造一个信号，再拿那个信号当答案 —— 一个长出来的菌落、一条胶上的带、一个反射光的角度、一个新的发射波长。 三个问题就能安放任何一种方法：问的是哪一对 · 在哪儿做 · 读出是什么。 酵母单杂交问「哪个蛋白结合这段 DNA」，ChIP 正好反过来问「这个蛋白结合哪些 DNA」； 酵母双杂交把已知的那半挂在 BD 上当诱饵、把文库挂在 AD 上当猎物； SPR 在芯片上读折射率，CoIP 和 GST pull-down 读沉淀和胶，FRET 读 1–10 nm 的距离，RNAi 根本不是检测、是扰动。 ⚠ 每一种都有它显示不出来的东西，而这个盲点决定了一个阳性结果值多少钱。 考场上只给三样 ——方法、读出、盲点 —— 说完就停，别背清单。
  4. 弄坏一个基因 — 五个难处，五种技术 反向遗传学：手上有序列、不知道功能，就故意弄坏，从出的问题反推它原本在干什么。 后面每一项技术，都在解决上一项带来的一个难处 —— ① 同源重组太罕见（动物 10⁻²–10⁻⁵）而且看不出来 → 正-负双向选择：neo 放在靶基因里面，HSV-tk 放在同源臂外面，重组只拷两臂之间，于是带进 neo、丢下 tk； ② 皿里的细胞不是一只动物 → 做在 ES 细胞上（能进生殖系）； ③ 完全敲除会杀死胚胎 → Cre/LoxP 条件敲除，两个 LoxP 放哪里就删掉哪一段； ④ 植物没有 ES 细胞 → 借农杆菌的 T-DNA； ⑤ 以上全都在等细胞自己重组 → CRISPR/Cas9 不等了，直接切。 ⚠ 两句要背死：切是工具，修复才是机制；筛选只做富集，从不做证明（活下来的仍要 PCR、Southern 或测序验）。
  5. 转录之外 — 每多一道手续，就多一个可调的位置 基因自己的结构，决定了它必须经过哪些手续；每一道手续都是一个调控可以坐下来的位置。 真核基因是断裂的 → 必须剪接：内含子 5′ 起始 GT、3′ 末尾 AG，这就是 GT–AG 法则 （⚠ 那是写在 DNA 上的写法，到了 mRNA 前体上读作 GU–AG）。 必须剪接，剪接就可以变着花样来 ——选择性剪接让同一个基因做出不止一个蛋白。 转录出来的大部分根本不编码蛋白：< 200 nt 的是 siRNA 和 miRNA，> 200 nt 的是 lncRNA； Dicer 把长双链 RNA 切成 21–23 nt，RISC（一个 Argonaute 握着一条单链引导 RNA）去切靶 mRNA。 mRNA 做出来还要被读：扫描模式 —— 40S 在 5′ 端上车、往 3′ 滑、见 AUG 就和 60S 合成 80S，而停在哪由 AUG 周围的 Kozak 序列决定。 ⚠ 落点一句：转录只是其中一层。

Say this

Five things sit under all of this, and pH comes first. pH is the negative logarithm of the hydrogen ion concentration, and a group is half dissociated when the pH equals its pKa. The titration curve, the buffer, the isoelectric point and the pH optimum of an enzyme are four readings of that one fact. The rest is molecular biology. Transformation, transduction and transfection each say which cell took up foreign nucleic acid and what carried it across the membrane, while electroporation says only how it was done. A knockout then asks what a gene does by breaking it, and CRISPR changed the economics: twenty new bases instead of a new protein. An interaction method asks whether two molecules touch, and each one reports a signal downstream of the contact rather than the contact itself. And transcription is only one layer: splicing, non-coding RNA and the control of translation initiation all sit after it.

约 45 秒 · 先把 pH 立起来，再顺着「送进去 → 弄坏 → 证明接触 → 转录之后」走一遍

广度 · Say this 说完还有时间，接着往下说 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Lipovová、Hudeček、Hodek

① 一个 pH 单位是浓度的十倍，而且方向是反的 pH

One pH unit is a factor of ten in hydrogen ion concentration, and the scale runs backwards, so more acid means a smaller pH. pH itself is the negative logarithm, to base ten, of that concentration in moles per litre. My textbook never defines pH, so I give that as general knowledge.

读的是位数，不是量。

② 七不是规定出来的，是从水自己的离子积算出来的 ion product of water

Neutral pH seven is not a definition but a result. Water dissociates into a hydrogen ion and a hydroxide, and at twenty-five degrees the product of those two concentrations is ten to the minus fourteen, so in pure water both are ten to the minus seven molar. That is general knowledge rather than my textbook.

中性 ＝ 两者相等，不是「等于 7」。

③ pH 说的是溶液，pKa 说的是一个基团 —— 它正好半解离时的那个 pH pKa

pH describes a solution, but the pKa describes one group: it is the pH at which that group is exactly half dissociated, and numerically the negative logarithm of its acid dissociation constant. Below its pKa the group keeps its proton, above it the group has let it go. A smaller pKa means a stronger acid.

pH 是溶液的性质，pKa 是一个基团的性质。

④ 曲线在 pKa 附近是平的，那一段平就是缓冲本身 Henderson–Hasselbalch

The titration curve is flat near the pKa because both forms are present and take up whatever is added — that flat stretch is the buffering, and away from the pKa the curve is steep. The Henderson–Hasselbalch relation says pH equals the pKa plus the logarithm of the ratio of dissociated to undissociated form. My textbook does not print it, so I give it as general knowledge.

平的那一段就是缓冲本身。

⑤ 挑哪一种和配多浓是两个独立的选择：pKa 定范围，浓度定容量 buffer · buffering range

Two independent choices: the pKa sets the range, roughly one pH unit either side of it, and the concentration sets the capacity, meaning how much acid can be absorbed. A buffer is a weak acid together with its own conjugate base in comparable amounts. My textbook has no section on buffers, so that is general knowledge.

选哪一种 vs 配多浓，两个选择。

⑥ 书外 · general · 要两个标准液，是因为电极在两个方向上漂：零点和斜率 calibrating a pH meter

Two standards are needed because the electrode drifts in two independent ways, its offset and its slope, and one point cannot fix both. I use standard buffers that bracket the sample, usually pH four, seven and ten, rinsing the electrode between them. Temperature matters, because both the electrode response and the standard's own pH change with it. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

两点定一条线：零点和斜率。

⑦ 三个数自己互相验证：2,4 和 9,8 的平均正好是 6,1 titration curve · isoelectric point

The three numbers check each other. The textbook's titration curve of glycine gives pKa one at two point four and pKa two at nine point eight, and it gives pI as the average of the two flanking pKa values, which is the printed isoelectric point of six point one. It adds that a protein is least soluble at its isoelectric point, because no net charge leaves no repulsion between neighbours.

2,4 ＋ 9,8 除以二正好 6,1。

⑧ 酶的 pH 最适不一定就是它工作的那个 pH ⭐ Lipovová pH optimum

An enzyme's pH optimum need not be the pH at which it works: papain has an optimum of eight and comes from a papaya. The textbook says enzyme activity against pH is a curve with a maximum, called the pH optimum, and puts the cause in the acid–base character of the active site; its own table gives pepsin one point five to two point five and trypsin seven point five to eight point five.

用书的表打书的话。

⑨ 小幅改 pH 是可逆的，只换电荷；大幅改才是变性 charge change vs denaturation

The difference is reversibility. A moderate change of pH only changes which groups carry a charge, and that is reversible. A large change denatures the protein, and the textbook lists strong acids and strong bases among its chemical denaturants, next to organic solvents, detergents, urea and guanidine.

先丢盐桥 → 展开 → 聚集。

⑩ 四个词说的是同一件事，分歧只在两个问题 transformation · transduction · transfection

All four name ways of getting foreign nucleic acid into a living cell, and the difference is two questions: which cell, and what carries the nucleic acid across the membrane. Electroporation is the one word that names a method rather than a destination. This is general molecular biology rather than my textbook.

三个去处，一个做法。

⑪ 两边都是负电，所以先加钙再热激 chemical competence

The DNA backbone and the bacterial surface are both negative and repel each other, so cells are held on ice in calcium chloride, whose divalent cation screens that repulsion. A brief heat shock, around forty-two degrees in ordinary practice, is thought to drive the DNA through. The detailed mechanism is still not settled.

「thought to」这三个字要说出口。

⑫ 一个是装错头，什么基因都能带；一个是切错位，只带整合位点旁边的 generalised vs specialised transduction

The difference decides which genes can travel. In generalised transduction a phage head takes a piece of host DNA by mistake while packaging, so any part of the genome can move. In specialised transduction an integrated phage cuts off centre when it leaves, so only genes next to its integration site move; phage lambda carrying gal or bio is the standard case.

一个错在装头，一个错在切出来。

⑬ 接触本身看不见，所以每种方法都报告一个下游信号 interaction methods

Two molecules touching is not something a microscope resolves, so every one of these methods builds a signal downstream of the contact and reports that instead — a colony, a band, an angle, a wavelength. Each method also has a blind spot, and the blind spot decides what a positive result is worth.

主动说盲点，比被挖出来好。

⑭ 酵母双杂交把一个转录因子拆成两半，再让互作把它们拼回去 yeast two-hybrid · bait and prey

A eukaryotic activator needs a DNA-binding domain and an activation domain, and they need not be on the same chain. The known protein fused to the binding domain is the bait; each library insert fused to the activation domain is a prey. A hit means two proteins bind when placed together in a yeast nucleus, and nothing more.

诱饵 ＝ 已知的、固定的那一半。

⑮ SPR 是这组方法里唯一不用标记的：读折射率，给的是一个量而不是一个「有」 ⭐ Hudeček surface plasmon resonance

Surface plasmon resonance is the label-free method here — no label and no dye — and it returns a quantity rather than a yes. It immobilises the bait on a dextran layer over a metal film of nanometre thickness and flows a protein mixture past it. Anything that binds raises the refractive index at the surface and shifts the resonance angle, the angle at which reflected light loses intensity.

先说物理量，再说结论。

⑯ 共沉淀的读数只有「在不在沉淀里」，所以它证明不了两个蛋白直接接触 ⭐ Hodek co-immunoprecipitation · ChIP

In co-immunoprecipitation presence in the pellet is the entire readout, so it shows a shared complex and not direct contact: an antibody against the target is coupled onto a solid matrix and everything hanging off it is spun down. Chromatin immunoprecipitation fixes complexes in living cells, cuts the chromatin, and uses an antibody to enrich the DNA fragments bound by one protein.

两个方法，一个工具。

⑰ neo 一个基因干两件事：放在关键外显子里，既破坏基因又当筛选标记 positive–negative selection

neo does two jobs at once: sitting inside the target's critical exon it both destroys the gene and gives resistance to G418. Homologous recombination is far too rare to find by looking, so HSV thymidine kinase, which turns ganciclovir into a poison, sits outside the matching arms — recombination copies in only what lies between them.

neo 既是破坏，又是标记。

⑱ Cre/LoxP 的意义是：致死基因也能研究，因为在哪删、什么时候删由开 Cre 的启动子说了算 Cre/LoxP · conditional knockout

What makes Cre/LoxP worth having is that a gene whose complete knockout is lethal can still be studied: putting the Cre gene under a tissue-specific or drug-inducible promoter decides when and where the target gene dies. Cre, a recombinase from a bacteriophage, recognises a thirty-four base pair site called LoxP and deletes everything between two LoxP sites pointing the same way.

同向才是删除，反向是倒位。

⑲ CRISPR 换靶就是那二十个核苷酸，Cas9 蛋白从来不重新设计 sgRNA · PAM

Retargeting CRISPR is twenty nucleotides and nothing else: the Cas9 protein is never redesigned. An sgRNA is the crRNA and tracrRNA pair fused into one hairpin, carrying that twenty-nucleotide guide complementary to the target, and Cas9 cuts only where a PAM, usually NGG, follows the target.

改的是经济账，不是生物学。

⑳ 基因还留在基因组里，所以 RNA 干扰只能是敲低，不是敲除 Dicer · RISC · knockdown vs knockout

The gene stays in the genome, so interference gives a knockdown rather than a knockout. Dicer, an RNase III enzyme, cuts long double-stranded RNA into short interfering RNAs of twenty-one to twenty-three nucleotides, and RISC, an Argonaute protein holding one guide strand, cleaves the matching message.

敲低是「少一点」，敲除是「没有」。

陷阱问法

  • "What is pH?" — The negative logarithm to base ten of the hydrogen ion concentration; one unit is a factor of ten. My textbook never defines it. · 主动说通用知识。
  • "What is a buffer?" — A weak acid with its own conjugate base, working about one pH unit either side of its pKa. · 别硬编书上的缓冲系统。
  • "Is the buffering range the same as the buffer capacity?" — No: the range is set by the pKa, the capacity by the concentration. · 位置 vs 容量。
  • "Is putting a plasmid into yeast a transfection?" — Strictly yes, but the field calls it yeast transformation; the naming is partly convention. · 承认分类的边界。
  • "Does a co-immunoprecipitation show that the two proteins touch?" — No, only that they are in the same pelleted complex. · 说 same complex，别说 directly。
  • "Is RNA interference a knockout?" — No, a knockdown: the message is destroyed and the gene stays in the genome. · 两个词说反是实测级别的错。
  • "Does no FRET signal mean no binding?" — No: it also fits two proteins that bind in the wrong orientation. · 阴性结果要会解释。

定义

  • pH — the negative logarithm, to base ten, of the hydrogen ion concentration in moles per litre · 氢离子浓度的负对数
  • pKa — the pH at which one particular group is exactly half dissociated · 半解离时的 pH
  • buffer — a weak acid together with its own conjugate base, resisting a change in pH · 缓冲液
  • isoelectric point — the pH at which a molecule carries no net charge and does not move in an electric field · 等电点
  • competence — the state in which a bacterium can take up DNA from outside · 感受态
  • transfection — the introduction of nucleic acid into a eukaryotic cell · 转染
  • transient vs stable transfection — the single difference is integration into a host chromosome · 瞬时与稳定转染
  • selection marker — a gene carried in with the foreign DNA whose product lets that cell survive a condition killing the rest · 选择标记
  • bait and prey — the known fixed half of a two-hybrid, and each library insert tested against it · 诱饵与猎物
  • reverse genetics — breaking a known sequence on purpose and reading the function off what then goes wrong · 反向遗传学
  • sgRNA — single guide RNA, one hairpin carrying a twenty-nucleotide guide · 单向导 RNA
  • PAM — protospacer-adjacent motif, usually NGG, required next to the target before Cas9 cuts · PAM
  • GT–AG rule — almost every intron starts with GT and ends with AG in the gene, read GU–AG in the transcript · GT–AG 法则
  • alternative splicing — one primary transcript spliced in more than one way, giving different proteins · 选择性剪接
  • Kozak sequence — the sequence around the start codon, A or G, two bases, AUG, then G, that stops the scanning ribosome · Kozak 序列
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • 40S subunit — the small subunit of the eukaryotic ribosome, which binds the messenger RNA first and scans it · 真核小亚基
  • 60S subunit — the large subunit of the eukaryotic ribosome, which joins the 40S to make the complete 80S · 真核大亚基
  • acid — a substance that gives up a proton · 酸
  • acid dissociation constant — Ka, the equilibrium constant for a group losing its proton; the pKa is its negative logarithm · 酸解离常数
  • activation domain — the half of a eukaryotic transcription activator that recruits the transcription machinery · 激活结构域
  • active site — the pocket of an enzyme where the substrate binds and the reaction takes place · 活性中心
  • Agrobacterium — a soil bacterium that transfers a piece of its own DNA into a plant cell, used to make transgenic plants · 农杆菌
  • antibody — a protein made by the immune system that binds one particular shape on one particular molecule · 抗体
  • Argonaute — the protein at the centre of RISC, which holds the guide strand and cuts the matching message · Argonaute 蛋白
  • base — a substance that takes up a proton · 碱
  • buffering capacity — how much acid or base a buffer can absorb before its pH moves, set by its concentration · 缓冲容量
  • buffering range — the pH span over which a buffer works, about one unit either side of its pKa · 缓冲范围
  • calcium chloride — the salt whose divalent cation screens the repulsion between DNA and the cell surface in chemical competence · 氯化钙
  • Cas9 — the CRISPR nuclease that cuts double-stranded DNA where the guide RNA directs it · Cas9 核酸酶
  • chromatin — DNA together with the histones and other proteins bound to it in the nucleus · 染色质
  • chromatin immunoprecipitation — using an antibody to find which DNA fragments one protein was bound to in living cells · 染色质免疫沉淀
  • co-immunoprecipitation — pulling one protein down with an antibody and seeing which proteins come down with it · 免疫共沉淀
  • complex — two or more molecules held together by non-covalent contacts and behaving as one particle · 复合物
  • concentration — the amount of a substance per unit volume of solution · 浓度
  • conditional knockout — a knockout that happens only in chosen tissues or at a chosen time · 条件敲除
  • conjugate base — what a weak acid becomes once it has given up its proton · 共轭碱
  • Cre — a recombinase from a bacteriophage that cuts and rejoins DNA at LoxP sites · Cre 重组酶
  • CRISPR — a bacterial defence system used as a tool, in which a guide RNA directs a nuclease to a chosen sequence · CRISPR
  • denaturation — loss of a protein's folded structure, so that it no longer works · 变性
  • Dicer — an RNase III enzyme that cuts long double-stranded RNA into pieces of 21 to 23 nucleotides · Dicer
  • dissociation — a molecule coming apart into two parts, here an acid releasing its proton · 解离
  • DNA-binding domain — the half of a transcription activator that recognises and binds a DNA site · DNA 结合结构域
  • electrode — the probe that develops a voltage depending on the solution around it, here on its hydrogen ion concentration · 电极
  • electroporation — a brief electric pulse that makes the membrane permeable, so nucleic acid can enter · 电穿孔
  • embryonic stem cells — cultured cells from an early embryo that can still give rise to every tissue, including the germ line · 胚胎干细胞
  • exon — the part of a gene that is kept in the mature RNA · 外显子
  • FRET — Förster resonance energy transfer, energy passing between two fluorophores only when they are within about one to ten nanometres · 荧光共振能量转移
  • G418 — an aminoglycoside antibiotic that kills animal cells unless they carry the neo gene · G418
  • ganciclovir — an antiviral compound that HSV thymidine kinase turns into a poison, so cells carrying that gene die · 更昔洛韦
  • gene — a stretch of DNA carrying the information for one RNA or one protein · 基因
  • generalised transduction — a phage head packages a piece of host DNA by mistake, so any gene can be carried to another cell · 普遍性转导
  • genome — the complete set of DNA an organism carries · 基因组
  • germ line — the cells that give rise to eggs and sperm, so a change in them is passed to the offspring · 生殖系
  • glycine — the smallest amino acid, whose titration curve shows the two pKa values and the pI between them · 甘氨酸
  • GST pull-down — an in vitro binding test in which one protein is fused to glutathione S-transferase and held on a glutathione resin · GST 沉降实验
  • guanidine — a compound that unfolds proteins at high concentration by breaking their hydrogen bonding · 胍
  • guide RNA — the short RNA whose sequence decides where Cas9 or Argonaute acts · 向导 RNA
  • heat shock — the brief warming, around forty-two degrees, that is thought to drive DNA into chemically competent bacteria · 热激
  • Henderson–Hasselbalch equation — pH equals the pKa plus the logarithm of the ratio of conjugate base to undissociated acid · 亨德森-哈塞尔巴尔赫方程
  • homologous recombination — exchange between two DNA molecules that match each other in sequence over a stretch · 同源重组
  • HSV thymidine kinase — the herpes simplex virus enzyme used for negative selection, because it converts ganciclovir into a poison · 单纯疱疹病毒胸苷激酶
  • intron — the part of a gene that is transcribed but removed from the RNA before translation · 内含子（= intron = intervening sequence）
  • ion product of water — the product of the hydrogen and hydroxide ion concentrations, ten to the minus fourteen at twenty-five degrees · 水的离子积
  • knockdown — lowering the amount of a gene product while the gene itself stays in the genome · 敲低
  • knockout — destroying a gene in the genome so that no working product is made · 敲除
  • label — a group attached to a molecule only so that it can be detected, such as a dye or an enzyme · 标记
  • library — a large collection of different DNA fragments or variants handled together in one experiment · 文库
  • lncRNA — long non-coding RNA, a transcript longer than two hundred nucleotides that is not translated · 长非编码 RNA
  • LoxP — the thirty-four base pair site Cre recognises; two of them in the same orientation delete what lies between · LoxP 位点
  • messenger RNA — the transcript that carries a gene's coding sequence to the ribosome · 信使 RNA
  • miRNA — microRNA, a short non-coding RNA that pairs with a message and represses it · 微 RNA
  • neo — a resistance gene used as a positive selection marker, giving resistance to G418 · neo 抗性基因
  • non-coding RNA — an RNA that is never translated and works as RNA · 非编码 RNA
  • nucleotide — one unit of a nucleic acid: a base, a sugar and a phosphate · 核苷酸
  • nucleus — the membrane-bounded compartment of a eukaryotic cell that holds the chromosomes · 细胞核
  • papain — a protease from the papaya, with a pH optimum of about eight · 木瓜蛋白酶
  • pellet — the solid packed at the bottom of the tube after centrifugation · 沉淀
  • pepsin — the protease of the stomach, active between pH one and a half and two and a half · 胃蛋白酶
  • pH meter — an instrument that measures the voltage at a glass electrode and converts it into a pH · pH 计
  • pH optimum — the pH at which an enzyme's activity is highest · pH 最适
  • phage lambda — the E. coli bacteriophage that integrates at one chromosomal site, the standard case of specialised transduction · λ 噬菌体
  • positive–negative selection — a resistance gene placed inside the target and a killing gene outside the arms, so only true recombinants survive · 正负双向选择
  • primary transcript — the RNA as first made, before splicing and the other processing steps · 初级转录本
  • promoter — the DNA sequence where RNA polymerase binds and begins transcription · 启动子
  • proton — a hydrogen ion, a hydrogen atom that has lost its electron · 质子
  • recombinase — an enzyme that cuts two DNA sites and rejoins the ends crosswise · 重组酶
  • refractive index — how much a material slows and bends light, which rises as molecules accumulate at a surface · 折射率
  • RISC — RNA-induced silencing complex, an Argonaute protein holding one guide strand against the target message · RNA 诱导沉默复合体
  • RNA interference — silencing a gene by a short RNA that guides a complex to its message · RNA 干扰
  • RNase III — the class of enzyme that cuts double-stranded RNA · III 型核糖核酸酶
  • salt bridge — an ionic attraction between two oppositely charged side chains in a folded protein · 盐桥
  • scanning model — the 40S subunit binds the 5′ end of the message and moves along it until it meets a suitable AUG · 扫描模式
  • siRNA — small interfering RNA, a short double-stranded RNA that directs cleavage of a message matching it exactly · 小干扰 RNA
  • solid matrix — the insoluble beads or resin an antibody is coupled to, so that whatever binds can be spun down · 固相基质
  • solubility — how much of a substance will dissolve in a given volume before it starts to come out of solution · 溶解度
  • solution — a substance evenly dissolved in a liquid, forming one phase · 溶液
  • specialised transduction — an integrated phage cuts out inaccurately, so only genes next to its integration site are carried away · 局限性转导
  • splicing — removing the introns from a primary transcript and joining the exons together · 剪接
  • standard buffer — a solution of accurately known pH, used to calibrate a pH meter · 标准缓冲液
  • strand — one of the two chains of a double-stranded nucleic acid · 链
  • surface plasmon resonance — a label-free method reading binding as a change in refractive index at a thin metal surface · 表面等离子共振
  • T-DNA — the piece of the Agrobacterium tumour-inducing plasmid that is transferred into the plant genome · T-DNA
  • titration curve — the plot of pH against added acid or base, flat near each pKa and steep away from it · 滴定曲线
  • transcription factor — a protein that binds DNA and controls how much a gene is transcribed · 转录因子
  • transduction — transfer of host DNA from one bacterium to another inside a phage particle · 转导
  • transformation — in bacteria, the uptake of free DNA from outside; in cancer biology, a normal cell becoming malignant · 转化（两个含义）
  • trypsin — the pancreatic protease that cuts after lysine and arginine, with a pH optimum near eight · 胰蛋白酶
  • urea — a compound that unfolds proteins at high concentration · 尿素

PESB · protein engineering and synthetic biology

卡 pe-clone · pe-expr · pe-pur · pe-sys · pe-evo · pe-cfps · 你自己的主场，⭐ Lipovová 整块在这里

  1. 克隆 — 每一种方法都在回答同一个问题 把一段基因和一个载体接成一个能在细胞里复制的环。七种方法听上去是七件事，其实都在答同一句： 怎么让两段 DNA 的末端互相找得到。末端的来处只有四个 —— 切在位点里面（经典酶切）· 切在位点外面（Golden Gate 的 type IIS）· 外切酶啃出单链（LIC 与 Gibson）· 根本不切（Gateway 的位点特异性重组）。 ⚠ 基因从哪来：查文献 → Addgene → RT-PCR → 全基因合成，越往后越贵。 ⚠ 真核基因组 DNA 里有内含子，原核没有剪接体 —— 直接从基因组扩出来放进大肠杆菌，表达出来是废的，所以走 mRNA 的 RT-PCR 或直接合成。
  2. 表达 — 几乎每一个麻烦都是同一个麻烦 从载体里的一个基因到管子里的蛋白。整张卡的脊梁只有一句： 蛋白被造出来的速度，快过细胞折叠它的速度。 折叠是分子内的，一条链自己塌下去；聚集是分子间的，要几条链碰上 —— 所以浓度一高，聚集的速率涨得比折叠快得多。 启动子有两个独立的轴：强度和紧密性，通常互相冲突（清单在 t-tools，别重讲）。 出事之后菌株表要倒着背：从症状问起，不从菌株名问起。 ⚠ 包涵体不是失败 —— 它是纯化上的好消息、产量上的坏消息。
  3. 纯化 — 五种模式，同样的四步 从澄清裂解液，到一管纯的、量得出来的蛋白。 所有模式都跑平衡 → 结合 → 洗涤 → 洗脱，模式之间只差一样：树脂抓的是哪一个性质。 遇到没见过的模式就问两句：它靠什么抓住？改什么它才放开？ 顺序是被逼出来的，不是挑出来的：捕获 → 中度纯化 → 精修， 因为体积从大到小，而要的分辨率从低到高。 ⚠ 亲和层析的判据是二元的 —— 它问「有没有那个标签」，不问「有多少电荷」， 所以它能一步做完别的模式要三步做的事。
  4. 选宿主 — 那张表是一条曲线，不是六个选项 是蛋白需要什么在选宿主，不是你在选宿主。 第一问就分出两类问题：有阶梯的和没有阶梯的 —— 不溶有一整条阶梯可以爬，二硫键也有；而缺糖基化机器连第一级都没有， 因为大肠杆菌没有糖基转移酶、也没有内质网，那是能力不存在，不是没调好。 第二问：糖必须像人的吗。必须像人 → 哺乳动物；只是要折叠帮助 → 昆虫或酵母，便宜得多。 剩下三问都在阵营内部问：二硫键 · 毒性 · 量级。 ⚠ 「它是真核生物所以会糖基化」是标准错答：酵母、昆虫、哺乳动物的糖不是同一种糖。
  5. 定向进化 — 造一堆，再把好的那个找出来 两个动作反复跑：造多样性 → 把好的找出来 → 再造一次。 「反复」和「每轮从上一轮的赢家出发」这两个词是定义里最重的两个。 脊梁一句：蛋白和编码它的基因必须物理连在一起 —— 因为蛋白既不能扩增，也不能测序，一千亿个分子里挑出那一个，没有基因你既读不出它、也做不出第二份。 所有展示方法只差「靠什么连」。 第二条轴是筛选还是选择，它决定了你的库能有多大。 ⚠ 你选什么就得到什么 —— 拿「谁抓得最紧」去选酶，挑出来的是产物抑制最严重的那些。
  6. 无细胞（= cell-free protein synthesis = CFPS = in vitro transcription–translation）与改写密码 — 先搬出来，才能改规矩 上半场：把翻译搬出细胞，在管子里跑。下半场：搬出来之后改它的规矩。 第二件事需要第一件事，这就是两讲合在一起教的原因。 铰链只有一句：重编程要的是减法 —— 某个天然合成酶必须不在场， 否则它一直把自己的氨基酸装到自己的 tRNA 上，靠数量取胜； 而一锅粗裂解物里你拿不掉任何一样东西，重构体系里你只要不加它就行了。 无细胞的所有优点其实是同一条优点：没有活细胞 —— 说出这一句，那张清单可以当场推出来。

Say this

This subject is really one project seen from six angles. First I join my gene to a vector, and every cloning method answers one question: how the two ends are made to find each other. Then I make the protein, usually in E. coli, where almost every problem is the same problem — the protein is made faster than the cell can fold it. Then I purify it, and every mode of chromatography runs the same four steps, differing only in which property the resin binds. If the protein needs modifications E. coli cannot make, I move up the scale of hosts — yeast, insect cells, mammalian cells — and I pay for each step with time and money. Finally, if the protein works but is not good enough, I stop optimising the conditions and change the protein itself: I make a library and either screen or select it. And if it cannot be made in a living cell at all, I go cell-free (also called cell-free protein synthesis, CFPS, in vitro transcription–translation).

约 50 秒 · 一条线：装上载体 → 做出来 → 纯出来 → 不够好就进化它

广度 · 六张卡压成一条线 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 3 位考官：Lipovová、Hudeček、Martínek

① 所有克隆方法都在回答同一个问题：两段 DNA 的末端怎么才配得上 joining two ends

Every method answers one question — what makes the two ends match — and there are only four answers: cut inside the site, cut outside it, chew back one strand, or do not cut at all. Cloning joins a gene to a vector to make a circle that replicates in a cell. In classical restriction cloning the sites are written on the primer tails and the enzyme cuts inside its own site, so the overhang sequence is dictated by the enzyme. The vector is dephosphorylated so that it cannot close on itself, which is the largest source of background colonies.

七种方法，四个来处；磷酸酶是记账，不是化学。

② 原核没有剪接体，所以直接从真核基因组扩出来的基因，表达出来是废的 source of the gene · introns

⚠ Prokaryotes have no spliceosome, so a eukaryotic gene amplified from genomic DNA still carries its introns (also called intervening sequence) and the product is useless. I therefore start from mRNA or synthesise the gene with codon optimisation. The gene itself comes from the literature, from Addgene, from RT-PCR, or from whole-gene synthesis, in rising order of cost.

直接从基因组扩，表达出来是废的。

③ Golden Gate：type IIS 酶切在识别位点外面，所以无疤痕，而且末端由你定 ⭐ Lipovová Golden Gate · BsaI

BsaI, BbsI and BsmBI are type IIS: the class that cuts outside its recognition sequence. Two things follow: the four-base overhang has a sequence I choose, so fragments assemble directionally in one tube; and the site sits on the discarded piece, so the product is scarless.

一件事推出两个结果。

④ domestication 付得起，全靠遗传密码的简并性 —— 用沉默突变删掉部件内部的位点 domestication

Domestication is possible only because the genetic code is degenerate: with silent mutations I remove every internal BsaI, BbsI or BsmBI site from my parts, which would otherwise be cut to pieces. It is a one-off cost, and the parts are then reusable for ever.

付一次，永久可重用。

⑤ 单链末端够长，退火本身就够稳 —— LIC 的十五个碱基根本不需要连接酶 Gibson assembly · LIC

Make the single-stranded end long enough and annealing alone holds the join: LIC uses T4 DNA polymerase as an exonuclease to expose about fifteen bases, which anneal so stably that no ligase is needed. Gibson assembly uses T5 exonuclease with overlaps of twenty to forty base pairs, in one tube at fifty degrees.

末端够长，就不需要连接酶。🪄 内切从中间切，外切从末端啃。

⑥ 同一个复制起点的两个质粒不能共存 —— 共表达两个蛋白时，这一条会直接把实验废掉 origin of replication · incompatibility

⚠ Two plasmids with the same origin cannot coexist in one cell, which decides everything whenever I co-express two proteins. The origin also sets the copy number. The minimal plasmid is an origin of replication plus a selection marker; the rest is added for a purpose.

共表达绝不能共转同一个复制起点。

⑦ 表达的脊梁：蛋白被造出来的速度快过细胞折叠它的速度 folding versus aggregation

Almost every problem in bacterial expression is one problem in a different place. Folding is intramolecular, so one chain collapses on itself. Aggregation is intermolecular, so several chains must meet. Aggregation therefore rises with concentration far more steeply than folding does.

所以降温、降诱导剂全是同一招。

⑧ 要强还是要紧，是由蛋白决定的，不是由启动子决定的 strength versus tightness

Which property matters is decided by the protein, not the promoter — a toxic protein needs tightness before it needs strength. Strength is how much I get after induction, tightness is how little leaks before it, and the two usually conflict. The promoters themselves I have listed already.

清单在 t-tools，这里只说两个轴。

⑨ 菌株表要倒着背：他从症状问起，不从菌株名问起 expression strains

Degraded product sends me to BL21, which lacks the Lon and OmpT proteases. A truncated eukaryotic product is codon bias, so Rosetta, which supplies rare-codon tRNAs. Cytoplasmic disulfides need Origami or SHuffle. All-or-none induction needs Tuner, which lacks lacY.

症状 → 菌株 → 它改了什么。

⑩ 包涵体的好处全在「弄出来」，一条都不解决「折回去」：复性得率只有百分之一到五 ⭐ Lipovová inclusion bodies

Every advantage of inclusion bodies is about getting the protein out, and none of it solves folding: ⚠ refolding yields are typically only one to five per cent. They are aggregates of misfolded recombinant protein in the cytoplasm, high in purity and stable, they let me make products that would otherwise kill the host, and they can be purified under denaturing conditions.

好处全在「弄出来」，没有一条解决「折回去」。

⑪ 五个手段其实只有两族：压低供给，或者提高细胞的处理能力 ⭐ Lipovová solubility tags · chaperones

Five measures, two families — lower the supply or raise the cell's folding capacity. I lower the temperature, lower the IPTG, add a solubility fusion such as SUMO, MBP or thioredoxin, co-express chaperones (also called heat shock protein (many of them)) — DnaK with DnaJ and GrpE, or GroEL with GroES — and only as a last resort change the host. The lecture's own words: adjust the conditions, do not abandon the construct.

融合伴侣放 N 端是机制，不是习惯 —— 它最先出核糖体、最先折好。

⑫ 三条复性路线的目标是同一个：把有效浓度压低，让折叠赢过聚集 refolding

All three routes do one thing — keep the effective concentration low so that folding beats aggregation. I solubilise in six molar guanidinium chloride or eight molar urea, then refold by dialysis, by rapid dilution, or on the column. On-column refolding is the best, because it makes aggregation physically impossible. A glutathione redox pair lets wrong disulfides reshuffle, and arginine suppresses aggregation intermediates.

三条路，一个目标。

⑬ 胞质是被主动维持成还原性的，所以二硫键只有两条出路：改掉那套系统，或者换个房间 disulfide bonds · periplasm

Disulfides do not form in the E. coli cytoplasm because it is kept reducing by thioredoxin and glutathione, so there are exactly two ways out. Either I remove the obstacle with an Origami or SHuffle strain, mutated in trxB and gor, or I avoid the compartment and send the protein to the periplasm with a signal peptide (also called signal sequence) such as pelB or OmpA.

周质天然是氧化性的，还自带 Dsb 校对系统。

⑭ 凝胶过滤的上样体积不能超过柱体积的 5%，所以它只能排在最后 capture · intermediate · polishing

⚠ Size exclusion (also called size-exclusion chromatography, gel filtration) must come last, because its load volume cannot exceed five per cent of the column volume. So the order is forced: capture takes the target out of crude lysate, usually by affinity, and is chosen for capacity; intermediate purification is ion exchange or hydrophobic interaction; polishing is that size exclusion step.

那 5% 是硬的物理上限，不是习惯做法。

⑮ EDTA 是常规裂解缓冲液成分，而它会把镍整个从柱子上抢走 EDTA · DTT strip the nickel

⚠ EDTA is a routine lysis-buffer component, added against metal-dependent proteases, and it is a chelator that strips the nickel off the resin — not an elution but the end of the column. DTT does the same by reducing the nickel. The column turns white and is finished. The imidazole elution I have already described.

咪唑洗脱在 t-tools；这里说的是把柱子弄报废的两样。

⑯ 「中和标签」在柱上，「移除标签」在柱后 —— 而 TEV 会永远留下一个甘氨酸 ⭐ Lipovová tag removal · TEV · SUMO protease

Neutralising means getting the protein off the resin, and the answer is imidazole; removing means cutting the tag off with a protease, afterwards, in a tube. The two proteases differ in what they leave behind: TEV recognises ENLYFQ and leaves an extra glycine on my protein for ever, while SUMO protease reads the fold rather than a sequence, so it leaves a native N-terminus.

一个在柱上，一个在柱后。

⑰ SDS 一次干三件事，把所有差别都压成分子量；A280 不需要标准曲线 ⭐ Hudeček purity and quantity

SDS does three jobs at once — it unfolds the chain, it gives every protein the same charge-to-mass ratio, and it makes everything rod-shaped — so only molecular weight is left to vary. A280 needs no standard curve, because the extinction coefficient is calculated from the sequence, mostly from tryptophan and tyrosine.

这是它跟 Bradford 的根本差别。

⑱ 书外 · general：只有比活能回答「它还活着吗」，跑胶回答不了 specific activity

Only specific activity says whether the target is still active; a gel shows that contaminant bands are gone and nothing more. Specific activity is units of activity per milligram, one row per step of the purification table beside total protein and total activity, and yield and purification fold follow from it. That is outside my textbook, so I give it as general knowledge.

跑胶只说杂带没了，不说活性还在。

⑲ 选宿主的第一问，是分出「有阶梯的问题」和「没有阶梯的问题」 choosing a host

Insoluble protein has a ladder — lower the temperature, change the strain, add a fusion, refold. Unformed disulfides also have a ladder, and it stays inside E. coli. ⚠ Missing glycosylation machinery has no ladder at all: E. coli has no glycosyltransferases and no endoplasmic reticulum, so the ability is absent, not badly tuned.

最常见的错答是把「有二硫键」当成「必须用真核系统」。

⑳ 只有哺乳动物细胞给人源复合型糖 —— 所以「它会糖基化」不是一个答案 glycosylation is not one thing

Only mammalian cells give the human complex type. Yeast builds high-mannose structures that are immunogenic in humans and vary between molecules in one batch, and insect cells modify, but not with human glycans either. So the question is not whether a host glycosylates, but whether it installs the glycan my protein needs.

两个后果分开说：免疫原性，和异质性。

㉑ E. coli 没有哪一条优点特别突出，靠的是累加；但同位素标记只有它做得起 E. coli

It is still the default after forty years because the advantages add up: it doubles in about twenty minutes, the media are cheap, scale-up is predictable, and the toolbox is enormous. It is also the only practical route to isotope labelling, which needs a minimal medium with one defined nitrogen and one defined carbon source.

「累加起来的」这句话本身就是答案的一部分。

㉒ 酵母的细胞壁极难破，所以 Pichia 的标准策略是把蛋白分泌出来 yeast · Pichia pastoris

Yeast's cell wall is very hard to break, which is why the standard strategy in Pichia pastoris is secretion, avoiding lysis altogether. Its other real weakness is high-mannose glycans. It is a eukaryote, so it performs most modifications, and it is still cheap to culture.

缺点推出策略。

㉓ 昆虫细胞存在的理由：要真核的折叠帮助，但糖链不必是人的 insect cells

I choose insect cells when the protein needs eukaryotic folding help but the glycans do not have to be human-like. They fold complex proteins and carry out many modifications, including disulfide bonds, at moderate cost, sitting between yeast and mammalian cells on the scale.

中间那一档的存在理由，一句话。

㉔ polyhedrin 启动子又强又删得起，是同一个原因：野外靠结晶保命，培养里不需要 baculovirus · polyhedrin promoter

The vector is a baculovirus, a circular double-stranded DNA virus of about one hundred and twenty kilobase pairs. It infects over six hundred species, mostly larvae, and ⚠ it does not replicate in vertebrates, which is the safety argument. The protein is made during the infection, not by a plasmid sitting quietly in the cell. My gene goes under the polyhedrin promoter, one of the strongest known: in the wild the virus must pack itself into polyhedrin crystals to survive outside a host, so that very late promoter has been pushed to be extremely strong. In culture the virus needs none of that, so polyhedrin can be replaced by my gene at no cost to replication.

强度和「删得起」是同一个原因。

㉕ 昆虫系统的代价是时间：第一个样品之前，先花一两周做出一支病毒 Bac-to-Bac · virus stock

The cost of this system is one to two weeks before the first sample, spent making a virus. In Bac-to-Bac the gene is cloned between Tn7 sites, transformed into DH10Bac, and transposed into a bacmid, a viral genome maintained in E. coli. Blue-white screening takes four days, the large bacmid DNA is handled gently, transfected into Sf9, and P1 virus is harvested after four to seven days, then amplified to P2 and P3.

第一个样品之前，先花掉一两周做病毒。

㉖ 感染时要定的只有一个数：病毒与细胞的比值 MOI；细胞系有三种，性格各不相同 MOI · Sf9 · Sf21 · Hi5 · S2

The one number to set at infection is the multiplicity of infection, the ratio of virus particles to cells, optimised between one to one and ten to one. Sf9 and Sf21, from the fall armyworm, are the robust standard at about six times ten to the sixth per millilitre; Hi5 often gives higher secreted yields; S2, from Drosophila, reaches the highest density. They grow at twenty-four to twenty-seven degrees.

半贴壁，很容易驯化成悬浮 —— 所以能放大。

㉗ 代次效应：滴度很高而表达在掉 ⭐ Lipovová passage effect

⚠ Some baculoviruses lose the gene of interest, especially after P3. My gene is pure burden to the virus — extra bases to replicate, no benefit — so viruses that drop the insert replicate faster and take over. The symptom is high titre with falling expression. I express from P1 or P2 and freeze a low-passage seed stock.

不知道这一条，现场会一头雾水。

㉘ 昆虫细胞往两个方向各比一次，答案才完整 insect cells compared both ways

Against E. coli: better folding, disulfides and modifications, but slower and dearer, because a virus stock must be made and amplified first. Against mammalian cells: cheaper and higher yielding, but ⚠ the glycans are not human, which matters if the protein is a therapeutic. S2 offers a third way — a stable line built in about three weeks, so no virus is made again.

两个方向都比，才不是背了一个位置。

㉙ 瞬时还是稳定，取决于「这个蛋白我要做多少次」，不是这一次要多少 CHO · HEK293 · transient versus stable

Only mammalian cells give correct folding, correct disulfide pairing and complex glycosylation; HEK293 is the research line and CHO the production line. Between the two modes the deciding question is how many times I will make this protein: ⚠ transient gives material in under a week but varies between batches, while stable takes weeks to build and then varies hardly at all.

不是「这一次要多少」。

㉚ 定向进化的脊梁：蛋白和编码它的基因必须物理连在一起 ⭐ Hudeček genotype–phenotype linkage

A protein cannot be amplified and cannot be sequenced, so the winner is lost without its gene. Phage display links them in a particle: ⚠ pIII at three to five copies allows monovalent display, which is required for true affinity, while pVIII at about 2700 copies selects weak binders that win by numbers. Ribosome display omits the stop codon (also called termination codon, nonsense codon); mRNA display uses puromycin. Yeast display I have already described.

多价会把亲和力测虚 —— Kd 要在单价上读。

㉛ 筛选还是选择，决定了你的库能有多大 screening versus selection · library size

In a screen I test every variant; in a selection I test nothing, and the pressure does the work. Synthesis gives about ten to the eighteen molecules, but getting into cells drops it to ten to the six or eight; in vitro display returns to ten to the thirteen. ⚠ I must screen three times the library size for coverage, because sampling is with replacement.

中间那个悬崖，整个是「细胞」两个字。

㉜ 怎么建库，取决于你已经知道多少 ⭐ Lipovová ⭐ Martínek error-prone PCR · DNA shuffling

If I know which residues, that is site-directed mutagenesis; if I know the positions but not the amino acids, saturation mutagenesis; if I want mutations but not where, error-prone PCR, which needs Taq and never a proofreading polymerase, biased with manganese and unbalanced dNTPs to give one to five mutations per gene. ⚠ The library is never unbiased. DNA shuffling instead recombines homologous genes with DNase I and a primerless PCR. De novo design starts from nothing at all.

太少全是野生型，太多全是死的。

㉝ 无细胞合成的所有优点其实是同一条：没有活细胞 cell-free protein synthesis

Translation runs in a tube, from a crude extract (top-down) or a reconstituted PURE system (bottom-up). Because no cell is alive, it makes toxic products, it accepts a linear PCR product directly as template, and it is the fastest of all, about one day. The costs are low yield, high price and unsolved scale-up.

三件套：核糖体 · 氨基酸连同 tRNA 和合成酶 · 能量再生。

㉞ 改写密码：先说自己在答哪一个问题 orthogonal pair · amber suppression · flexizyme

There are two independent questions: how the monomer gets onto the tRNA, and which codon points to it. The heavy route evolves an orthogonal synthetase and tRNA pair for every new monomer; the shortcut is a flexizyme, a ribozyme that charges tRNA by reading only an activating group and the 3-prime CCA, so the synthetase question never arises. Amber suppression answers the second question, using TAG, the least-used codon in E. coli.

2026-09-01 丢掉的就是这一问 —— 他要的是 flexizyme。

㉟ 蛋白有毒就用 pLysS：T7 溶菌酶是 T7 聚合酶的天然抑制剂，专吃诱导前漏出来的那点 leaky expression · pLysS

The promoter driving T7 RNA polymerase in a DE3 strain is slightly leaky, so a little polymerase is made before induction. For a toxic protein that is enough to kill the culture. Two fixes: put glucose in the medium, which keeps the lac system repressed, and use BL21(DE3) pLysS, which carries a plasmid making T7 lysozyme. ⚠ T7 lysozyme is a natural inhibitor of T7 RNA polymerase: it mops up the small amount made before induction, but is overwhelmed once induction starts. pLysE makes more of it and represses harder.

BL21 和 DE3 是两件事：BL21 缺 Lon 和 OmpT 两个蛋白酶，DE3 是染色体上那个带 T7 聚合酶基因的溶原体。⚠ IPTG 不是加在你基因的启动子上 —— 你的基因前面是 T7 启动子，IPTG 打开的是聚合酶的启动子，隔了一层。

陷阱问法

  • "How do you neutralise a His-tag?" — Imidazole, which competes for the nickel. A protease removes the tag, which is a different question. · 洗脱层答咪唑，移除层才答蛋白酶。
  • "It is a eukaryote, so glycosylation is solved?" — No. Yeast, insect and mammalian glycans are different glycans, and only mammalian cells give the human complex type. · 「会糖基化」不是答案。
  • "My protein has disulfide bonds, so I need a eukaryotic host?" — No. I stay in E. coli and use Origami or SHuffle, or the periplasm. · 二硫键有阶梯。
  • "You added IPTG to a pET plasmid and got nothing." — The strain has no DE3 cassette, so the host never makes T7 RNA polymerase. · 宿主里没有那个盒子。
  • "Why are the white colonies still wrong?" — Blue-white screening only shows that lacZ-alpha was interrupted; it says nothing about orientation or sequence. · 白色只说明插进去了。
  • "Your baculovirus titre is high but expression is falling." — That is the passage effect: viruses that have lost the insert replicate faster and take over. · 用 P1 或 P2 表达。
  • "You selected for binding and got a poor enzyme." — You get what you select for; display selects binding, so it enriches product inhibition. Catalysts need compartmentalisation in droplets. · 选什么得什么。

定义

  • recombinant — DNA joined together outside the organism · 重组
  • heterologous expression — expressing a gene in a species it does not come from · 异源表达
  • type IIS enzyme — a restriction enzyme that cuts outside its own recognition site, such as BsaI · IIS 型限制酶
  • domestication — removing internal recognition sites from a part by silent mutation · 驯化
  • inclusion body — an aggregate of misfolded recombinant protein in the cytoplasm · 包涵体
  • solubility tag — a highly soluble fusion partner such as SUMO or MBP that keeps the product soluble · 可溶性标签
  • IMAC — immobilised metal affinity chromatography, the mode a His-tag binds on Ni-NTA · 固定化金属亲和层析
  • specific activity — units of enzyme activity per milligram of total protein · 比活
  • baculovirus — the insect-cell expression vector; a large circular double-stranded DNA virus that does not replicate in vertebrates · 杆状病毒
  • bacmid — a baculovirus genome maintained as a plasmid in E. coli · 杆粒
  • multiplicity of infection — the ratio of infectious virus particles to cells at infection · 感染复数
  • passage effect — loss of the gene of interest from a baculovirus stock over successive amplifications · 代次效应
  • directed evolution — rounds of screening or selection separated by further diversification · 定向进化
  • screen versus selection — in a screen every variant is tested; in a selection only survivors remain · 筛选与选择
  • error-prone PCR — amplification under conditions that lower fidelity, giving one to five mutations per gene · 易错 PCR
  • DNA shuffling — fragmenting homologous genes with DNase I and reassembling them into chimaeras by primerless PCR · 基因重排
  • orthogonal pair — a synthetase and tRNA that work only with each other and not with the host's · 正交对
  • amber suppression — reading the stop codon（= stop codon = termination codon = nonsense codon） TAG as an amino acid instead of a stop · 琥珀抑制
  • flexizyme — an artificial ribozyme that charges any tRNA with almost any activated monomer · 柔性酶
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • leaky expression — the small amount of protein made from an inducible system before any inducer is added · 漏表达
  • A280 — absorbance at 280 nanometres, which measures protein because tryptophan and tyrosine absorb there · 280 nm 吸光度
  • Addgene — a non-profit repository that stores and distributes plasmids deposited by laboratories · Addgene 质粒库
  • affinity chromatography — separation by specific binding of the target to a ligand fixed on the resin · 亲和层析
  • aggregation — several unfolded chains sticking to one another instead of each folding on its own · 聚集
  • aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase — the enzyme that attaches one particular amino acid to its own tRNA · 氨酰-tRNA 合成酶
  • arginine — an amino acid added to refolding buffers, where it suppresses aggregation intermediates · 精氨酸
  • Bac-to-Bac — a system in which the gene is transposed into a bacmid inside E. coli to make a recombinant baculovirus · Bac-to-Bac 系统
  • BbsI and BsmBI — two further type IIS restriction enzymes used in Golden Gate assembly · 另两种 IIS 型酶
  • binding — the chromatography step in which the sample is loaded and the target attaches to the resin · 结合
  • BL21 — an E. coli expression strain lacking the Lon and OmpT proteases · BL21 菌株
  • bottom-up — building a cell-free system only from purified components, as in the PURE system · 自下而上
  • Bradford assay — measuring protein by a dye that changes colour on binding it, read against a standard curve · 考马斯亮蓝法
  • BsaI — a type IIS restriction enzyme, cutting outside its own recognition sequence · BsaI
  • capture — the first purification step, taking the target out of crude lysate and chosen for capacity · 捕获
  • CCA end — the three nucleotides at the 3′ end of every tRNA, to which the amino acid is attached · tRNA 的 CCA 末端
  • cell line — a population of cultured cells of one type that can be grown on indefinitely · 细胞系
  • cell-free protein synthesis — translation run in a tube from extract or purified components, with no living cell · 无细胞蛋白合成
  • chaperone — a protein that binds an unfolded chain and stops it aggregating while it folds · 分子伴侣（= chaperone = heat shock protein (many of them)）
  • CHO cells — Chinese hamster ovary cells, the standard mammalian line for producing therapeutic proteins · 中国仓鼠卵巢细胞
  • chromatography — separation by partitioning a mixture between a stationary phase and a mobile phase · 层析
  • clarified lysate — the supernatant after broken cells have been centrifuged, with debris removed · 澄清裂解液
  • codon — three consecutive bases of the message, specifying one amino acid or a stop · 密码子
  • codon optimisation — rewriting a gene's codons to suit the host's tRNA supply, without changing the protein · 密码子优化
  • column — a tube packed with resin, through which the sample and buffers are passed · 层析柱
  • complex glycosylation — the mature human type of N-glycan, which only mammalian cells build · 复合型糖基化
  • de novo design — designing a protein sequence from first principles rather than starting from a natural one · 从头设计
  • degeneracy — the property of the genetic code that most amino acids are specified by more than one codon · 简并性
  • denaturing conditions — buffers containing urea or guanidinium that keep the protein unfolded throughout a step · 变性条件
  • dephosphorylation — removing the vector's 5′ phosphates so that it cannot be ligated closed on itself · 去磷酸化
  • DH10Bac — the E. coli strain that holds a bacmid and the helper functions for transposing a gene into it · DH10Bac 菌株
  • dialysis — removing a small solute slowly through a membrane that keeps the protein behind · 透析
  • dissociation constant — the concentration of free ligand at which half the binding sites are occupied · 解离常数
  • DnaK, DnaJ and GrpE — an E. coli chaperone system that binds exposed stretches of a new chain and releases them cycle by cycle · DnaK 伴侣系统
  • DNase I — an enzyme that cuts DNA, used to break homologous genes into fragments for shuffling · DNase I
  • DTT — dithiothreitol, a reducing agent; on a nickel column it reduces the nickel and ruins the resin · 二硫苏糖醇
  • E. coli — Escherichia coli, the bacterium used as the default expression host · 大肠杆菌
  • EDTA — ethylenediaminetetraacetic acid, a chelator that binds divalent metals and strips nickel off an IMAC resin · EDTA
  • elution — changing the conditions so that the bound target leaves the resin and comes off the column · 洗脱
  • ENLYFQ — the sequence TEV protease recognises and cuts after · TEV 识别序列
  • equilibration — running binding buffer through the column first, so the resin is in the right conditions before loading · 平衡
  • exonuclease — an enzyme that removes nucleotides one at a time from the end of a strand · 外切酶
  • folding — one chain collapsing on itself into its defined three-dimensional structure · 折叠
  • Gateway — a cloning system that joins DNA by site-specific recombination instead of cutting it · Gateway 克隆
  • Gibson assembly — joining fragments through overlaps of twenty to forty base pairs in one isothermal reaction · Gibson 组装
  • glutathione redox pair — reduced and oxidised glutathione together, letting wrongly paired disulfides reshuffle · 谷胱甘肽氧化还原对
  • glycan — the sugar chain attached to a protein · 糖链
  • glycosylation — the attachment of sugar chains to a protein after translation · 糖基化
  • glycosyltransferase — an enzyme that adds one sugar to a growing glycan; E. coli has none · 糖基转移酶
  • Golden Gate — one-tube directional assembly with type IIS enzymes, giving chosen overhangs and a scarless product · Golden Gate 组装
  • GroEL and GroES — the E. coli chaperonin, a barrel that encloses one protein while it folds · GroEL/GroES
  • guanidinium chloride — a salt that unfolds proteins by breaking their hydrogen bonding, used at six molar to dissolve inclusion bodies · 盐酸胍
  • HEK293 — human embryonic kidney cells, the standard mammalian line for research-scale expression · HEK293 细胞
  • Hi5 — an insect cell line that often gives higher yields of secreted protein · Hi5 细胞
  • high-mannose glycan — the yeast type of N-glycan, carrying many mannose residues and immunogenic in humans · 高甘露糖型糖链
