  • hydrophobic interaction chromatography — binding proteins by their exposed hydrophobic patches in high salt and eluting in low salt · 疏水相互作用层析
  • insect cells — cultured moth or fly cells used with a baculovirus as an expression system · 昆虫细胞
  • intermediate purification — the middle step, ion exchange or hydrophobic interaction, between capture and polishing · 中度纯化
  • intron — the part of a eukaryotic gene that is transcribed but removed before translation · 内含子（= intron = intervening sequence）
  • ion exchange chromatography — separation by net charge, eluting on a salt gradient · 离子交换层析
  • isotope labelling — growing cells on a single defined nitrogen or carbon source so the protein carries that isotope · 同位素标记
  • lacY — the gene for the lactose permease; without it IPTG enters only by diffusion, so induction is even across the culture · 乳糖透过酶基因
  • library — the whole collection of sequence variants made in one round of diversification · 文库
  • LIC — ligation-independent cloning, exposing about fifteen single-stranded bases that anneal without a ligase · 不依赖连接酶的克隆
  • Lon and OmpT — two E. coli proteases that would degrade the product, absent from BL21 · Lon 与 OmpT 蛋白酶
  • manganese — the metal added to an error-prone PCR to lower the polymerase's fidelity · 锰离子
  • MBP — maltose-binding protein, a large and very soluble fusion partner · 麦芽糖结合蛋白
  • monovalent display — showing about one copy per particle, which is what makes a measured affinity a true affinity · 单价展示
  • mRNA display — linking each protein to its own messenger RNA through puromycin, entirely in vitro · mRNA 展示
  • multivalent display — showing many copies per particle, so that weak binders are selected by numbers · 多价展示
  • mutagenesis — deliberately introducing changes into a DNA sequence · 诱变
  • Origami — an E. coli strain mutated in trxB and gor, so disulfide bonds can form in its cytoplasm · Origami 菌株
  • overhang — a short single-stranded stretch at the end of a duplex, which pairs with a complementary one · 突出末端
  • pelB and OmpA — two signal peptides used to send a recombinant protein into the E. coli periplasm · pelB 与 OmpA 信号肽（= signal peptide = signal sequence）
  • periplasm — the compartment between the inner and outer membranes of a Gram-negative bacterium, which is oxidising · 周质
  • phage display — showing each variant as a fusion to a phage coat protein, with its gene inside the same particle · 噬菌体展示
  • Pichia pastoris — a yeast that grows on methanol, usually made to secrete the product · 毕赤酵母
  • pIII — a minor coat protein of filamentous phage, present in three to five copies per particle · pIII 外壳蛋白
  • plasmid incompatibility — two plasmids sharing one origin of replication cannot be kept in the same cell · 质粒不相容性
  • polishing — the last and highest-resolution purification step, usually size exclusion · 精修
  • polyhedrin promoter — the very late baculovirus promoter, one of the strongest known, and dispensable in culture · polyhedrin 启动子
  • proofreading polymerase — a DNA polymerase with a 3′→5′ exonuclease that removes a wrongly added base · 高保真聚合酶
  • protease — an enzyme that cuts peptide bonds（= peptide bond = amide bond） · 蛋白酶
  • PURE system — a translation system reconstituted only from purified components, so anything unwanted is simply left out · PURE 体系
  • purification fold — the rise in specific activity compared with the starting material · 纯化倍数
  • puromycin — an antibiotic resembling the end of a charged tRNA, which joins the growing chain and ends synthesis · 嘌呤霉素
  • refolding — returning a solubilised, unfolded protein to its active structure · 复性
  • resin — the solid beads packed in a column, carrying the groups that bind the sample · 层析介质
  • ribosome display — leaving out the stop codon so that protein, ribosome and messenger RNA stay together as one particle · 核糖体展示
  • ribozyme — an RNA molecule that catalyses a chemical reaction · 核酶
  • Rosetta — an E. coli strain supplying tRNAs for codons that are rare in E. coli · Rosetta 菌株
  • RT-PCR — reverse transcription followed by PCR, amplifying a sequence from messenger RNA so that introns are absent · 逆转录 PCR
  • S2 cells — a Drosophila cell line, reaching the highest density and usable as a stable line without a virus · S2 细胞
  • saturation mutagenesis — replacing chosen positions by all twenty amino acids · 饱和突变
  • SDS — sodium dodecyl sulfate, a detergent that unfolds proteins and gives them a uniform negative charge · 十二烷基硫酸钠
  • secretion — the cell exporting the product into the medium, so that it need not be released by lysis · 分泌
  • Sf9 and Sf21 — the standard insect cell lines, both from the fall armyworm · Sf9 与 Sf21 细胞
  • SHuffle — an E. coli strain with an oxidising cytoplasm and a disulfide isomerase, for disulfide-bonded proteins · SHuffle 菌株
  • signal peptide — a short N-terminal sequence directing a protein into an export route, cut off during transport · 信号肽
  • silent mutation — a base change that leaves the encoded amino acid unchanged · 沉默突变
  • site-directed mutagenesis — changing chosen residues to chosen amino acids deliberately · 定点突变
  • size exclusion chromatography — separation by size on a porous resin that binds nothing, so the sample is diluted · 凝胶过滤（= size-exclusion chromatography = gel filtration）
  • spliceosome — the RNA and protein machine that removes introns; prokaryotes have none · 剪接体
  • stable line — a cell line with the gene integrated in its genome, so it expresses without being retransfected · 稳定细胞系
  • SUMO — a small protein used as a solubility fusion, removed afterwards by SUMO protease · SUMO 标签
  • SUMO protease — a protease that recognises the folded SUMO domain rather than a sequence, leaving a native N-terminus · SUMO 蛋白酶
  • suspension culture — cells grown freely in stirred liquid rather than attached to a surface, which is what allows scale-up · 悬浮培养
  • T4 DNA polymerase — used in LIC for its 3′→5′ exonuclease, to expose long single-stranded ends · T4 DNA 聚合酶
  • T5 exonuclease — the enzyme that chews back one strand in Gibson assembly to expose the overlaps · T5 外切酶
  • tag — a short sequence added to a protein to give it a property it lacks, such as binding a nickel column · 标签
  • Taq — the thermostable DNA polymerase without proofreading, which is why error-prone PCR uses it · Taq 聚合酶
  • TEV protease — the tobacco etch virus protease, which cuts after ENLYFQ and leaves one extra glycine behind · TEV 蛋白酶
  • thioredoxin — a small protein that keeps the E. coli cytoplasm reducing, and is also used as a solubility fusion · 硫氧还蛋白
  • titre — the concentration of infectious virus particles in a stock · 病毒滴度
  • Tn7 — the transposon sites through which the gene is moved into the bacmid · Tn7 位点
  • top-down — making a cell-free system from a crude cell extract rather than from purified parts · 自上而下
  • transient transfection — putting DNA into cells that express it for a few days without integrating it · 瞬时转染
  • transposition — a transposon moving a piece of DNA into a defined target site · 转座
  • trxB and gor — the genes for thioredoxin reductase and glutathione reductase, both mutated in Origami · trxB 与 gor
  • Tuner — an E. coli strain lacking lacY, so the induction level can be set rather than being all or none · Tuner 菌株
  • whole-gene synthesis — having the gene built chemically from scratch instead of amplifying it from a source · 全基因合成
  • yield — how much of the starting protein or activity is still present after a step · 回收率

Lab techniques

实验技术 34 题 · 答案都是你自己写过的，这里压成一句一条

  1. 配一管溶液 — 一条算术，两个坑 质量 ＝ 摩尔浓度 × 体积 × 分子量，稀释是 C₁V₁ ＝ C₂V₂。整门课的算术就这两条。 ⚠ 摩尔浓度是「每升溶液」，不是「每升溶剂」 —— 这五个字就是为什么要定容： 先用少于终体积的溶剂溶开，再补到刻度。溶质自己占体积，先量一升水再把粉倒进去，配出来一定偏稀。 分子量照瓶子上印的取，带结晶水的盐每摩尔更重。 标准溶液的定义是「浓度被准确知道」，所以定量转移（称量舟要冲洗进瓶）、定容、贴标签，每一步都是在保这个「准确」。 ⚠ 标准品的误差是系统误差：它原封不动传给每一个照它测出来的样品 —— 标准液坏了不是打折扣，是整批作废。 两个试剂各有各的坑：1 M EDTA 的坑是溶解度（游离酸要用氢氧化钠把 pH 提到约 8 才溶得开）； 6 M 盐酸胍的坑是体积（固体本身占掉很大空间，先小体积溶、再定容），而且它溶解强烈吸热，会又冷又慢。
  2. pH 与缓冲 — 你挑的从来不是酸或碱，是一个 pKa pH ＝ 氢离子活度的负对数，差一个单位就是十倍。 缓冲液 ＝ 一个弱酸 ＋ 它自己的共轭碱：加进来的酸被碱型吃掉，加进来的碱被酸型吃掉。 pH = pK_a + log([共轭碱]/[弱酸]) —— 要用的那一层读法只有一句： 两种形式相等时 log 项为零，pH ＝ pKa，所以挑一个 pKa 贴着目标 pH 的缓冲液，工作区间是 pKa ± 1。 ⚠ 「酸性缓冲液 vs 碱性缓冲液」是个假对立：机制一模一样，差别只是那个 pKa 落在 7 的哪一边 （乙酸盐 ≈ 4.8 在下，Tris ≈ 8.1 在上）。 两个要盯死：Tris 的 pKa 随温度强烈移动，室温调好的 Tris 进冷室就是另一个 pH；磷酸盐会和钙镁生成沉淀。 pH 计那一题接得上：它测的是电压再换算成 pH，斜率和截距都会漂，所以要用两个把目标夹在中间的标准液 （先 7 定截距，再 4 或 10 定斜率），同温校准，用新鲜标准液，电极吸干不能擦。
  3. 量出一个数 — 每台仪器都只量一个物理量 吸光度 ＝ ε × c × l，这条线性关系就是分光光度法能定量的全部理由。 仪器四件：光源 · 单色器 · 固定光程的比色皿 · 检测器；先读空白，把缓冲液和比色皿自己的吸收减掉。 读数超出线性范围就稀释后重测，绝不外推 —— 那里检测器正好饱和。 标准曲线的要害是：它校准的是整个流程，不只是仪器 —— 所以标准品必须和样品同缓冲液、同温育、同一批次测。 ⚠ OD600 是这一族里的假成员：细胞在 600 nm 不吸收，它们把光散射出光路，所以它没有 ε，也分不出死活。 核酸定量三层递进：A260（快，但凡带碱基的都吸）→ 必须连比值一起报（A260/A280 ≈ 1.8 是 DNA，≈ 2.0 是 RNA）→ 只对双链发光的荧光染料法才特异 → 要看完整性就跑胶。 而这一切的下游是移液器：准确度（离真值多近）靠校准和选量程，精密度（重复之间多一致）靠手法 —— ⚠ 一支移液器可以非常精密，同时稳定地错着。
  4. 把混合物分开 — 力、分配、筛孔，三种分法 离心靠一个远大于重力的力，沉降速率由大小、形状、密度决定。 差速离心（低速沉大的、留上清、再提速沉下一档）把裂解液变成澄清裂解液； 密度梯度离心分的不是大小而是浮力密度，样品走到自身密度等于介质那一点就停。 ⚠ rcf 正比于「半径 × 转速²」，所以同一个 rpm 换个转子就是另一个力 —— 方法里写 × g，不写 rpm。 层析只有一个想法：混合物在固定相和流动相之间分配，作用强的走得慢；每一种模式都跑同样四步 —— 平衡 · 结合 · 洗 · 洗脱。模式之间变的只有一样：固定相被选来利用哪一种物理性质 （电荷 → 离子交换 · 疏水性 → HIC 与反相 · 大小 → 凝胶过滤 · 特异结合 → 亲和）。 ⚠ 一个诊断习惯：读跑柱先看流穿液 —— 待在那里的蛋白根本没结合过，问题在结合条件，不在洗脱。 电泳的前提只有一句：单位质量电荷必须一致，否则你同时在量电荷和大小，两个都读不出来。 核酸天生满足（每个核苷酸一个磷酸），蛋白不满足 —— 所以要加 SDS 人为造出来； 而两块胶是为了让所有蛋白从同一条起跑线出发：浓缩胶只浓缩，分离胶才筛分。
  5. 认出一个分子 — 抗体认形状，质谱认质量 免疫测定的分工是固定的：抗体提供特异性，标记提供信号。 夹心 ELISA 三次加样（捕获抗体 → 样品 → 针对另一个表位的检测抗体）， ⚠ 要两支抗体认两个表位，这正是它特异的原因；定量还是靠标准曲线。 Western blot 是同一个想法换个摆法：分离 → 转印 → 探测， 而转印是必需的不是图方便 —— 让分离成立的那些胶孔太细，笨重的抗体钻不进去。 ⚠ 上样对照验的是 Western blot 自己验不了的那个假设（每道起点总蛋白相同）， 挑它的条件不是「丰度高」，而是已被独立证明不随处理而变；用法是做比值，不是看有没有。 质谱换一条路：它不问形状，它测质荷比。三个部件（离子源 · 质量分析器 · 检测器）、 两种软电离（电喷雾 · MALDI）、一条 bottom-up 流程（胰蛋白酶酶解 → 液相色谱 → 选一条肽碎裂 → 从质量差读序列）。 落点一句：它给的是翻译后修饰，那正是测序告诉不了你的东西。
  6. 做出一个分子，再别弄伤自己 — 表达、PCR、无菌、安全 细菌表达五步一条线：质粒 → 转化 → 长到对数中期诱导 → 收菌破碎 → 亲和层析 ＋ 跑胶验。 ⚠ 质粒上的元件第二句就主动报完，别等他问：T7 启动子 · 核糖体结合位点 · 复制起点 · 抗性标记 · 亲和标签。 菌株的选择对应三个不同的难处：转录（BL21(DE3) 带 T7 聚合酶）· 翻译（Rosetta 补稀有密码子 tRNA）· 折叠（Origami／SHuffle 的胞质还原性较弱，二硫键能形成），蛋白酶保护垫在这三样下面。 IPTG 替代的是别乳糖，而且 ——诱导是把一个阻挡拿掉，不是把一个基因打开。 给表面受体加标签，整道题的轴是拓扑：先定要检测哪一侧，再避开会被切掉的信号肽。 PCR 六样里镁最该讲道理：它两头都有代价，少了酶慢，多了错配引发； 而 Taq 没有校正活性，所以要克隆、表达、测序的一律换高保真酶。 最后两件事的判据都极干净：灭菌的标准永远是杀死芽孢（121 ℃ 就是这么定出来的，0.22 µm 过滤是除菌不是灭菌）； 危险化学品按控制层级答，而 PPE 排在最后是因为它只保护一个人，失效的时候没有声音。

Say this

Almost every technique here measures one physical quantity, and the honest answer to "what is the principle" is to name that quantity first. A spectrophotometer measures absorbance, and the Lambert-Beer (also called Beer–Lambert law, Beer's law) law makes it proportional to concentration. OD600 is the exception that proves it: cells do not absorb at that wavelength, they scatter light out of the beam. A centrifuge applies a force, and that force depends on the rotor radius, so I report times g rather than rpm. Chromatography measures partitioning between a stationary and a mobile phase. Electrophoresis measures size, but only once the charge per unit mass is uniform, which is why proteins need SDS. An immunoassay measures antibody binding, and mass spectrometry measures the mass to charge ratio. Once I have named the quantity, the control and the working range follow from it.

约 45 秒 · 先报物理量，再报它的对照和线性范围 —— 这个顺序本身就是答案

广度 · 34 题，一题一两句 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 4 位考官：Hudeček、Hodek、Šulc、Lipovová

① 「每升溶液」这四个字决定了操作：定容到刻度，而不是加一升溶剂 molarity

Molarity is moles of solute per litre of solution, not per litre of solvent, so I dissolve in less than the final volume and make up to the mark — the solute takes up volume itself. Mass equals molarity times volume times molecular weight, dilutions follow C1V1 equals C2V2, and I take the molecular weight from the bottle, because a hydrated salt weighs more per mole.

溶质自己占体积。

② 标准液里的误差是系统误差，会传进每一个未知样 standard solution

An error in a standard solution is systematic: it passes into every unknown measured against it. That is why every step protects the accurately known concentration — I transfer quantitatively, rinsing the weighing boat into the volumetric flask, make up to the mark, mix by inversion and label.

留在称量舟里的都是误差。

③ 两个试剂两个坑：EDTA 卡在 pH，6 M 盐酸胍卡在体积 EDTA · guanidine hydrochloride

Two reagents, two different traps. The free acid of EDTA will not dissolve at all until sodium hydroxide raises the pH to about eight, and I set the pH before making up to volume. Six molar guanidine hydrochloride goes into a smaller volume first, because the solid takes up so much space, and dissolving it is strongly endothermic.

一个卡在 pH，一个卡在体积。

④ 等量的酸和它的共轭碱，pH 正好等于 pKa —— 所以缓冲液是按 pKa 挑的 pH · buffer · Henderson-Hasselbalch

Equal amounts give pH equal to the pKa, which is how a buffer is chosen. A buffer is a weak acid with its conjugate base: added acid is absorbed by the base form, added base by the acid form. Henderson-Hasselbalch says pH equals the pKa plus the log of that ratio, and pH itself is the negative logarithm of the hydrogen ion activity.

所以挑 pKa 贴着目标 pH 的那一个，范围 ±1。

⑤ Tris 的 pKa 随温度跑，这才是这道题真正的坑；酸碱缓冲液机制完全一样 acidic versus basic buffers

⚠ Tris has a strongly temperature-dependent pKa, which is the practical trap here. Otherwise the mechanism is identical and only the chosen pKa differs: acetate, near four point eight, buffers below pH seven, and Tris, near eight point one, above it; phosphate precipitates with calcium and magnesium.

室温调好的 Tris 进冷室就是另一个 pH。

⑥ 电极在斜率和零点两个方向上都会漂，所以一点校准没用 calibrating a pH meter

A glass electrode drifts in both slope and offset, so a single point cannot fix both. I use two standard buffers that bracket my target — conventionally pH seven for the offset, then pH four or ten for the slope — calibrate at the temperature I will measure at, use fresh standards, and blot the electrode rather than wipe it. The meter is really converting a voltage into a pH.

单点校准正好掩盖坏掉的那一项。

⑦ 吸光度等于消光系数乘浓度乘光程，这个线性关系才让它能定量 ⭐ Hudeček Lambert-Beer law

Absorbance equals the molar extinction coefficient times concentration times path length, so absorbance is linearly proportional to concentration — which is what makes spectrophotometry quantitative. I use it for protein at 280 nanometres, nucleic acid at 260 and NADH at 340, and it fails at high concentration and assumes no scattering.

280 是色氨酸和酪氨酸在吸。

⑧ 吸光度是两束光的比值取对数，所以它没有单位，也所以空白本身就是测量的一部分 ⭐ Hudeček spectrophotometry

Absorbance is the logarithm of incident over transmitted light, so it has no units, and the blank — everything except the analyte — is part of the measurement rather than a formality: read first, it subtracts buffer and cuvette. The instrument is a light source, a monochromator, a cuvette of fixed path length and a detector.

280 nm 以下要用石英比色皿。

⑨ 标准曲线校准的是整个流程，不只是仪器 standard curve

I read the unknown off a line built from standards measured in the same run, so the method never measures concentration directly. Two constraints carry every failure: the unknown must fall inside the range of the standards, and the standards must be treated exactly like the samples.

超出范围就稀释重测，绝不外推。

⑩ OD600 叫「光密度」不叫「吸光度」，因为光是被细胞散射出去的，不是被吸收 ⭐ Hudeček OD600

OD600 is called an optical density and not an absorbance, because the light is not absorbed: the cells scatter it out of the beam. Optical density at six hundred nanometres is therefore a turbidity reading standing in for cell density. It counts living and dead cells alike and is linear only to about 0.6 to 0.8.

要真正的活菌数就涂板数 CFU。

⑪ A260 ＝ 1 对双链 DNA 是 50 µg/mL、对 RNA 是 40 —— 它本身分不清两者，所以要报比值 quantifying DNA and RNA

An A260 of one is about 50 micrograms per millilitre of double-stranded DNA but about 40 for RNA, and the reading itself cannot tell them apart. So absorbance at 260 nanometres is quickest but I report ratios too: A260 over A280 near 1.8 for DNA and 2.0 for RNA, while a fluorescent dye is the specific option.

A260/A230 偏低 ＝ 带进了胍盐或苯酚。

⑫ 一支移液器可以非常精密，同时一直是错的 —— 准确度靠校准，精密度靠手法 accuracy and precision of pipetting

A pipette can be very precise and consistently wrong. Accuracy comes from calibration, checked gravimetrically with distilled water where one milligram is one microlitre, and from using the pipette in the upper part of its range; precision comes from technique — first stop to aspirate, second only to expel, vertical, slow, and pre-wet the tip.

不预湿，第一次排液就偏少。

⑬ 那层空气会胀也会缩，所以易挥发、粘稠、密度大的液体要用正向置换 types of micropipettes

Air displacement puts a cushion of air between piston and liquid and is unreliable for volatile, viscous or dense liquids, because that cushion expands and contracts. Positive displacement has the piston touching the liquid in a disposable capillary, and it is also what I use in PCR work, since there is no air path for an aerosol.

单道 · 多道 · 连续分液 · 电动，是形式不是机制。

⑭ 差速离心分的是沉降速度，密度梯度分的是浮力密度 —— 后者有终点，前者没有 role of a centrifuge

Differential centrifugation separates by rate and a density gradient by position: the first pellets the largest things and spins the supernatant harder, while in a gradient each species stops where its own buoyant density matches the medium. A centrifuge applies a force far greater than gravity, and sedimentation rate is set by size, shape and density.

对侧管一定要配平。

⑮ 只写 rpm 的方法是重复不出来的，因为力还取决于转子半径 rpm versus rcf

A protocol quoting rpm alone cannot be reproduced, because the same rpm in a different rotor gives a different force. rpm is how fast the rotor turns; rcf is the force the sample feels, as multiples of gravity, and it is proportional to the radius times the square of the speed.

换算表只对某一个转子半径成立。

⑯ 五种模式只有一条原理：跟固定相作用越多，走得越慢 principles of chromatography

Whatever interacts more with the stationary phase moves more slowly — that is the whole of it, and the mixture is partitioned between a stationary and a mobile phase. Every mode runs equilibrate, bind, wash and elute, isocratically or on a gradient. I read a run starting from the flow-through, because protein sitting there never bound at all.

上样前先换到结合缓冲液。

⑰ 凝胶过滤是唯一什么都不结合的模式，所以它温和，但会稀释 types of chromatography

Size exclusion (also called size-exclusion chromatography, gel filtration) is the only mode that binds nothing, so it is gentle but dilutes. Ion exchange uses net charge and elutes on a salt gradient; hydrophobic interaction binds in high salt and elutes in low; affinity, usually a histidine tag on a nickel column, is the highest-resolution single step; reverse phase uses hydrophobicity under organic solvent, so it denatures and is analytical.

HIC 与反相同一原理，区别是蛋白活不活得下来。

⑱ 电泳按大小分的前提是单位质量电荷一致 principle of gel electrophoresis

Charged molecules move through a porous gel and are sieved by size, but only if the charge per unit mass is the same for everything — otherwise I measure charge and size at once and can read neither. Nucleic acid meets that for free, one phosphate per nucleotide; a protein does not, so SDS imposes a uniform negative charge.

DNA 用嵌入染料，蛋白用考马斯或银染。

⑲ 浓缩胶只浓缩，分离胶才筛分 stacking and resolving gel

Separation needs every protein to start from the same line, but a loaded well is millimetres deep. The stacking gel, large pores at pH 6.8, compresses the sample into a thin band by isotachophoresis without separating anything; the resolving gel, small pores at pH 8.8, then sieves by size.

做堆积的是缓冲液里的甘氨酸，不是胶。

⑳ 碱裂解能单独留下质粒，靠的是小闭环能重新退回去，染色体 DNA 不行 DNA extraction from bacteria

For plasmid I use alkaline lysis, which works because on neutralisation the small closed circle renatures and stays soluble while chromosomal DNA precipitates. Genomic extraction is four steps: lyse with SDS after lysozyme opens the peptidoglycan, remove protein with proteinase K and RNA with RNase A, separate with phenol-chloroform or a silica column, then precipitate with salt and cold ethanol.

留着 RNA 会把 A260 抬高。

㉑ 夹心法之所以特异，是因为同一个分子必须同时被两个表位认出来 ⭐ Hodek immunoassays · sandwich ELISA

A sandwich ELISA is specific because one molecule must be recognised at two different epitopes at once: a capture antibody coats the plate and a detection antibody against the second epitope carries the enzyme. In any immunoassay the antibody supplies specificity and the label supplies signal, using the specific binding of an antibody to its antigen in a complex mixture.

空白孔 ＋ 不加检测抗体的孔，两个对照。

㉒ 转印是必需的：让分离成为可能的那些胶孔，对抗体来说太细了 ⭐ Hodek Western blot

The transfer is necessary, not a convenience: the very gel pores that made separation possible are too fine for a bulky antibody, and the soft gel would not survive repeated washing. The three separable steps are separate by SDS-PAGE, transfer to nitrocellulose or PVDF, then probe with a primary and a labelled secondary antibody.

Southern 是 DNA，Northern 是 RNA，Western 是蛋白。

㉓ 上样对照验的是 Western blot 自己验不了的那个假设 loading control

It checks that every lane started with the same total protein, an assumption the blot depends on and cannot otherwise verify, since uneven pipetting, uneven transfer or partial degradation all look like a real change in my protein. The condition for choosing it is that the protein is known not to respond to my treatment, and I use it as a ratio within the lane.

肌动蛋白、微管蛋白、GAPDH 各有已发表的例外。

㉔ 难处在「要让它带电，又不能把它打碎」，所以才有电喷雾和 MALDI ⭐ Šulc mass spectrometry

The difficulty is ionising a large molecule without destroying it, which is why electrospray and MALDI exist. Mass spectrometry then measures the mass to charge ratio of ions, with an ion source, a mass analyser and a detector. The bottom-up workflow digests with trypsin, separates the peptides, then fragments one and reads the sequence from mass differences.

磷酸化与未磷酸化：同一个基因，不同的分子。

㉕ 五步里有两个选择是有理由的：对数中期诱导，降温换可溶性 ⭐ Lipovová protein expression workflow

Two of the steps are choices with reasons: I induce at mid exponential phase, and at a lower temperature, trading rate for solubility. I clone the gene into a plasmid carrying a T7 promoter, a ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence), an origin of replication, an antibiotic marker and an affinity tag, transform BL21(DE3), induce with IPTG, sonicate on ice, then purify by affinity chromatography and check every fraction on a gel.

元件那一串别等他问，第二句就报完。

㉖ 三个菌株对着三个不同的难处：转录、翻译、折叠 ⭐ Lipovová BL21(DE3) · Rosetta · Origami

Three strains, three difficulties. Transcription: DE3 means the strain carries the T7 RNA polymerase gene, so it works with pET vectors, and BL21 also lacks the Lon and OmpT proteases. Translation: Rosetta adds tRNAs for codons rare in E. coli. Folding: Origami or SHuffle have a less reducing cytoplasm, so disulfide bonds can form.

没有 (DE3)，pET 质粒什么都不表达。

㉗ 诱导是拿掉一个阻挡，不是打开一个开关 IPTG

Induction removes a block rather than switching a gene on: IPTG and the natural inducer allolactose both bind the LacI repressor of the lac operon and make it release the operator. IPTG is not metabolised, so the level stays where I set it, while lactose is degraded by the very operon it turns on.

50 µM～1 mM，对数中期加。

㉘ N 端信号肽会在转运时被切掉，所以标签绝不能放在它前面 ⭐ Lipovová tagging a surface receptor

⚠ The N-terminal signal peptide (also called signal sequence) is cleaved during translocation, so a tag placed in front of it is lost; I put it after the signal peptide or on the C terminus, on a flexible linker and away from the ligand site. A surface receptor also sits in three compartments, so I first decide which side to detect: an extracellular tag can be labelled on intact living cells, a cytoplasmic one needs permeabilisation.

破坏转运的标签，回答的是另一个问题。

㉙ 六样成分里镁是两头都有代价的那一个：少了酶慢，多了出非特异 components of a PCR mixture

Magnesium is the one component costly in both directions: it is the polymerase cofactor and it also stabilises primer-template pairing, so too little makes the enzyme slow and too much gives mispriming and primer dimers. The six are template, two primers, a thermostable polymerase, the four dNTPs, magnesium and buffer, and the primers flank the target, so they fix both ends and set the annealing temperature.

出非特异条带，先把镁降下来。

㉚ 校正是在同一个酶内部完成的，不是另外一条修复途径 proofreading

A mismatch pairs badly and stalls extension, so the primer terminus moves into the three prime to five prime exonuclease site, the wrong base is excised and synthesis resumes — a correction inside the enzyme, not a separate repair pathway. Taq lacks it, while Pfu and Kapa have it, so anything cloned, expressed or sequenced needs a proofreading enzyme.

早期循环的一个错会被扩增成一大部分。

㉛ β-内酰胺酶是分泌出去的，所以只有氨苄板上会长卫星菌落 ampicillin versus kanamycin

Satellite colonies appear only on ampicillin because resistant cells secrete beta-lactamase, which clears a halo around the colony where non-resistant cells then grow. Ampicillin has a hydrolysable beta-lactam ring, destroyed by heat as well; kanamycin is an aminoglycoside with no such ring, and neither is autoclaved — both are filter sterilised into cooled medium.

卡那抗性在胞内修饰药物，培养基仍有选择性。

㉜ 灭菌的标准永远是杀死芽孢 sterilising lab equipment

Every method is set by the endospore, not by the vegetative cell. The default is autoclaving: saturated steam at 121 degrees for fifteen to twenty minutes, with dry heat for metal and glass that must stay dry. Heat-labile things go through a 0.22 micrometre filter, which removes bacteria rather than killing them.

胶带只证明它热过，要用芽孢条验证。

㉝ 危险化学品按控制层级答，PPE 排在最后 hierarchy of controls

I answer in order: substitution first, a SYBR-type stain instead of ethidium bromide; then engineering controls, a fume hood or shielding, which protect everyone without depending on memory; then administrative controls, reading the safety data sheet and segregating waste. PPE comes last, because it protects one person and fails silently.

洒了先提醒周围的人，再确认是什么。

㉞ PCR 的镁两头都是坑：太少没产物，太多丢特异性 Mg2+ · EDTA · TE buffer

Taq polymerase cannot work without magnesium: the real substrate is not the free dNTP but the dNTP with a magnesium ion on it, and the active site uses magnesium to do the chemistry. ⚠ With too little, there is no product at all; with too much, the reaction loses its specificity — primers bind where they nearly match, and you get extra bands. ⚠ EDTA is a chelator: it takes divalent metal ions away. That is why EDTA in the loading buffer stops a restriction digest, and why DNA is stored in TE buffer, Tris plus EDTA — with no magnesium, any contaminating DNase is dead.

看见 EDTA 就想「它在抢二价离子」。所以 His-tag 洗脱用咪唑，不用 EDTA —— EDTA 会把柱子上的镍一起洗掉。

陷阱问法

  • "Your reading is above the highest standard — what now?" — Dilute and read again; never extrapolate, because the detector saturates there. · 稀释重测，绝不外推。
  • "Is OD600 absorbance?" — No. Cells scatter light out of the beam, so there is no extinction coefficient. · 散射不是吸收。
  • "Can I just quote rpm?" — Not reproducibly; rcf depends on the rotor radius, so a protocol must say times g. · 写 × g。
  • "Which component actually creates the stacking effect?" — Glycine in the running buffer, not the gel: slow at pH 6.8, fast at pH 8.8. · 是甘氨酸。
  • "Why do satellite colonies appear only on ampicillin?" — Beta-lactamase is secreted and clears the drug around the colony. · 卡那是胞内修饰。
  • "Why does a T7 system use IPTG at all?" — The T7 polymerase gene itself sits under lac control in DE3, so the control is one layer removed. · 隔了一层。
  • "What can mass spectrometry tell you that sequencing cannot?" — Post-translational modifications, and whether the protein was actually made. · 翻译后修饰。
  • "Two antibodies against one epitope?" — They compete, so the signal falls instead of rising. · 必须认不同表位。
  • "Why is PPE last?" — It protects one person, depends on being done right every time, and fails silently. · 失效时没有提示。

定义

  • molarity — moles of solute per litre of solution · 摩尔浓度
  • buffer — a weak acid together with its conjugate base, resisting change in pH · 缓冲液
  • pKa — the pH at which a group is exactly half dissociated · 半解离时的 pH
  • absorbance — the logarithm of incident over transmitted light, and dimensionless · 吸光度
  • extinction coefficient — the absorbance of a one molar solution over a one centimetre path · 消光系数
  • accuracy — closeness to the true value · 准确度
  • precision — reproducibility between replicates · 精密度
  • rcf — the force on the sample as multiples of gravity, set by radius times speed squared · 相对离心力
  • isotachophoresis — concentrating a sample between a fast and a slow ion front · 等速电泳
  • chaotrope — a salt such as guanidine hydrochloride that disrupts hydrogen bonding and unfolds proteins · 离液剂
  • epitope — the part of an antigen that an antibody actually binds · 表位
  • loading control — a second, unchanging protein used to normalise lanes on a blot · 上样对照
  • endospore — the dormant bacterial form that sets the sterilisation standard · 细菌芽孢
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • chelator — a molecule that grips a metal ion and takes it out of solution, for example EDTA · 螯合剂
  • TE buffer — Tris plus EDTA, the standard buffer for storing DNA, where the EDTA removes the magnesium that DNases need · TE 缓冲液
  • 0.22 µm filter — a membrane with pores too small for bacteria to pass, which removes them rather than killing them · 除菌滤膜
  • acetate buffer — a buffer of acetic acid and acetate, with a pKa near four point eight, for the acid side · 醋酸缓冲液
  • affinity chromatography — separation by specific binding of the target to a ligand fixed on the resin · 亲和层析
  • air displacement pipette — a pipette with a cushion of air between piston and liquid, unreliable for volatile, viscous or dense liquids · 空气置换移液器
  • alkaline lysis — a plasmid preparation in which alkali denatures everything and only the small closed circle renatures on neutralisation · 碱裂解法
  • allolactose — the natural inducer of the lac operon, which IPTG replaces · 别乳糖
  • ampicillin — a beta-lactam antibiotic used for plasmid selection, destroyed by beta-lactamase and by heat · 氨苄青霉素
  • annealing temperature — the temperature at which the primers bind the template, set by their sequence · 退火温度
  • antibody — a protein made by the immune system that binds one particular shape on one particular molecule · 抗体
  • antigen — the molecule an antibody binds · 抗原
  • assay — a procedure that measures how much of something is present, or whether it is active · 测定方法
  • autoclaving — sterilising with saturated steam under pressure, at 121 degrees for fifteen to twenty minutes · 高压蒸汽灭菌
  • beta-lactam ring — the four-membered ring of penicillins, which beta-lactamase hydrolyses · β-内酰胺环
  • beta-lactamase — the secreted bacterial enzyme that destroys ampicillin by opening that ring · β-内酰胺酶
  • BL21(DE3) — the E. coli expression strain carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene and lacking the Lon and OmpT proteases · 常用表达菌株
  • blank — a sample containing everything except the analyte, read first so the buffer and cuvette are subtracted · 空白
  • bottom-up proteomics — digesting the protein into peptides first, then identifying it from the peptide masses · 自下而上蛋白质组学
  • buoyant density — the density of a particle itself, which decides where it stops in a density gradient · 浮力密度
  • C1V1 = C2V2 — the dilution equation: concentration times volume is the same before and after dilution · 稀释公式
  • calibration — setting an instrument against known values so that its readings correspond to true ones · 校准
  • centrifugation — spinning a sample so that particles sediment under a force much greater than gravity · 离心
  • chromatography — separation by partitioning a mixture between a stationary phase and a mobile phase · 层析
  • cofactor — a non-protein component an enzyme needs in order to work, such as magnesium for a polymerase · 辅因子
  • concentration — the amount of a substance per unit volume of solution · 浓度
  • control — a parallel sample with a known expected result, run to show that the measurement itself is working · 对照
  • Coomassie stain — a blue dye that binds protein, the routine way of visualising bands on a gel · 考马斯亮蓝染色
  • cuvette — the small vessel of fixed path length holding the sample in a spectrophotometer · 比色皿
  • DE3 — the cassette in the chromosome carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene under lac control · DE3 盒
  • denaturation — loss of a protein's folded structure, so that it no longer works · 变性
  • density gradient centrifugation — spinning through a medium of rising density, where each species stops at its own buoyant density · 密度梯度离心
  • detector — the component that converts the light or ions reaching it into an electrical signal, and which saturates above its range · 检测器
  • differential centrifugation — pelleting the largest particles at low speed and then spinning the supernatant harder · 差速离心
  • dilution — adding solvent to lower the concentration by a known factor · 稀释
  • dNTP — a deoxynucleoside triphosphate, the building block the polymerase adds to the growing strand · 脱氧核苷三磷酸
  • dry heat sterilisation — sterilising in a hot oven, for metal and glass that must stay dry · 干热灭菌
  • EDTA — ethylenediaminetetraacetic acid, a chelator of divalent metals, whose free acid dissolves only above about pH eight · EDTA
  • electrophoresis — driving charged molecules through a gel with an electric field, so that they are sieved by size · 电泳
  • electrospray ionisation — ionising a large molecule gently by spraying its solution from a charged needle · 电喷雾电离
  • ethidium bromide — a DNA stain that slots between base pairs, replaceable by a safer dye · 溴化乙锭
  • exponential phase — the part of the growth curve where the doubling time is constant, and where protein expression is induced · 对数期
  • extrapolation — reading a value outside the range the standards covered, which the calibration does not support · 外推
  • flow-through — the liquid leaving the column during loading, holding whatever did not bind · 流穿液
  • fume hood — a ventilated enclosure that draws vapours away from the worker · 通风橱
  • GAPDH — glyceraldehyde 3-phosphate dehydrogenase, a glycolytic enzyme often used as a loading control · GAPDH
  • gel — a porous solid network through which molecules are driven and sieved by size · 凝胶
  • glass electrode — the pH-sensitive probe whose voltage depends on the hydrogen ion concentration outside it · 玻璃电极
  • glycine — the amino acid in the running buffer whose slow-moving front creates the stacking effect · 甘氨酸
  • gravimetric check — weighing the water a pipette dispenses, where one milligram of water is one microlitre · 称重校准
  • Henderson-Hasselbalch equation — pH equals the pKa plus the logarithm of the ratio of conjugate base to acid · 亨德森-哈塞尔巴尔赫方程
  • hierarchy of controls — the fixed order for handling a hazard: substitution, engineering controls, administrative controls, then PPE · 控制层级
  • His-tag — a short run of histidines added to a protein so that it binds a nickel column · 组氨酸标签
  • hydrophobic interaction chromatography — binding by exposed hydrophobic patches in high salt and eluting in low salt · 疏水相互作用层析
  • induction — adding a molecule that relieves repression, so the cloned gene begins to be transcribed · 诱导
  • intercalating dye — a dye that slots between the base pairs of DNA and fluoresces there · 嵌入染料
  • ion exchange chromatography — separation by net charge, eluting on a salt gradient · 离子交换层析
  • IPTG — isopropyl thiogalactoside, a lactose analogue that binds LacI and is not metabolised · 异丙基硫代半乳糖苷
  • kanamycin — an aminoglycoside antibiotic, with no beta-lactam ring, inactivated inside the cell by modifying the drug · 卡那霉素
  • lac operon — the E. coli gene set for lactose use, controlled by the LacI repressor · 乳糖操纵子
  • LacI — the repressor protein that sits on the lac operator until an inducer binds it · lac 阻遏蛋白
  • Lambert-Beer（= Beer–Lambert law = Beer's law） law — absorbance equals the molar extinction coefficient times concentration times path length · 朗伯-比尔定律
  • linear range — the span of concentrations over which the signal stays proportional to the amount present · 线性范围
  • lysate — the whole mixture released when cells are broken open · 裂解液
  • lysozyme — the enzyme that cuts the peptidoglycan of the bacterial wall, used to open cells · 溶菌酶
  • magnesium — the divalent ion a DNA polymerase needs, which also stabilises primer–template pairing · 镁离子
  • MALDI — matrix-assisted laser desorption ionisation, ionising a molecule from a solid matrix with a laser pulse · 基质辅助激光解吸电离
  • mass spectrometry — measuring the mass to charge ratio of ions, with an ion source, a mass analyser and a detector · 质谱
  • mass-to-charge ratio — an ion's mass divided by its charge, the quantity a mass spectrometer actually measures · 质荷比
  • medium — the nutrient mixture, liquid or solidified, in which cells are grown · 培养基
  • mobile phase — the liquid that carries the sample through the column · 流动相
  • molecular weight — the mass of one mole of a substance, taken from the bottle because a hydrated salt weighs more · 分子量
  • monochromator — the component that selects one wavelength out of the light source · 单色器
  • nitrocellulose — one of the two membrane materials protein is transferred onto in a Western blot（= Western blot = immunoblot） · 硝酸纤维素膜
  • OD600 — optical density at 600 nanometres, a turbidity reading standing in for cell density · 菌液浊度读数
  • Origami and SHuffle — E. coli strains with a less reducing cytoplasm, in which disulfide bonds can form · 可形成二硫键的菌株
  • path length — the distance the light travels through the sample, fixed by the cuvette · 光程
  • permeabilisation — making holes in the cell membrane so that an antibody can reach the inside · 通透化
  • Pfu and Kapa — DNA polymerases with 3′→5′ proofreading, used whenever the product will be cloned or sequenced · 高保真聚合酶
  • pH meter — an instrument that measures a voltage at a glass electrode and converts it into a pH · pH 计
  • phenol-chloroform extraction — separating nucleic acid into the water phase while protein goes to the organic phase and the interface · 酚-氯仿抽提
  • positive displacement pipette — a pipette whose piston touches the liquid in a disposable capillary, with no air path · 正向置换移液器
  • PPE — personal protective equipment, the last line of control because it protects one person and fails silently · 个人防护装备
  • precipitation — bringing a dissolved substance out of solution as a solid, here DNA with salt and cold ethanol · 沉淀
  • primer — a short single-stranded DNA that pairs with the template and gives the polymerase a 3′ end to extend · 引物
  • primer dimer — two primers annealing to each other and being extended, giving a short unwanted product · 引物二聚体
  • proofreading — the 3′→5′ exonuclease activity inside the polymerase that excises a wrongly added base · 校对活性
  • proteinase K — a robust protease used to remove protein during a nucleic acid preparation · 蛋白酶 K
  • PVDF — polyvinylidene difluoride, the other membrane material used for Western blotting · PVDF 膜
  • quantitative transfer — getting all of the weighed solid into the flask, by rinsing the weighing vessel into it · 定量转移
  • quartz cuvette — a cuvette that transmits ultraviolet light, where glass and plastic absorb it · 石英比色皿
  • resolving gel — the lower gel, of small pores at pH 8.8, in which proteins are actually separated by size · 分离胶
  • reverse phase chromatography — separation by hydrophobicity under organic solvent, which denatures the protein · 反相层析
  • RNase A — the enzyme added to destroy RNA during a DNA preparation · 核糖核酸酶 A
  • Rosetta — an E. coli strain supplying tRNAs for codons that are rare in E. coli · Rosetta 菌株
  • rotor — the part of the centrifuge holding the tubes, whose radius decides the force at a given speed · 转子
  • rpm — revolutions per minute, how fast the rotor turns, which is not itself a force · 每分钟转数
  • safety data sheet — the document listing a chemical's hazards, handling and disposal · 安全数据表
  • sandwich ELISA — an assay in which a capture and a detection antibody bind two different epitopes of one molecule · 夹心 ELISA
  • satellite colony — a small non-resistant colony growing in the zone where secreted beta-lactamase has destroyed the ampicillin · 卫星菌落
  • SDS — sodium dodecyl sulfate, a detergent that unfolds proteins and gives them a uniform negative charge · 十二烷基硫酸钠
  • SDS-PAGE — polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis in SDS, separating proteins by molecular weight alone · SDS 聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳
  • signal peptide — a short N-terminal sequence directing a protein into an export route, cut off during translocation · 信号肽（= signal peptide = signal sequence）
  • silica column — a spin column whose silica membrane binds nucleic acid in high salt and releases it in water · 硅胶柱
  • silver stain — a far more sensitive protein stain than Coomassie, depositing metallic silver at the bands · 银染
  • size exclusion chromatography — separation by size on a porous resin that binds nothing, so the sample is diluted · 凝胶过滤（= size-exclusion chromatography = gel filtration）
  • solute — the substance dissolved in the solvent, which takes up volume of its own · 溶质
  • solvent — the liquid in which the solute is dissolved · 溶剂
  • sonication — breaking cells with ultrasound, done on ice because it heats the sample · 超声破碎
  • spectrophotometry — measuring how much light of one wavelength a sample absorbs · 分光光度法
  • stacking gel — the upper gel, of large pores at pH 6.8, which compresses the sample into a thin band without separating it · 浓缩胶
  • standard curve — the line built from standards measured in the same run, off which an unknown is read · 标准曲线
  • standard solution — a solution whose concentration is accurately known, used as the reference · 标准溶液
  • stationary phase — the solid phase packed in the column, which the sample interacts with · 固定相
  • sterilisation — killing every living thing including endospores, as opposed to merely removing bacteria · 灭菌
  • supernatant — the liquid left above the pellet after centrifugation · 上清
  • SYBR stain — a fluorescent DNA stain used in place of ethidium bromide · SYBR 染料
  • systematic error — an error in the same direction every time, which repeating the measurement cannot remove · 系统误差
  • T7 promoter — a promoter read only by T7 RNA polymerase · T7 启动子
  • T7 RNA polymerase — the single-subunit phage polymerase that transcribes only from T7 promoters · T7 RNA 聚合酶
  • Taq — the thermostable DNA polymerase of Thermus aquaticus, which has no proofreading · Taq 聚合酶
  • template — the strand the polymerase reads in order to build the complementary new strand · 模板
  • topology — which parts of a membrane protein lie on which side of the membrane · 拓扑
  • transfer — in blotting, moving the separated bands out of the gel onto a membrane · 转膜
  • Tris — a common buffer with a pKa near eight point one, which moves strongly with temperature · Tris 缓冲液
  • trypsin — the protease used to digest a protein into peptides before mass spectrometry, cutting after lysine and arginine · 胰蛋白酶
  • volumetric flask — a flask calibrated to one exact volume at its mark, used to make a solution up · 容量瓶
  • water of crystallisation — water built into a salt's crystal, which makes each mole of that salt weigh more · 结晶水
  • Western blot — proteins separated by SDS-PAGE, transferred to a membrane and detected with antibodies · 蛋白质印迹

Microbiology

微生物 32 题 · 答案都是你自己写过的，这里压成一句一条

  1. 细菌长什么样 — 一切从壁开始 肽聚糖＝N-乙酰葡糖胺和 N-乙酰胞壁酸交替成链，再由短肽交联；做交联的是转肽酶。 细菌相对环境是高渗的，水一直往里灌，壁是唯一挡住裂解的那一层。 革兰阳性：单层膜外一层厚肽聚糖，贯穿磷壁酸。革兰阴性：薄肽聚糖躲在周质里，外面还有一层脂多糖外膜。 ⚠ 别说革兰阴性「没有肽聚糖」——它有，只是薄。外膜那句后面接的是 lipid A ＝ 内毒素，他很容易追过去。 壁外面还有两样：荚膜（多糖，抗吞噬）和饿出来的芽孢（不是繁殖）。
  2. 怎么养 · 怎么繁殖 · 怎么算 繁殖只有一个词：二分裂。所以一个菌落 ＝ 一个细胞的克隆后代，划线分离才算得上纯化手段。 生长曲线是细胞数取对数对时间作图，四个期：延滞 · 对数 · 稳定 · 衰亡。 ⚠ 只有对数期里「生长速率」才是一个有意义的数，表达蛋白也在对数中期诱导。 量它靠 OD₆₀₀——但那是光散射，死活一起算，要区分就另做活菌计数。 代谢分类两条独立的轴交叉：碳源（自养／异养）× 能源（光养／化能养，化能养再分无机／有机），第三条轴是末端电子受体。
  3. 怎么认出是谁 — 表型一路走到序列 老路是生化试验组合：过氧化氢酶 · 氧化酶 · 凝固酶加糖发酵，读的是 pH 指示剂的颜色。 ⚠ 定菌的是一整组答案的模式，不是任何单独一个试验；而且前提是纯培养，混样会给你一个很自信的错答案。 新路是16S rRNA 基因测序：保守区让一对通用引物扒得住，可变区携带种的信号，不用培养。 ⚠ 测的是 16S 的基因（DNA），不是 rRNA 本身。 Carl Woese 当年就是拿这条分子比出了古菌和三域——今天鉴定还用 16S，用的就是他那套方法。
  4. 基因怎么在细菌之间传 — 进化不是一棵树 水平基因转移三条路：转化（感受态细胞吃裸 DNA）· 转导（噬菌体捎带宿主 DNA）· 接合（质粒经性菌毛直接过去）。 ⚠ 后果是一个基因可以一步跳进一个很远的谱系，所以基因组是镶嵌的：核心基因组 ＋ 附属基因组。 质粒是这件事的主要载具，它的定义性质是自带复制起点、独立复制。 噬菌体两条程序：裂解和溶原——溶原那条正是 DE3 的原理。
  5. 实验室里怎么用细菌 — 克隆 · 菌株 · 开关 克隆按「接口是怎么做出来的」分组，不是背一串名字：酶切–连接 · Golden Gate · Gibson，外加 TA／TOPO、Gateway、RF。 判据一句话：克隆株是 recA⁻ endA⁻（DH5α），表达株带 DE3 且缺蛋白酶（BL21(DE3)）。 ⚠ 菌株名里没有 (DE3)，pET 质粒什么都不会产——口试上这句比机制本身更得分。 细菌那一侧还有两个特有的零件：多顺反子 mRNA（每个基因自带 RBS，一个开关管一整套）和紧凑的启动子（−10／−35，σ 因子直接认）。 真核是扣帽扫描＋一整套通用转录因子，所以表达载体是分宿主的，两边不通用。
  6. 抗生素 · 耐药 · 群体行为 按靶点分五组，统一原理是选择性毒性：壁 · 核糖体 · 核酸 · 叶酸 · 膜。 她天天用的三个正好打三个靶：氨苄（转肽酶）· 卡那（30S）· 环丙沙星（DNA 旋转酶）。 ⚠ 抗生素不制造耐药，它筛选群体里本来就有的变异体。四种机制：毁药 · 修饰药 · 改靶点 · 挡在外面（外排泵）。 群体那一层：群体感应靠自诱导物＋阈值＋正反馈，所以是骤然开关；生物膜里的耐受是生理性的 tolerance，不是遗传性的 resistance。

Say this

Microbiology begins at the wall. Peptidoglycan, cross-linked by transpeptidases, holds the cell against turgor, and the Gram stain simply reads its thickness. A thick wall traps the crystal violet iodine complex through alcohol decolourisation and stays purple. A thin wall under a lipopolysaccharide outer membrane loses that complex and takes up safranin. The wall is also what ampicillin attacks; the other classes hit the ribosome, DNA gyrase (also called DNA gyrase, topoisomerase II (bacteria)) or folate synthesis, always by selective toxicity. Bacteria reproduce only by binary fission, passing through lag, exponential, stationary and death phases. Everything else — transformation, transduction, conjugation — is horizontal gene transfer, and that is why resistance spreads faster than mutation alone could explain. To name an organism I sequence the 16S ribosomal RNA gene, the molecule Carl Woese used to discover the Archaea.

约 45 秒 · 一根线：壁 → 染色 → 抗生素 → 怎么长 → 怎么传 → 怎么认

广度 · 32 题，一题一两句 · 看标题就能背 ⭐ 2 位考官：Hodek、Lipovová

① 差别只有一个，在壁上；染色结果和临床后果都是它的推论 Gram-positive vs Gram-negative

One difference, in the wall, and everything else follows from it. A Gram-positive cell has thick peptidoglycan outside a single membrane, with teichoic acids running through it. A Gram-negative cell has thin peptidoglycan in the periplasm, between an inner membrane and an outer membrane of lipopolysaccharide, which carries lipid A, the endotoxin.

先说壁，其余都是它的后果。

② 肽聚糖是唯一挡住膨压、不让细菌裂开的东西 peptidoglycan · murein

Peptidoglycan, also called murein, is the only thing stopping the cell from bursting, because a bacterium is hypertonic to its surroundings. It is chains of alternating N-acetylglucosamine and N-acetylmuramic acid, cross-linked by short peptides, and transpeptidases make those cross-links.

交联酶正是 β-内酰胺的靶。

③ 革兰染色四步，只有脱色那一步在真正干活 Gram stain

The step that does the work is the brief decolourisation with alcohol: the thick wall dehydrates and traps the complex, the thin wall cannot. I flood a heat-fixed smear with crystal violet, add iodine as a mordant, decolourise, then counterstain with safranin.

脱色过头或培养物太老，阳性读成阴性。

④ 荚膜给病原干两件事：抗吞噬，并且把底下的抗原藏起来 ⭐ Hodek capsule

A capsule does two things for the pathogen: it resists phagocytosis and it hides the surface antigens from antibodies. It is an organised polysaccharide layer outside the wall, firmly attached unlike a slime layer. Conjugate vaccines link capsular polysaccharide to a carrier protein, turning a weak response into a lasting one.

Hodek 做抗体；纯多糖疫苗无记忆，所以要做成 conjugate。

⑤ 一个细胞只出一个芽孢，所以这是存活反应，不是繁殖 endospore formation · sporulation

One cell gives one spore, so this is survival and not reproduction. The cell divides asymmetrically into a forespore and a mother cell, which engulfs the forespore. A cortex and protein coat are laid down, the core dehydrates and accumulates calcium dipicolinate, and small acid-soluble proteins protect the DNA.

抗性来自结构，所以高压灭菌要 121 °C。

⑥ 延滞期不是什么都没干：细胞在造新培养基需要的酶和核糖体 bacterial growth curve

Lag phase is not idle: the cells are making the enzymes and ribosomes the new medium needs. In exponential phase the doubling time is constant, in stationary phase division and death balance, and then the viable count falls in death phase. The curve plots the logarithm of cell number against time.

表达蛋白在对数中期诱导。

⑦ 测的是 OD₆₀₀，但它把死的也一起算进去，所以要另外做活菌计数 growth-curve experiment · OD600

Optical density at 600 nanometres measures light scattering, so it counts living and dead cells alike, and a viable count by dilution and plating is added when the two must be separated. I inoculate from a fresh overnight culture, incubate with shaking, and sample at fixed intervals against sterile medium.

出线性范围先稀释；对数期点要密，才拟合得出倍增时间。

⑧ 分离培养的目标是纯培养，而划线就是用接种环做的稀释 streak plating · pure culture

A colony is the clonal progeny of one colony-forming unit, so a single well-separated colony is the working definition of pure. I flame the loop between sectors, incubate, then re-streak one colony to confirm purity.

选择性培养基压住别的，鉴别性培养基让它们长得不一样。

⑨ 污染在站稳之前看不见，一旦长起来，后面每一个结果说的都是另一个物种 aseptic technique

Contamination is invisible until it has taken hold, and a faster-growing contaminant then takes over, so every result afterwards concerns the wrong species. Aseptic technique protects in both directions, my culture and me. I work near a flame, flame the loop and the bottle neck, and incubate an uninoculated control plate.

对照板长了，实验作废，不是打折。

⑩ 繁殖只有二分裂，水平基因转移和产孢都不算 binary fission

Reproduction in bacteria is binary fission and almost nothing else. The chromosome replicates from a single origin, the copies move to opposite poles, and a septum forms at mid-cell to give two identical daughters. A few species bud and filamentous bacteria fragment. Transformation, transduction, conjugation and sporulation do not raise the cell number.

「什么不是繁殖」那一句才是考官在等的。

⑪ 那些长名字是两条轴交叉拼出来的：能源在前，碳源在后 types of bacterial metabolism

The long names are two axes crossed, energy source then carbon source. Phototrophs use light and chemotrophs chemical oxidation, splitting into lithotrophs and organotrophs; autotrophs fix carbon dioxide and heterotrophs use organic carbon. So cyanobacteria are photoautotrophs, nitrifying bacteria chemolithoautotrophs, and Escherichia coli a chemoorganoheterotroph.

第三条轴是末端电子受体：氧／硝酸盐、硫酸盐／发酵。

⑫ 保守区旁边就紧挨着可变区 —— 一对通用引物就能从未知菌里扩出来，还不用培养 16S ribosomal RNA gene

What makes the 16S ribosomal RNA gene the standard gene for identifying bacteria is that its conserved regions sit next to variable regions: one universal primer pair amplifies it from an unknown organism, and the variable regions identify the species against a reference database. It is also essential and universal, so every bacterium has it.

优势是免培养，局限是常常只到属。

⑬ Woese 改的是方法：用序列而不是形态，结果把生命重画成三个域 Carl Woese · Archaea

The lasting change was the method: he compared 16S ribosomal RNA sequences rather than morphology, and molecular sequence became the basis of phylogeny. He found that organisms then called bacteria fell into two deeply separate groups, and life was reorganised into Bacteria, Archaea and Eukarya.

方法那一半直接接回 ⑫。

⑭ 生化试验靠一整组答案的模式定菌，不是任何单独一个试验 biochemical tests

Each test asks whether one enzyme is present. Catalase separates staphylococci from streptococci, oxidase separates Pseudomonas from the Enterobacteriaceae, and coagulase separates Staphylococcus aureus. Sugar fermentation is read as a colour change of a pH indicator.

前提是纯培养；分辨率不如 16S。

⑮ 三步只是三个温度，所以同一个耐热聚合酶能一轮一轮循环下去 PCR

The three steps are only three temperatures, which is why one thermostable polymerase can run the cycle over and over: denaturation near ninety-five degrees separates the strands, annealing lets two primers bind facing each other, and extension near seventy-two degrees builds the new strands. In microbiology the uses are diagnostic: 16S amplification, and quantitative PCR for viral load.

终点 PCR 只能说「有」，要说「多」得用 qPCR（= qPCR = real-time PCR (≠ RT-PCR)）。

⑯ 谱系是由基因组序列定义的，所以只有测序能定它，诊断用的 RT-qPCR 给不出来 SARS-CoV-2 lineage

A lineage is defined by genome sequence, so only sequencing can assign one. The genome is RNA, so I reverse transcribe it to cDNA, amplify the whole genome by tiled multiplex PCR, sequence the amplicons and assemble against the reference. The consensus sequence is then assigned a lineage from its mutations, using Pango nomenclature.

诊断用的 RT-qPCR 给不出 lineage。

⑰ 一个基因可以一步跳到很远的类群里，所以细菌进化不是一棵树 horizontal gene transfer

A gene can appear in a distant lineage in one step, so genomes are mosaic — a core genome plus an accessory genome — and evolution is not a tree. The three routes are transformation, uptake of free DNA by a competent cell; transduction, transfer by a bacteriophage; and conjugation, direct plasmid transfer through a pilus.

最清楚的例子是耐药基因跨种。

⑱ 天然的转化和台上做的转化是两回事，只是共用一个词 bacterial transformation

Two different things share the word. Naturally, a competent cell binds extracellular DNA, degrades one strand and imports the other, which homologous recombination integrates. At the bench I wash cells in ice-cold calcium chloride, heat-shock at forty-two degrees, recover in non-selective medium, then plate on selection.

电穿孔效率高两到三个数量级。

⑲ 加太多 DNA 会把细胞饱和，效率反而读低 —— 所以用少量已知的对照质粒 transformation efficiency · CFU/µg

I transform a small known mass of a supercoiled control plasmid, because too much DNA saturates the cells and reads falsely low. I plate a known fraction of a known dilution, count, then divide by the fraction plated and by the micrograms of DNA.

两个对照：不加 DNA 的板 ＋ 已知对照质粒。

⑳ 质粒的定义性质只有一条：自带复制起点，所以独立于染色体被复制 plasmid

The defining property is a single one: its own origin of replication, so it is copied independently of the chromosome. Everything it carries is accessory — antibiotic resistance, virulence factors, extra metabolic capabilities, and the transfer functions of a conjugative plasmid. As a vector it needs that origin, which also sets copy number, a selectable marker and a multiple cloning site.

同一个复制起点的两个质粒不相容。

㉑ 溶原周期就是 DE3 菌株的原理：噬菌体基因组作为前噬菌体待在染色体上 bacteriophage

The lysogenic cycle is what a DE3 strain is built on: the genome integrates as a prophage and sits there until stress induces lysis, while in the lytic cycle the cell lyses and releases progeny. The genome is injected while the capsid stays outside, which is the observation behind the Hershey and Chase experiment.

溶原正是 DE3 的原理。

㉒ 克隆按「接口是怎么做出来的」分组，不是按名字 ⭐ Lipovová types of cloning

Restriction–ligation cuts compatible ends and seals them with a ligase. Golden Gate uses type IIS enzymes cutting outside their recognition site, so I choose the overhang. Gibson assembly needs no restriction enzyme: homologous overlapping ends anneal in one isothermal reaction.

再顶三个：TA／TOPO · Gateway · RF cloning。

㉓ 克隆株靠两个突变各挡一件事：recA1 挡重排，endA1 挡质粒被降解 ⭐ Lipovová DH5α · XL10-Gold

Two mutations each remove one specific hazard. In DH5α, the workhorse, recA1 removes homologous recombination so repeats are not rearranged, and endA1 removes an endonuclease that would degrade plasmid during a miniprep. XL10-Gold or TOP10 strains are very highly competent and tolerate difficult DNA.

通则：克隆株 recA⁻ endA⁻，表达株带 DE3 且缺蛋白酶。

㉔ 宿主自己的聚合酶根本读不了 T7 启动子，所以整个控制隔了一层 ⭐ Lipovová DE3 cassette

The host's own polymerase cannot read a T7 promoter at all, so the control is one layer removed: IPTG relieves LacI repression at a lacUV5 promoter, the cell makes T7 RNA polymerase, and only then is my gene transcribed. DE3 is that λ prophage in the chromosome carrying the T7 polymerase gene.

菌株名里没有 (DE3)，pET 什么都不产。

㉕ 营养缺陷型的用处是：一个不需要抗生素的选择标记 auxotrophy

In the laboratory auxotrophy is a selectable marker needing no antibiotic: a strain deleted for LEU2 or URA3 grows only if a plasmid restores the gene, and it is also the readout of the yeast two-hybrid assay. An auxotroph cannot make some compound it needs, so the medium must supply it; the opposite is a prototroph, which grows on minimal medium.

酵母工作完全靠它。

㉖ 每个基因自带一个核糖体结合位点，所以细菌一条 mRNA 能编好几个蛋白；真核靠扫描，只能一个 polycistronic mRNA

Each gene on the transcript has its own ribosome binding site (also called Shine–Dalgarno sequence), so ribosomes initiate independently and one mRNA can encode several proteins. Several genes with related functions then share one promoter, as the lac operon does, and one switch controls the whole set. Eukaryotic mRNA is monocistronic, because initiation works by cap binding and scanning.

所以细菌操纵子搬进真核细胞表达不出来。

㉗ 细菌启动子被 σ 因子直接认出；真核的只能间接认出，要先搭一整套起始复合物 bacterial vs eukaryotic promoters

A bacterial promoter is read directly: two conserved elements near minus ten and minus thirty-five are recognised by the sigma factor of the holoenzyme, and swapping sigma factors switches whole gene sets at once. A eukaryotic core promoter such as a TATA box (also called Goldberg–Hogness box) is read only indirectly, needing general transcription factors to assemble a pre-initiation complex.

后果：表达载体分宿主，两边不通用。

㉘ 氨苄、卡那、环丙沙星打三个不同的靶，抗性每次都跟着靶点走 ampicillin · kanamycin · ciprofloxacin

Ampicillin, a beta-lactam, inhibits the transpeptidases cross-linking peptidoglycan, and beta-lactamase hydrolyses its ring. Kanamycin, an aminoglycoside, binds the 30S subunit and causes misreading; resistance phosphorylates the drug. Ciprofloxacin, a fluoroquinolone, inhibits DNA gyrase; resistance is a point mutation.

壁 · 核糖体 · DNA，三个都是杀菌性的。

㉙ 五组抗生素的统一原理是选择性毒性：打的都是细菌有、我们没有的东西 classes of antibiotics · selective toxicity

Every class hits something bacteria have and human cells do not, which is what selective toxicity means. Cell wall synthesis is hit by beta-lactams and by vancomycin; protein synthesis on the 30S by aminoglycosides and tetracyclines, on the 50S by macrolides and chloramphenicol. Nucleic acid synthesis is hit by fluoroquinolones and rifampicin, folate synthesis by sulfonamides and trimethoprim, and the outer membrane by polymyxins.

每一类打的都是细菌有、我们没有的东西。

㉚ 抗生素不制造耐药，它只是把群体里本来就有的变异体筛出来 antibiotic resistance

The antibiotic selects variants already present in the population rather than creating them; resistance arises by mutation or by horizontal gene transfer, and transfer dominates clinically. Resistance means growth at a concentration that inhibits other members of the species, by four mechanisms: destroy the drug, modify it, alter the target, or keep it out by low permeability and efflux pumps.

外排泵常常一次覆盖好几种不相关的药。

㉛ 阈值加正反馈，所以这个开关是「啪」一下开的，不是慢慢开 quorum sensing · autoinducer

A threshold plus positive feedback makes the switch turn on sharply rather than gradually: every cell constantly releases an autoinducer whose outside concentration rises with the population, and above a threshold it binds a regulator that switches on a gene set and also raises autoinducer synthesis. Gram-negative bacteria signal with acyl-homoserine lactones, Gram-positive with peptides.

经典例子：费氏弧菌发光 · 毒力因子 · 生物膜 · 感受态。

㉜ 生物膜难清不是因为耐药基因：深处的细胞长得慢，而药只对正在长的起作用 biofilm

What makes a biofilm hard to clear is not a resistance gene: the matrix limits penetration, deep cells grow slowly and so escape drugs acting on growing cells, and some are dormant persisters. The matrix is extracellular polymeric substances — polysaccharide, protein and extracellular DNA — and formation runs from reversible to irreversible attachment, then maturation and dispersal.

导管、植入物、心脏瓣膜之所以难清。

陷阱问法

  • "Do Gram-negative bacteria have peptidoglycan?" — Yes, a thin layer in the periplasm; only the thickness differs. · 有，只是薄。
  • "Is sporulation a form of reproduction?" — No. One cell gives one spore, so the cell number does not rise. · 不是繁殖。
  • "Does OD600 count living cells?" — No, it measures light scattering, so live and dead cells are counted together. · 死活一起算。
  • "Do you sequence the 16S rRNA?" — I sequence the gene, which is DNA, not the ribosomal RNA itself. · 测的是基因。
  • "Does the antibiotic create the resistance?" — No, it selects variants already present in the population. · 只是筛选。
  • "Is ampicillin active on cells that are not dividing?" — No, it blocks building of new wall, it does not destroy existing wall. · 只杀正在长的。
  • "Is anaerobic respiration the same as fermentation?" — No, anaerobic respiration keeps an electron transport chain（= respiratory chain = electron transport chain）; fermentation has none. · 两回事。
  • "Is biofilm resistance the same as antibiotic resistance?" — No, biofilm tolerance is physiological and reversible; resistance is genetic. · tolerance ≠ resistance。
  • "Which strain for a pET plasmid?" — One carrying DE3; without it there is no T7 RNA polymerase and nothing is made. · 没 DE3 不表达。

定义

  • peptidoglycan (murein) — the bacterial wall polymer, two alternating sugars cross-linked by short peptides · 肽聚糖
  • lipid A — the endotoxin part of Gram-negative lipopolysaccharide · 内毒素
  • colony-forming unit — one cell able to give rise to one colony · 菌落形成单位
  • transformation efficiency — colony-forming units obtained per microgram of plasmid DNA · 转化效率
  • competent cell — a cell in a state able to take up free DNA · 感受态细胞
  • auxotroph — an organism unable to make a compound it needs, so the medium must supply it · 营养缺陷型
  • prototroph — an organism that grows on minimal medium · 原养型
  • polycistronic mRNA — one messenger RNA encoding more than one protein · 多顺反子 mRNA
  • prophage — a phage genome integrated into the host chromosome · 前噬菌体
  • conjugative plasmid — a plasmid carrying the genes for its own transfer through a pilus · 接合型质粒
  • selective medium — medium that suppresses everything except the target organism · 选择性培养基
  • differential medium — medium on which several organisms grow but look different · 鉴别性培养基
  • autoinducer — the small diffusible signal molecule of quorum sensing · 自诱导物
  • persister — a dormant cell that survives a drug without being genetically resistant · 休眠存活细胞
  • selective toxicity — hitting a target bacteria have and human cells do not · 选择性毒性
术语表 · 这个主题里出现的名词，被问「那是什么」时一句话答完
  • −10 and −35 elements — the two conserved sequences upstream of a bacterial transcription start, recognised directly by the sigma factor · −10／−35 区
  • 16S ribosomal RNA gene — the gene for the small-subunit ribosomal RNA, present in every bacterium and used to identify species · 16S rRNA 基因
  • 30S subunit — the small subunit of the bacterial ribosome, the target of aminoglycosides and tetracyclines · 30S 小亚基
  • 50S subunit — the large subunit of the bacterial ribosome, the target of macrolides and chloramphenicol · 50S 大亚基
  • accessory genome — the genes present in only some members of a species, largely acquired by transfer · 附属基因组
  • acyl-homoserine lactone — the class of small signal molecule Gram-negative bacteria use for quorum sensing · 酰基高丝氨酸内酯
  • aminoglycoside — a class of antibiotic binding the 30S subunit and causing misreading, such as kanamycin · 氨基糖苷类
  • ampicillin — a beta-lactam antibiotic that blocks the transpeptidases cross-linking peptidoglycan · 氨苄青霉素
  • anaerobic respiration — respiration using an electron transport chain with something other than oxygen as the final acceptor · 无氧呼吸
  • annealing — the PCR step in which the two primers bind the template facing each other · 退火
  • antibiotic — a compound that kills bacteria or stops them growing · 抗生素
  • antibiotic resistance — the ability to grow at a drug concentration that inhibits other members of the same species · 耐药性
  • Archaea — one of the three domains of life, separated from the Bacteria by ribosomal RNA sequence · 古菌
  • aseptic technique — working so that nothing enters the culture and nothing escapes from it · 无菌操作
  • autotroph — an organism that builds its carbon compounds from carbon dioxide · 自养生物
  • bacteriophage — a virus that infects bacteria · 噬菌体
  • beta-lactam — the antibiotic class defined by a four-membered ring, which blocks cell wall cross-linking · β-内酰胺类
  • beta-lactamase — the bacterial enzyme that hydrolyses the beta-lactam ring and so destroys the drug · β-内酰胺酶
  • binary fission — one bacterial cell dividing into two identical daughters, the only way the cell number rises · 二分裂
  • biofilm — a community of cells attached to a surface and held in a matrix they secrete · 生物膜
  • calcium chloride — the salt cells are washed in before heat shock, whose divalent cation screens the repulsion to DNA · 氯化钙
  • calcium dipicolinate — the calcium salt that accumulates in the spore core as it dehydrates · 吡啶二羧酸钙
  • capsule — an organised polysaccharide layer outside the wall, firmly attached and resisting phagocytosis · 荚膜
  • Carl Woese — the scientist who compared ribosomal RNA sequences, discovered the Archaea and defined the three domains · 三域系统的提出者
  • catalase — the enzyme that breaks hydrogen peroxide down; its test separates staphylococci from streptococci · 过氧化氢酶
  • chemotroph — an organism taking its energy from chemical oxidation rather than from light · 化能营养型
  • chloramphenicol — an antibiotic acting on the 50S subunit · 氯霉素
  • ciprofloxacin — a fluoroquinolone antibiotic that inhibits DNA gyrase · 环丙沙星
  • coagulase — the enzyme whose test identifies Staphylococcus aureus · 凝固酶
  • colony — a visible mass of cells on a plate, all grown from one cell · 菌落
  • conjugate vaccine — a capsular polysaccharide linked to a carrier protein, which turns a weak response into a lasting one · 结合疫苗
  • conjugation — direct transfer of a plasmid from one bacterium to another through a pilus · 接合
  • consensus sequence — the sequence assembled from many reads by taking the most frequent base at each position · 共识序列
  • conserved region — a stretch of sequence nearly identical between species, so one primer pair binds them all · 保守区
  • core genome — the genes shared by every member of a species · 核心基因组
  • crystal violet — the purple first stain of the Gram procedure · 结晶紫
  • culture — cells growing in liquid medium or on a plate under controlled conditions · 培养物
  • DE3 — the lambda prophage in the chromosome carrying the T7 RNA polymerase gene under lac control · DE3 盒
  • death phase — the last part of the growth curve, where the viable count falls · 衰亡期
  • decolourisation — the brief alcohol wash of the Gram stain, which the thick wall survives and the thin wall does not · 脱色
  • denaturation — the PCR step, near ninety-five degrees, that separates the two strands · 变性
  • DH5α — the standard E. coli cloning strain, carrying recA1 and endA1 · DH5α 菌株
  • DNA gyrase — the bacterial topoisomerase that introduces negative supercoils, and the target of fluoroquinolones · DNA 旋转酶（= DNA gyrase = topoisomerase II (bacteria)）
  • efflux pump — a membrane transporter that pushes a drug back out, often covering several unrelated drugs at once · 外排泵
  • endA1 — the mutation removing an endonuclease that would otherwise degrade plasmid during a miniprep · endA1 突变
  • endospore — the dormant, highly resistant form some bacteria make when starved · 芽孢
  • Enterobacteriaceae — the Gram-negative family that includes E. coli, oxidase-negative · 肠杆菌科
  • exponential phase — the part of the growth curve where the doubling time is constant · 对数期
  • extension — the PCR step, near seventy-two degrees, in which the new strands are built · 延伸
  • extracellular polymeric substances — the biofilm matrix of polysaccharide, protein and extracellular DNA · 胞外聚合物
  • fermentation — energy metabolism with no electron transport chain, regenerating the carrier inside the cell · 发酵
  • fluoroquinolone — the antibiotic class inhibiting DNA gyrase, such as ciprofloxacin · 氟喹诺酮类
  • forespore — the smaller compartment of the asymmetric division, which the mother cell then engulfs · 前芽孢
  • Gibson assembly — joining fragments through homologous overlapping ends in one isothermal reaction, with no restriction enzyme · Gibson 组装
  • Golden Gate — assembly with type IIS enzymes that cut outside their recognition site, so the overhang is chosen · Golden Gate 组装
  • Gram stain — the four-step stain that reports the thickness of the peptidoglycan wall · 革兰染色
  • Gram-negative — a cell with thin peptidoglycan in the periplasm and a lipopolysaccharide outer membrane · 革兰阴性
  • Gram-positive — a cell with thick peptidoglycan outside a single membrane, threaded with teichoic acids · 革兰阳性
  • growth rate — how fast the cell number rises, a constant only during exponential phase · 生长速率
  • Hershey and Chase — Alfred Hershey and Martha Chase, who showed that a phage injects its DNA while the coat stays outside · 证明注入的是 DNA
  • heterotroph — an organism that takes its carbon from organic compounds · 异养生物
  • homologous recombination — exchange between two DNA molecules matching each other in sequence · 同源重组
  • horizontal gene transfer — a gene moving between cells that are not parent and offspring · 水平基因转移
  • inoculum — the culture material transferred in to start a new culture · 接种物
  • iodine — the mordant of the Gram stain, forming a large complex with crystal violet inside the cell · 碘液
  • kanamycin — an aminoglycoside binding the 30S subunit; resistance phosphorylates the drug · 卡那霉素
  • lac operon — the E. coli genes for lactose use, several proteins under one promoter · 乳糖操纵子
  • lacUV5 — the mutant lac promoter driving the T7 polymerase gene in a DE3 strain · lacUV5 启动子
  • lag phase — the first part of the growth curve, in which cells build the enzymes and ribosomes the new medium needs · 延滞期
  • LEU2 and URA3 — yeast genes for making leucine and uracil, deleted so a plasmid restoring them can be selected · 酵母营养缺陷标记
  • lipopolysaccharide — the molecule of the Gram-negative outer leaflet, sugar chains on a lipid A anchor · 脂多糖
  • lithotroph — a chemotroph that oxidises inorganic compounds for energy · 无机营养型
  • lysogenic cycle — the phage genome integrating into the chromosome and staying there until stress induces it · 溶原周期
  • lytic cycle — the phage replicating at once, bursting the cell and releasing progeny · 裂解周期
  • macrolide — the antibiotic class acting on the 50S subunit · 大环内酯类
  • minimal medium — medium supplying only salts and one carbon source, on which only a prototroph grows · 基本培养基
  • monocistronic — a messenger RNA encoding only one protein, as eukaryotic messages are · 单顺反子
  • mordant — a reagent that fixes a stain in place, iodine in the Gram stain · 媒染剂
  • multiple cloning site — a short stretch containing many unique restriction sites, for inserting a gene · 多克隆位点
  • N-acetylglucosamine — one of the two alternating sugars of the peptidoglycan chain · N-乙酰葡糖胺
  • N-acetylmuramic acid — the other sugar of that chain, the one carrying the short peptide · N-乙酰胞壁酸
  • operon — several genes transcribed from one promoter into one messenger RNA · 操纵子
  • organotroph — a chemotroph that oxidises organic compounds for energy · 有机营养型
  • outer membrane — the second membrane outside the Gram-negative wall, built of lipopolysaccharide on its outer face · 外膜
  • Pango — the naming system used for SARS-CoV-2 lineages · Pango 命名系统
  • pathogen — an organism that causes disease in its host · 病原体
  • periplasm — the compartment between the inner and outer membranes of a Gram-negative cell, holding its thin wall · 周质
  • pH indicator — a dye that changes colour with pH, used to read sugar fermentation · pH 指示剂
  • phagocytosis — a host cell engulfing a whole particle or bacterium · 吞噬作用
  • phototroph — an organism taking its energy from light · 光能营养型
  • pilus — a surface appendage, here the one through which a conjugative plasmid is transferred · 菌毛
  • plasmid — a DNA molecule with its own origin of replication, copied independently of the chromosome · 质粒
  • polymyxin — the antibiotic class acting on the Gram-negative outer membrane · 多粘菌素
  • Pseudomonas — a Gram-negative genus, oxidase-positive unlike the Enterobacteriaceae · 假单胞菌属
  • pure culture — a culture holding only one organism, obtained in practice from one well-separated colony · 纯培养
  • quantitative PCR — PCR read cycle by cycle, so the starting amount can be calculated rather than only detected · 定量 PCR
  • quorum sensing — cells switching a gene set on only once their own signal molecule passes a threshold · 群体感应
  • recA1 — the mutation removing homologous recombination, so repeated sequences on a plasmid are not rearranged · recA1 突变
  • restriction–ligation — classical cloning: cutting compatible ends with restriction enzymes and sealing them with ligase · 酶切-连接克隆
  • reverse transcription — copying RNA into DNA, needed before an RNA genome can be amplified · 逆转录
  • rifampicin — an antibiotic that blocks bacterial RNA synthesis · 利福平
  • safranin — the red counterstain of the Gram procedure, taken up by decolourised cells · 番红
  • septum — the wall that grows inward at mid-cell and separates the two daughter cells · 隔膜
  • sigma factor — the subunit of bacterial RNA polymerase that recognises the promoter, and swapping it switches whole gene sets · σ 因子
  • small acid-soluble proteins — the spore proteins that bind the DNA and protect it · 小分子酸溶性蛋白
  • sporulation — the starvation response in which one cell makes one endospore inside itself · 产芽孢
  • Staphylococcus aureus — a Gram-positive pathogen, separated from other staphylococci by a positive coagulase test · 金黄色葡萄球菌
  • stationary phase — the part of the growth curve where division and death balance, so the count stops rising · 稳定期
  • streak plating — spreading cells over a plate in sectors with a flamed loop, a dilution done with the loop · 划线分离
  • sulfonamide and trimethoprim — two antibiotics blocking folate synthesis at different steps · 磺胺与甲氧苄啶
  • T7 RNA polymerase — the phage polymerase that reads only T7 promoters, supplied by a DE3 strain · T7 RNA 聚合酶
  • TATA box — a eukaryotic core promoter element, read only indirectly through general transcription factors · TATA 框（= TATA box = Goldberg–Hogness box）
  • teichoic acid — the anionic polymer running through the thick wall of a Gram-positive cell · 磷壁酸
  • terminal electron acceptor — the molecule that takes the electrons at the end of a respiratory chain · 末端电子受体
  • tetracycline — an antibiotic class acting on the 30S subunit · 四环素类
  • thermostable polymerase — a DNA polymerase that survives the heating step, so one enzyme lasts the whole reaction · 耐热聚合酶
  • tiled multiplex PCR — amplifying a whole genome as many overlapping products in one reaction · 覆瓦式多重 PCR
  • tolerance — surviving a drug because of a physiological state, reversible and not carried in a gene · 耐受
  • TOP10 and XL10-Gold — very highly competent E. coli cloning strains, used for difficult DNA · 高效感受态克隆菌株
  • transduction — transfer of host DNA from one bacterium to another inside a phage particle · 转导
  • transformation — a cell taking up free DNA from outside · 转化
  • transpeptidase — the enzyme that makes the peptide cross-links in peptidoglycan, and the target of beta-lactams · 转肽酶
  • turgor pressure — the outward pressure of water entering the cell, which the wall has to resist · 膨压
  • vancomycin — an antibiotic blocking cell wall synthesis, not a beta-lactam · 万古霉素
  • variable region — the parts of the 16S gene that differ between species and carry the identification · 可变区
  • viable count — counting only living cells, by diluting and plating and counting the colonies · 活菌计数
  • Vibrio fischeri — the luminous marine bacterium in which quorum sensing was first described · 费氏弧菌
  • virulence factor — a molecule a pathogen uses to establish infection or to damage the host · 毒力因子
  • yeast two-hybrid — a method testing whether two proteins bind, read out by growth of an auxotrophic yeast · 酵母双杂交

⚠ 这一页只帮你想起「有哪几件事」。「为什么」在卡上—— 它替代的是「卡在第三个想不起第四个」那种空白，不是理解。
⚠ 临床那几个病名都是 general，你的教材不是医学教材，多半不问； 放这儿只因为成本低，万一问到就是白拿。
